# 五术联合 · 疾病

同一个问题,几门各自怎么看,按主题并排。每条一行,原文在各门自己的主题文件里。

One question, each art side by side, by topic. One line per rule; the source text is in each art's own topic file.

已收:六爻。还没收:梅花易数、大六壬、奇门遁甲、紫微斗数。

## 总则(Principles: whose illness, which line stands for the patient, strong and weak)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/总则.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSQS.04** 诸爻持世诀说：官鬼爻持世，事情难得安稳；占自身，就算没有病，也会遇上官司；财物时时担心丢失，亲人分离了很难再聚。
  The verse on each line holding the Self line says: with the Pressure line holding the Self line, matters are hard to settle; if one divines about oneself, even without illness one meets an official case; property is always at risk of loss, and kin who are parted are hard to bring together again.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.05** 六亲持世歌里讲病的几句：父母爻持世，占胎产、问疾病反而凶；子孙爻持世，占胎产、问疾病重重有喜；官鬼爻持世，占婚姻、问疾病都是凶兆；财爻持世，若占病、占产，官鬼爻就是胎。
  The lines on illness in the verse on the six relations holding the Self line: with Resource holding the Self line, divining a pregnancy or asking about illness is ominous; with Output holding the Self line, divining a pregnancy or asking about illness brings joy upon joy; with Pressure holding the Self line, divining marriage or asking about illness are both ill omens; with Wealth holding the Self line, if divining illness or birth, Pressure is the womb.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.22** 断易总论里讲病的几句：要消散官事、医治疾病，全靠子孙；月破落在用爻上，做事没有结果，病不得痊愈；五墓落在世爻或卦身上，问病是一生九死。
  The lines on illness in the general discussion of Duanyi: to disperse official matters and to treat illness, rely wholly on Output; with the Month-break falling on the Subject line, nothing done comes to completion and the illness is not cured; with the Five Tombs on the Self line or the Body, in asking about illness it is nine deaths to one life.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.24** 阐幽精要的身命章说：父母爻持世，一生辛勤劳碌；兄弟爻持世，疾病连绵。
  The chapter on the person and life in the Chanyou Jingyao says: with Resource holding the Self line one toils and labours; with Peer holding the Self line, illness drags on.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.31** 疾病论一篇开头说：凡是占疾病，先向天神地祇祷告；去问生死祸福，要把得病的缘由问得详细，再告诉他前贤应验过的例子。
  The chapter on illness opens: in divining illness, first pray to the spirits above and below; in asking about life and death, calamity and fortune, the cause of the illness must be learned in detail, and then the confirmations of the former sages told.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.01** 易卦发蒙歌说：克我的是鬼（官鬼）。自注说：凡是疾病，都看这一爻，从它知道鬼神的情状；比如金宫的卦，见到巳、午的火爻就是。
  The Yigua Fameng Ge says: what controls me is the ghost (Pressure). Its own note adds: in all illness look to this line, and from it know the state of the ghosts and spirits; in a Metal-palace hexagram, for instance, the Fire lines of Si and Wu are it.
- **LY.ILL.CA.01** 疾病先要看身和命：遇到青龙、见到子孙，可以放心。妻财入了命，多半沉重；官鬼临在身上，更加不堪。身犯勾陈，凶是有准的；命上加了玄武，痊愈就难。螣蛇、白虎，忧的是丧事；身命落空亡，一定入棺。金鬼值身，伤骨节；身临水鬼，血液浓稠聚积；贴着身的火鬼，疮痍烦恼；木鬼在身宫，百骨酸痛；土鬼在身，身体肿胀；杀和刑加在命上，命被摧残。
  In illness first look at the Body and the natal line: meeting the Dragon and seeing Output, set the heart at ease. Wealth entering the natal line: mostly heavy; the Pressure line sitting on the Body: still worse. The Body offending Hook Earth: ill is certain; the natal line with the Dark Tortoise added: recovery is surely hard. The Coiling Snake and White Tiger: worry of a funeral; the Body and natal line Void: he will surely enter the coffin. A Metal ghost on the Body: injury to the bone joints; the Body on a Water ghost: blood thick and gathered; a Fire ghost close to the Body: the vexation of sores; a Wood ghost in the Body palace: all the bones ache; an Earth ghost on the Body: the body swells; a killing and punishment added to the natal line: life is ruined.
- **LY.ILL.CA.04** 凡是有人占疾病，先从卦里找本命，还要看来占的是什么人，克它的又是什么、加在世应上。福德持世，鬼暂时退去；官鬼旺相的日子又进一步。卦里没有鬼，病难医；鬼旺、财兴，难保命。鬼杀临在身上、又有五墓侵犯，鬼神不应验，药也无灵。第一爻是五脏，第二爻是皮肉，第三爻是体骨、腰和足；第四爻是肺和心经，第五、第六爻是头。游魂卦恍惚，像被神鬼触到；只有归魂卦，渐渐向安；吉凶我细细地为你嘱咐。
  If someone divines an illness, first look in the hexagram for the natal line, and still look at who has come, and what it is that controls, added to the Self and Other. Output holding the Self: the ghost retreats for a time; on a day when the Pressure line is strong it advances again. With no ghost in the hexagram the illness is hard to treat; with the ghost strong and Wealth rising life is hard to keep. A ghost-killing sitting on the Body with the Five Tombs invading: the ghosts and spirits do not respond and medicine has no effect. The first line is the five viscera, the second skin and flesh, the third body, bones, waist and feet; the fourth is the lungs and heart channel, the fifth and sixth the head. The Wandering Soul hexagram: dazed, as if touched by spirits; only the Returning Soul gradually turns to peace; good and ill I tell you in detail.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.02** 占疾病，先在卦里找出病人的本命爻，再看来占的是什么人；福德持世，鬼暂时退去，可是爻旺的日子又进一步；卦里没有鬼，病难医；鬼旺、财兴，命难保；鬼煞临在身上又加了五墓，向鬼神祷告也不必了，药也无灵。
  In divining illness, first find the sick person's natal line in the hexagram and look at who has come to divine. With Output holding the Self the ghost retreats for a time, but on a day when the line is strong it advances again. With no ghost in the hexagram the illness is hard to treat; with the ghost strong and Wealth rising life is hard to keep. A ghost-killing sitting on the Body with the Five Tombs added: there is no use praying to ghosts and spirits, and medicine has no effect.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.09** 占病先看宅的衰旺，再看六神吉凶的象，还要看本属爻有没有，以及各种杀有没有动着临在身爻和世爻上。
  In divining illness first look at the strength or weakness of the house, then at the good or ill images of the six spirits, and look further at whether the line of its own kind is there and at the killings moving and sitting on the Body and the Self.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.19** 问病要知道病因：卦静的话先找世下伏的神，再看鬼爻藏伏在哪里，再拿发动的爻去察它的真相。用爻是病人，宜有气；福德是医师，喜欢贴着身；子孙发动，不要服药；卦宫旺相，病就迟迟拖延。六位没有财，吃不进东西；兄弟交重，气积在里面；鬼多、不止一个，原来就有病；用爻发动却休囚，损伤病人。
  In asking about illness one must know its cause: with the hexagram still first look for the spirit hidden under the Self, then look at where the ghost line lies hidden, and further examine the truth by the moving lines. The Subject is the sick person and should have qi; Output is the physician and likes to be close to the Body; with Output moving do not take medicine, and with the hexagram's palace strong the illness lingers. With no Wealth in the six places he cannot take food; with Peer crossing heavily the qi piles up; with many ghosts, not just one, there was an illness from before; with the Subject moving while resting or trapped, it harms the sick person.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.20** 问病应先问占卜的人是谁：父母爻现，说的是父母；官鬼临它，忌讳旺相；子孙克世，药不灵。财是禄命，忌被飞爻克；印绶交重，病困顿沉重；最忌世和官乘着月建，又嫌墓爻发动克它的身。鬼在内，夜里重；官在外，夜里轻；内外都有鬼，人昏沉困顿，不然就是旧病再来侵。代占最怕应爻持鬼，官的墓持在身上，命也倾覆。
  In asking about illness first ask who the person divining is: the Resource line appearing means the parents; the Pressure line sitting on it must not be strong; Output controlling the Self means the medicine has no effect. Wealth is the salary and life and must not be attacked by a flying line; with the seal ribbon (Resource) crossing heavily the illness is deep and heavy; it is worst if the Self and the ghost ride the Month, and it also dislikes a tomb line moving to attack the Body. With the ghost inside the night is heavy, with the ghost outside the night is light; with ghosts both inside and outside the person is dazed and worn, or else an old illness comes again to invade. In divining for another the worst is the Other line holding a ghost, and with the tomb of the ghost line holding the Body life too is overturned.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.22** 小畜、未济、家人、旅、讼、益、颐这七个卦，卦里都没有鬼；占病的时候，可喜也可悲。
  Xiaochu, Weiji, Jiaren, Lü, Song, Yi (Increase) and Yi (Nourishment): in all these seven hexagrams there is no ghost; in asking after illness it may be glad and it may be sad.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.24** 官鬼这一爻没有形体却有用处，卦里不能没有，宜静不宜动；带着吉神动也能有福，加上凶煞动没有不成祸的。占病而卦里没有鬼，一定没有可以叩告（祷告）的门路，这是天年命数到头了，病难疗。
  Pressure has no form but has its use; it must not be absent from the hexagram, and should be still and not move; moving with a lucky spirit it can also bring fortune, moving with a fierce star it always brings calamity. In divining illness with no ghost in the hexagram, there is surely no door to knock at and pray, for the heavenly years and the fate are used up, and the illness is hard to treat.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.49** 总断歌里说到病的几句：父亲有病，忌讳大杀；没有鬼，病难以治疗；鬼旺，主发狂；请看考察鬼历，祷告酬谢，得到安康。
  The lines of the song of general judgments that speak of illness: for a father's illness the Great Killing is disliked; with no ghost the illness is hard to treat; with the ghost strong it means madness; look up and examine the ghost calendar, pray and give thanks, and health and peace are gained.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.51** 彭城的密诀把空亡落在各处的后果编成了一首歌诀，其中管病的有这几句：病落了空亡，适合做福事（断易天机明写是「暴病」，卜筮大全和火珠林只写「病」）；久病落了空亡，断易天机写「身不真」，卜筮大全和火珠林写「身下亡」；父母爻落了空亡，要担心生病。断易天机另有一句「女值空亡忧病生」，另外两本没有。
  The secret verse of Pengcheng sets out, in a song, what follows when each thing falls Void. The lines that concern illness: when the illness falls Void it is fitting to perform blessing rites (the Duanyi Tianji says outright 'sudden illness'; the Bushi Daquan and the Huozhu Lin say only 'illness'); when a long illness falls Void, the Duanyi Tianji reads 'the body is not true' while the Bushi Daquan and the Huozhu Lin read 'the body goes down and is lost'; when Resource falls Void, worry that illness will arise. The Duanyi Tianji alone adds the line 'if a woman falls Void, worry that illness will arise'.
- **LY.ILL.EL.08** 以用神为主，元神为凭。占疾病，必须求用象兴旺；元神旺相又发动，一定亨通；忌神千万不要在发动的位置上；日、月到来，要配合它的克与生；动的要看变爻才知道去留；没有（用象）就找伏象，才知道亏盈。注说：凡是占疾病，专看用爻，卦里如果用爻有气、元神发动、忌神安静，就没有妨碍；忌神怕动，但如果元神同时发动，转过来帮助用爻，病反而安稳；卦里元神不动，闲神又休囚，再遇上月建或日辰来克，也主倾危。
  The Subject is the main line, and the Support is the ground. In illness one must seek a thriving Subject image; the Support strong and moving surely means success; the Adversary must on no account be in a moving position; when the Day and Month arrive, match them with their control and generation; for a moving line look at the changed line to know whether it goes or stays; if there is none, search for a hidden image to sense its waning and waxing. The commentary says: in divining illness look only at the Subject line; if in the hexagram the Subject line has qi, the Support moves and the Adversary is still, there is no obstruction; the Adversary should not move, but if the Support moves with it, it turns to help the Subject line and the illness on the contrary settles; if the Support in the hexagram does not move, the idle spirits are resting or trapped, and the Month or Day also controls it, danger of collapse is indicated.
- **LY.ILL.EL.10** 卦里如果没有用象，就要察看伏神，再推日、月；日、月也没有主象、伏神又被刑伤的，再查互卦；互卦里体、用两爻也没有用神的，才说没有救。主卦虽然没有用象，如果伏神出来而没有受伤，一定不会损命；伏神出来即使遭刑克，再远查日、月和互卦之中有一个用爻，也没有害处。
  If the hexagram has no Subject image, examine the hidden line, and further the Day and Month; if the Day and Month too lack the chief image and the hidden line is wounded by punishment, then examine the nuclear trigrams; only if the body and use lines within the nuclear trigrams also have no Subject can one say there is no rescue. Though the main hexagram has no Subject image, if the hidden line comes out unharmed, life is surely not lost; even if the hidden line, when it comes out, is punished or controlled, if further examination of the Day, Month and nuclear trigrams finds one Subject line, there is no harm.
- **LY.ILL.EL.13** 不要用卦名去推祸福，不要拿神煞去断忧祯。注说：其中有拿卦名来论的，切不可；经里说，易卦的渊源是论五行阴阳的道理，本来生生不息，可怜愚昧无知的人颠倒阴阳去论卦名，如今有人拿神煞来论的也不对；经里又说，易卦阴阳在变通，五行生克妙无穷，现在的人不明白阴阳的道理，拿神煞来定吉凶。比如儿子占父亲的病，父爻旺相，墓门煞或大煞又动，用爻有气，怎么会死？又如丈夫占妻子的病，财爻无气，父爻兄弟爻都动，纵然月解、天医同时发动，用爻既然受了伤，怎么能不推断为死？所以以五行为重，神煞难以凭信，星家专门忌讳本命空亡，这不是正道。
  Do not push fortune and misfortune by the names of hexagrams, and do not decide grief and omen by spirit-stars. The commentary says: those who judge by hexagram names must on no account be followed; the classic says the source of the Yi hexagrams is to judge by the principles of the five phases and yin and yang, which by nature give birth without end, and pitiable ignorant people turn yin and yang upside down and judge by hexagram names, and those who now judge by spirit-stars are also wrong; the classic says again that in the hexagrams yin and yang lie in change, and the generation and control of the five phases are marvellous without end, but people now do not understand the principle of yin and yang and use the spirit-stars to fix fortune. For example, if a son divines a father's illness and the Resource line is strong, and the Tomb Gate star or the Great Killing also moves, the Subject line having qi, how could he die? Again, if a husband divines a wife's illness and the Wealth line has no qi, and the Resource and Peer lines both move, even if Month Release and Heavenly Doctor move together, the Subject being already wounded, how can one judge otherwise than death? Therefore the five phases are weighty and the spirit-stars hard to rely on, and that astrologers particularly fear the Void of the natal branch is not the right way.
- **LY.ILL.EL.17** 自己占病，先凭世爻的象，再凭卦身，怕遇上月破、旬空，喜欢见到生、扶、拱、合；克世的爻是忌客，来生世的象是元神；随官入墓，灾难痊愈；助鬼伤身，命必沉沦。注说：自己占自己的病，专看世，爻如果临月破，不论新病旧病，命也难保全。世如果落空，要分缓急：急症还能医，旧病就不救；如果空中有气，眼下无妨，后来难保。世象不临旬空月破，再看旺衰：世如果休囚，又被动爻来克、或遭日月来伤、或变成死绝，毫无救助的爻，怎么会不是天年命尽？世即使休囚，如果得到动爻生助、或遇日月扶持、或变为有气，纵然临危也不会死；世如果旺相，虽然没有生助的爻，也没有什么害处。凡是克世的叫忌象，宜静不宜动；生世的叫元神，宜旺不宜空。占他人的病，身、世随官入墓，不必忌讳，命随鬼入墓就凶了。只有自己占身、世，命随鬼入墓的命不会回生，助鬼伤身也是同样的道理；又看卦身，如果墓、绝于月建，或者墓、绝于变爻，一定没有救了。
  In divining one's own illness, first rely on the image of the Self, then on the Body; it fears meeting Month-break or Void, and is glad to see generation, support, flanking and combination; the line that controls the Self is the adversary guest, and the image that comes to generate it is the Support; following the Pressure line into a tomb, the illness is hard to cure; helping the ghost to harm the Self, life surely sinks. The commentary says: in divining one's own illness, look at the Self alone; if the Self line sits in Month-break, whether the illness is new or old, life is hard to preserve. If the Self is Void, distinguish slow from urgent: an urgent illness can still be treated, an old illness is not saved; if there is qi in the Void, there is no harm for the moment, but later it is hard to keep. If the Self image does not sit in Void or Month-break, look next at strength and weakness: if the Self is resting or trapped and is controlled by a moving line, or wounded by the Day or Month, or changes into death or Extinction, with no line of rescue at all, how can it be anything but the end of one's allotted years? Though the Self is resting or trapped, if a moving line generates and helps it, or the Day and Month support it, or it changes into qi, even at the brink it does not die; if the Self is strong, even without a generating line there is no harm. Whatever controls the Self is called an adversary image and should be still, not moving; whatever generates the Self is called the Support and should be strong, not Void. In divining another's illness, the Body and Self following the Pressure line into a tomb need not be feared, but the natal branch following the ghost into a tomb is ominous. Only in divining for oneself, with the Body and Self, does a natal branch following the ghost into a tomb mean life will not be restored, and helping the ghost to harm the Self is the same in principle; look also at the Body: if it is in a tomb or Extinction under the Month, or in a tomb or Extinction under the changed line, there is surely no rescue.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.01** 占问灾病，先要问是什么人得病：父母爻出现，说的就是父母的事；官鬼临上去，愁它旺相；子孙克世爻，吃药没有灵验。财是禄命，怕被飞爻克制（原文「忘非克」，含混）；父母（印绶）交重发动，病重而困顿；最忌世爻落空又遇月破，也嫌墓爻发动伤了身体。眉批说：父亲得病要看病（官鬼）；鬼怕旺相；子克世爻，服药不灵；财是禄命，印绶发动命沉，世空、月破、墓绝都凶。
  In asking about an illness or calamity, first ask whose it is: if Resource appears, it is the parents' matter that is set out; if Pressure sits on it, one worries about its being strong; Output controlling the Self line makes medicine ineffective. Wealth is the salary and life and fears being controlled by the flying line (the text reads 'wang fei ke', which is unclear); Resource (the seal ribbon) crossing and moving heavily makes the illness heavy and trapped; most to be avoided is the Self Void together with Month-break, and the tomb line moving to harm the body is also disliked. The marginal comment says: for a father's illness, look at the illness; the ghost fears strength; if Output controls the Self line, taking medicine is ineffective; Wealth is the salary and life, the seal ribbon moving means life sinks, and the Self Void, Month-break, tomb and Extinction are all ominous.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.10** 用爻伏着、被上面的飞神克制，如果用爻不动，就要日辰旺，把它生出来或者冲出来，飞神无气的日子是生不出来的；用爻有动的，在克它的那一日旺相、生出它，也可以取。书里的例子：占妻的病，申日得比之坤，五爻发动，癸亥伏在戌的下面，本来不好，却得到日辰能生扶它出来，死中得生。
  If the Subject line is hidden and controlled by the flying line above it, then if it is still it needs the Day to be strong to generate it out or clash it out, and on a day when the flying line has no qi it cannot be generated; if it moves, being strong on the day that controls it and generated out can also be taken. The book's example: divining a wife's illness, on a Shen day the hexagram Bi changing to Kun, the fifth line moves, and Guihai lies hidden under Xu; it was originally not good, but the Day was able to generate and support it out, and life was found in death.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.17** 海底眼的六亲持世一节，鬼世一首：官鬼持世事难安（眉批：鬼持世是祸殃）；占身不病也要遭官司（眉批：病、官司都因官鬼）。
  In the section on the six relations holding the Self in the Haidi Yan, the verse for the ghost holding the Self: with Pressure holding the Self, affairs are hard to settle (comment: the ghost holding the Self is misfortune); in divining oneself, even if not ill one meets official trouble (comment: illness and lawsuits both come from Pressure).
- **LY.ILL.HJC.01** 占病时把官鬼爻当作凶恶的煞：病的症状轻重、得病的根由，都看这一爻；卦里只有一爻独自发动的，也可以照它来推。
  In divining illness the Pressure line is taken as the malign killer: how heavy or light the symptoms are, and how the illness was contracted, are both read from this line; a line that moves alone in the hexagram can also be reasoned from in the same way.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.33** 再看主象，也就是用神，就是病人：占丈夫用官鬼，占妻子用妻财。旧注还说，自己占以世爻为主象，替别人占以应爻为主象。主象怕遇到刑、冲、克、害，那就是病人被病折磨；被克害的地方如果得到生扶（旧注：得世爻生合、旁边的爻扶持），一定不至于死。
  Next look at the main image, which is the Subject and stands for the patient: in divining a husband take Pressure, in divining a wife take Wealth. The old commentary adds that in a divination for oneself the Self line is the main image, and in a divination for another the Other line is. The main image fears meeting punishment, clash, control or harm, which is the patient being ground down by the illness; but if the place of harm is generated and supported (the old commentary: by the Self line generating or combining with it and the lines beside it supporting it), he will surely not die.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.121** 《黄金策》身命章说：「鬼爻持世，疾病缠绵。」旧注解释：官鬼是殃祸，遇到它，一生疾病缠身，或者带着病，或者常常招来官司；如果是贵人遇到，就不这样断。王洪绪说：官是祸殃，遇到主带疾病、招官讼，贵人并临则贵。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on the person and fate says: 'With the ghost line holding the Self line, illness lingers.' The old commentary explains: Pressure is calamity, and meeting it means illness clinging to the body for a whole life, either carrying an illness or often drawing a lawsuit; if a person of rank meets it, it is not judged this way. Wang Hongxu says: Pressure is calamity, and meeting it means carrying illness or drawing a lawsuit, but if a noble star is on it as well, it means rank.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.149** 《黄金策》总断千金赋说：「日辰是六爻的主宰，喜的是它能灭项以安刘。」旧注：日辰是卜筮的主宰，不看日辰就不知道轻重；日辰能冲动安静的爻、合起安静的爻，发动的它能制住，凶恶的能压抑，强旺的能挫折，衰弱的能扶持；所以日辰能救事、也能坏事，是六爻的主宰。书中例：七月乙未日占兄弟的病，得同人变无妄，卦中鬼爻动克主象、绝爻又动，凶是可以想见的；幸好日辰是未土，克坏了官爻、合起了主象，后来果然无事。王洪绪的说法一样，并说：如果忌神旺动、用神休囚，得日辰克制忌神、生扶用神，凡事转凶为吉。
  The Huangjin Ce's Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements says: 'The Day is the master of the six lines, and one welcomes how it destroys Xiang to secure Liu.' The old commentary: the Day is the master of divination, and without looking at the Day one does not know what is light and what is heavy; the Day can clash a still line into motion and combine and raise it, can restrain what moves, suppress what is ominous, break down what is strong and support what is weak; so the Day can save an affair and can spoil an affair, and is the master of the six lines. A case in the book: on a Yiwei day in the seventh month a brother's illness was divined and Tongren changing to Wuwang obtained; in the hexagram the Pressure line moved and controlled the principal image and the Extinction line moved too, so the ill omen could be imagined; fortunately the Day was Wei Earth, which broke the Pressure line and combined the principal image into life, and in the end all was well. Wang Hongxu says the same, and adds: if the Adversary is strong and moving and the Subject is resting or trapped, and the Day controls the Adversary and supports the Subject, in everything ill turns to good.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.01** 占疾病，应是药，世是身；世若坐在墓、鬼上，病主昏沉。卦有三墓（宫墓、鬼墓、财墓），以世为身，忌讳生鬼爻；本宫的墓、鬼落在世爻上，主自身该有灾：暴病还不能好，久病必死。以应为药，忌讳坐着鬼爻，旺相就凶；本宫的墓、鬼落在应上，主没有药，服药不见效。最怕申、酉爻持世，还有木爻独发、鬼爻旺相伏在世下、旺又动来克世。财墓是吉兆，占的是妇人时遇到它却要大忌；鬼旺伏在世下，是病跟着我，所以忌。
  In divining illness the Other is the medicine and the Self is the Body; if the Self sits on a tomb or a ghost the illness means stupor. A hexagram has three tombs (the palace tomb, the ghost's tomb and the Wealth tomb); taking the Self as the Body it is taboo to generate the Pressure line; when the own palace's tomb or ghost falls on the Self line the person is due a calamity: a sudden illness cannot yet recover and a long illness surely ends in death. Taking the Other as the medicine, it is taboo for it to sit on a Pressure line and if strong it is ominous; when the own palace's tomb or ghost falls on the Other there is no medicine and taking it has no effect. Most feared are a Shen or You line holding the Self, a Wood line moving alone, and the Pressure line strong and hidden under the Self, strong and also moving to control the Self. The Wealth tomb is a lucky sign, but when a woman is being divined for, meeting it is a great taboo; a strong ghost hidden under the Self is the illness following me, and so it is a taboo.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.04** 以财为禄，以鬼为祟；鬼爻旺相、独发，大忌。占妇人病，喜子孙旺相、持世、安静；忌财伏在鬼下、兄弟持世、兄弟独发。世克应、内克外，主吐；应克世、外克内，主泻。问答：妇人病喜子孙旺相、世安，是用财而以子孙辅助的道理；忌财伏鬼、兄弟持世，是用财伏在兄弟下的道理。内克外为什么主吐？内是腹、外是口；外克内主泻。
  Take Wealth as the salary and the ghost as the curse; the Pressure line strong and moving alone is a great taboo. In divining a woman's illness, it is welcome for Output to be strong, holding the Self and still; the taboos are Wealth hidden under the ghost, Peer holding the Self and Peer moving alone. The Self controlling the Other, the inner controlling the outer, means vomiting; the Other controlling the Self, the outer controlling the inner, means diarrhoea. Questions and answers: that in a woman's illness Output strong and the Self at peace is welcome is the principle of taking Wealth as the Subject with Output to help it; that Wealth hidden under the ghost and Peer holding the Self are taboo is the principle of the Subject Wealth hidden under Peer. Why does the inner controlling the outer mean vomiting? The inner is the belly and the outer is the mouth; the outer controlling the inner means diarrhoea.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.05** 主爻伏在鬼下，或伏在兄弟下，或伏在父母下，旺相是大忌。卦乱动时只取主爻：大抵休囚、伏在兄弟、父母、官鬼之下、又克世的，会死；因为兄弟没有食、父母没有药、官鬼是真病。凡得八纯卦、游魂卦，病人一定沉重，占小儿主死。问答：主爻伏鬼、伏兄伏父之下，就是财伏兄、财伏父母、官伏兄的道理，举一隅而三隅反；八纯、游魂、归魂三种卦，世持父母、官鬼、兄弟，或子孙伏在父母下，占大人是病重，占小儿是病死。
  The Subject line hidden under the ghost, or under Peer, or under Resource: if strong it is a great taboo. When the hexagram moves confusedly take only the Subject line: mostly one that is resting or trapped, hidden under Peer, Resource or the Pressure line and also controlling the Self, will die; for Peer has no food, Resource has no medicine and the Pressure line is a real illness. Whoever gets a pure hexagram or a Wandering Soul hexagram, the sick person is surely heavy, and for a small child it means death. Questions and answers: the Subject line hidden under the ghost, under Peer or under Resource is the principle of Wealth hidden under Peer, Wealth hidden under Resource, the official hidden under Peer, and 'raise one corner and the other three come back'; in the three kinds of hexagram, pure, Wandering Soul and Returning Soul, with the Self holding Resource, the Pressure line or Peer, or Output hidden under Resource, divining an adult it is a heavy illness and divining a small child the illness ends in death.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.07** 占病一定要察看用爻，占父母就一定要父母有气。纵然遇到凶卦，也只主沉重，不致丧亡；若用爻空亡或不上卦，又遇凶杀，一定不起。用爻无气，若有旁爻动来生扶，就和生旺一样，决没有灾。若凶杀临在父母上，或父母空，就可以说双亲有病，别的爻也一样。鬼爻持世，沉重，绝日轻可。鬼化鬼，病忽进忽退，或有变病，或旧病再发，或症候驳杂，一卦有两个鬼也一样。鬼爻持世，病难除根；鬼带杀持世，是瘵病，难脱体，是养老的病。
  In divining illness one must look at the Subject line; divining a parent the Resource line must have qi. Even meeting an ominous hexagram it only means heaviness and does not lead to death; but if the Subject line is Void or not in the hexagram and meets a killing as well, he surely does not rise. A Subject line without qi, if another line moves to generate and support it, is the same as strong and there is surely no harm. If a killing sits on Resource, or Resource is Void, one can say a parent is ill, and the other lines are the same. The Pressure line holding the Self: heavy, and light on a day of Extinction. A ghost changing into a ghost: the illness advances and retreats, or there is a changed illness, or an old illness breaks out again, or the symptoms are mixed, and two ghosts in one hexagram is the same. The Pressure line holding the Self: the illness is hard to root out; a ghost bringing a killing and holding the Self is a wasting illness that is hard to shake off, the illness of old age.
- **LY.ILL.TXF.01** 国家的治乱，靠的是风俗纪纲；人的寿夭，不在身体强弱。养生不得法，终究会有疾病；请祷能占卜，就能看出死生的决断。凶杀临在父母爻上，要为堂上的双亲担忧；妻财爻落空，要为闺中的妻子担心灾祸。注说：占疾病，先看来占的是什么人：儿子占父母，要父母爻上卦；妻子占丈夫看鬼爻；丈夫占妻子看财爻；父亲占儿子看子孙；占兄弟、占朋友都看兄弟爻；占奴仆也看子孙爻。用爻不上卦或空亡的，多凶少吉；哪一爻空亡、哪一爻受克，吉凶就照这个来断。
  That a state is in order or disorder is nothing but a matter of customs and discipline; whether a person's life is short or long is not argued from frailty or strength. If one does not nourish life in the proper way, in the end there will be illness; if prayers can be divined, life and death can be seen decided. A killing sitting on Resource: worry for the parents in the hall; Void reaching Wealth: fear calamity for the woman in the inner chamber. The commentary says: in divining illness, first look at who the person is who has come to divine: a son divining about his parents needs the Resource line in the hexagram; a wife divining about her husband looks at the ghost, a husband about his wife at Wealth, a father about his son at Output; in divining about a brother or a friend, look at the Peer line; in divining about a servant, also look at Output. If the Subject line is absent from the hexagram or Void, there is mostly ill and little good; whichever line is Void, whichever is controlled, good and ill are judged by this.
- **LY.ILL.TXF.14** 卦爻安静，重（zhòng）为济世之人；土杀一齐兴动，一定成了黄泉的客人。注说：占病，六爻安静就吉，病可以治；一卦之中凶杀常常多，而吉神常常少，吉凶悔吝都出自动，动就成咎。比如六亲之中，不过子孙动的吉；六神之中，不过青龙动的吉；其余的未必都是凶，也不能成为福，发动对我有什么益处呢？这是吉少凶多。不如卦爻安静，虽然有凶煞，终究没有遭到伤害；如果有吉神当权，自然能为福。土杀就是辰、戌、丑、未，交相变换，运行在四季之间，占病遇上它，多凶少吉。所有诸煞都见后面的凶煞章；如果有一件发动，就成了凶咎。
  With the hexagram and lines still, weighty is he who saves the world; with Earth and the killings both rising, he is sure to be a guest of the Yellow Springs. The commentary says: in divining illness, six lines still is auspicious and the illness can be treated; in a hexagram the malign killings are often many and the lucky spirits often few; good and ill, regret and shame all come out of movement, and movement makes blame. For example, among the six relations only Output moving is auspicious; among the six spirits only the Azure Dragon moving is auspicious; the rest are not all ominous but cannot make blessing either, and what use to me is their moving? That is little good and much ill. Better that the hexagram and lines be still: though there are malign killings, in the end no harm is done; if a lucky spirit holds power, it can of itself make blessing. The Earth killings are Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei, changing in turn and moving through the four seasons; met in divining illness they are mostly ill and little good. All the killings are given in the later chapter on malign killings; if one of them moves, it makes blame.
- **LY.ILL.YM.09** 生死有命，不是祈祷能改的；疾病在于人，不可以忽视；没生病之前就谨慎，是君子的做法。已经生了病，能不占问它的吉凶而加以调理吗？本命跟着鬼入了墓，是治不好的病。比如戌月甲辰日，占儿子的病，他生于己亥年，得同人卦，卦中子孙旺相、六爻安静，病应当不会死，但是亥命随官入了墓，按法大凶；后来他的病没有死，却不到半年溺水而死。
  Life and death have their appointed lot and cannot be prayed away; illness lies with the person and must not be neglected; to take care before falling ill is the way of the gentleman. Once ill, can one fail to divine its fortune and adjust the treatment? If the natal branch follows the ghost into a tomb, it is an untreatable illness. For example, in the Xu month on the Jiachen day, someone divined a son's illness; he was born in a Jihai year and the hexagram was Tongren, with Output strong and the six lines still, so the illness should not kill; but the Hai natal branch followed Pressure into a tomb, which by the method is a great ill omen; afterwards he did not die of the illness, but within half a year he drowned.
- **LY.ILL.YM.20** 占自己的病，世爻就是用神；随墓、助伤、入墓、入绝、反吟、伏吟，这几样里碰上一样，即使世爻旺相也凶。占别人的病，应爻就是用神；随墓、反吟、伏吟、入墓、入绝，即使应爻得了生，这几样里也一样都不能碰上。
  In divining one's own illness, the Self line is the Subject; following into the tomb, helping to harm, the tomb, Extinction, Reversed Chant and Hidden Chant: if any one of these is met, even a strong Self is ominous. In divining another person's illness, the Other line is the Subject; following into the tomb, Reversed Chant, Hidden Chant, the tomb and Extinction: even when the Other is generated, not one of these may be met.
- **LY.ILL.YY.01** 人不幸有了病来占，不可以不谨慎、不周详。大的方面有四件事：占他是死是活，占他是什么病症，占医和药，占有没有鬼崇；至于病是哪一天起的、在哪里得的，是疾病附带要占的事。
  When a person is unfortunate enough to fall ill and divines about it, one must be careful and thorough. There are four main heads: whether he will live or die, what the illness is, doctors and medicine, and ghostly influences; when the illness began and where it was caught are subsidiary matters of illness.
- **LY.ILL.YY.03** 占病取用爻：自己占，拿世爻、身爻、命爻做用；替别人占，拿应爻做用。占家里的亲人，祖父祖母用官鬼，父亲、兄弟、妻子、子女各用自己所属的那一类爻；这五类亲属如果不上卦，就按《黄金策》分爻的办法取用。奴仆丁口占家主，拿五爻做用；占主母，拿二爻做用。
  Taking the Subject line in divining illness: in divining for oneself, the Self line, the Body line and the destiny line are the Subject; in divining for another, the Other line is the Subject. In divining for kin of the household, grandfather and grandmother use the Pressure line, and father, brother, wife and child each use the relation to which they belong; if these five kinds of relative do not appear in the hexagram, take the Subject by the Huangjin Ce's division of the lines. A servant or hired hand divining for the master of the house takes the fifth line as the Subject, and for the mistress the second line.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.14** 卦里没有用神，而日、月作了用神的，不必去找伏神，断他马上就好；伏神衰弱的，应当再占一次。凡是用神不出现，都应当再占。
  When the hexagram has no Subject but the Day or Month acts as the Subject, one need not look for the hidden line; promise recovery at once. If the hidden line is weak, one should divine again. Whenever the Subject does not appear, one should divine again.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.15** 一个卦看不清、或者问到应期的时候，不要硬断，叫问卜的人再占几个卦，把几个卦合起来详细看，吉凶发生在哪个月就能看出来。
  When one hexagram is unclear, or when the time of fulfilment is asked, do not press a judgment: have the asker divine several more hexagrams and read them together in detail, and the month in which good or ill fortune falls can be known.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.22** 占病时不靠猜症候，先单占一卦问吉凶：卦吉就说调理调理就好，不必吃药求神；卦凶就叫他请医生；卦里鬼动克用神的，再占鬼神，或者占家宅、占坟墓，一定要弄清楚是犯了什么、用什么办法治。拿五行脏腑、六神去猜发冷发热这类症候，病人本来就知道，对他没有用处。
  In divining illness do not rely on guessing the symptoms, but first divine a hexagram on good or ill fortune alone: if it is lucky, say that with care he will recover and there is no need for medicine or prayer; if ill, tell him to call a doctor; if the hexagram shows Pressure moving to control the Subject, divine the ghosts and spirits again, or the house or the tomb, and be sure to find out what he has offended and how to treat it. To guess symptoms such as chills and fever from the five phases, the organs and the six spirits tells the patient nothing he does not already know, and is of no use to him.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.28** 野鹤再拿船上被风阻住的例子来说明：子孙是解忧的神。占什么时候顺风，别人按木爻、兄弟爻断风云，他看的是子孙：子孙持世又变空，连着几天风雨，等它出空那天才晴，再等到被日辰合住的地方被冲开的那天才顺风。他从前也按木、兄弟去断，屡试不验，后来看到子孙值日开船就顺风的比比皆是，才悟出这个道理，和疾病篇把子孙当解忧之神是一个理。
  The Old Man takes the case of a boat held up by the wind to show that Output is the spirit that relieves worry. In divining when the wind would be fair, another judged the wind and cloud by the Wood and Peer lines, but he looked at Output: Output holding the Self line and changing into Void meant wind and rain for several days, clearing on the day it leaves the Void, and a fair wind only on the day the place held by the Day's combination is clashed open. He too used to judge by Wood and Peer and it failed again and again, and only after seeing that boats setting sail on a day when Output rules the day have fair wind time after time did he come to understand the principle, which is the same as taking Output as the spirit that relieves worry in the chapter on illness.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.39** 官鬼是忧神、子孙是喜悦解忧之神，虽然是古法，用起来很恰当；但也有不把鬼当忧神的，全看来问的人心里想问什么。他问病的吉凶：鬼爻持世、鬼在卦里发动，是忧神；子孙持世、子孙发动，是解忧喜悦的神。他问病是犯了哪路鬼神：卦里出现金鬼，一定冲犯了武神；水鬼，一定得罪了河神这一类。他问病源：卦里出现火鬼，一定属心经；水鬼，一定在肾部。
  That the Pressure line is the spirit of worry and Output the spirit of joy that relieves worry, though an old method, is very apt in use; but there are cases in which Pressure is not taken as the spirit of worry, and it all depends on what the asker has in mind to ask. If he asks about the fortune of the illness: Pressure holding the Self line or moving in the hexagram is the spirit of worry, and Output holding the Self line or moving is the spirit of joy that relieves worry. If he asks which ghost or spirit the illness offends: a Metal Pressure appearing in the hexagram means he has surely offended a martial god, and a Water Pressure that he has offended the river god and the like. If he asks about the source of the illness: a Fire Pressure appearing in the hexagram surely belongs to the Heart channel, and a Water Pressure surely lies in the Kidney.
- **LY.ILL.ZSZ.06** 周易尚占八卦断例里有四个卦对占病给的是劝告：履卦说不要占疾病，大壮卦说不要占官司和病，蒙卦说不要营谋、倒适合占病，恒卦说病要赶快治。
  In the judgments by example of the Zhouyi Shangzhan four hexagrams give advice about divining illness rather than a verdict: Lü (Treading) says do not divine illness, Dazhuang says do not divine lawsuits or illness, Meng says one cannot yet scheme but it suits divining illness, and Heng says illness must be treated quickly.

## 症候(Symptoms: which organ or part is ill, by phase, trigram, position and spirit)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/症候.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSQS.01** 卜筮全书的「六爻诸占定位」表里，疾病一行按初爻到六爻的次序是：足、腿肢、（空缺）、（空缺）、心肺、头脑。
  In the table of the position of the six lines for the various divinations in the Bushi Quanshu, the row for illness runs, from the first line to the sixth: the feet, legs and limbs, (missing), (missing), heart and lungs, head and brain.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.26** 柳神经里把鬼爻的五行、所在的位置和病挂在一起：火鬼动是心气病，土鬼摇是脾胃病；青龙在六位、巽宫，家里有头风；玄武在初爻、鬼动，生脚湿的人；初爻的杀落空，小孩难养；六爻木鬼，老人中风；上面透出金爻，终年眼暗；五爻火鬼，痨瘵缠身。
  The Liushen Jing ties the element of a ghost line, its place and an illness together: a Fire ghost moving is illness of the heart qi, an Earth ghost stirring is trouble of the spleen and stomach; the Azure Dragon in the sixth place and the Xun palace gives the household head-wind; the Dark Tortoise on the first line with the ghost moving gives someone with damp feet; a Void killing on the first place makes a small child hard to raise; a Wood ghost on the sixth line, an old person has a stroke; a Metal line showing through above, dim eyes all the year; a Fire ghost on the fifth line, consumption clings to the body.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.28** 参玄赋里讲家宅时提到病的几句：父爻入了墓，家里多有生病的人；兄爻克金财，妻子有病；日辰克火爻，要生眼病；日辰冲坎位，一定主耳聋。
  The lines on illness in the Canxuan Fu's discussion of a house: with the Resource line entering a tomb, the household has many sick people; with the brother line controlling a Metal Wealth line, the wife is ill; when the Day controls the Fire line, eye disease will arise; when the Day clashes the Kan place, deafness is sure.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.32** 疾病论里按五行和地支配病：金、木动，骨骼疼酸；水、火兴，皮肤寒热；丑、未交重必伤脾；戌、辰发动是因为腹部的病；土伏在土爻下，生肿毒；金藏在金位，动心包；妻财在土上兴旺，呕吐；木下伏木、木多，忽冷忽热；水伏在水下、水多，冷淋。
  The chapter on illness matches illnesses to the five elements and branches: Metal or Wood moving, bones and joints ache; Water or Fire rising, the skin has chills and fever; Chou or Wei moving is sure to harm the spleen; Xu or Chen moving comes from trouble of the belly; Earth hidden under an Earth line gives swollen toxin; Metal hidden in a Metal place stirs the pericardium; Wealth on Earth and rising, vomiting; Wood under Wood, with much Wood, blowing hot and cold; Water hidden under Water, with much Water, cold strangury.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.33** 疾病论里按八卦配病位：巽宫鬼爻兴旺，大腿痛；震宫官鬼旺动，脚病不愈；离宫旺相、内卦发动，是疮疡一类的病；火鬼休囚、外卦交重，是心和眼睛的灾；兑宫官鬼发动，是面部的病；坎宫鬼爻交重，是腹中的灾；乾宫官鬼旺相，是头痛；坤宫鬼爻兴旺，是肚子疼；坎卦水爻兴旺，只因为耳病；艮宫土爻发动，一定是手的灾。
  The chapter on illness matches the trigrams to the place of illness: the ghost of Xun thriving, pain in the thigh; the Pressure line of Zhen strong and moving, a disease of the foot that does not heal; Li strong and its inner lines moving, a disease of sores and wounds; a Fire ghost resting or confined with the outer lines moving, trouble of the heart and eyes; the Pressure line of Dui moving, illness of the face; a Kan ghost moving, trouble within the belly; the Pressure line of Qian strong, headache; the Kun ghost thriving, belly pain; the Water line of Kan rising, only an ear disease; the Earth line of Gen moving, sure to be trouble of the hand.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.43** 五行论病歌：金鬼兴隆，病在牙和头，再加白虎，血和脓流；木鬼发动是风病，头晕眼花；水兴是发冷发热，多半是疟疾，痢疾、伤风不容易治；火鬼是虚劳加上虚弱，眼睛疼痛也有来由；土鬼是脾胃虚、黄肿，间爻动要担心膈的病。
  The verse on illness by the five elements: a Metal ghost thriving, the teeth and the head, with the White Tiger as well, blood and pus flow; a Wood ghost moving, a wind illness, with dizziness and dim eyes; Water rising, chills and fever mostly from malaria, and dysentery and wind-damage are not easily treated; a Fire ghost, consumption and weakness, and eye pain has its cause too; an Earth ghost, weak spleen and stomach and yellow swelling, and if the between lines move, worry about a diaphragm illness.
- **LY.ILL.CA.02** 水是腰和肾，金是肺，火主心经，木主肝；土爻是脾胃，不要遇上杀；发动、交重时要仔细看。虎和鬼一起兴动，应该哭泣；龙和子孙一起旺，保平安。乾是头，坤是腹，巽是手，震是足，兑是口，艮是鼻，坎主耳，离主目。哪一宫杀旺，就是那一处的灾；子孙动、青龙兴，保平复。
  Water is the waist and kidneys, Metal the lungs; Fire governs the heart channel, Wood the liver; the Earth line is the spleen and stomach and must not meet a killing; if it moves or crosses heavily, look carefully. The Tiger and the ghost rising together: there will surely be weeping; the Dragon and Output both strong: peace is assured. Qian is the head and Kun the belly, Xun the hands and Zhen the feet, Dui the mouth and Gen the nose, Kan governs the ears and Li the eyes. Where a killing is strong in one palace, the calamity is the same there; with Output moving and the Dragon rising, recovery is assured.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.01** 鬼谷子辨爻法在占疾病一章的表：初爻管足，二爻管腿和股，三爻管腰，四爻管脊，五爻管心，六爻管头。表的第一行另有丧门、吊客、哭声、棺木、行兆、坟墓六个名目，按六列的次序对着初爻到六爻。
  Guigu's table of the lines for the chapter on illness: the first line governs the feet, the second the legs and thighs, the third the waist, the fourth the spine, the fifth the heart and the sixth the head. The table's first row also gives six names, Mourning Gate, Guest of Condolence, weeping, the coffin, Xingzhao and the grave, which by the order of the six columns stand over the first to the sixth lines.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.03** 一爻管五脏，二爻管皮肉，三爻管体骨、腰和足，四爻管肺和心经，五、六爻管头。游魂卦人恍惚，像被神鬼触到；只有归魂卦渐渐向安。再看八卦：乾是头，坤是腹，坎是耳，震是足，巽是肠，艮是手，兑是口，离是目；鬼在哪一卦里，那一处就生病受伤，时下的术士要仔细推它落在哪里。
  The first line governs the five viscera, the second skin and flesh, the third body, bones, waist and feet, the fourth the lungs and heart channel, the fifth and sixth the head. In a Wandering Soul hexagram the person is dazed, as if touched by spirits; only a Returning Soul hexagram gradually turns to peace. Then the trigrams: Qian the head, Kun the belly, Kan the ears, Zhen the feet, Xun the bowels, Gen the hands, Dui the mouth and Li the eyes; whichever of them the ghost is in, that part is ill and injured, and the diviners of the day must carefully work out where it comes to rest.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.04** 木主酸痛，火主疮，水和土主虚黄和肿毒，金是牙齿痛得厉害，务必仔细推详。外卦克内卦、应克世，有药也频频泻肚肠；内卦克外卦、世克应，没有鬼爻的话多是呕吐所伤。还要看五鬼配五脏，病源是长是短就在这里。
  Wood governs aching pain and Fire governs sores; Water and Earth govern deficiency with yellowing and swollen toxins; Metal is toothache beyond the ordinary; work it out carefully. The outer trigram controlling the inner, the Other controlling the Self: there is medicine, yet he purges from the bowels again and again; the inner controlling the outer, the Self controlling the Other: with no Pressure line it is mostly injury by vomiting. Look further at the five ghosts matched to the five viscera: there is where the source of the illness, short or long, is found.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.10** 鬼爻持世临身，一听见人说话就生气，眼睛发慌看不见，狂言乱语失了精神，吃喝也难；四墓持世也一样。鬼是酉爻，眼睛遭殃；土鬼肿胀气虚；金鬼持世，伤骨节；木鬼，身体酸痛，头闷心郁结。卦里鬼爻入了墓，又带休囚和杀气，或本宫的白虎入墓，就凶；子孙不动，难救。
  With the Pressure line holding the Self and the Body, at the first sound of a voice he grows angry, his eyes are flurried and cannot see, he raves and loses his wits, and food and drink are hard; the Four Tombs holding the Self are the same. A ghost on the You line: the eyes suffer; an Earth ghost: swelling and deficient qi; a Metal ghost holding the Self injures the bone joints; a Wood ghost: aching in the body, a heavy head and a knotted heart. With the Pressure line entering a tomb in the hexagram, together with resting or trapped and a killing aura, or the White Tiger of the hexagram's own palace entering a tomb, it is ominous; if Output does not move he can hardly be saved.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.11** 鬼在初爻，两足受伤；在二爻，两腿的病不同寻常；在三爻，腰背常发软；在四爻，心、腹和肚肠；在五爻，肾脏，多气胀；在六爻，头上的病是灾殃。
  The ghost on the first line: both feet are injured; on the second: an unusual trouble in both legs; on the third: the waist and back are often limp; on the fourth: the heart, belly and bowels; on the fifth: the kidneys, often with distension of qi; on the sixth: trouble in the head is the calamity.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.12** 鬼在乾是头，在坤病在腹；坎要患耳，离伤目；兑要忧口，艮在手上；震要防足，巽患在股。木爻是鬼，身体疼痛；水、土爻持着，必生痈肿；克象若说是金鬼，以此类推，是牙和骨头痛。
  The ghost in Qian is the head, in Kun the illness is in the belly; Kan must mean the ears, Li injures the eyes; Dui: worry for the mouth; Gen: the hands; Zhen: guard the feet; Xun: trouble in the thighs. A Wood line as the ghost: pain in the body; Water or Earth holding it: there will be abscess and swelling; if the controlling image is a Metal ghost, by analogy it is pain in the teeth and bones.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.13** 内戒卦，病伤五脏；外戒卦，病伤皮肤；骸骨卦，羸弱消瘦；棺椁卦，心惊多恐惧；血脉卦，疮痍；游魂卦，恍惚不安宁；归魂卦，精魄暗中扶着身子，气息沉静，懒得说话。
  An Inner Warning hexagram: the illness injures the five viscera; an Outer Warning hexagram: it injures the skin; a Bones hexagram: frail and wasted; a Coffin hexagram: a startled heart and much fear; a Blood Vessels hexagram: sores; a Wandering Soul hexagram: dazed and not at rest; a Returning Soul hexagram: the spirit-souls quietly support the body, the breath is still and the heart too listless to speak.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.21** 金和木，病在四肢，感寒、喘、气亏体弱；辰、戌，胃和胸，呕逆；丑连着肚腹，吐而伤脾；火动，势头到极点，三焦干渴，血、心、眼目有疮痍；水、土发寒，是因受冷得的，泄泻、虚劳，耳和肾衰弱。
  Metal and Wood: the four limbs, catching a chill, quick panting, deficient qi and frailty; Chen and Xu: the stomach and chest, vomiting and retching; Chou links with the belly and abdomen, vomiting injures the spleen; Fire moving: at its extreme, the Triple Burner parched, and blood, heart and eyes have sores; Water and Earth cause chills, caught from cold, diarrhoea, consumption, and weakness of the ears and kidneys.
- **LY.ILL.EL.07** 以官鬼为主，旺衰为凭。占疾厄是假是真，官鬼交重，病一定兴起；旺相的也会遭这个患；落空、遇绝，就是虚名。随官入墓、助鬼伤身，灾就不是假的，祸一定兴起；日、月制住官鬼，病是实还是不实要看；子孙旺动，病就难成立。注说：占是不是病，只论官爻，再没有别的说法；鬼如果交重，一定成这个症；官虽不动，如果逢生旺，也断有这个灾；卦如果没有鬼、或落空亡、或临绝处，似是而非，病不真；凡是遇上随官入墓、助鬼伤身，其祸应当兴起，灾难回避；要知道病什么时候散，要看子孙，如果子旺官衰、日月和福德，卦内子爻或动而克制，病就散。
  The Pressure line is the main line, and strength or weakness is the ground. To divine whether an ailment is false or real: if the Pressure line moves, the illness will surely rise; strong, it too suffers this trouble; Void or meeting Extinction, it is an empty name. Following into a tomb and helping the ghost to harm the Self mean the calamity is no false one, and misfortune will surely rise; if the Day and Month control the Pressure line, it remains to be seen whether it is real; Output strong and moving, and the illness is hard to make out. The commentary says: in judging whether it is an illness or not, go by the Pressure line alone, with no other opinion; if the ghost moves, it will surely make this illness; though the Pressure line does not move, if it meets generation or strength, it too means this calamity; if the hexagram has no ghost, or it is Void, or sits at Extinction, it seems so but is not, and the illness is not real; when one meets following into a tomb or helping the ghost to harm the Self, the misfortune should rise and the calamity is hard to avoid; to know when the illness will disperse, look at Output: if Output is strong and Pressure weak, or the Day and Month and the fortune spirit are on it, or Output in the hexagram moves and controls the ghost, the illness disperses.
- **LY.ILL.EL.16** 百病的轻重，不出五行的生死；万民的生死，逃不出八卦的兴衰。注举了用神为水、为金、为土、为火的几种情形，一一说明畏什么、喜什么：水是主象，怕土，也怕金以外的受制；卦里土静火兴，不必畏忌；爻内火安静、土动，却不吉祥；怕遇上巳、午两个时辰，喜遇上木、金；又如火土都动，见木反而凶，得到酉、申来助水，这病才会痊愈。假如金藏在土下，飞神能生伏神就好；木爻安静，日辰忌卯、寅，木象发动，时辰忧亥、子，这就是所说的遇金则吉、遇水则凶。再拿土作用神，卦里见水、木、火三个爻同时发动，不当作凶来推，应当按吉断；遇午、申的时候，土得到生扶就吉，逢卯、巳的日子，土临死绝就被囚。又论火是用爻，只有水发动的日子遇上戌、辰、丑、未，水被土克无妨；一见酉、申就命尽，只求寅、卯就身安。如果用爻居于衰位、木化入金乡，卦里没有亥、子和父爻兴旺，一定难以救度，查哪一天逢金，就断哪个时候办丧事；倘若金空，就不这样断，节气产到亥、子，命也会回生，细究哪个爻动静，就知道哪天存亡。其中有贪生忘克、救处遭伤，盈虚要精细详审，怎么可以寻常一概而论？
  The lightness or heaviness of a hundred illnesses does not go beyond the birth and death of the five phases; the life and death of the people of ten thousand houses cannot escape the rise and fall of the eight trigrams. The commentary gives cases with the Subject as Water, Metal, Earth and Fire, and says of each what it fears and what it welcomes: with Water as the chief image, it fears Earth; if in the hexagram Earth is still and Fire thriving there is no need to fear it; if in the lines Fire is still and Earth moves, it is not auspicious; it fears meeting the hours Si and Wu, and is glad to meet Wood and Metal; again, if Fire and Earth both move, meeting Wood is on the contrary ominous, and when You and Shen come to help Water the illness will be cured. If Metal lies hidden under Earth, it is good if the flying line can generate the hidden one; if the Wood line is still, the Mao and Yin days are to be feared, and if the Wood image moves, the Hai and Zi hours are a worry: this is what is meant by 'meeting Metal is auspicious, meeting Water ominous'. Taking Earth again as the Subject, if the hexagram shows Water, Wood and Fire lines all three moving, do not judge it ominous but by the auspicious; at the hours Wu and Shen Earth gains generation and support and it is auspicious, and on Mao and Si days Earth on death or Extinction is imprisoned. Again, with Fire as the Subject line, when only Water moves on a day that meets Xu, Chen, Chou or Wei, Water is controlled by Earth and it does not matter; at the first sight of You or Shen life is at an end, and only if one hopes for Yin or Mao is the body safe. If the Subject line sits in a weak place and Wood changes into Metal's country, and the hexagram has no Hai or Zi or a thriving Resource line, rescue is hard, and to find on what day it meets Metal is to decide at what time the coffin is made; if Metal is Void, it is not judged so, and when the solar term reaches Hai and Zi life is restored, and if one examines which line moves and which is still, one knows on which day one lives or dies. Among these are those who, greedy for generation, forget control, or are harmed at the place of rescue, and fullness and emptiness must be examined finely: how can they be judged by the common general rule?
- **LY.ILL.HDY.05** 病症要按五行、爻位、身体部位来断：肺病属金，木主四肢；感受寒邪、痰喘、气弱消瘦；辰戌是胃和胸，生出呕吐逆气；丑运转肠肚，木克伤脾。火动热到极点，三焦枯竭，还有血、心、眼目；水主发寒，是受冷得的；世爻泻泄、虚劳、耳肾衰弱。土动主吐，水动主泻，水动发寒，火动发热，金动四肢和胀闷。木主足，金主头，土主胸腹，火主手，水主脾肾。飞爻、伏爻都旺相时，飞爻是起病的原因，伏爻（原文作「代」）是受病的。世爻是动爻，病在里面下面；应爻是动爻，病在外面上面；间爻动，受阻隔。《易镜》说：长男得病，适宜纯震不摇动；少女染病，适宜兑卦不动。
  Symptoms are judged by the five phases, the line positions and the parts of the body: lung illness is Metal, with Wood governing the four limbs; catching cold, phlegm and wheezing, qi weak and emaciated; Chen and Xu are the stomach and chest, giving rise to vomiting and retching; Chou moves the bowels and belly, and Wood wounds the spleen. Fire moving: heat to the extreme, the Triple Burner exhausted, and also blood, heart and eyes; Water governs chills, got through cold; the Self line: diarrhoea, consumptive weakness, decline of ears and kidneys. Earth moving governs vomiting, Water moving governs diarrhoea, Water moving brings chills, Fire moving brings fever, Metal moving the four limbs and fullness and oppression. Wood governs the feet, Metal the head, Earth the chest and belly, Fire the hands, Water the spleen and kidneys. When the flying and hidden lines are both strong, the flying line is the cause that begins the illness and the hidden line (the text has 'substitute') is what receives it. If the Self is the moving line, the illness is received inside, below; if the Other is the moving line, outside, above; if the between line moves, there is obstruction. The Mirror of the Changes says: when the eldest son falls ill, it is fitting that the pure Zhen does not shake; when the youngest daughter is infected, that the Dui hexagram does not move.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.12** 海底眼的支神类属，每一组地支（五行）有一首七言诗和一段散句，说它在世间管的事物，其中带着身体和症状：亥子（水）是耳、肾、腰，泻冷精逆；巳午（火）是眼目、心、疮；寅卯（木）是手足、皮毛、肝胆；申酉（金）是肺气咳喘、骸骨肢筋、眉头；辰戌丑未（土）是脾胃、皮肤、肿腹泻肚、时疫呕吐。
  In the classes of the branch spirits in the Haidi Yan each group of branches (each phase) has a seven-character poem and a prose passage on what it governs in the world, and these carry the body and its symptoms: Hai and Zi (Water): the ears, kidneys and waist, purging, chill and the essence in reverse; Si and Wu (Fire): the eyes, the heart, sores; Yin and Mao (Wood): hands and feet, skin and hair, liver and gall bladder; Shen and You (Metal): lung qi, coughing and wheezing, bones, limbs and sinews, the brow; Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei (Earth): spleen and stomach, the skin, swelling of the belly and diarrhoea, seasonal epidemics and vomiting.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.02** 官鬼爻的五行，定哪一脏受病：火是心经（发热、咽干、口燥），水是肾（怕冷、盗汗、遗精白浊），金是肺（咳嗽、虚怯、气喘），木是肝（感冒风寒、四肢不和），土是脾胃（虚黄、浮肿、瘟疫时气）。鬼爻衰弱病轻、旺相病重；静则安卧，动则煎熬躁急。
  The phase of the Pressure line fixes which organ is ill: Fire is the Heart channel (fever, dry throat, dry mouth), Water the Kidney (aversion to cold, night sweats, seminal emission and cloudy urine), Metal the Lung (cough, weak timidity, panting), Wood the Liver (catching wind-cold, limbs out of harmony), Earth the Spleen and Stomach (sallow weakness, swelling, epidemic and seasonal disease). A weak Pressure line means a light illness and a strong one a heavy illness; still, the patient lies quiet, and moving, he is tormented and restless.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.03** 官鬼爻在坎宫，病是中满、血虚，兼有湿毒；在离卦，病是内虚、眼红、尿黄。旧注分得更细：坎宫属水，火鬼主血虚、水鬼主湿毒；离宫属火，火鬼主眼红、水鬼主尿黄。
  With the Pressure line in the Kan palace, the illness is fullness in the middle and blood deficiency, together with damp toxin; in the Li hexagram it is inner deficiency, red eyes and yellow urine. The old commentary divides it more finely: the Kan palace belongs to Water, where a Fire Pressure means blood deficiency and a Water Pressure damp toxin; the Li palace belongs to Fire, where a Fire Pressure means red eyes and a Water Pressure yellow urine.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.04** 鬼爻落在哪一宫，病就在哪个部位：坤是腹、乾是头，兑是喉风咳嗽，艮和震是手足，巽是瘫痪、肠风。以鬼在坤宫为例：火鬼腹痈，水鬼腹痛，动化财爻或财化水鬼是泻痢，土鬼是癖块食积或沙胀蛊症，木鬼是绞肠痧痛或大肠有病，金鬼是胁肋痛（在上胸痛、在下腰痛）。别的卦照此推。
  Where the Pressure line falls in the palaces fixes where the illness is: Kun is the belly and Qian the head; Dui is throat wind and cough; Gen and Zhen are the hand and foot; Xun is paralysis and intestinal wind. Taking Pressure in the Kun palace as the example: a Fire Pressure is an abdominal abscess, a Water Pressure pain in the belly; moving and changing into a Wealth line, or Wealth changing into a Water Pressure, is diarrhea and dysentery; an Earth Pressure is abdominal masses and food accumulation, or sha bloating and gu-swelling; a Wood Pressure is twisting colic or trouble in the large intestine; a Metal Pressure is pain in the flanks (chest if high, loins if low). The other palaces follow by analogy.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.05** 官鬼爻临着哪个六神，就是哪一种病：螣蛇鬼坐卧不安、心神不定；青龙鬼酒色过度、瘦弱无力；勾陈鬼胸腹胀满、脾胃不和；朱雀鬼狂言乱语、身热面赤；白虎鬼跌打损伤，女人则血崩血晕、产后各症（白虎是血神）；玄武鬼色欲过度、忧闷在心，在本宫主阴虚。最后一句叫人变通着断。
  Which of the six spirits the Pressure line sits on fixes the kind of illness: Coiling Snake Pressure, unable to sit or lie at ease and the mind unsettled; Azure Dragon Pressure, excess of wine and lust, thin and without strength; Hook Earth Pressure, fullness and swelling, Spleen and Stomach out of harmony; Vermilion Bird Pressure, wild talk, hot body and red face; White Tiger Pressure, falls and blows and injury, and in a woman flooding and blood-faintness and the ailments after childbirth (the White Tiger is the spirit of blood); Dark Tortoise Pressure, excess of lust and gloom pent in the heart, and in the home palace yin deficiency. The last sentence tells one to judge flexibly.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.14** 卦里水爻动化火、火爻动化水，不论是不是鬼爻，只要冲犯世爻或主象的，都是忽冷忽热的症候；水、火两爻都动，也是这样。水旺火衰是冷多热少；水受伤、火得助，是常热而偶尔发冷。坎宫火动是里冷外热，离宫水动是皮冷骨热。再带上日辰，一定是疟疾。
  In the hexagram, a Water line moving and changing into Fire, or a Fire line changing into Water, whether or not it is the Pressure line, if it offends the Self line or the main image, is a syndrome of alternating chills and fever; if a Water line and a Fire line both move, it is the same. Water strong and Fire weak means more chill and less fever; Water harmed and Fire helped means constant heat with an occasional chill. Fire moving in the Kan palace is cold inside and hot outside; Water moving in the Li palace is cold skin and hot bones. If it also carries the Day, it is surely malaria.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.15** 上下卦都有鬼，病一定是里外两处感受。两个鬼都动、或者都静，是一起受病；两个鬼互相冲，刚感受了邪就受了伤。内卦的鬼动来冲外卦的鬼，是先受伤后感邪；外卦的鬼动来冲内卦的鬼，是先感邪后受伤。要分轻重，用刑、合来断。
  With Pressure in both the upper and lower trigrams, the illness is surely a double contagion of inside and outside. Both Pressure lines moving, or both still, means falling ill together; two Pressure lines clashing each other means hardly is the evil felt than harm is done. If the inner Pressure moves and clashes the outer, it is first harm and then contagion; if the outer Pressure moves and clashes the inner, first contagion and then harm. To tell heavy from light, judge by punishment and combination.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.16** 火的性子向上，财爻代表饮食。占病遇到火鬼动来克外卦的财爻，一定是呕吐，重的是反胃、吃不下饭。财爻带着螣蛇，吐出来的还带虫；财爻在间爻，是消渴、容易饿的症候。
  Fire's nature is to flare upward and Wealth stands for food and drink. In divining illness, a Fire Pressure moving to control the outer Wealth line is surely vomiting, and when heavy, a stomach that turns and cannot take food. If Wealth carries the Coiling Snake, worms are brought up as well; if Wealth is on the between line, it is the syndrome of thirst and quick hunger.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.17** 水行的官鬼动了，变出土来回头克它，又落在本宫的内卦（下三爻）：小便不通；这一爻属阴，大便也不通；阳宫里是阴的象、阴宫里是阳的象，大小便都不通。内卦的水爻起克、又动、又加白虎：阳爻是尿血，阴爻是泻血（白虎是血神）；再带刑是痔漏。
  A Water Pressure line that moves and changes into Earth, which turns back to control it, and falls in the inner trigram of the home palace (the lower three lines): the urine is blocked; if the line is yin, the stool is blocked too; a yang palace with a yin image, or a yin palace with a yang image, blocks both. If the Water line in the inner trigram controls, moves and has the White Tiger added: a yang line means blood in the urine, a yin line bloody stool (the White Tiger is the spirit of blood); with punishment added it is piles and fistula.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.18** 鬼爻在兑宫，病在口里。金鬼变出火、或者火爻变成金鬼，必生牙疔；没有变到火爻，只是牙痛。金鬼遇冲，牙一定松动。
  A Pressure line in the Dui palace puts the illness in the mouth. A Metal Pressure changing out into Fire, or a Fire line changing into a Metal Pressure, surely brings a tooth carbuncle; with no change into a Fire line it is only toothache. A Metal Pressure that meets a clash surely loosens the teeth.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.19** 鬼在震宫，病在脚上：加勾陈，脚一定肿；加白虎，一定摔断打伤、破损。土鬼变成木，得脚气；木鬼是酸痛麻木，水鬼是湿气，火鬼主疮毒，金鬼是踝、膝疼痛和骨头痛，或者被刀刃所伤。
  A Pressure line in the Zhen palace puts the illness in the foot: with Hook Earth added the foot surely swells, with the White Tiger it is surely broken, wounded or torn. An Earth Pressure changing into Wood is beriberi; a Wood Pressure is aching, soreness and numbness, a Water Pressure is damp, a Fire Pressure means sores and toxin, and a Metal Pressure is pain in the ankle joints, the knees and the bones, or a wound from a blade.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.20** 离宫的鬼变成水（或水爻动变成鬼）是痰火的症候；乾宫的鬼变成木（或木爻动变成鬼）是头风、眩晕。震宫的鬼在外卦，不要按脚上的病断，可以说他坐卧不安、心神恍惚（震是主动的卦）；再加螣蛇发动，是癫狂、惊痫，小孩是惊风；遇冲有翻墙上屋的乱动之患。艮宫的鬼遇上火鬼，一定生痈疽；变出土鬼，可以说浮肿、蛊胀一类。其余照此类推。
  A Pressure line of the Li palace changing into Water (or a moving Water line changing into Pressure) is the syndrome of phlegm-fire; a Pressure line of the Qian palace changing into Wood (or a moving Wood line changing into Pressure) is headwind and dizziness. A Zhen Pressure line in the outer trigram is not judged as a foot illness: say the patient cannot sit or lie at ease and his mind wanders (Zhen governs movement); with the Coiling Snake added and moving it is mania and fright-epilepsy, in a child convulsions; meeting a clash there is the danger of climbing over walls and onto roofs. A Gen Pressure line meeting a Fire Pressure surely brings carbuncles; if an Earth Pressure changes out, one can speak of swelling and drum-like distension. The rest follows by analogy.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.21** 财爻管饮食。卦里没有财爻，吃不下东西：财爻落空，吃不下；不上卦，不想吃。财爻动而变成官鬼，这病一定是吃喝来的；财爻临在身爻、世爻上，或者变出财爻，病再重也还能吃。
  The Wealth line governs food and drink. With no Wealth in the hexagram, food and drink are not taken: if the Wealth line falls into Void, nothing goes down; if it is absent from the hexagram, he has no wish to eat. A Wealth line that moves and changes into Pressure means the illness came from drink and food; a Wealth line sitting on the Body or the Self line, or a change into a Wealth line, means that even in a heavy illness he can still eat.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.22** 世爻和应爻中间的位置，是病人胸膈的地方。官鬼临在这里，胸膈一定堵闷不通：金鬼是胸肋骨头痛，土鬼是胀饱憋闷、不舒畅，木鬼是心里发痒、胃里嘈杂，水鬼是痰饮堵塞，火鬼多半是心口痛。如果变出财爻，或财爻变成鬼，一定是隔夜的食物没消化，弄得胸膈不舒服。
  The place between the Self and Other lines is where the patient's chest and diaphragm are. With a Pressure line on it the chest is surely stuffed and blocked: a Metal Pressure is pain in the ribs and chest bones, an Earth Pressure a full, oppressive feeling with no relief, a Wood Pressure an itching heart and a gnawing stomach, a Water Pressure phlegm and fluid choking it up, and a Fire Pressure mostly heart pain. If it changes into a Wealth line, or a Wealth line changes into Pressure, it is surely food from before left undigested, so that the chest and diaphragm are not at ease.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.24** 世爻衰弱又落进墓里，病人昏沉困倦。世爻有气（旺相），不过是懒得动；衰弱就不说话：怕亮喜暗，不想吃东西，爱睡，怕起床，懒得睁眼。再坐在阴宫上，一定是阴症。用爻入墓也这样断；鬼爻持世入墓、鬼的墓临在用爻上，都照这个断。
  A weak Self line falling into the tomb makes the patient drowsy and clouded. If the Self line is strong and has force, he is only too listless to move; if it is weak, he does not speak: he shrinks from the light and likes the dark, has no wish to eat, loves to sleep, dreads getting up and is too listless even to open his eyes. If it also sits in a yin palace it is surely a yin syndrome. A Subject line entering the tomb is judged the same way; so are a Pressure line on the Self line entering the tomb and the tomb of Pressure falling on the Subject line.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.27** 卦里没有鬼爻，就看谁来刑、冲、克、害用爻来断。用爻被金伤，四肢一定酸痛；用爻遭木克，皮和骨一定伤残；火是仇敌，主喘咳的灾病；水来相害，主恍惚的症候。动的金克过来，木爻受伤，四肢关节酸痛；动的木克过来，土爻受制，皮肉伤损。其余照此类推。
  With no Pressure line in the hexagram, judge by whatever punishes, clashes, controls or harms the Subject line. A Subject line wounded by Metal: the limbs surely ache and are sore; controlled by Wood: skin and bones are surely injured and maimed; with Fire as the enemy, the trouble of panting and cough; with Water coming to harm, a dazed, wandering state. So when a moving Metal line controls, the Wood line is wounded and the limbs and joints ache; when a moving Wood line controls, the Earth line is held down and flesh and skin are injured. The rest follows by analogy.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.28** 第三爻管腰。第三爻无缘无故落空，是腰软；有缘故而空，或者旺相而空，是腰痛；不空，但遇到动爻、日辰、官鬼来克或冲它，是闪了腰的痛；动而空的也是这样。鬼爻就在第三爻上，也主腰痛。
  The third line is the waist. If the third line is Void of itself without cause, the waist is weak; if it is Void with a cause, or Void while strong, the waist hurts; if it is not Void but a moving line, the Day or a Pressure line controls or clashes it, it is a strained waist; a moving Void line is the same. A Pressure line on the third line also means waist pain.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.29** 病不只在鬼爻所坐的地方，被鬼克到的地方也有病痛：克到最上面一爻（上六），主头痛；克到间爻，或者鬼坐在间爻上，主胸膈不舒服；忌神也一样。其余照此类推。
  The illness is not only where the Pressure line sits; the place it wounds also has pain. Controlling the top line brings headache; controlling a between line, or sitting on one, makes the chest and diaphragm uneasy; the Adversary does the same. The rest follows by analogy.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.30** 财爻在卦中动：动在上卦，主呕吐；动在下卦，主腹泻。如果被合住，就想吐吐不出、想泻泻不出，像呕逆恶心、里急后重一类。
  When the Wealth line moves in the hexagram: moving in the upper trigram it means vomiting, and in the lower trigram diarrhoea. If it is held by a combination, he wants to vomit and cannot, wants to purge and cannot, as in retching and nausea, or urgency with straining at stool.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.31** 木爻旺在世爻上发动，病不是发痒就是发疼：寅木主痛，卯木主痒；如果又在本宫的内卦，还有气不顺的毛病。
  When a Wood line rises on the Self line, the illness is either itching or pain: a Yin Wood line means pain, a Mao Wood line itching; if it is also in the inner trigram of the home palace, the qi is not flowing smoothly.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.46** 兄弟爻持世，饮食一定比平时少，这病也是因为吃得太多得的。旧注补充：如果兄弟不动、又没有气，而卦里财爻、子孙爻旺，就不是这样，那是气短力弱的症候，不容易痊愈。
  When a Peer line holds the Self line the patient surely eats less than usual, and the illness was also got through eating too much. The old commentary adds: if the Peer line does not move and has no force, while Wealth and Output are strong in the hexagram, it is not so; it is then a syndrome of short breath and weak strength, not easily healed.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.139** 《黄金策》家宅章说：「四个鬼爻贴着身，要防生出灾祸。」旧注：贴身的鬼爻带着破碎杀，主有伤了相貌的病；按八卦推：乾主头、脸、小肠，喘急咳嗽的病；坎主两耳，肾脏之间的病；艮主背和肋，手指的病；震主下半身，腰脚疼痛的病；巽主两条大腿和两条腿，头发、血气的病；离主眼睛，心经的病；坤主肠子之间，脾胃的病；兑主口和牙，以及嘴唇的病；其余照这样推。王洪绪只解释「四鬼」：是年、月、日、时都值着官鬼，持世、临身、临命，主有灾祸。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on the household says: 'Four ghosts clinging to the Body: beware of calamity.' The old commentary: a ghost clinging to the Body and carrying the Breaking killing means an illness that spoils the looks; by trigram: Qian governs the head, face and small intestine, illnesses of rapid panting and cough; Kan the two ears, illnesses of the region of the kidneys; Gen the back and ribs, illnesses of the fingers; Zhen the lower body, illnesses of pain in the waist and feet; Xun the two thighs and two legs, illnesses of the hair and of blood and qi; Li the eyes, illnesses of the heart channel; Kun the intestines, illnesses of the spleen and stomach; Dui the mouth and teeth and the lips; the rest by analogy. Wang Hongxu explains only the 'four ghosts': the year, month, day and hour all fall on Pressure, holding the Self line, sitting on the Body or on the natal line, which brings calamity.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.140** 《黄金策》坟墓章说：「叠刃、刑、鬼、破、劫、亡（这些凶煞）叠加，就有疲癃残疾。」旧注：鬼爻临着破碎杀，又带三刑六害、叠刃、亡神劫煞，一同克用爻，按八宫主病：乾主头脸、喘急、咳嗽、小肠；坎主手臂脸面、两耳、小便、气血、腰痛、肋和心；艮主鼻疮、手指腿脚；震主骨、脚、肝、腿、三焦、笑言；巽主须鬓膝、血气风邪；离主脾胃痈疽、眼痛、心惊心惧、热症、阳火气；坤主肚腹呕吐、便血泻痢、黄肿；兑主口齿残缺、唇翻、皮肤；金鬼乘旺是痨嗽，木鬼是风邪，火鬼是热症，水鬼是吐泻，土鬼是黄浮肿；其中说不完，要按道理推详。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on graves says: 'Doubled Blade, Punishment, ghost, break, Robbery and Loss together: infirmity and disablement.' The old commentary: a ghost on the Breaking killing, with the three punishments and six harms, the Doubled Blade, Loss and Robbery together controlling the Subject line, means illness by palace: Qian, the head and face, rapid panting, cough and the small intestine; Kan, the arms and face, the two ears, urine, blood and qi, waist pain, the ribs and heart; Gen, nose sores and the fingers, legs and feet; Zhen, the bones, feet, liver, legs, the triple burner and raving laughter and talk; Xun, the beard, temples and knees, blood and qi and wind-evil; Li, abscesses of the spleen and stomach, eye pain, heart fright and fear, heat symptoms and yang-fire qi; Kun, the belly, vomiting, blood in the stool, diarrhoea and dysentery, and yellow swelling; Dui, mouth and missing teeth, a turned-up lip and the skin; a Metal ghost in strength is consumption and cough, a Wood ghost wind-evil, a Fire ghost heat symptoms, a Water ghost vomiting and diarrhoea, an Earth ghost yellow puffy swelling; there is too much in between to set out and it is to be worked out by reason.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.03** 土动主泻，木动发寒，金动主四肢或满闷，火动发热。木主足，金主头，土主胸腹，火主手目，水主耳肾。飞伏都旺相时，飞是化出病的原因，以伏为受病的地方。又：世是动爻，在内则下部受病；应是动爻，在外则上部受病；间爻动，主胸膈的病。《易镜》说：长男受病，宜纯震卦不动；小女染病，宜兑卦不动。大忌申、酉持世，木爻独发，因为申是丧车，酉是丧服，木是棺椁。
  Earth moving means diarrhoea, Wood moving means chills, Metal moving means the four limbs or oppression, Fire moving means fever. Wood governs the feet, Metal the head, Earth the chest and belly, Fire the hands and eyes, Water the ears and kidneys. When the flying and the hidden are both strong, the flying is the cause the illness turns on and the hidden is where the illness is received. Again, the Self as the moving line, inside, the lower part receives the illness; the Other as the moving line, outside, the upper part receives it; a between line moving means an illness of the chest and diaphragm. The Yijing says: for a person of the eldest son to fall ill it is fitting that the pure Zhen hexagram does not move, and for the youngest daughter to be infected that the Dui hexagram does not move. It is greatly feared that Shen and You hold the Self and a Wood line moves alone, for Shen is the mourning cart, You the mourning dress and Wood the coffin.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.20** （十八）占疾病：壬午火鬼，正好在九四爻，火鬼主热。若占父母，九二的木财发动必然伤（克父母），幸好九五的金一制，他的病可以痊愈，只是牵连着还没有脱身。其余照这个类推。
  (18) Divining illness: the Ren Wu Fire ghost falls exactly on the ninth in the fourth place, and a Fire ghost means heat. If a parent is divined for, the Wood Wealth on the ninth in the second place moving will surely injure (control Resource), but luckily the Metal on the ninth in the fifth place checks it, and his illness can be cured, only it is still tangled and has not yet shaken free. The rest follow by analogy.
- **LY.ILL.TXF.03** 要定症候，必须看鬼临着的五行。金鬼：肺腑的病，咳嗽气喘，或者疮疖、血光，或者筋骨疼痛。木鬼：四肢不利，风气、肝胆病，左瘫右痪，口眼歪斜，头疼发热，三焦口渴；加朱雀，说胡话、乱语，是阳症伤寒。水鬼：沉寒痼冷，遗精白浊，小便淋沥，吐泻呕逆。火鬼：寒热往来，脾寒疟疾。土鬼：脾胃发瘴，水肿虚浮，瘟疫时气。如果前一卦是火鬼、后一卦是水鬼，前面的症候一定是阳症，服冷药过多，变成了阴症；如果午时以前占，遇到这两个鬼，应当说不要服药；过了头，以后必有变化。这说的是在火旺的月日占卦的情形。
  To fix the symptoms, one must look at the phase the ghost sits on. A Metal ghost: illness of the lungs and viscera, coughing and wheezing, or sores and boils, or bleeding, or aching of the sinews and bones. A Wood ghost: the four limbs not free, wind-qi and illness of the liver and gall, left and right palsy, mouth and eyes awry, headache and fever, thirst in the Triple Burner; with the Vermilion Bird added, wild and confused speech, a yang cold-damage illness. A Water ghost: deep cold and chronic chill, seminal emission and turbid urine, dribbling urination, vomiting, diarrhoea and retching. A Fire ghost: alternate chills and fever, spleen chill and malaria. An Earth ghost: spleen and stomach miasma, water swelling and puffy weakness, epidemic and seasonal qi. If the earlier hexagram is a Fire ghost and the later a Water ghost, the earlier symptoms were surely yang, and taking too much cold medicine has turned them into a yin condition; if the divination is made before noon and these two ghosts are met, one should say do not take medicine; if it is overdone, there will surely be a change afterwards. This speaks of divining in a month or day when Fire is strong.
- **LY.ILL.YM.26** 鬼落在哪一爻，分的是一身的哪一处；鬼动在哪一宫（哪一卦），分的是四肢百体的哪一部分；鬼属哪一行，分的是哪个脏；鬼上的是哪个六神，分的是哪种情绪（七情）。注引了四家为证：卜筮元龟按爻位，从初爻的脚到六爻的头；黄金策按八卦，乾头坤腹、巽腰震足、兑口艮背、坎耳离目；海底眼按五行和地支；解云按六神：白虎是兵祸丧痛、青龙是酒色风流、玄武是忧愁思虑、勾陈是夹带怀胎、朱雀是恼怒焦烦、螣蛇是惊惶恐怖。
  By which of the six lines the ghost flows through, the part of the whole body is told; by which of the eight palaces it stirs in, the four limbs and trunk; by which of the five phases it belongs to, which of the viscera; by which of the six spirits it appears in, which of the seven emotions. The note quotes four authorities: the Bushi Yuangui by line position, from the foot on the first line to the head on the sixth; the Huangjin Ce by trigram, Qian the head and Kun the belly, Xun the waist and Zhen the foot, Dui the mouth and Gen the back, Kan the ear and Li the eye; the Haidi Yan by the five phases and branches; and the explanation by the six spirits: the White Tiger is war, mourning and grief, the Azure Dragon wine, lust and dissipation, the Dark Tortoise worry and anxious thought, Hook Earth complication and pregnancy, the Vermilion Bird anger and vexation, the Coiling Snake fright and terror.
- **LY.ILL.YM.31** 有人问病症发作是不是真的，鬼爻实就是真的，鬼爻虚就不是。有人占担心会不会成病，遇到随墓、助伤，病就已经成了；用神如果坏了，这个病怎么能治好呢？占在外面养病的，遇到随墓、助伤，世爻受损、鬼动，不行；在这里避暑的，也是这个道理。注：官鬼空、破、散、绝叫做虚，旺相、有生扶叫做实；用神空、破、绝、散就会成病，旺相有生扶就不会成病。
  If someone asks whether the outbreak of symptoms is real: when the ghost is full it is real, when the ghost is empty it is not. If someone divines whether a worry will become an illness, following into the tomb and helping to harm mean the illness has already formed; if the Subject is spoiled, how can the illness be cured? For one who divines convalescing away from home, following into the tomb, helping to harm, the Self broken and the ghost moving will not do; for one escaping the summer heat here, it is the same. The note says: the Pressure line Void, broken, scattered or in Extinction is called empty, and thriving and strong, generated and supported, is called full; the Subject Void, broken, in Extinction or scattered, the illness forms, and thriving and strong, generated and supported, it does not form.
- **LY.ILL.YY.11** 断病症以官鬼为凭据：鬼爻持在身、世、命爻、用爻上，或者鬼发动来刑、害、克、冲它们，或者官鬼伏在它们下面，或者它们发动变出官鬼，都各按官鬼的五行来断。鬼属阴爻、在内卦的：金是肺，木是肝胆，水是肾，子（水）是膀胱，火是心和小肠，土是脾、胃、大肠。鬼属阳爻、在外卦的：金是四肢、骨节、牙齿、右耳、小便，木是筋骨、左耳，水是口、嘴、皮、尿、痰涎、血、汗，土是鼻尖、腹背、肌肉，火是眼睛、胸、手心、脚底；另外辰、戌是头顶，丑、未是肩背。再按八卦：乾是头，坤是腹，震是脚，巽是大腿，坎是耳朵，离是眼睛，艮是背和手指，兑是口和舌头。
  The symptoms are judged by the Pressure line: if the Pressure line holds the Body, the Self, the destiny line or the Subject line; or the ghost moves to punish, harm, control or clash them; or the Pressure line is hidden beneath them; or they move and change into Pressure: in every case judge by the element of Pressure. If the ghost is yin and in the inner trigram: Metal is the lungs, Wood the liver and gallbladder, Water the kidneys and Zi the bladder, Fire the heart and small intestine, Earth the spleen, stomach and large intestine. If the ghost is yang and in the outer trigram: Metal is the limbs, bone joints, teeth, right ear and urine; Wood the sinews and bones and the left ear; Water the mouth, lips, skin, urine, phlegm and spittle, blood and sweat; Earth the tip of the nose, belly and back, and flesh; Fire the eyes, chest, palms and soles; Chen and Xu are the crown of the head and Chou and Wei the shoulders and back. And by the eight trigrams: Qian is the head, Kun the belly, Zhen the feet, Xun the thighs, Kan the ears, Li the eyes, Gen the back and fingers, and Dui the mouth and tongue.
- **LY.ILL.YY.12** 再按爻位来分：初爻是脚，二爻是大腿和膝，三爻是腹、小腹、腰、臀、肛门、小便，四爻是胸、胃、乳，五爻是脸、颈项、手（水是口，火是眼睛，土是鼻，水是左耳，金是右耳），六爻是头和脑。凡是哪一爻遇到鬼伏在下面、变出鬼、或者鬼发动来伤到它，那个部位一定留下毛病。
  Again by the position of the lines: the first line is the feet, the second the thighs and knees, the third the belly, lower belly, waist, buttocks, anus and urination, the fourth the chest, stomach and breasts, the fifth the face, neck and hands (Water the mouth, Fire the eyes, Earth the nose, Water the left ear, Metal the right ear), and the sixth the head and brain. Wherever a line has a ghost hidden beneath it, or changes into a ghost, or a ghost moves to harm it, that place will surely be left with an ailment.
- **LY.ILL.YY.13** 用爻遇到金鬼：属阴是肺腑的病，属阳是骨节疼痛、脓血淋漓；金鬼旺，是泻肚子再带一样生僻的病；金鬼衰，是血里生脓。遇到木鬼：属阴是肝胆的病，属阳是四肢风气酸疼、口眼歪斜、头疼口干；木动的，阳的痒、阴的疼；木鬼旺，是风病带热；木鬼衰，是在同一个字的病上再生出风来。遇到水鬼：属阴是遗精、盗汗、肾气枯竭，女的是血枯闭经，属阳是呕吐泄泻；水鬼旺，是呕吐带咳嗽；水鬼衰，是受寒腹泻兼虚弱。遇到火鬼：属阴是心和眼睛的病，属阳是疮痍疖毒、疟热呻吟；火鬼旺，先冷后热；火鬼衰，先热后冷。遇到土鬼：属阴是虚浮气喘、水蛊、脾泄，属阳是瘟疫时气；辰鬼是咽喉肿塞，戌鬼是腹痛胃伤，丑鬼是气促痿痹，未鬼是翻胃、噎膈、痨病咳嗽。
  If the Subject line meets a Metal ghost: yin means a disease of the lungs and viscera, yang means aching bone joints and dripping pus and blood; a strong Metal ghost brings diarrhea together with a rare ailment, and a weak Metal ghost brings pus forming within the blood. Meeting a Wood ghost: yin means a disease of the liver and gallbladder, yang means wind-qi with aching limbs, a crooked mouth and eyes, headache and a dry mouth; when Wood moves, yang itches and yin aches; a strong Wood ghost brings wind sickness with heat, a weak one wind growing upon the same rare ailment. Meeting a Water ghost: yin means seminal emission, night sweats and exhausted kidneys, and in a woman dried-up blood and stopped menses; yang means vomiting and diarrhea; a strong Water ghost brings vomiting with cough, a weak one cold diarrhea with weakness. Meeting a Fire ghost: yin means a disease of the heart and eyes, yang means sores, boils and poison, malarial fever and groaning; a strong Fire ghost brings cold first and then heat, a weak one heat first and then cold. Meeting an Earth ghost: yin means puffy weakness and wheezing, water-swelling and spleen diarrhea, yang means epidemic and seasonal qi; a Chen ghost is a swollen blocked throat, a Xu ghost belly pain and a damaged stomach, a Chou ghost shortness of breath and limp numbness, and a Wei ghost regurgitation, choking in the gullet, consumption and cough.
- **LY.ILL.YY.14** 水鬼变火、火鬼变水的，是忽冷忽热；内卦的鬼冲外卦、外卦的鬼冲内卦的，是内外都受了伤。鬼在内卦发动，是下元的病症；鬼在外卦发动，是上焦的病症。世爻上的鬼出现，是阳症；世爻上的鬼伏藏，是阴症。阴鬼变阳，阴症变成阳症；阳鬼变阴，阳症变成阴症。
  A Water ghost changing into Fire, or a Fire ghost into Water, means alternating chills and fever; an inner ghost clashing the outer, or an outer ghost the inner, means injury both within and without. A ghost moving in the inner trigram is a disorder of the lower origin; a ghost moving in the outer trigram is a disorder of the upper burner. A ghost at the Self appearing openly is a yang condition, and one at the Self hidden is a yin condition. A yin ghost changing into yang: a yin condition turns yang; a yang ghost changing into yin: a yang condition turns yin.
- **LY.ILL.YY.20** 卦里没有财，或者财爻空、绝，或者四个墓库的爻持着用爻的，吃不下东西；禄（财）落入绝地的，吃不下饭而死；鬼发动变出财的，饮食还能吃；财变出鬼、兄的，是因为吃东西使病加重；应爻发动合用爻、又变出财来伤用爻的，忌讳吃人家送来的东西。忌口：丑鬼忌牛肉，卯鬼忌兔肉，未鬼忌羊肉，酉鬼忌鸡肉，戌鬼忌狗肉，亥鬼忌猪肉；水鬼忌鱼腥、冷的、咸的东西，木鬼忌带核的果子、酸的东西，金鬼忌葱、蒜、姜、辣的东西，火鬼忌煎炒烤炙、香味浓的、苦的东西，土鬼忌米面、茄子、芋头、瓜菜、甜的东西。
  If there is no Wealth in the hexagram, or Wealth is Void or in Extinction, or the four tombs hold the Subject line, food and drink are not taken in; if the emolument (Wealth) falls into a place of Extinction, he dies without taking food; if the ghost moves and changes into Wealth, food and drink can still be taken; if Wealth changes into a ghost or Peer, the illness worsens because of eating; if the Other line moves to combine with the Subject line and changes into Wealth that harms it, gifts of food sent over must be avoided. Foods to avoid: a Chou ghost must avoid beef, a Mao ghost rabbit, a Wei ghost mutton, a You ghost chicken, a Xu ghost dog, and a Hai ghost pork; a Water ghost must avoid fishy, cold and salty things, a Wood ghost fruit with stones and sour things, a Metal ghost onions, garlic, ginger and hot pungent things, a Fire ghost fried, roasted and strongly scented or bitter things, and an Earth ghost rice and wheat, aubergine, taro, melons and vegetables and sweet things.
- **LY.ILL.YY.36** 每一宫都有十六个变卦，占的人遇到变入本宫卦的，灾福应验十分。外戒卦，吉凶从外面来；内戒卦，祸福从里面起；骸骨卦，活着的人羸弱消瘦，死了的人不得埋葬；棺槨卦，病人必定死亡；血脉卦，主血疾和漏下；绝命卦，事情多反复，为人孤独、和世俗不合；游魂、肌肉卦，精神恍惚，像做梦、像痴呆；归魂、坟墓卦，坟墓吉，事可以成。
  Each palace has sixteen changed hexagrams, and when the diviner meets a change into a hexagram of the own palace, calamity and fortune are borne out to the full. An Outer Warning hexagram: good and ill come from outside; an Inner Warning hexagram: calamity and fortune arise from within; a Bones hexagram: the living are frail and thin, the dead are not buried; a Coffin hexagram: in illness the person surely dies; a Blood Vessels hexagram means blood disease and leakage from below; an End of Life hexagram: affairs mostly reverse and repeat, and the person is solitary and out of tune with the customs; a Wandering Soul or Flesh hexagram: the spirit is dazed, as in a dream or as if foolish; a Returning Soul or Grave hexagram: graves are auspicious and the matter can succeed.

## 病因(Cause: how the illness was contracted, and from what)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/病因.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSQS.13** 占家宅的歌里说：杀爻旺相，官灾就起；杀爻遇到休囚，疾病就生。旧注：旺的杀主官非，衰的杀主疾病。
  The verse on dwellings says: when the killing line is strong, official calamity arises; when the killing line meets rest or confinement, illness arises. The old note: a strong killing spirit brings official trouble, a weak one brings illness.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.29** 家宅秘诀说：鬼爻的墓临在身上，常有女人卧病在床；阴爻的鬼主阴司作祟的病症，阳爻的鬼是户役方面的官非。
  The secret rules on the house say: with the ghost's tomb falling on the Body, there is often a woman lying ill in bed; a yin ghost means illnesses sent by the netherworld office, a yang ghost means official trouble over household duties.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.34** 疾病论里按六神看病因：财逢玄武和咸池，病是因为贪恋女色；妻财值青龙、又逢日建，病因是沉迷喝酒、伤了身体；金爻的兄发动，气喘；木鬼交重，身体疼痛；勾陈临在土爻或木爻上，病因是争地争田；螣蛇入了火爻土爻，灾是遇到惊吓怪异；白虎发动、临在财上，是因为办丧事得的病；青龙兴起、遇到鬼，是在喜庆的地方染上的。
  The chapter on illness reads the cause of an illness by the six spirits: Wealth meeting the Dark Tortoise and the Xianchi star, the illness comes from craving flowers and loving lust; Wealth on the Azure Dragon and also the Day branch, the illness comes from indulging in wine and harming the body; a Metal brother line moving, panting; a Wood ghost moving, bodily pain; Hook Earth on an Earth or Wood line, the illness comes from quarrelling over land and fields; the Coiling Snake entering a Fire or Earth line, the trouble comes from fright and strange things; the White Tiger moving and on Wealth, it is got from a funeral; the Azure Dragon rising and meeting a ghost, it is caught at a happy occasion.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.03** 易卦发蒙歌说六神的自注里有两条讲到病：玄武这个神，发动、克到身、世、应，主女的多有疾病、还有盗贼的事；勾陈这个神，发动、克到身、世、应，主有疾病、官司、争夺田土房屋山林的事，如果刑到世、应，主坟墓不安宁。
  Two of the notes on the six spirits in the Yigua Fameng Ge speak of illness: the Dark Tortoise spirit, when it moves and controls the Body, Self or Other, means much illness among women and matters of thieves; the Hook Earth spirit, when it moves and controls the Body, Self or Other, means illness, lawsuits and quarrels over fields, houses, hills and forests, and if it punishes the Self or Other, the graves are unquiet.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.08** 推占来情的歌说：胎、没两个卦（春天巽、离，夏天坤、兑，秋天乾、坎，冬天艮、震）出现，多半是占怀孕和病，是鬼神作祟，或者逃走、或者失财；凡是囚、死的卦，一定是占官司囚禁的事，并且犯到疾病灾厄和亲戚的丧亡。
