  The verses on reading the purpose of the asker say: when the two hexagrams Embryo and Sunk appear (in spring Xun and Li, in summer Kun and Dui, in autumn Qian and Kan, in winter Gen and Zhen), it is mostly a divination of pregnancy or illness, with ghosts and spirits afflicting, or flight, or loss of wealth; a hexagram in the trapped or dead state is surely a divination of lawsuits and imprisonment, and runs into illness, calamity and the death of relatives.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.11** 推占来情的歌说：应来克世，是别人谋我；世动克应，是我谋别人；旺相又生着官，会成阻碍；休囚又带着杀、鬼，就是互相侵犯。自注说：彼此相克相害，也要看爻的旺衰；克害的爻旺相、又带官鬼官符，是官司；克害的爻休囚、又带着杀和死符，病就是主病，也就是这件事主的是病。
  The verses on reading the purpose of the asker say: when the Other comes to control the Self, others are scheming against me; when the Self moves to control the Other, I am scheming against others; if strong and generating officials, it makes obstruction; if resting or trapped and carrying a killing and a ghost, they encroach on each other. Its own note says: where each controls and harms the other, one must also look at whether the line is strong or weak; if the line that controls and harms is thriving and strong and carries Pressure or the Official Tally, it is a lawsuit; if the line that controls and harms is resting or trapped and carries killings and the Death Tally, the illness is the main matter, that is, what it signals is illness.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.14** 鬼爻在初爻，是在堂屋里得的病；在三爻和二爻，是在门庭；在四、五爻，是在道路上，或者遇上风雨，伤身惊心。鬼爻入坎，是河海上得的病；丧门临在象上，是死丧之家；福德的象来到，是因酒食；本宫五鬼的象，是喧哗吵闹。
  The Pressure line on the first line: caught in the hall of the house; on the third and second: at the gate and courtyard; on the fourth and fifth: on the road, or meeting wind and rain it harms the body and startles the heart. The Pressure line entering Kan: illness from rivers and seas; Mourning Gate sitting on the image: a house of death and mourning; when the Output image comes it is through wine and food; the image of the Five Ghosts of the own palace is clamour and uproar.
- **LY.ILL.EL.03** 要知道灾星从哪里起，要把交（动）、复（变）的方位弄准：爻静就取外卦；没有官鬼，就到本宫去找；要推官鬼伏在哪一爻下面，鬼上面的飞爻才有缘由。比如占得家人卦，鬼伏在三爻辛酉金之下，只看飞神是己亥水，就说西北方冒犯了灵神；飞神、伏鬼都空的，病人终究没有邪祟侵扰。注说：占从哪里得灾、冒犯了哪个方位的祟，不论卦里有鬼没有鬼，只要见动爻，就用动爻来取；火动是南，水动是北；卦如果安静而有官鬼，就取外卦来断，外巽是东南起祸，外乾是西北招殃；卦安静又没有鬼的，不取外卦，看鬼伏在哪一爻之下，由鬼上面的飞爻定方向，比如讼卦安静，鬼伏在三爻午火之下，就说病起南方；又如遁卦三爻、六爻动，有动爻就以动爻为准，动申是西南，戌是西北，病从一处来哪有两个方向，一定要两个并看，就只说西方；又如遁卦安静，卦里虽有鬼却没有动爻，就用外卦来断，祸从西北来；又如小畜卦初爻、二爻动，官鬼虽然没有，动爻可以取，子当正北，寅是东北，两个象一起推，总说在北；又如小畜卦，无官难取外卦，才看鬼伏在三爻之下，祟从西北方来；卦里如果没有鬼，伏鬼又空，就没有神祟。
  To know from where the star of calamity rose, get the directions of the moving and the returning lines right: if the lines are still, take the outer trigram; if there is no Pressure line, look in the home palace; work out under which line the Pressure line lies hidden, for the flying line above the ghost gives the reason. Suppose Jiaren is obtained, with the ghost hidden under the third line, Xinyou Metal; just look at the flying line, Jihai Water, and say the northwest has offended a spirit; if both the flying line and the hidden ghost are Void, the patient in the end is not invaded by evil influences. The commentary says: to divine from where the calamity was got and in which direction the haunting was offended, whether or not the hexagram has a ghost, if there is a moving line, take it by the moving line; a moving Fire is south and a moving Water is north; if the hexagram is still but has a Pressure line, judge by the outer trigram, so that an outer Xun means misfortune rising in the southeast and an outer Qian means calamity from the northwest; if the hexagram is still and has no ghost, do not take the outer trigram, but see under which line the ghost lies hidden and fix the direction by the flying line above the ghost, so that in a still Song with the ghost hidden under the third line, Wu Fire, one says the illness rose in the south; again in Dun with the third and sixth lines moving, with moving lines go by the moving lines, a moving Shen is southwest and Xu northwest, and since the illness comes from one place and not two directions, if the two must be looked at together, say only west; again in a still Dun, although the hexagram has a ghost there is no moving line, so judge by the outer trigram, and the misfortune comes from the northwest; again in Xiaoxu with the first and second lines moving, although there is no Pressure line the moving lines can be taken, Zi being due north and Yin northeast, and reckoning the two images together one says only north; again in Xiaoxu, when there is no Pressure line and the outer trigram is hard to take, look at the ghost hidden under the third line, and the haunting comes from the northwest; and if the hexagram has no ghost and the hidden ghost is again Void, there is no haunting by a spirit.
- **LY.ILL.EL.04** 坤宫鬼旺，是遭了坟地野外；艮宫官动，是起于山林；离宫，或是到了窑炉的地方；乾、兑，是曾冲犯寺庙的门；震、巽，是树林花草和道路；坎卦，是江河池沼村落。鬼在本宫，不是出了境；官鬼在别的宫惹祸，又另外评断。注说：官鬼发动时，不像前面那样断：鬼在坤宫，一定是往西南犯了祟、或者登了坟墓、或者在田野之间；鬼在艮宫，曾经去过东北，不然就是往山林；鬼在离宫，南方染的祸、或者去了火场、或者到了窑炉的地方；鬼在兑宫，正西有所冒犯，或者去了庵堂、或者住在水口；鬼在震宫，正东惹祸，或者去了树林下、或者上了船轿；巽宫鬼发，曾经走过东南、或者到了竹园旁边、或者住在柴草旁；鬼在坎宫，病从北方来，或者踏过江湖之口、或者遇上骤雨淋身。鬼如果独发，再看地支所属的方位，比如贲卦初爻独发，病起东方，其余都照这样；鬼不是独发的，就不必说；卦爻乱动，照前面的八卦推，再看鬼在本宫或在内卦，就说当地的灾；鬼在外卦或者在他宫动的，要推病起于外地，学的人自己要变通。
  A ghost thriving in Kun means graves and open country; Pressure moving in Gen rises from mountain and forest; in Li, perhaps a kiln or furnace; in Qian and Dui, having struck against the gate of a temple; in Zhen and Xun, woods, flowers and paths; in Kan, rivers, pools and villages. A ghost in its home palace has not left the region; trouble incurred in another quarter is judged otherwise. The commentary says: when the Pressure line moves, judgment is not as above: a ghost in Kun surely means offending a haunting in the southwest, or climbing a grave, or being in the fields; in Gen he has gone northeast, or else to the mountains and forest; in Li, misfortune caught in the south, or a trip to a fire ground or a kiln; in Dui, an offence in the due west, or a trip to a hermitage, or living by a water mouth; in Zhen, misfortune in the due east, or being under the trees or in a boat or sedan; a ghost moving in Xun, he has walked the southeast, or been by a bamboo garden, or by firewood and grass; a ghost in Kan, the illness came from the north, or he trod on the mouth of a river or lake, or was soaked in a sudden rain. If the ghost moves alone, look again at the direction the earthly branch belongs to, so that in Bi with the first line moving alone the illness rose in the east, and the rest follow this; if the ghost does not move alone there is no need to say more; if the lines move in disorder, reckon by the eight trigrams as above, and see whether the ghost is in the home palace or the inner trigram, which is a local calamity; a ghost in the outer trigram, or moving in another palace, calls for the illness to have risen in a place outside, and the learner must adapt.
- **LY.ILL.EL.05** 鬼带青龙发动，是因为权势和喜悦，或者是去了亲友办喜事的人家、到了树林芳草的地方、或者叨扰了酒席和簪花——灾由喜处来，或探亲访友、酬酒簪花、游山、居树下、谒贵人，这些地方病由此得。鬼带朱雀，是因为恼怒，是非、词讼和文章，看书、写字、唱歌都是生灾惹祸的缘由——灾由怒气来，或被闲非词讼、或写书修书、或遇喧哗、或逢歌耍、或看火场沾上灾祸。鬼带勾陈发动，病难痊愈，或许到过田边、坟前坟后，或是为别人修造、自己动工，莫非是犯了禁忌牵连上了——灾由跌磕来，或登过坟左墓右、行过地后田前、内外动过工，所以有灾瘴。螣蛇带着鬼爻兴旺，病是惊惶恐吓造成的，或是被七情伤了气血，或是遇上鬼魅作妖精——灾由惊恐、多思虑、忧愁或遇妖邪来。白虎临着官鬼兴旺，或听见邻近有悲声、看见撤席行丧经过、看见兵戈和宰牲——祸起于哀声，邻里的丧事、新朋的撤席、去了孝堂之内、看见刀兵或遇上宰杀，这几样祸都从这里出。玄武是从水路来的，或贪酒色得的灾，或洗浴又遇雨，或受了寒邪病更乘势——玄武临官，灾由酒色、往江河、冒雨、失物、被盗惊起病。
  A ghost moving with the Azure Dragon comes from power and delight, or from going to the house of a relative or friend holding a happy event, to woods and fragrant grass, or from taking part in wine drinking and putting flowers in the hair; the calamity comes from a place of joy, or from visiting relatives and friends, toasting and wearing flowers, touring the hills, dwelling under trees or calling on a noble, and the illness was contracted in one of these ways. A ghost with the Vermilion Bird comes from vexation and anger, quarrels, lawsuits and writing; reading, writing and singing are all ways of bringing on calamity; the calamity comes from anger, from idle quarrels and lawsuits, from writing or revising books, from meeting uproar, from songs and play, or from watching a fire ground. A ghost moving with Hook Earth makes the illness hard to cure; perhaps he went by fields or in front of or behind a grave, or built for another or did works himself, and surely a taboo is implicated; the calamity comes from a knock or fall, from climbing to the left or right of a grave, walking behind the ground or before the field, or doing works inside or outside, and hence the calamity and miasma. If the Coiling Snake carries a thriving Pressure line, the illness is made by fright and terror, or the seven emotions have harmed the qi and blood, or ghosts and sprites have made demons; the calamity comes from fright, from much brooding, from grief or from meeting demons and evil. The White Tiger sitting on a thriving Pressure line: perhaps he heard weeping nearby, watched a funeral banquet cleared away or a funeral pass, or saw weapons or the slaughter of animals; the misfortune rises from the sound of mourning, a neighbour's funeral, a new friend's cleared table, going into a house of mourning, or seeing weapons or meeting slaughter, and from these several kinds misfortune comes. The Dark Tortoise comes by the water road, or the calamity is got from greed for wine and lust, or he bathed and also met rain, or took in cold evil, and the illness rode further; with the Dark Tortoise on the Pressure line, the calamity comes from wine and lust, going to river or lake, being out in the rain, losing goods or being frightened by theft.
- **LY.ILL.EL.42** 卦安静，就该找外卦的象；没有官鬼，另外看伏神；鬼上面的飞爻，方向就定下来了。注说：卦里没有动爻，只取外卦：比如外卦属乾宫，病就从西北来；外卦临坤，祸就起在西南；六爻内有鬼的，这样来看。内外都没有官鬼，又不取外卦为凭据，另外去找鬼伏在哪一爻之下，由鬼上面的飞神来定它的方向：假如占得颐卦安静，鬼伏在三爻之下，就取飞神庚辰，辰是东南方，断定一定没有差错；又如讼卦安静，鬼也伏在三爻午火之下，就说是南方；伏鬼再落空，就不要说是祸祟。
  When the hexagram is still, look for the image of the outer trigram; if there is no Pressure line, look separately at the hidden line; the flying line above the ghost fixes the direction again. The commentary says: if the hexagram has no moving line, take only the outer trigram: for example, if the outer belongs to the Qian palace, the illness comes from the northwest; if the outer sits on Kun, misfortune rises in the southwest; where there is a ghost among the six lines, look at it so. If neither inner nor outer has a Pressure line, and one does not rely on the outer trigram, look separately under which line the ghost lies hidden, and fix its direction by the flying line above the ghost: if, for example, a still Yi is obtained with the ghost hidden under the third line, take the flying line Gengchen, Chen being the southeast, and the judgment will surely not err; or in a still Song the ghost is also hidden under the third line, Wu Fire, and one says south; if the hidden ghost is again Void, do not speak of haunting.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.03** 看病先要弄清病因。六爻安静的时候，先找世爻下面伏着的神；再看官鬼藏伏在什么地方；再用爻象去考察它的真假。用爻是病人，应该有气；福德（子孙）是医师，能保全愈；子孙发动就不必服药（原文作「误」，校作「勿」）；卦宫旺相，病就迟疑退缩；六个位置里没有财，吃不下东西；兄弟交重，气积得深；鬼有好几个而不止一个，是原来就有的病；用爻发动却休囚，受损于病因。眉批把这几句压缩成了词条。
  In an illness, first find its cause. When the six lines are still, first look for the spirit under the Self; next see where the Pressure line lies hidden; and further use the images of the lines to examine its truth. The Subject line is the sick person and should have qi; Output (the fortune spirit) is the doctor and can bring full recovery; when Output moves, do not take medicine (the text has 'error', which the editor corrects to 'do not'); if the hexagram's palace is strong, the illness hesitates and shrinks back; with no Wealth in the six positions, food is not taken; with Peer crossing heavily, qi is accumulated deep; with many ghosts, not just one, the illness was there before; if the Subject moves while resting or trapped, it is harmed by the cause of the illness. The marginal comment compresses these sentences into headings.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.04** 鬼伏在哪一爻下面，要用五行来分辨。父母爻：因为忧虑得的，或是动了土，或是到修造的地方得的。兄弟爻：因为饥饱失常，或者因口舌气恼得的。官鬼出现：惊恐怪异，或者是在寺观庙宇里得的。子孙出现：或者因为欲事太过。妻财：饮食上得的，或者买东西得的。五行伏着五行：土伏土，生疮肿胀；火下火，手足的病；金遇金，大寒大热（中间「问乱未下」原文含混）；水下水，冷病；金下金，气喘咳嗽。阳宫里世爻发动主吐，阴宫里财爻发动主泻。鬼出现，病在外表；鬼伏着，病在心腹；病在里面，或是动的爻在下面受病，用爻也一样；鬼在外面，或是动土变成病，用爻也一样。
  Where the ghost hides, under which line, discriminate by the five phases. The Resource line: got from worry, or from moving earth, or got at a place of repair and building. The Peer line: from irregular hunger and overeating, or from quarrels and anger. The Pressure line appearing: fright and strangeness, or got in a temple, abbey or shrine. Output appearing: or from excess in matters of desire. Wealth: got through food and drink, or through buying things. Phase under phase: Earth hidden under Earth, sores and swelling; Fire under Fire, the hands and feet; Metal meeting Metal, great chills and fever (the words 'wen luan wei xia' in the middle are unclear); Water under Water, a cold illness; Metal under Metal, wheezing and coughing. In a yang palace the Self moving governs vomiting, in a yin palace Wealth moving governs diarrhoea. The ghost showing: the illness is on the outside surface; the ghost hidden: the illness is in the heart and belly; the illness inside, or the moving line below where the illness is received, the Subject line likewise; the ghost outside, or a moved earth turning into illness, the Subject line likewise.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.09** 用爻伏着的，那伏着的是我的爻，它上面的飞爻是对方；有动爻就看动爻和变爻，安静的就看有气的爻；如果占官事、疾病，六个爻都安静，就看世爻，把它当作父爻来看，问事情是因为什么得来的。
  If the Subject line is hidden, the hidden one is my line and the flying line above it is the other party; where there is movement look at the moving and changed lines, and where all is still look at the line that has qi; if divining a lawsuit or illness and the six lines are all still, look at the Self and take it as the Resource line, asking from what the matter was obtained.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.14** 海底眼的六亲属类里，官鬼管的物类有：神、邪、病、崇、盗，失物、输赢、灾、梦、忧；散句里还有「灾害伤身，鬼崇凶祸」「疾病疮疖，怪梦虚惊」——官鬼是病和鬼神作祟这一类事。
  In the classes of the six relations in the Haidi Yan, among what Pressure governs are gods, evil, illness, curses and thieves, lost things, winning and losing, calamity, dreams and worry; the prose adds 'calamity harms the body, ghosts and curses, ill-fortune and misfortune' and 'illness, sores and boils, strange dreams and false alarms' — Pressure is illness and the matters of ghosts and spirits afflicting.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.06** 占病时，本宫的官鬼出现并且发动，得病之初就知道根由；本宫的官鬼不出现、而别的宫有官鬼出现，是病早就伏在身上，这一回因七情四气感冒才发出来；本宫和别的宫都没有官鬼，是不知不觉得的病，起初谁也说不出为什么。旧注又附了两组按卦名的忌讳：明夷、观、贲、需、临最忌世爻和身爻入墓；大畜、丰、同人、蛊、夬不要遇上财爻鬼爻一起兴旺。
  In divining illness: if the Pressure of the home palace appears and moves, the source is known as soon as the illness begins; if the home palace's Pressure does not appear but another palace's Pressure does, the illness has long lain hidden in the body and now breaks out through the seven emotions and the four airs; if neither the home palace nor another palace has Pressure, the illness was contracted unseen and at first no one can say why. The old commentary adds two groups of taboos by hexagram name: for Mingyi, Guan, Bi, Xu and Lin, beware above all of Self and Body entering the tomb; for Daxu, Feng, Tongren, Gu and Guai, do not meet Wealth and Pressure both stirring.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.07** 本宫的官鬼伏在世爻下面，一定是旧病重新发作。如果伏着的鬼不伤用爻、用爻反而被世爻克，以前病轻、现在反倒沉重；如果伏着的鬼刑伤用爻、而用爻反而得到世爻的生扶，以前病重、现在轻。用爻要看问的是谁：比如占妻子的病就看财爻。
  If the home palace's Pressure is hidden beneath the Self line, it is surely an old illness breaking out again. If the hidden Pressure does not harm the Subject line but the Subject is controlled by Self, the illness was light before and is heavier now; if the hidden Pressure punishes and harms the Subject but the Subject is generated and supported by Self, it was heavy before and is lighter now. The Subject line depends on whom one asks about: for a wife's illness, for example, look at the Wealth line.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.08** 鬼伏在哪个六亲下面，就是怎么得的病：伏在妻财下，是饮食不节、饥饱失度，或者因为钱财、妇人得病；伏在子孙下，是喝酒过度、放纵房事，夏天露宿贪凉、冬天穿得太暖，或者吃多了养生炼丹的药；伏在父母下，是劳心劳力、忧虑伤神，或者因为动土修造、尊长得病；伏在兄弟下，是口舌争竞、停食生气，或者有人诅咒、因为赌钱争财得病。
  Which relative the Pressure is hidden beneath shows how the illness was got: beneath Wealth, eating without restraint and hunger and surfeit, or illness through money or a woman; beneath Output, too much drinking and unrestrained sexual indulgence, sleeping out in the cool in summer, dressing too warmly in winter, or too many drugs for nourishing and refining; beneath Resource, toil of mind and body and worry that harms the spirit, or breaking ground and building, or illness through an elder; beneath Peer, quarrels and rivalry, food stopped and anger felt, or a curse laid, or gambling and struggling for money.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.10** 鬼爻化出哪个六亲，就是在哪里、怎么得的病：化出父母，是在修造的地方得病，在五爻又属水，是在路上淋了雨；化出兄弟，是口舌呕气或者伤食，在三爻又属木，是在房里脱衣露体着了风寒；化出子孙，是在僧道寺院，或者打鱼打猎游戏时得的；化出财爻，多是伤食，或者因为妻子奴仆、买卖交易得病。六亲化出鬼爻，也照这样断。
  Which relative the Pressure line changes into shows where and how the illness was got: changing into Resource, it was got at a place of repair or building, and if it is on the fifth line and belongs to Water, it was got braving rain on the road; changing into Peer, it is from quarrels and vexation or harm by food, and if on the third line and Wood, from undressing and baring the body in the room and catching wind and cold; changing into Output, it was got at a temple of monks and Daoists, or while fishing, hunting and at play; changing into Wealth, it is mostly harm by food, or through wife or servants, or through buying and selling. Where a relative line changes into Pressure, judge in the same way.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.11** 鬼在本宫，是在家里得的病；鬼在别的宫（别的卦），是在外面染上的。鬼在下面三爻，一定是内伤的症候，不然病也在里面；鬼在外宫、又在上三爻，一定是外感风邪，不然也是外科的症候；上下都有鬼，是内伤兼外感，症候不止一种。
  Pressure in the home palace means the illness was got at home; Pressure in another palace or hexagram means it was caught elsewhere. Pressure in the lower three lines is surely a syndrome of inner damage, or else the illness is in the inside; Pressure in the outer palace and in the upper three lines is surely outer wind evil from without, or else a surgical syndrome; with Pressure both above and below it is inner damage together with outer contagion, and the syndrome is mixed.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.25** 应爻上坐着官鬼，来刑、克、合世爻（王洪绪说是用爻），一定是因为探望亲友的病而被缠染上。鬼爻属土的，是流行的时疫。用爻临在应爻上，一定病倒在别人家里。
  When a Pressure line sits on the Other line and punishes, controls or combines with the Self line (Wang Hongxu says the Subject line), the illness was surely caught by entanglement through visiting a sick relative or friend. If the Pressure line is Earth, it is an epidemic of the season. If the Subject line sits on the Other line, the patient surely lies ill in someone else's house.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.26** 官鬼爻持世，病本来有旧根；再伤到用爻，一定是旧病又犯。不然也难脱身，要靠祈禳保佑才吉。卦身上坐着鬼，也是旧病。
  When a Pressure line holds the Self line, the illness surely has an old root; if it also wounds the Subject line, it is an old illness breaking out again. Otherwise it is hard to shake off, and protective rites are needed to be lucky. A Pressure line on the hexagram Body is also an old illness.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.132** 《黄金策》年时章（占一年的事）说：「更嫌遇到土（鬼），千家万户的疫病就像符契一样相同。」旧注：土鬼发动，这一年多瘟疫，生旺的更厉害，休囚的稍轻；在艮宫发动，瘴气也重；克世爻，人多死于疫病，世爻克它就不妨；鬼带白虎，也是瘟疫的征兆。王洪绪：土鬼发动，或者临白虎，都主瘟疫，如果克世，人多病死，有制伏就不妨。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on the year (divining a whole year) says: 'Still worse if it meets Earth: the epidemics in a thousand households tally as one.' The old commentary: an Earth ghost moving, the year has many epidemics, worse if strong and thriving, lighter if resting or confined; moving in the Gen palace, the miasma is heavy too; controlling the Self line, many people die of the epidemic, but if the Self line controls it, it does no harm; a ghost carrying the White Tiger is also a sign of epidemic. Wang Hongxu: an Earth ghost moving, or sitting on the White Tiger, both mean epidemic; if it controls the Self line many people fall ill and die, but if it is restrained, no harm.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.141** 《黄金策》坟墓章说：「月卦的勾陈和土鬼，瘟疫就会侵袭。」旧注：月卦是月将、勾陈，土鬼临在世爻、身爻上，主时灾、瘟疫侵袭。王洪绪的说法一样。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on graves says: 'The Hook Earth of the month hexagram and an Earth ghost: epidemic attacks.' The old commentary: the month hexagram is the Month general and Hook Earth; an Earth ghost sitting on the Self line or the Body means a seasonal calamity, with epidemic attacking. Wang Hongxu says the same.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.150** 《黄金策》总断千金赋说：「最要紧的是卦身的位置，喜欢被扶持，不喜欢被伤害。」旧注：身就是月卦身，取法见第一卷；成卦以后，先看卦身出现不出现，和月建、日辰、动爻有没有牵涉，吉凶就显出来了，所以卜易以身为最要紧；例如占得困卦，卦身在午，兑宫以午火为官鬼，官鬼旺就是官非，衰就是疾病。王洪绪：身是月卦身，占事就是事体，占人就是人身，只喜欢生扶拱合，不适宜克害刑冲。
  The Huangjin Ce's Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements says: 'What matters most is the place of the Body: it welcomes support and does not welcome harm.' The old commentary: the Body is the Body of the month's hexagram, with its method in the first volume; after the hexagram is formed, first see whether the Body appears, and whether it bears on the Month, the Day or the moving lines, and good and ill show, which is why the Body is the most important in divining the Changes; for example, if the Kun (Confinement) hexagram is obtained and the Body is at Wu, then in the Dui palace Wu Fire is Pressure, and when Pressure is strong it means official trouble, when weak, illness. Wang Hongxu: the Body is the Body of the month's hexagram; in divining an affair it is the matter itself, in divining a person the person's body; it only welcomes generation, support, flanking and combination and does not suit control, harm, punishment and clash.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.02** 看鬼伏在哪一爻下，再按金木水火土分辨：伏在父母下，是忧心得的病，或动了土、去修造的地方得的；伏兄弟下且动，是饥饱失节得的，或因口舌、生气得的；伏子孙下且动，是牵扯招惹得的，或欲事太过得的；伏财下，是饮食上得的，或买东西得的；官鬼出现，是惊恐怪异，或在寺观庙宇里得的。土下伏土生疮肿，火下伏火病在手足，金见金闷乱，木下木寒热，水下水冷疾，金下伏火喘满；阳宫财动主吐，阴宫财动主泻。鬼爻现在外、金鬼爻伏在里，主心腹病；鬼在内，下部受病；鬼在外而动，上部受病；用爻同。
  Look at which line the ghost is hidden under, and tell it apart by Metal, Wood, Water, Fire and Earth. Hidden under Resource: caught through worry, or by disturbing the earth, or by going to a place of building and repair. Hidden under Peer and moving: caught through the lapse of hunger and fullness, or through quarrels and anger. Hidden under Output and moving: caught through entanglement, or through excess of desire. Hidden under Wealth: caught through food and drink, or through buying things. The Pressure line appearing: fright and strange things, or caught in a temple or monastery. Earth hidden under Earth: sores and swelling; Fire under Fire: the hands and feet; Metal meeting Metal: oppression and confusion; Wood under Wood: chills and fever; Water under Water: a cold illness; Metal under Fire: panting and fullness. In a yang palace Wealth moving means vomiting, in a yin palace Wealth moving means diarrhoea. With the Pressure line showing outside and the Metal Pressure line hidden inside, it is an illness of the heart and belly; a ghost inside, the lower part is ill; a ghost outside and moving, the upper part is ill; the Subject line is the same.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.10** 鬼爻在内，病从里面生；鬼在外，灾从外面来。火鬼必在南方；金鬼必在西方，路上生灾，又主胸；金鬼则病在肺，逢火成脓，见木生风，遇螣蛇则虚闷。
  The Pressure line inside, the illness arises from within; the ghost outside, the calamity comes from outside. A Fire ghost must be in the south; a Metal ghost must be in the west, calamity arising on the road, and it governs the chest as well; a Metal ghost then has the illness in the lungs, meeting Fire it makes pus, seeing Wood it breeds wind, and meeting the Snake it is a deficient oppression.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.19** 卦里有鬼没有子孙，多怪梦、并且绝嗣；有鬼没有财，主疾病多端。
  With a ghost and no Output, many strange dreams and no heir; with a ghost and no Wealth, illness in many forms.
- **LY.ILL.TXF.09** 已经讲了染灾的表里，还要说得病的因由。注说：占病，先断定症候，然后议论病因；能把这一章弄明白，才称得上通神的占卜。六兽临在官鬼上，要分内外；八宫值鬼，要分阴阳。注说：但凡鬼临着六神，就看它在哪一爻：在内，是在家里得病；在外，是在别处招来的灾殃。青龙鬼：酒色过度，或者因为财而有伤，妇人是因喜事有病；青龙鬼落空，有堕胎的忧患。朱雀鬼：口舌和咒骂，在二爻是在灶下咒骂，在三爻是当门咒骂，或者是家堂神前咒骂；如果朱雀临着金鬼发动，是敲着锅铛咒骂。勾陈鬼：饮食不节制，饥饱失常，脾胃不调；临着木鬼，是在修造动土的地方得的病。螣蛇鬼：事务缠心，忧愁思虑，或者虚惊怪异，因而得病，这一爻大多是心家受病。白虎鬼：斗殴、跌扑，伤筋损骨，或者是寒酸冲肺。玄武鬼：色欲过度，饥饱失常、冒雨。
  Having discussed the outside and inside of the infection, one must now speak of the cause of the illness. The commentary says: in divining illness, first settle the symptoms, and then discuss the cause; if one can understand this chapter, one may truly be called one whose divination reaches the gods. When the six beasts sit on the Pressure line one must distinguish inside and outside; when the ghost falls in the eight palaces one should tell yin from yang. The commentary says: whenever a ghost sits on one of the six spirits, look at which line it is on: inside, the illness was got at home; outside, calamity was brought on elsewhere. The Azure Dragon ghost: excess in wine and lust, or injury through wealth, and in a woman illness from a joyful event; the Azure Dragon ghost Void: the danger of miscarriage. The Vermilion Bird ghost: quarrels and cursing; on the second line, cursing beside the stove; on the third line, cursing at the door, or cursing before the household gods; if the Vermilion Bird sits on a Metal ghost that moves, cursing while banging pots and pans. The Hook Earth ghost: irregular eating, hunger and overeating, the spleen and stomach out of order; if it sits on a Wood ghost, the illness was got at a place of building and digging. The Coiling Snake ghost: affairs entangling the heart, worry and brooding, or a false fright and strangeness, and so the illness is contracted; this line is mostly an illness of the heart. The White Tiger ghost: brawls and falls, injury to sinews and bones, or cold and sourness striking the lungs. The Dark Tortoise ghost: excess of lust, hunger and overeating, and exposure to rain.
- **LY.ILL.TXF.10** 卦里如果没有鬼，病就难医，症候也难决断。只能用克的杀发动的来断；不动的，就用克用爻的来断，再用伏着的鬼来断。如果鬼伏在父母爻下：忧心得的病，或者穿衣服得的病，穿得过多或不足，冬天就说衣服单薄，夏天就说衣服厚重。鬼伏在兄弟下：赌博争财；伏在子孙下：被外面的情事牵惹；伏在财下：饮食太过；伏在杀下：旧病再发，也难除根。老人的卦忌旺相，小孩的卦忌休囚。最好是病人的本命上卦，大吉。遇到阴鬼，夜里重白天轻；阳鬼，夜里轻白天重；阴阳各自依爻上来看。
  If there is no ghost in the hexagram, the illness is hard to treat and the symptoms hard to decide. One can only judge by the killing that controls and moves; if it does not move, judge by what controls the Subject line, and also judge by the hidden ghost. If the ghost is hidden under Resource: illness got from a worried heart, or from clothing, too much or too little: in winter say the clothes were thin, in summer say the clothes were heavy. The ghost hidden under Peer: gambling and quarrels over money; hidden under Output: entangled by an affair outside; hidden under Wealth: excess in food and drink; hidden under a killing: an old illness flares again, and it is also hard to root out. An old person's hexagram fears being strong, a child's hexagram fears being resting or trapped. Best is for the sick person's natal line to be in the hexagram, which is greatly auspicious. A yin ghost is heavy at night and light by day; a yang ghost is light at night and heavy by day; yin and yang are each read from the line.
- **LY.ILL.YM.27** 鬼从世爻来的，病由自己而起；从应爻来的，病由别人而来；从间爻来的，是体内中间的病。鬼伏在哪个六亲之下，就看因为什么事生了病；哪个六亲变出了鬼，就看因为什么事出现了病兆。因为父母爻管的是劳累辛苦，妻财爻管的是饮食，子孙爻管的是淫乐，兄弟爻管的是生气发怒，官鬼爻管的是邪祟妖异。
  Coming from the Self, the illness is of one's own doing; coming from the Other, it is from another person; coming from the between line, it is an illness of the interior. Under which relation the ghost hides shows from what matter the illness sprouted, and which relation turns into the ghost shows by what matter the illness was foreshadowed. For the Resource line is toil, the Wealth line is food and drink, the Output line is debauchery and pleasure, the Peer line is temper and anger, and the Pressure line is evil and monstrous things.
- **LY.ILL.YM.29** 病是从哪个方向起的，吉凶都生在动上：鬼跟着别的爻一起动的，就去察看那个鬼的方位，因为鬼是祸；鬼爻自己独发的，就去考察它所在的宫；卦象安静的，就去察看外卦，因为祟孽是从外面进来的；卦里没有鬼的，就去考察伏鬼上面压着的飞爻。注举例：动爻所在的方位就是病起的方位，离卦里卯动，病从东边来；鬼和别的爻一起动，就取鬼爻的方位，离卦里亥、卯都动，病从西北来；鬼独发，就查它在哪一宫动，离卦里亥独发，病从南边开始；六爻都静，考外卦，离卦静着，病从南方的地方缠染上；卦里没有官鬼，就取伏鬼上面飞爻的方位，卦里有官鬼而动爻多的，先找忌爻的方位，没有忌爻，才考外卦。
  From what direction did the illness rise? Fortune and misfortune are born of movement: where the ghost moves along with the moving lines, examine the direction of that ghost, for the ghost is the calamity; where the Pressure line moves alone, examine its palace; where the hexagram is still, look at the outer trigram, for haunting and calamity enter from outside; where the hexagram has no ghost, examine the flying line above the hidden ghost. The note gives examples: the direction of the moving line is the direction from which the illness arose, so if in Li Mao moves the illness comes from the east; if the ghost moves along with others, take the direction of the Pressure line, so if in Li Hai and Mao both move the illness comes from the northwest; if the ghost moves alone, seek in which palace it moves, so if in Li Hai alone moves the illness starts from the south; if all six lines are still, examine the outer trigram, so if Li is still the illness was caught from the south; if the hexagram has no Pressure line, take the direction of the flying line above the hidden ghost, and where it has Pressure but many moving lines, first seek the direction of the Adversary line, and if there is none, examine the outer trigram.
- **LY.ILL.YM.30** 所以乾、兑两宫鬼旺，祸因出在寺庙；艮、坤两宫官鬼发动，忧患牵涉丘陵；坎、离说的是南北，震、巽说的是园林。木鬼是砍伐惹的，金鬼是刀兵的惊恐，土鬼是开挖动土的妨害，水是江河，火是城市。玄武是因为酒色风寒，青龙是唱歌吟咏歌舞，朱雀是争斗生气，白虎是哭泣丧事，螣蛇是从惊吓来的，勾陈是从跌倒起的。注举了四个例子：同一天占的几卦，乾宫发动的是被马撞伤，坎宫玄武是捕鱼得病，火鬼带螣蛇是在客馆听到失火吓出来的，巳午两鬼带青龙咸池是酒色所致——都不是按乾兑寺庙、坎为江河、火鬼城市、震巽园林去断，所以总的告诫是：不要死抠一条，只看合不合理，以及人心的灵巧善于推求。
  So when the ghost thrives in Qian or Dui, the misfortune comes from temples and shrines; when Pressure moves in Gen or Kun, the trouble is tied to mounds and hills; Kan and Li mean south and north, Zhen and Xun mean gardens and groves. A Wood ghost comes from felling trees, a Metal ghost from the fear of blades and soldiers, an Earth ghost from the hindrance of breaking ground; Water is rivers, and Fire is towns. The Dark Tortoise comes from wine, lust and wind-cold; the Azure Dragon is singing, chanting and dance; the Vermilion Bird is quarrels and temper; the White Tiger is weeping and mourning; the Coiling Snake comes from fright, and Hook Earth arises from a fall. The note gives four cases, all divined on the same day: a Qian-palace mover meant being trampled by a horse, a Dark Tortoise ghost in Kan meant catching fish, a Fire ghost with the Coiling Snake meant a fright at hearing of a fire at an inn, and Si and Wu ghosts with the Azure Dragon and Xianchi meant wine and lust; none was judged by temples for Qian and Dui, rivers for Kan, towns for a Fire ghost or gardens for Zhen and Xun, and so the closing warning: do not cling to one rule; go only by what reason fits, and by the mind's quick skill at working things out.
- **LY.ILL.YY.17** 看病是怎么得的，按鬼爻带的六神：带青龙发动，男的是酒色过度，或者去探望贵人、问候亲戚，因为钱财而得病，女的是因为喜事得病，遇空是堕胎；带朱雀发动，是生气、口舌，或者被人咒骂诅咒得病，在二爻的是灶边咒骂，在三爻的是当门咒骂，在五爻的是香火下、道路上咒骂，金鬼带朱雀发动的是敲着锅咒骂；带勾陈发动，是跌打损伤、饥饱失常，伤了脾胃，勾陈的鬼再加木发动，是因为修造得病；带螣蛇发动，是忧愁惊吓、思虑伤神，或者遇到妖邪得病；带白虎发动，是斗殴受伤，或者在丧葬、刀兵、宰杀的场合得病；带玄武发动，是色欲过度、忍饥冒雨，或者丢了东西被盗而得病。
  How the illness was caught, by the six spirits the ghost carries. With the Dragon and moving: in a man from excess of wine and women, or from visiting a noble or calling on kin, caught over money; in a woman from a joyful event, and if Void a miscarriage. With the Vermilion Bird and moving: from anger and quarrels, or from being cursed — on the second line, cursing at the stove; on the third, at the door; on the fifth, beneath the incense-fire or on the road; a Metal ghost with the Vermilion Bird, cursing while banging a pot. With the Hook Earth and moving: injury from falls and blows, hunger and overeating out of turn, an ailment of the spleen and stomach, and a Hook Earth ghost with Wood added and moving is caught from building work. With the Coiling Snake and moving: worry and fright, thought that harms the spirit, or meeting a demon or evil. With the Tiger and moving: injury from brawling, or caught at a funeral, in a scene of blades and weapons, or of slaughter. With the Dark Tortoise and moving: excess of lust, enduring hunger, braving rain, or losing things to theft.
- **LY.ILL.YY.18** 鬼伏在父母爻下、或父母爻发动变出鬼的，是忧心得病，或者衣服冷暖穿脱不及时得病；鬼伏在兄爻下、或兄爻发动变出鬼的，是因为赌博争财得病；鬼伏在财爻下、或财爻发动变出鬼的，是饮食没有节制而起：财爻在阳宫发动主呕吐，在阴宫发动主泄泻，财带土发动的吐，财带水发动的泻；鬼伏在子孙爻下、或子孙发动变出鬼的，是被外面的情事牵引，或者酒色致病（带青龙是嗜酒，带玄武是好色）；子孙爻空亡、绝的，没有钱调理；间爻上的鬼发动，胸膈不舒畅。
  A ghost hidden beneath the Resource line, or Resource moving and changing into a ghost: the illness is caught from anxiety of heart, or from clothing not changed in time for cold and warmth. Beneath the Peer line, or Peer moving and changing into a ghost: from gambling and quarrels over money. Beneath Wealth, or Wealth moving and changing into a ghost: from intemperate eating and drinking — Wealth moving in a yang palace means vomiting, in a yin palace diarrhea, Wealth with Earth and moving vomiting, Wealth with Water and moving diarrhea. Beneath Output, or Output moving and changing into a ghost: from being drawn away by outside affairs, or wine and women (with the Dragon a taste for drink, with the Dark Tortoise a love of women); if the Output line is Void or in Extinction there are no means for treatment and care; a ghost moving on a between line makes the chest and diaphragm tight.
- **LY.ILL.YY.33** 病是从哪个方向来的，以卦里的动爻来断：火是南，水是北，木是东，金是西，不管有没有鬼。卦里有鬼但不动的，就取外卦来断（乾西北、坤西南、巽东南、艮东北、离南、坎北、震东、兑西）。卦是安静的又没有鬼的，就看鬼伏在哪一爻下面，拿鬼上面的飞神来定灾祸的地方：伏在子下是北方，丑、寅下是东北，卯下是东，辰、巳下是东南，午下是南，未、申下是西南，酉下是西，戌、亥下是西北。卦乱动而鬼也动的，也拿鬼所在的卦来断；官鬼独自发动的，又拿鬼爻所值的地支来断。另外，鬼在本宫的内卦，就说是在本地得病；在别的宫、外卦，就说是在别处得病。
  Where the illness came from is judged by the moving lines in the hexagram: Fire is south, Water north, Wood east, Metal west, whether or not there is a ghost. If the hexagram has a ghost that does not move, judge by the outer trigram (Qian northwest, Kun southwest, Xun southeast, Gen northeast, Li south, Kan north, Zhen east, Dui west). If the hexagram is still and has no ghost, see under which line the ghost is hidden and use the flying line above the ghost to fix the place of the affliction: hidden beneath Zi, the north; beneath Chou or Yin, the northeast; beneath Mao, the east; beneath Chen or Si, the southeast; beneath Wu, the south; beneath Wei or Shen, the southwest; beneath You, the west; beneath Xu or Hai, the northwest. If the hexagram has lines moving in disorder and the ghost moves, judge by the trigram in which the ghost stands; if the Pressure line moves alone, judge by the branch that the Pressure line holds. Also a ghost in the inner trigram of the home palace: the illness was caught at home; in the outer trigram of another palace: caught elsewhere.
- **LY.ILL.YY.34** 病是哪年哪月哪天得的，以鬼爻长生的年、月、日来定：比如土鬼发动、火生土，是午年、五月、午日得的病；土的长生在申，或者是申年、七月、申日得的病；鬼落在辰、戌、丑、未上，或者是辰、戌、丑、未的年、月、日得的病；其他金、木、水、火的鬼，都照这样推。
  When the illness was caught is fixed by the year, month or day in which the Pressure line is born: for example, if an Earth ghost moves and Fire generates Earth, the illness was caught in a Wu year, the fifth month, on a Wu day; Earth is born at Shen, or the illness was caught in a Shen year, the seventh month, on a Shen day; a ghost on Chen, Xu, Chou or Wei, the illness was caught in a year, month or day of Chen, Xu, Chou or Wei; for the Metal, Wood, Water and Fire ghosts, work by the same pattern.

## 轻重(Light or heavy, new or old, better or worse by day and night)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/轻重.md`

- **LY.ILL.EL.22** 以福神为主，用象为凭。身处灾难、相貌残损，鬼兴鬼动，一定缠绵不去；随官入墓，终究难脱；助鬼伤身，永不痊愈；世爻受官鬼所伤，成为痼疾；子孙一动，立刻安好。六冲的卦，灾殃旦夕就散；没有鬼，病终究不缠；鬼如果空亡，没有疾厄；忌神旺相发动，病患延续多年；鬼变出福神，他日就解；用象兴隆，祸事不相干。注说：占带不带疾，最嫌官鬼兴隆，官鬼如果旺相，病一定缠身，纵然不旺相，发动也是这样；若遇上随官入墓、或助鬼伤身，必成痼疾；鬼克世爻也难脱体；如果得到子孙旺相、或发动、或持世、或临日月，病就离身。六冲的卦，这个病容易消，合处逢冲的后来才解；鬼不上卦以及落空亡，永远没有殃疾；纵然有鬼象，如果变出福神，眼下虽然凶，后来当解脱；虽然凭官鬼，也要看用爻，用爻遇旬空月破，病也难痊愈；土象休囚，又被忌神发动，岂不成凶？用爻纵然弱，如果遇上动出元神，虽然有这个灾，决不至损寿；用爻父母若得兴隆，官鬼又居衰地，起初虽然见病，终究不成殃。
  The fortune spirit is the main line, and the Subject image is the ground. The body in trouble, the features worn and disfigured; the ghost thriving and moving surely clings and lingers; following the Pressure line into a tomb, it is hard in the end to shake off; helping the ghost to harm the Self, it never heals; the Self wounded by Pressure becomes a chronic illness; when Output moves, ease comes at once. A Six-Clash hexagram disperses calamity by morning or evening; with no ghost the illness in the end does not cling; if the ghost is Void there is no illness; the Adversary strong and moving makes an affliction of many years; a ghost that changes into the fortune spirit is resolved some day later; the Subject image thriving, misfortune does not touch. The commentary says: for whether one carries an illness or not, most feared is a thriving Pressure line; if the Pressure line is strong the illness surely clings to the body, and even if it is not strong the same is true if it moves; meeting following into a tomb or helping the ghost to harm the Self, it will surely become a chronic illness; a ghost controlling the Self line also can hardly be shaken off; if Output is strong, or moves, or holds the Self, or sits on the Day or Month, the illness leaves the body. A Six-Clash hexagram makes this ailment easy to dispel, and a clash met in a combination is resolved only later; if the ghost is absent from the hexagram or falls into Void, there will never be affliction; even if there is a ghost image, if it changes into the fortune spirit, though it is ominous for the moment it will be resolved later; though one relies on the Pressure line, one must also look at the Subject line, and if the Subject meets Void or Month-break the illness too is hard to heal; if an Earth image is resting or trapped and the Adversary moves, how can it fail to be ominous? Though the Subject line is weak, if the Support comes out moving, then although there is this calamity it does not cost life; if the Resource line as Subject is thriving and the Pressure line sits in a weak place, then although at first there is illness, in the end it does no harm.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.02** 鬼在内卦，夜里病重；鬼在外卦，夜里一定较轻；内外都有鬼，人昏沉困顿，要提防旧病再来侵扰。替别人占，最怕应爻持鬼；官鬼持了身，命也会倾倒。眉批说：鬼在内，夜里重；鬼在外，灾厄解除；内外都有鬼，危险；代占时应爻有鬼、又逢官鬼的墓，命一定倾倒。后面的散句又把它重述了一遍：病因怕（鬼）旺相；子孙是药，不宜克世；财是禄命，忌被飞爻克；父母交重病重；最怕世空，又嫌月破，更嫌身世入墓；内外有鬼旧病再发；代占应爻持鬼凶。
  A ghost in the inner trigram makes the illness heavy at night; a ghost in the outer trigram, at night it is surely lighter; with ghosts both inside and outside the person is faint and trapped, and one must beware of the old illness coming again to invade. In divining for another, most to be feared is the Other line holding the ghost; if the Pressure line holds the body, life too topples. The marginal comment says: ghost inside, heavy at night; ghost outside, calamity released; ghosts both inside and outside, dangerous; in divining for another, the Other with the ghost and the official tomb, life surely topples. The prose that follows restates it: for the cause of illness the ghost is feared when strong; Output is the medicine and should not control the Self; Wealth is the salary and life and fears the flying line controlling it; Resource crossing makes the illness heavy; most feared are the Self Void and Month-break, and more disliked still the body and Self entering a tomb; ghosts both inside and outside bring the old illness back; in divining for another, the Other line holding the ghost is ominous.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.12** 鬼在内卦，病夜里重；鬼在外卦，病白天重；内外都有鬼，昼夜都不轻。如果卦里虽然有两个鬼，但一个旺一个空、或者一个动一个静，就是白天轻夜里重，或者白天重夜里轻，要分上下来断。
  Pressure in the inner trigram, the illness is heavy at night; Pressure in the outer trigram, heavy by day; Pressure both inside and outside, not light by day or night. If the hexagram has two Pressure lines but one is strong and one Void, or one moving and one still, it is light by day and heavy at night, or heavy by day and light at night, and one judges by upper and lower.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.13** 占卦时动爻是开头、变爻是结尾。卦中动爻生扶用爻、而变爻刑克用爻，病一定早上凉快、晚上发热，或者白天轻夜里重；动爻克用爻、变爻生用爻，反过来断。鬼爻化出所喜的爻、所喜的爻化出鬼爻，也是这样。
  In every divination the moving line is the beginning and the changed line the end. If the moving line in the hexagram generates and supports the Subject while the changed line punishes and controls it, the illness is surely cool in the morning and hot in the evening, or light by day and heavy at night; if the moving line controls the Subject and the changed line generates it, judge the reverse. Where a Pressure line changes into a welcome line, or a welcome line changes into Pressure, it is the same.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.34** 官鬼爻持世，病就是轻，也难医治：一定难以脱身。整个卦象再轻，也不容易痊愈。就算用爻有气、子孙当权，眼前能医好，以后也一定断不了根。
  When a Pressure line holds the Self line, even a light illness is hard to treat: it is surely hard to shake off. However light the whole image, it does not easily heal. Even if the Subject line has force and Output holds power, so that it can be cured for now, the root will surely not be cut later.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.36** 别的占都喜欢旺相，只有占病不喜欢：人在病里一定衰弱，平时再强健的人这时也瘦了，哪来的旺？所以用爻得时有气、又遇到日辰、动爻来生扶，是「太刚则折」的兆头，到生旺的日子一定凶；如果有动爻、日辰来刑克它，就不嫌它旺。
  Every other divination likes strength, but divining illness does not: a person in illness is surely weak, and even one who is usually robust grows thin by then, so where is the strength? Hence a Subject line that is in season and has force and also meets generation or support from the Day or a moving line is a sign of 'too rigid, it snaps', and on the day of birth or strength it is surely unlucky; if a moving line or the Day punishes or controls it, its strength is not held against it.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.68** 官鬼带着日辰、又动出在卦里的，一定是近来突然得的病，不是久病。如果日辰虽然是官鬼，却没有出现在卦里，就不是这样；可以说这病眼下正厉害，必须等这一阵过去，才可以用药。
  A Pressure line that carries the Day and moves out in the hexagram is surely an illness got suddenly in these days, not a long one. If the Day is Pressure but does not appear in the hexagram, it is not so; one can say the illness is at its fiercest just now, and only after this has passed can medicine be used.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.130** 《黄金策》总断千金赋说：「刑和害不适宜临在用爻上。」旧注：凡是刑爻成为主象，一定不利：占事，事不成；占物，物不好；占病，病一定死；占人，这个人有病；占妇人，一定不贞洁；占文书，一定有破绽；占官司，有利也一定有罪责；害爻成为主象，也一定坏事，大致和刑爻相似，化入的也一样，但要按衰旺分它的轻重。王洪绪：用神、身、世遇到日辰相刑，一定不利，占病是病沉重，占人是有病。
  The Huangjin Ce's Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements says: 'Punishment and harm should not fall on the Subject.' The old commentary: whenever a punishing line becomes the principal image it is sure to be unfavourable: an affair does not come to completion, a thing is not good, in illness the illness is sure to be fatal, in divining a person the person is ill, a woman is surely not chaste, documents are sure to have a flaw, a lawsuit brings blame even with a gain; a harming line as the principal image surely spoils things too, much like a punishing line, and so too one that is changed into, but its weight must be told by strength and weakness. Wang Hongxu: when the Subject, Body or Self meets a Day branch that punishes it, it is sure to be unfavourable, in illness the illness is grave, and in divining a person he is ill.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.154** 《黄金策》总断千金赋说：「凶爻变成长生，祸势炽盛而没有散。」旧注：主事的爻化入长生都吉，只是成得迟；只有坏事的爻化入长生，祸根才刚萌发，一天天增长，凶恶的势头一定要到盛了才停；比如占病，福神（子孙）化成长生，病势一天天减轻就可以；鬼（官鬼）化成长生，病势一天比一天沉重，一直到墓、绝的日子才杀掉它的势头。王洪绪：用爻化入长生的吉；凶神化入长生，祸根日渐增长，一定等到墓、绝日才锄掉它的势头。
  The Huangjin Ce's Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements says: 'An ominous line changing into Birth blazes and does not disperse.' The old commentary: when the line that governs the matter changes into Birth, all is auspicious, only the completion is late; only when the line of a bad matter changes into Birth does the root of its calamity just begin to sprout and grow day by day, and the ominous force does not stop until it has become full; for example in divining illness, if the Fortune line (Output) changes into Birth, the illness lessening day by day will do; if the ghost (Pressure) changes into Birth, the illness grows heavier day by day, until the Tomb or Extinction day, when its force is killed. Wang Hongxu: a Subject line changing into Birth is auspicious; an ominous spirit changing into Birth, the root of calamity grows day by day, and one must wait for the Tomb or Extinction day before its force is hoed out.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.156** 《黄金策》总断千金赋说：「卦里遇到凶星，避开它就吉。」旧注：凶星，就是刑、冲、克、害；避开它，就是六甲空亡；用爻无缘无故自己落空是真空，万事不成；如果被日辰、动爻刑冲克害到用爻而用爻在空，这是避凶而空，就是《金锁玄关》说的「克空为用」，不会坏，只是眼下略有阻碍，过了旬就成。书中例：六月壬申日占儿子的病，得姤变大过，父母旺动、用爻无气，本是凶兆，喜得用爻落在空亡避开了它，果然到丙子日病好了。王洪绪：凶星就是忌神；用爻被月建日辰伤克，不论空伏，无处可避；如果没有月、日伤克、只是遇到忌神发动来伤，用爻值旬空伏藏而不受克，叫做避，等到冲克忌神的日子，凶就自己散了。
  The Huangjin Ce's Rhapsody of a Thousand Golden Judgements says: 'A hexagram that meets an ominous star: avoiding it is auspicious.' The old commentary: an ominous star is punishment, clash, control or harm; avoiding it is the Void of the six jia; a Subject line that falls Void by itself without cause is true Void and nothing succeeds; if the Subject line is punished, clashed, controlled or harmed by the Day or a moving line and is in Void, this is Void to avoid ill, which is what the Jinsuo Xuanguan calls 'Void under control is used', it will not spoil, only for the moment there is some obstruction, and once the cycle has passed it succeeds. A case: on a Renshen day in the sixth month a son's illness was divined and Gou changing to Daguo obtained; the Resource line was strong and moving and the Subject line had no qi, which would be an ominous omen, but fortunately the Subject line was in Void and so avoided it, and indeed on the Bingzi day the illness was cured. Wang Hongxu: an ominous star is the Adversary; when the Subject line is hurt or controlled by the Month or the Day, whether Void or hidden, it cannot be avoided; if there is no hurt from the Month or Day and it is only an Adversary that moves to harm it, then if the Subject line is in the ten-day Void or hidden and does not suffer the control, this is called avoiding, and when the day comes that clashes and controls the Adversary, the ill disperses of itself.
- **LY.ILL.TXF.02** 要断定病的轻重存亡，专门看鬼爻；要定金木水火各是哪个方位，可以分出症候。注说：鬼爻旺的日子病沉重，入库的日子困顿，绝的日子轻可。鬼化鬼，病时进时退，或者有变症，或者旧病再发，或者症候驳杂。鬼持世，病难除根；鬼爻带杀持世，是瘵疾。卦中鬼爻遇日辰旺，是暴病；月建旺，是过了整月的病；鬼爻无气临身的，是久病，很难痊可。卦里没有鬼，病难安；鬼动伤身，症候急。凡占，鬼旺而不死，病容易好；鬼衰而不死，病难治。
  To decide the light or heavy and the life or death, look only at the Pressure line; to fix the quarters of Metal, Wood, Water and Fire, one can distinguish the symptoms. The commentary says: on a day when the Pressure line is strong the illness is heavy, on its store day it is trapped and hard, on its Extinction day it is light and tolerable. The ghost changing into a ghost: the illness advances and retreats, or there is a change of symptoms, or an old illness flares, or the symptoms are mixed. The ghost holding the Self: the illness is hard to root out; the Pressure line carrying a killing and holding the Self is a wasting illness. If the Pressure line in the hexagram is strong on the Day, it is a sudden illness; if strong on the Month, an illness of a full month; a Pressure line without qi sitting on the body is a long illness that can hardly be cured at once. With no ghost in the hexagram the illness is hard to settle; the ghost moving and wounding the body, the symptoms are urgent. In every divination, a strong ghost that does not kill means an easy cure, a weak ghost that does not kill means an illness hard to treat.
- **LY.ILL.TXF.04** 青龙得位，终究见到安康；白虎伤身，必成凶咎。注说：青龙如果临着子孙又克世，到日期就痊愈安康；临在应爻上克世，服药有效；临在用爻上发动，纵有凶险也不受伤；带着鬼爻，也不是凶恶发狂的病；在三爻发动，香火是福。苍屏说：青龙空亡，和福德一起，病人平日没有阴德；遇土动、虎伤身，那一年一定有悲哭；你来占病断存亡，白虎加临主哭丧；临着、克着父爻，就有咎，妻财的位置一定受伤；子孙遇上它，终究不成，兄弟遇上更不昌；再逢官爻持世，自身要恐怕有灾殃。凡遇白虎交重，是占病的大咎，无气还可以，旺相大凶；如果临着丧门、吊客、死符、丧车、又持着或克着用爻，决定是不起的征兆；如果得到子孙旺相发动，月解兴隆，才可以回生。
  With the Azure Dragon in its place one sees peace and health at last; with the White Tiger wounding the body it becomes ill and blame. The commentary says: if the Azure Dragon sits on Output controlling the Self, recovery and peace come on the appointed day; if it sits on the Other line controlling the Self, taking medicine is effective; if it sits on the Subject line and moves, even if there is danger no harm is done; if it carries a Pressure line, it is still not a fierce and frenzied illness; if it moves on the third line, the incense fire is the blessing. Cangping says: the Azure Dragon Void, together with Output, the sick person had no hidden merit in ordinary life; meeting Earth moving, the Tiger wounds the body, and that year there will surely be weeping; you who come to divine an illness to decide life or death, the White Tiger added governs wailing and mourning; sitting on and controlling the Resource line there will be blame, and the Wealth and wife positions will surely be wounded; Output meeting it ends in failure, Peer meeting it is still less prosperous; and if the Pressure line holds the Self, one's own person should fear calamity. When the White Tiger crosses heavily it is the great misfortune of divining illness: without qi it is still tolerable, strong it is greatly ominous; if it sits on Mourning Gate, Guest of Condolence, Death Tally or Funeral Carriage and holds or controls the Subject line, it surely is a sign that he will not rise; if Output is strong and moves and Month Release thrives, there may be a return to life.
- **LY.ILL.WWJQ.04** 文王金钱课的六十四卦课（原有的二十六卦）里，这五个卦的断辞说病不好：遁「占病不安」，否「疾病口舌是牵连」，坎「疾病未愈」，屯「疾病未好」，睽「病不愈」。
  In the sixty-four hexagram readings of the Wenwang Jinqian Ke (the twenty-six that the book originally has), the verdicts of these five hexagrams say that the illness does not mend: Dun, 'divining illness: unsettled'; Pi, 'illness and quarrels are entangled with it'; Kan, 'illness is not yet cured'; Zhun, 'illness has not mended'; Kui, 'illness is not cured'.
- **LY.ILL.WWJQ.06** 文王金钱课文末补录的网上传本里，无妄卦的卦辞和推断都说病不好：卦辞「疾病忧犯口舌愁」（病是一件忧事，又添上口舌之愁），推断「疾病不宜」。
  In the internet copy that the library appended at the end of the Wenwang Jinqian Ke, both the hexagram verse and the judgment of Wuwang say that illness does not fare well: the verse 'illness is a worry, and the grief of quarrels is added', and the judgment 'illness: not fit'.
- **LY.ILL.YM.24** 鬼是病的来源，是祟作怪的样子：所以鬼旺病就重，鬼衰病就轻；鬼动，病来得急而且会变；鬼被合住，灾就染上身、缠着不放；鬼被冲散，病快要解了；鬼落空，病还不是真的。
  The ghost is the source of illness and the shape of the haunting: so when the ghost is strong the illness is heavy, and when the ghost is weak it is light; when the ghost moves the trouble comes on suddenly and changes; when the ghost combines the calamity clings and lingers; when the ghost scatters the ailment is about to be resolved; when the ghost is Void the illness is not yet real.
- **LY.ILL.YM.25** 用日破、月破来分病是成了一半；用鬼在内、在外来分病在里还是在表；用鬼在变爻上还是伏着来分病是新的还是旧的。注说：问有没有病的，病算成了一半；问是不是没有病的，病算解了一半；内是里、外是表，变出来的是新病，伏着的是旧病，就是这个意思。
  Day-break and Month-break are used to tell a half-formed illness; being in the inner or in the outer to tell inside from surface; sitting in a change or in hiding to tell new from old. The note says: asked whether there is illness, it is half formed; asked whether there is none, the illness is half resolved; the inner is inside and the outer is surface, the changed is new and the hidden is old: this is the meaning.
- **LY.ILL.YY.15** 鬼属阴的，白天轻、夜里重；鬼属阳的，白天重、夜里轻。外卦的鬼旺、内卦的鬼衰，先轻后重；动爻克、变爻生，先重后轻。游魂卦里鬼做用爻，说话恍惚；归魂卦里鬼做用爻，昏闷不说话。
  If the ghost is yin, the illness is lighter by day and heavier by night; if yang, heavier by day and lighter by night. If the outer ghost is strong and the inner weak: light at first, then heavy; if the moving line controls and the changed line generates: heavy at first, then light. A ghost taken as the Subject line in a Wandering Soul hexagram: speech is confused; in a Returning Soul hexagram: drowsy, oppressed and silent.
- **LY.ILL.YY.16** 鬼伏藏的，病来了不觉得；鬼变鬼的，是膏肓的重病；鬼伏在鬼下、鬼变出鬼的，不是病症有变，就是两种病同时发作；用爻下面伏着鬼的，鬼旺时复发。世爻持着子孙、下面伏着鬼的，子孙旺的那天病退，鬼旺的那天病重。鬼生旺的日子病重，鬼入墓、胎的日子病困，鬼死、绝的日子病轻；原神当值的日子一定轻，忌神当值的日子一定重；财爻入墓、绝的日子也重。卦是六冲又乱动的，重。用爻带着青龙、福德而被动爻伤到的，虽然重也不会死；鬼发动伤到用爻，而月、日冲鬼的，临到凶险也能免。
  If the ghost is hidden, the illness comes unnoticed; a ghost changing into a ghost is a disease lodged in the vitals; a ghost hidden beneath a ghost, or a ghost changing into a ghost, is either a change of symptoms or two illnesses at once; a ghost hidden beneath the Subject line makes it recur when the ghost is strong. If the Self holds Output with a ghost hidden beneath it, the illness retreats on the day Output is strong and worsens on the day the ghost is strong. It is heavy on days the ghost is born or strong, wearied on days the ghost is in a tomb or Womb, and light on days it is in Death or Extinction; on days when the Support is on duty it is surely light and when the Adversary is on duty surely heavy; it is also heavy on days when the Wealth line is in a tomb or Extinction. A Six-Clash hexagram with lines moving in disorder is heavy. If the Subject line carries the Dragon and Fortune and is harmed by a moving line, it is heavy but he does not die; if the ghost moves to harm the Subject line but the Month and Day clash the ghost, he escapes even at the brink of danger.
- **LY.ILL.YY.19** 鬼旺盛发动伤到用爻的，是急症；鬼临在日辰上又发动的，是暴病；临在月建上，是拖了一个月的病；衰弱的鬼临在太岁上，是拖了一年的病；又，衰弱的鬼持着用爻发动的，是久病，难以除根。
  A strong moving ghost that harms the Subject line: an acute condition. A moving ghost on the Day: a sudden illness. On the Month: an illness of a month's standing. A weak ghost on the Tai Sui: an illness of a year's standing. Also a weak ghost holding the Subject line and moving: a long illness, hard to root out.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.09** 官鬼持世，病虽轻也难治，有两种情形。自己占病：官鬼持世，病难痊愈；就算子孙发动、克掉身边的鬼，眼前好了，终究断不了根，不然就又生别的病，叫「病不离身，身不离药」；官鬼持世又临帝旺、长生的，一定得长久的病。替别人占六亲的病：官鬼持世是忧郁的样子，只喜欢子孙发动、克掉这份忧郁，就算用神衰弱也管保平安。觉子补充：这个办法用于占自己和兄弟、儿子、妻子；占父母的病，官鬼是父母的元神；妻子占丈夫的病，鬼是用神，子孙一动反而伤它。
  A Pressure line holding the Self line means that even a light illness is hard to treat, and there are two cases. In divining one's own illness: with Pressure holding the Self line the illness is hard to heal; even if Output moves and controls away the Pressure by one's side, so that he recovers for the present, the root is never cut, or else another illness grows, which is called 'the illness does not leave the body, the body does not leave medicine'; if Pressure holds the Self line and sits on Peak or Birth, he surely gets a long-lasting illness. In divining a relation's illness for another: Pressure holding the Self line is the picture of worry and gloom, and all that is wanted is Output moving to control away this gloom, and then even if the Subject is weak, peace is assured. Juezi adds: this method serves for divining oneself and a brother, son or wife; in divining a parent's illness Pressure is the Support of the Resource line, and when a wife divines her husband's illness Pressure is the Subject, so a moving Output harms it instead.
- **LY.ILL.ZSZ.03** 周易尚占八卦断例里，这十五个卦的断辞说病平安、顺利：乾「问病获安」，既济「病者安康」，革「孕病皆吉」，贲「病安婚遂」，渐「病疾渐安」，豫「病者获安」，解「病者安宁」，随「疾病安康」，家人「病讼无忧」，益「词病俱平」，颐「讼吉病安」，鼎「婚成病利」，比「讼病解散」，萃「病讼终吉」，咸「利讼利病」。
  In the judgments by example of the Zhouyi Shangzhan the line of each of these fifteen hexagrams says that illness fares peacefully and well: Qian (Heaven), 'asking about illness: safety is gained'; Jiji, 'the sick are at peace and in health'; Ge, 'pregnancy and illness are both auspicious'; Bi (Adornment), 'the sick are safe, marriage is achieved'; Jian (Gradual Progress), 'illness gradually settles'; Yu, 'the sick gain peace'; Xie, 'the sick are at ease'; Sui, 'illness is eased and health returns'; Jiaren, 'neither illness nor lawsuit brings worry'; Yi (Increase), 'lawsuit and illness are both calm'; Yi (Nourishment), 'the lawsuit is auspicious and the illness safe'; Ding, 'marriage is made, illness fares well'; Bi (Union), 'lawsuit and illness are dissolved'; Cui, 'illness and lawsuit end auspiciously'; Xian, 'favourable to lawsuits and favourable to illness'.
- **LY.ILL.ZSZ.04** 周易尚占八卦断例里，这七个卦的断辞说病难好、拖得久：姤「病者难安」，遁「病者迁延」，晋「病者难瘥」，丰「病人沉滞」，师「病人迟瘥」，井「问病稽迟」，夬「病讼迟延」。
  In the judgments by example of the Zhouyi Shangzhan the line of each of these seven hexagrams says that illness is hard to mend and drags on: Gou, 'the sick will find it hard to be at peace'; Dun, 'the sick one drags on'; Jin, 'the sick will find it hard to recover'; Feng, 'the sick one is weighed down and lingering'; Shi, 'the sick recover late'; Jing, 'asking about illness: delay and slowness'; Guai, 'illness and lawsuit are delayed'.

## 生死(Life or death: will the patient recover, and the signs of danger)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/生死.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSQS.03** 卜筮全书的六神空亡诀说：白虎落了空亡，病可以痊愈。
  The Bushi Quanshu's verse on the six spirits falling Void says: if the White Tiger is Void, the illness can be cured.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.20** 占行人是否平安的歌里说：行人的爻旺相、没有入墓就活，囚死、落空亡就像水沫一样散去；卦和爻不变化入墓，都平安稳当，反过来一定死，只好去招魂、办殡宫。
  The verse on whether a traveller is well says: if the traveller's line is strong and does not enter the tomb it lives; if it is confined, dead or Void it drifts away like foam on water; if the hexagram and lines do not change into a tomb, all is peaceful and secure; the reverse is certain death, and one can only go and call the soul and prepare the mourning hall.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.35** 疾病论里看死生的一段：财爻是禄命，怕落空亡，伏着的是命爻，要防克害；先拿十死卦当凶，最凶的是用爻不上卦、又落空亡，有救的是福位（子孙）扶持得力；春天忌需、蒙，夏天嫌观、蛊，秋天忧剥、节，冬天怕旅、临；禄命随着官鬼，难救，世爻和身入了墓，难医；官鬼遇到大杀，怕伤身；鬼爻入了用爻，就主死。
  A passage of the chapter on illness on reading life and death: Wealth is the salary and fate and fears falling Void, and if it lies hidden the life line must be guarded from control and harm; first take the ten fatal hexagrams as ill-omened, the worst being a Subject line that is absent or Void, while what can be saved is when the Output place supports it with force; in spring avoid Xu and Meng, in summer dislike Guan and Gu, in autumn fear Bo and Jie, in winter dread Lu and Lin; with the fate line following the Pressure line, rescue is hard, and with the Self and the Body entering the tomb, treatment is hard; Pressure meeting a great killing, beware harm to the Body; a ghost entering the Subject line means death.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.36** 疾病论里接着讲死的征兆：要忌催尸杀，怕它落在病人的爻上；春天的辰、夏天的未最难当，秋天的戌、冬天的丑发动还是死；又嫌天转杀、地转杀，是最凶的神，再遇到日辰旺相，就可以说出死的地方；春天忌卯爻卯日，夏天嫌午位午爻，秋天的金爻、酉日最忌，冬天的子爻遇上子日；不论是白面少年，还是雪白鬓发的老人，家产再多，也总要拿一条命去见阎王。
  The chapter on illness goes on with the signs of death: one must avoid the Urging-the-Corpse killing and fear it falling on the sick person's line; Chen in spring and Wei in summer are the hardest to bear, and Xu in autumn and Chou in winter, rising, still mean death; also dislike the Heaven-Turning and Earth-Turning killings, the most ill-omened spirits, and if they meet a strong Day one may name the place of death; in spring avoid a Mao line and a Mao day, in summer a Wu place and Wu line, in autumn the Metal line and the You day, in winter a Zi line meeting a Zi day; whether a fair-faced youth or an old person with snow-white temples, however much wealth a family has, one must still go with one's life to see Lord Yama.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.53** 神煞歌例里三个主病人死亡的煞：墓门杀，甲乙见金、丙丁见水、戊己见木、庚辛见火、壬癸见土，病人不死也伤财；丧车杀，春鸡夏鼠秋兔冬马，有人来占病，没有别的可断，劝你赶紧去买棺材；沐浴杀，春辰夏未秋戌冬丑，发动，病人死亡。
  Three killings in the verses on spirit-stars that mean a sick person's death: the Tomb Gate killing, Jia and Yi meeting Metal, Bing and Ding Water, Wu and Ji Wood, Geng and Xin Fire, Ren and Gui Earth: the sick person, if he does not die, at least loses money; the Mourning Cart killing, the rooster in spring, the rat in summer, the rabbit in autumn and the horse in winter: a person who comes to divine illness has nothing else to be judged, and I advise you to buy a coffin at once; the Bathing killing, Chen in spring, Wei in summer, Xu in autumn and Chou in winter: if it moves, the sick person dies.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.05** 日六神内动的歌说：白虎在内卦发动，忧惊、疾病。自注说：白虎是金神；白虎也可以落在水爻上，这时水爻就是白虎，入了墓，占病大凶；例如庚、辛日得乾卦，甲子水爻动，变辛丑土，水墓在辰，白虎又是金神，叫两重入墓，必见死亡；白虎在秋天申、酉爻上动，旺相，更凶，休囚则吉。
  The song on the spirits of the Day moving within says: when the White Tiger moves in the inner trigram, there is worry, fright and illness. Its own note says: the White Tiger is the Metal spirit; it can also come on a Water line, and then the Water line is the White Tiger; if it enters the tomb, illness is greatly ominous; for example, on a Geng or Xin day, casting Qian, the Water line Jiazi moves and turns into Xinchou Earth, the Water tomb is at Chen, and since the White Tiger is also the Metal spirit, it is called entering the tomb twice, and death is sure to be seen; when the White Tiger moves on a Shen or You line in autumn it is thriving and strong and still more ominous, and when resting or trapped it is auspicious.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.10** 推占来情的歌说：世、应碰上日、月的破和刑，有官灾、盗贼、死亡、惊恐；如果不是和人争讼的事，就有死亡、盗贼和哭泣的声音。自注说：凡是世、应落在月破、月刑上、又来刑到本日的，一定有官司、盗贼、死亡、惊恐；再看有没有刑杀。《洞林》说，世应三刑又并着杀阴，是十死一生。
  The verses on reading the purpose of the asker say: when the Self and the Other meet the break and punishment of the Day and Month, there is official calamity, thieves, death and fright; if it is not a matter of quarrelling with others, there are death, thieves and the sound of weeping. Its own note says: whenever the Self and Other sit in the Month-break or Month-punishment and also come to punish the day itself, there are surely lawsuits, thieves, death and fright; then look also for punishment and killing. The Dongling says: when the Self and Other meet the three punishments together with the Yin Killing, it is ten deaths to one life.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.36** 怎么知道这家有人病得要死：卜筮正宗说是用神没有救护又入了墓；卜筮大全说是身、命、世、鬼入了墓，照这样推断。
  How to know that someone in a household is ill unto death: the Bushi Zhengzong says the Subject has no rescue and enters a tomb; the Bushi Daquan says the Body, the destiny, the Self and the ghost enter a tomb, and one judges by that.
- **LY.ILL.CA.03** 丰、蛊、困、明夷，夬和同人，一样推断；冬天的旅、春天的需，都困重；夏天的观、秋天的剥，都倾危。六爻安静，尤其难痊；杀和鬼交重，就不要医。财旺而身空，身一定丧；官生而命死，命应衰。两重官鬼，是因劳累复发；鬼化为兄，病不宜；鬼化为财，凶有准；鬼爻化子，痊愈无疑。
  Feng together with Gu, Kun (Confinement) and Mingyi, and Guai and Tongren: reason the same for all; in winter Lü, in spring Xu: all are trapped and heavy; in summer Guan, in autumn Bo: all topple and are in danger. With the six lines still it is still harder to recover; with killing and ghost crossing heavily one cannot treat it. Wealth strong and the Body Void: the body will surely be lost; the Pressure line alive and the natal line dead: life is surely weak. Two Pressure lines: a relapse from overwork; a ghost changing into Peer: the illness is not favourable; a ghost changing into Wealth: ill is certain; a Pressure line changing into Output: recovery is beyond doubt.
- **LY.ILL.CA.06** 明夷、蛊、夬、剥、丰、同（人）这六个卦，怎么经得起占病的凶；财、鬼两爻都发动，丧门、吊客热闹地聚在一起。丰、观、需、节、旅、贲、临，明夷、蛊、夬和同人，占病如果遇到这些卦，赶紧回家去办后事。
  Mingyi, Gu, Guai, Bo, Feng and Tong(ren): how can these six hexagrams bear the ill of divining illness; with Wealth and the ghost both moving, Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence crowd noisily together. Feng, Guan, Xu, Jie, Lü, Bi and Lin, Mingyi, Gu, Guai and Tongren: if in divining illness one meets these hexagrams, hurry home and make ready for what lies ahead.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.06** 明夷、蛊、剥、夬、丰、同这六个卦，占病时哪里愿意遇上；财、鬼两爻都发动，丧门、吊客闹哄哄地赶来；六重白虎临在身上凶恶，若在别的爻上也不好；鬼爻和应爻一起发动，化成螣蛇、入了墓库，身命就终了。
  Mingyi, Gu, Bo, Guai, Feng and Tong: who would wish to meet these six hexagrams in divining illness? With Wealth and the ghost both moving, Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence crowd in noisily and in haste; a six-fold White Tiger sitting on the Body is vicious, and if it is on another line it is also not good; the Pressure line and the Other line moving together, changing into the Coiling Snake and entering the tomb, the body's life ends.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.07** 卦变到墓乡，必定丧命，不然也主昏沉困顿。乾、坎、震、巽是生魂，在外不死，在内难活；离、坤、艮、兑是人变成鬼，在内一定活，在外就死。勾陈爻动，就要开坟了；乾、兑，片刻之间就有人做孝子。孙膑这个诀很难领会，只在于善于推算。
  If the hexagram changes into the tomb quarter life will surely be lost, and otherwise it also means a dull, hazy exhaustion. Qian, Kan, Zhen and Xun are the living soul: outside he does not die, inside he can hardly survive; Li, Kun, Gen and Dui are the person becoming a ghost: inside he surely lives, outside he dies. When the Hook Earth line moves the grave is opened; Qian and Dui: in a moment someone is a filial mourner. Sun Bin's secret here is hard to know and lies only in being good at working it out.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.15** 丰、观、需、剥、节、旅、贲、明夷、蛊、夬，还有同人，不论四季、也不论生旺，「十死」是分明的，不必多说。
  Feng, Guan, Xu, Bo, Jie, Lü, Bi, Mingyi, Gu, Guai and Tongren: regardless of the four seasons or of strength, the 'ten deaths' are plain and there is no need to say more.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.17** 占疾病，财爻和鬼不要连在一起，两爻都发动，命有危难；同人、丰、夬成了凶的一对，蛊、剥、明夷是死的缘由。杀、世、鬼、身，三样入了墓，人的魂魄就要入黄泉；药爻在世，还是难医；虎动、占住身，病没有痊愈；卦入墓乡，并且丧命，卧在床席上，病绵绵不断。
  In illness the Wealth line and the ghost must not be linked, and if both move life is in danger; Tongren, Feng and Guai make an ominous set, and Gu, Bo and Mingyi are causes of death. The killing, the Self, the ghost and the Body, three of them entering the tomb, the person's spirit-souls are about to enter the Yellow Springs; the medicine line on the Self, hard to treat; the Tiger moving and taking hold of the Body, the illness is not cured; the hexagram entering the tomb quarter, life is lost, and lying on the bed-mat the illness drags on and on.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.23** 辰、戌、丑、未是五行的库（墓）：凶爻要入库，吉爻不可入墓，入墓就成了休囚。墓有三种：人墓、事墓、鬼墓。人墓：比如占兄弟的病，墓在辰，当日是戌，虽然合着入了墓，也不会死；人旺墓衰，不会死；兄弟旺、墓爻衰，不会死。鬼墓：占病得到鬼墓，鬼是庙里的神；占病而墓旺，鬼难脱身、病难安、多半死；占病时鬼是庙神，若墓旺，就是大庙的神，不是小小的神祠。墓这个字，要冲克破它，这是按墓爻的活法来论，也就是解神。
  Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei are the storehouses (tombs) of the five phases: a fierce line should enter one, a lucky line must not be in a tomb, and in a tomb it is resting or trapped. There are three tombs: the person's tomb, the matter's tomb and the ghost's tomb. The person's tomb: for example divining a brother's illness, the tomb is Chen and the day is Xu; though it combines and enters the tomb he does not die; if the person is strong and the tomb weak he does not die; if the brother is strong and the tomb line weak he does not die. The ghost's tomb: divining illness and obtaining the ghost's tomb, the ghost is a temple god; divining illness with the tomb strong, the ghost is hard to shake off, the illness hard to settle and mostly ends in death; where the ghost is a temple god in divining illness, if the tomb is strong it is a god of a great temple and not a small shrine. As for the word tomb, it must be clashed, controlled or broken; this is judged by the living method of the tomb line, and it is the resolving spirit.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.25** 绝处逢生：比如申日占卜，卦里的用爻属木，木遇到申就是绝，这木爻就没有用了，和没有一样；如果有水爻发动，水在申长生，动来相生，木爻又重新有用，好比人在困窘的时候遇到贵人扶持，必有寒谷回春的样子。占病：用爻和吉神在绝处逢生，病将死而又活过来；鬼爻和忌神在绝处逢生，病快要好了却又发作。
  Meeting life in a place of extinction: for example, on a Shen day the Subject line in the hexagram belongs to Wood; Wood meeting Shen is in Extinction and this Wood line is of no use, the same as absent; if a Water line moves, Water having its Long Life at Shen moves to generate it, and the Wood line is again of use, like a person in hard straits who meets a patron to support him, and there is surely the look of spring returning to a cold valley. Divining illness: if the Subject line and a lucky spirit meet life in a place of extinction, the illness about to end in death revives; if the Pressure line and the Adversary meet life in a place of extinction, the illness about to settle breaks out again.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.26** 合处逢冲：比如占得雷地豫卦，世应相生，六爻相合，吉，没有不利的，是事事可以成的样子；却是在子日占得，子冲应上的午、害世上的未，这就是合处逢冲。只有占官司和占病，喜欢遇到合处逢冲：合，事就一定成；逢冲，灾就一定散；事将危而有救，病欲死而复生。其余照这样类推。又说：吉神合的地方不可以冲，凶神合的地方喜欢逢冲。大概可以照这个断。
  A clash where there is a combination: for example, divining the hexagram Thunder over Earth, Yu: the Self and the Other generate each other, the six lines combine, lucky and nothing unfavourable, the image of every matter succeeding; but it is divined on a Zi day, and Zi clashes with the Wu on the Other line and harms the Wei on the Self line, which is meeting a clash where there is a combination. Only in divining a lawsuit and an illness is meeting a clash where there is a combination welcome: combining, the matter surely succeeds; meeting a clash, calamity surely scatters; a matter about to be in danger is rescued, an illness about to end in death revives. The rest follow this. It also says: where a lucky spirit combines it must not be clashed, and where a fierce spirit combines a clash is welcome. One may perhaps follow this judgement.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.27** 随官入墓有三种：身随鬼入墓、世随鬼入墓、命随鬼入墓。以上三种墓，不问占什么事，都不是吉兆。占病遇到它，十占里有九个死。
  Following the ghost into a tomb has three kinds: the Body following the ghost into a tomb, the Self following the ghost into a tomb, and the natal line following the ghost into a tomb. These three tombs, whatever is divined, are none of them a lucky sign. Divining illness and meeting one, nine out of ten die.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.31** 春天是丑、夏天是辰、秋天是未、冬天逢戌，这是三丘；五墓要取三丘的对宫。病人就是作福（祈福）也难留住。这是占病所忌，只有得吉神来解救才能免去。
  In spring Chou, in summer Chen, in autumn Wei and in winter meeting Xu: these are the Three Mounds; the Five Tombs are taken from the opposite palace. The sick person, though he makes offerings for fortune, can hardly be kept. This is a taboo in divining illness, and it is escaped only if a lucky spirit comes to rescue.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.32** 丧车：春天是鸡、夏天是鼠，秋天是马、冬天是羊，好安排。有人来占病，断他是起身离去（去世）了，赶紧去讨钱、火速买棺材。
  The Mourning Cart: in spring it is the rooster, in summer the rat, in autumn the horse and in winter the sheep, which is well arranged. A person who comes to divine illness is judged to be setting off to leave; hurry to ask for money and buy a coffin in all haste.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.36** 神煞月令表里凶神的三行：事气（正月起午、未、申、酉、戌、亥、子、丑、寅、卯、辰、巳），病必死，丧事凶；吊客（辰、丑、戌、未循环），病人死亡、忧愁、疾病，凶；哭杀（巳、午、未、申、酉、戌、亥、子、丑、寅、卯、辰），流行的瘟病，凶。
  Three fierce rows of the table of spirits and killings by month: Affair Qi (from the first month Wu, Wei, Shen, You, Xu, Hai, Zi, Chou, Yin, Mao, Chen, Si): in illness surely dead, mourning is ominous; Guest of Condolence (Chen, Chou, Xu, Wei in a cycle): the sick person dies, worry and sickness, ominous; the Weeping Killing (Si, Wu, Wei, Shen, You, Xu, Hai, Zi, Chou, Yin, Mao, Chen): epidemic plague is ominous.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.48** 占在任的官有没有疾病：凡是卦变到囚、死的地方，在任的官身上有病，以至衰亡；无气又带杀，终究有祸，再遇上空亡，必定有丧。鬼动，若逢到休、废的地方，这个人退位，终究没有官位；胎没并起来，世自己就衰微，再加刑、杀，就是衰败了。
  On whether an official in office has illness: whenever the hexagram changes into a place of trapped or dead, the official in office falls ill, even to decline and death; without qi and bearing a killing, in the end there is calamity, and meeting Void as well there is surely a death. The ghost moving, if it meets a place of resting or ruin, this person leaves office and in the end has no post; with Womb and Submerged joined, the Self itself is faint, and with punishment and killings added it is decline.
- **LY.ILL.EL.11** 再说用爻上卦正好遇旬空，要看病是近病还是久病：暴病遇空可救，久病遇空必死。虽然遇空亡，还要分衰旺：空的用爻如果旺相，纵然是久病也没有妨碍；空的用爻如果休囚，只要遇上日冲也不会死。倘若是立时空又遇上旬空，不论近病久病，没有不死的。用爻虽然不落空，如果临月破，必定伤身。
  Again, when the Subject line is in the hexagram and falls exactly on Void, look at whether the illness is recent or long: a sudden illness meeting Void can be saved, a long illness meeting Void surely dies. Though it meets Void, one still divides strong from weak: if the Void line is strong, even a long illness is no hindrance; if the Void line is resting or trapped, meeting a Day clash it still does not die. If it is Void at the moment and also falls into the ten-day Void, then whether the illness is recent or long, none escapes collapse. Though the Subject line is not Void, if it sits in Month-break it will surely harm the body.
- **LY.ILL.EL.14** 空身、空命都不必忌讳，无鬼、无财哪里够得上凭据。注说：比如甲子旬占，空的是戌、亥，天下万万属猪、属狗的人，难道都命绝了？前人又说病人无鬼必死，难道没有验证过吗？《天玄赋》里虽然说占病无鬼，就没有叩告的门路，是天年命尽，这病不能好。可是比如哥哥占弟弟的病，鬼是忌神，怎么该在卦里？又如父亲占儿子的病，鬼是仇人，怎么可以用它？如果按道理论病，只取用爻的旺衰生克，就能断定存亡。凡是卦里无鬼，不过是没有神祟罢了，难道就是天年命尽？何况现在屡次试验没有官鬼的卦，也未必死。又辨别没有财的说法更加差错：财爻虽然是饮食，没有财，不过是眼下不吃东西，怎么能丧命？只有丈夫占妻子、主人占仆人来看，才忌讳财空，其余占的都不忌讳。
  The Void of the Body and the Void of the natal branch need not be feared, and no ghost or no Wealth is hardly enough to rely on. The commentary says: for example, when a divination is made in the Jiazi decade the Void branches are Xu and Hai, and of the innumerable people under heaven born in the year of the pig or the dog, are all to have their lives cut off? The men of old also said that a patient with no ghost must die, and was there no proof? Although the Tianxuan Fu says that in divining illness with no ghost there is no door to knock on and appeal, it is the end of one's allotted years, and the illness will not be cured. But for instance, if an elder brother divines a younger brother's illness, the ghost is the Adversary, and how should it be in the hexagram? Or if a father divines a son's illness, the ghost is an enemy, and how can it be used? If the illness is discussed on principle, only the strength and the generation and control of the Subject line are taken, and life or death is decided. A hexagram with no ghost only lacks a haunting, and is it therefore the end of one's allotted years? Moreover, no-Official hexagrams have been tried many times and the person did not necessarily die. Again the claim about lacking Wealth is even more mistaken: though the Wealth line is food and drink, lacking it only means that for the moment he does not eat, how can that cost his life? Only in a husband divining a wife or a master a servant is a Void Wealth line feared, and in all other divinations it is not feared.
- **LY.ILL.EL.15** 只有六冲这一种，能分缓急，病的来源要自己斟酌衡量：刚起的灾遇上它应当全好，久病遇上它命必伤；合处逢冲也是这个意思，不看主象是弱还是强；再看土爻临鬼发动，凶多吉少，灾祸难以禳解。注说：病得六冲、六冲变冲、合处逢冲，都要审察病是远是近、是轻是重：暴病来占，朝夕之间就应当痊愈；如果久病来占，用象虽然旺相，也主身亡。又说近病逢冲就好，重病逢冲就死；卦纵然不冲，用爻也旺，凡是遇上财爻、鬼爻发动的，都可以断死；只有占尊长，鬼是元神，发动了就祈祷禳解可以治疗。
  Only the Six-Clash tells slow from urgent, and the source of the illness must be weighed by oneself: a fresh calamity meeting it should be wholly cured, a long illness meeting it surely brings harm to life; a clash met in a combination has the same meaning, without regard to whether the chief image is weak or strong; and further, an Earth line on a moving ghost is mostly ominous and seldom auspicious, and the misfortune is hard to exorcise. The commentary says: when an illness has a six-clash, or a six-clash changing into a clash, or a clash met in a combination, one must examine whether it is near or far, light or heavy: a sudden illness divined should be cured by morning or evening; if a long illness is divined, though the Subject image is strong it still means death. It says also that a recent illness meeting a clash is cured and a heavy illness meeting a clash dies; even if the hexagram is not clashing and the Subject line is strong, wherever the Wealth or Pressure line moves one may say death; only in divining an elder, the ghost being the Support, moving it can be treated by prayer and exorcism.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.11** 死气发动的时候，病和官司凶。死气，是占病、占官司时，动爻旺相，克了世和身。月破：忧愁的事散去，病的人死，事情不成，财没有气，这是用爻被月建冲的缘故。
  When the death-qi issues, illness and lawsuits are ominous. The death-qi: in divining an illness or a lawsuit, a moving line that is strong controls the Self and the body. Month-break: the one worried is dispersed, the sick person dies, the matter is not achieved and wealth has no qi, because the Subject line is clashed by the Month.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.37** 主象落空（王洪绪：或入墓、绝、被破），占病是大忌；要有救才行，没救就会死在半路。
  A main image that is Void (Wang Hongxu: or in a tomb, in extinction, or broken) is a great taboo in divining illness; it must have rescue, and without rescue the patient will die on the way.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.38** 占病，妻财是食禄，父母是寿命，卦里不能没有，宜静不宜动。卦里没有财爻或者财爻落空，是不想吃东西；父母爻动是占病的大忌，因为它克制子孙（福神），官鬼这个凶煞就能肆意逞凶，吃药不见效、祷告不灵，所以说「反促天年」。旧注还说：主象遇到绝，或变到绝地，财爻遇到叫禄绝，父母爻遇到叫寿绝，整个卦象如果凶，这是死的兆头。
  In divining illness, Wealth is the food-emolument and Resource is the span of life; neither may be missing from the hexagram, and both should be still and not moving. If there is no Wealth line or it is Void, he has no wish to eat; a moving Resource line is a great taboo, because it controls Output (the spirit of fortune) and lets the Pressure killer run wild, so medicine has no effect and prayer is not answered, hence 'it shortens the span of life instead'. The old commentary adds: if the main image meets extinction or changes into a place of extinction, then Wealth meeting it is the emolument cut off and Resource meeting it the life span cut off, and if the whole image is bad this is a sign of death.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.41** 世爻持着官鬼，本来就不是吉兆；如果再遇到日辰带着墓爻，就叫「随官入墓」；本命爻随官入墓也凶。世爻、用爻变入鬼的墓，以及世爻、命爻带着鬼动、变入墓的，还有用爻、命爻、世爻持着鬼墓发动的，都主大凶。
  A Pressure line holding the Self line is in itself no lucky sign; if it also meets a tomb line carried by the Day, this is called 'following Pressure into the tomb'; a natal line following Pressure into the tomb is also unlucky. The Self or Subject line changing into the tomb of Pressure, the Self or natal line carrying Pressure, moving and changing into a tomb, and the Subject, natal or Self line holding the tomb of Pressure and moving, all mean great misfortune.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.44** 用神连着鬼煞，眼前一定看到危险：没有子孙、日辰、动爻来解救，危险就在眼前。
  When the Subject is joined to the Pressure killer, danger is surely in view: with no Output, Day or moving line to relieve it, the danger is immediate.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.47** 用爻不宜落在死绝的地方，因为一克它就倒。卦里有动爻来生它、帮它，叫绝处逢生，是凶里转吉的象，病虽危险也有救。
  The Subject line should not be in a place of death or extinction, because one control topples it. If a moving line in the hexagram generates and helps it, this is called meeting life in a place of extinction, an image of turning to luck out of misfortune: the illness is in danger, but there is rescue.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.48** 用爻虽然不宜太旺，也不宜太弱。太弱，病人就体虚力怯，一时难以痊愈。如果得到日辰、动爻来生、合、扶助，最吉，就算是十分重的病，也不至于死。
  Though the Subject line should not be too strong, it should not be too weak either. If weak, the patient is frail and timid in strength and cannot quickly be healed. If it gets generation, combination and support from the Day and moving lines it is most lucky: even a most heavy illness will not lead to death.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.49** 官鬼这一爻虽然是恶煞，也不能没有：得病的原因、症候的虚实、疾病的安危、鬼神的情状，都由它来决定。它不上卦，就要去找它伏在哪里才能推测；如果伏着的爻又落空，这病一定好不了：就算有好医生，也找不到病源；就算有鬼神，也没有可以叩告的门路，是天年命尽。病轻的不在此论。
  The Pressure line is a malign killer, yet it cannot be missing: how the illness was got, whether it is deficient or excess, whether it is safe or dangerous, and the state of ghosts and spirits are all decided by it. If it is not in the hexagram one must look for where it lies hidden in order to reason it out; if the hidden line is also Void, the illness surely cannot be cured: even a good doctor cannot reach its source, even the spirits have no door to be petitioned, and the span of life is used up. A light illness is not counted here.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.53** 卦身上坐着鬼，而本宫的官鬼又伏在世爻下面；或者世爻持着官鬼，而本宫的官鬼伏在用爻下面；或者自己占，遇到世爻的飞爻、伏爻上下都是官鬼：总叫双官夹身，最不利。就算大局不到死，也是沉重缠困、拖到寿终的病。王洪绪补充：身指卦身和用神；鬼夹在身爻或用爻的前后，或者世爻上有鬼、身爻上又有鬼，都叫双官夹用夹身。
  When the hexagram Body holds Pressure while the Pressure of the home palace lies hidden under the Self line; or the Self line holds Pressure while the Pressure of the home palace lies hidden under the Subject line; or in a divination for oneself the Self line, flying and hidden, has Pressure both above and below: all this is called double Pressure clamping the body, and is most unfavorable. Even if the whole image does not show death, it is a heavy, lingering illness that lasts to the end of his days. Wang Hongxu adds: the 'body' means the hexagram Body and the Subject; Pressure lines flanking the body line or the Subject line before and after, or Pressure on the Self line and also on the body line, are all called double Pressure clamping the Subject and the body.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.54** 身、命、世随着鬼入墓，墓有卦墓、鬼墓、世墓、主墓几种。凡是动出来在卦里的，人人都看得见，那个墓叫明墓；变进墓里去的，人看不见，那个墓叫幽墓。不管幽的明的，都不是吉兆：大局就算不死，病也危险困顿；如果大局本来就凶，又遇到墓发动，或者世爻和主象都入了墓，一定死。得到动爻、日辰把墓爻冲破，才可能没事。
  The Body, natal and Self lines follow Pressure into a tomb: there are the tomb of the hexagram, the tomb of Pressure, the tomb of the Self and the tomb of the main image. A tomb that moves out in the hexagram, which everyone can see, is a bright tomb; one that a line changes into, which people cannot see, is a dark tomb. Whether dark or bright, neither is a lucky sign: even if the whole image does not show death, the illness is dangerous and hard; if the whole image is already bad and a tomb also moves, or the Self line and the main image both enter a tomb, he will surely die. If a moving line or the Day clashes and breaks the tomb line, there may be no harm.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.56** 官鬼爻发动，灾祸就成了。如果得到日辰或动爻来冲它，叫冲散，病虽然凶险，一定不至于死。忌爻发动，遇到冲也吉；如果没有遇到冲，但得到日辰或动爻来合住它、或者制克它，也都是遇到危险而没有忧患的象。
