The Self controlling the Other line, or the inner trigram controlling the outer, can cure his calamity; if the Other generates the Self, or the outer generates the inner line, or the hexagram meets six-combine, one comes with purpose and host and guest suit each other; Wealth strong, Output moving, the ghost still and Peer at peace, the medicine is effective and the profit doubled; if the Other is Void, he surely has no heart to come to me; if the Self sits in Void, my own heart is dispirited and lazy, and the doctor may not succeed.
- **LY.ILL.EL.28** 兄弟发动，倘若遭遇同辈阻挠；财空，不要指望谢金来；六冲，怎么能把事做完；官化官，担心病又变糟；应克世身、兄弟和朱雀发动，反而遭到是非和官司，不要开怀。注说：兄弟发动或克世爻，一定被同行霸占；木如果遇空，药金不要指望；六冲的卦，医治不能有始有终；鬼变出鬼爻、或卦里有两个官鬼都动，病重又变病，怎么能治？应如果克世，兄动、财空，又加上朱雀吵嚷，不光求不到谢礼，反而惹来闲是非。
  Peer moving, he may meet obstruction from his fellows; Wealth Void, do not look for the fee; a Six-Clash hexagram, how can the matter be finished; Pressure changing into Pressure, one worries the illness turns bad again; the Other controlling the Self, Peer and the Vermilion Bird moving, one meets quarrels and lawsuits instead, and should not be light-hearted. The commentary says: Peer moving or controlling the Self line, one is surely muscled out by fellow physicians; if Wood is Void, do not look for medicine money; in a Six-Clash hexagram the treatment cannot have a beginning and an end; if the ghost changes into a Pressure line, or the hexagram has two Pressure lines both moving, the illness changes again into another, and how can it be treated? If the Other controls the Self, and Peer moves, Wealth is Void and the Vermilion Bird adds its noise, one not only fails to get the fee but brings on idle quarrels.
- **LY.ILL.HDY.13** 海底眼的六亲属类里，子孙管的物类里有医药：「缉捕医药闲渔僧」，散句里还有「师巫捕抗，苻药医卜」——子孙是医生和药物（还有符药、卜算）。
  In the classes of the six relations in the Haidi Yan, among what Output governs are doctors and medicines: 'arrest, doctors and medicines, idle fishermen and monks', and in the prose 'shamans, catching and resisting, charms, medicines, doctors and diviners' — Output is the doctor and the medicine (and also charm-medicines and divining).
- **LY.ILL.HJC.32** 断病先看子孙爻。子孙能克制官鬼这个凶煞，古人叫它解神，又叫福德，占病时它又是医药。卦里没有子孙，鬼就没有东西制伏，吃药没有效果，祷告求神也不灵。子孙要得令有力，不要衰弱、被困、死、绝；日辰、动爻来生它、扶它就吉，来冲它、破坏它就凶。替父母或丈夫占病，子孙不宜发动：动了会伤克夫星，又克伤父母爻的原神。
  In judging illness look first at Output. Output can control and restrain the Pressure killer; the ancients called it the relieving spirit and also Output-virtue, and in divining illness it is also the medicine. If there is no Output in the hexagram, Pressure has nothing to restrain it: medicine has no effect and prayer to the spirits is not answered. Output should be in season and have force, not resting, trapped, dead or extinct; if the Day or a moving line generates and supports it, it is lucky, and if they clash and spoil it, unlucky. In divining the illness of a parent or a husband, Output should not move: moving, it harms and controls the husband star and also harms the Support of the Resource line.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.35** 一卦的「身」是整个卦的主体：身上坐着子孙（福德），一定没有忧患，病势再凶险也有救，用药医治一定痊愈。
  The 'body' is the substance of the whole hexagram: if Output (Output-virtue) sits on it, there is surely nothing to fear; even if the illness looks dangerous it can be saved, and treated with medicine it will surely be cured.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.40** 子孙爻既是医药，又是酒肉。子孙落空亡、临在死绝上，或者不上卦，病中一定没有肥甘的东西调养，病也缠缠绵绵难好。如果日辰或者应爻带着子孙，来生、合世爻和用爻，一定有人送礼物、送吃的来补养。
  The Output line is both medicine and wine and meat. If Output is Void, sits on death or extinction, or is not in the hexagram, the patient surely lacks rich food to build him up during the illness, and the illness also lingers and is hard to cure. If the Day or the Other line carries Output and generates or combines with the Self and Subject lines, someone will surely send gifts or food to nourish him.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.42** 占病把子孙当解神，身上临着它是大吉的兆头；如果父母爻发动来克伤它，仍然不好（王洪绪补一句：父母爻自己被制住就没关系）。
  In divining illness Output is the relieving spirit, and its sitting on the body is a very lucky sign; if the Resource line moves to control and harm it, it is still not good (Wang Hongxu adds: if the Resource line is itself restrained, there is no harm).
- **LY.ILL.HJC.52** 日辰坐在子孙上，来生、扶用爻（王洪绪：或拱、合），病一定靠药力好起来。
  When the Day sits on Output and generates and supports the Subject line (Wang Hongxu: or embraces or combines with it), the patient will surely recover through the power of medicine.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.58** 病了不去求医，能保全性命的很少；药不对症，白白死掉的很多。占医药时，应爻是医生，子孙是药。请医生：应爻落空，医生一定不来；应爻发动来生合世爻，医生一定听请；动了又落空、变出来也是空，一定去了别处；克世、冲世，都是不来。卦里没有子孙，吃药没有效果，子孙落空也没有功效。
  One who falls ill and does not seek a doctor seldom keeps his life whole, and one who takes medicine that does not fit the illness dies in vain more often than not. In divining medicine the Other line is the doctor and Output the medicine. In calling a doctor: if the Other line is Void, the doctor surely will not come; if it moves and generates or combines with the Self line, he surely obeys the call; if it moves, is Void and changes into Void, he has surely gone elsewhere; controlling or clashing the Self line, he will not come. With no Output in the hexagram medicine has no effect, and if Output is Void it does no good either.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.59** 占药要官鬼爻安静、没有气才吉。鬼一发动，病正在最厉害的时候，就算有好医生和妙药，一时也很难见效；休囚的还稍可，旺的就更凶。要等鬼爻入墓、入绝的时候用药才有功效；勉强吃药，病一定加重。
  In divining medicine the Pressure line should be still and without force. If it moves, the illness is at its fiercest, and even with a good doctor and wonderful medicine it is hard to gain effect for the moment; if resting or trapped it is a little more bearable, and if strong so much the worse. One must wait until the Pressure line meets its tomb or extinction before medicine has any effect; if he forces himself to take medicine, the illness will surely grow heavier.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.60** 世爻落空，病人不会专心去求医；世爻无缘无故落空，请医生决请不成；勉强去做，药也没有效果。占服药时世爻落空，是药不对症。
  When the Self line is Void, the patient will not set his mind on seeking a doctor; if it is Void for no reason, the call will surely not succeed; if he forces it, the medicine also has no effect. In divining the taking of medicine, a Void Self line means that the medicine does not fit the illness.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.61** 占药，官爻没有气、子孙旺相，药能胜过病，才有效。如果子孙休囚，官爻反而旺，就是药轻病重，就算吃了，也一定不能奏效。
  In divining medicine, if the Pressure line has no force and Output is strong, the medicine can overcome the illness, and then it is effective. If Output is resting or trapped and the Pressure line is strong instead, the medicine is light and the illness heavy, and even if he takes it, it surely cannot take effect.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.62** 世爻是病人一家，应爻是医生一家。世应相合相生，不是亲戚就是朋友；相冲相克，亲戚也不往来。应旺世衰，病人一家贫困，医生一定富；应衰世旺，反过来断。
  The Self line is the patient's family and the Other line the doctor's. If they combine and generate each other, the two are kin or friends; if they clash and control each other, even kin do not visit. If the Other line is strong and the Self line weak, the patient's family is poor and the doctor surely rich; if the Other line is weak and the Self line strong, judge the reverse.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.63** 卦身和世爻都不宜临在官鬼、父母两爻上，遇到了，药就不中用。父母爻持世，可以许下经醮保禳的愿；鬼爻持世，可以祷告鬼神护佑，然后吃药才有效验。
  Neither the hexagram Body nor the Self line should sit on the Pressure or Resource lines; if they do, the medicine does not hit the mark. With Resource holding the Self line one may vow scriptures, offerings and protective rites; with Pressure holding the Self line one may pray to the ghosts and spirits for protection, and only then will the medicine be effective.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.64** 占医药，遇到官鬼变官鬼，病一定有变化：症候不一样，或者病势不稳定，用药也不见效。
  In a medicine hexagram, when Pressure changes into Pressure, the illness surely changes: the symptoms are not uniform, or the course of the illness is unsettled, and medicine also has no effect.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.65** 子孙是占药用的用神，只喜欢出现一位：有气、不落空，又有日辰、动爻扶持，就是最吉的兆头。如果卦里重叠太多，或者子孙又变成子孙，就是医生不精，用药杂乱而没有功效。
  Output is the Subject in divining medicine and is glad to appear as one only: with force, not Void, and supported by the Day and moving lines, it is the best sign. If there are too many of them in the hexagram, or Output changes again into Output, the doctor is not skilled and his medicines are mixed and do no good.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.66** 有动就有变。子孙动了，变出父母就回头来克它，变出财爻就去扶持官鬼，变出兄弟药不精洁，变出官鬼这个凶煞药反而助长病，子孙变子孙是用药太杂，不能见效。所以不如安静；如果变出的爻伤了世爻、克了用爻，一定会招来因为吃药而伤命的祸。
  Where there is movement there is change. If Output moves and changes out Resource, it turns back to control it; out Wealth, it supports Pressure; out Peer, the medicine is not refined or clean; out a Pressure killer, the medicine feeds the illness instead; Output changing into Output is medicine too mixed to work. So stillness is better; if the changed line harms the Self line or controls the Subject line, disaster through medicine, harm to life, is sure to follow.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.67** 应爻上临着官鬼，这位医生一定不是良医；如果他再来刑克世爻、身爻和主象，要提防他用错药伤人。应爻变出忌爻或官鬼，都不宜用这个人的药。
  When the Other line sits on Pressure, the doctor is surely not a good one; if it also punishes or controls the Self line, the body and the main image, beware of wrong medicine that harms. If the Other line changes into an Adversary line or into Pressure, this man's medicine is unsuitable in every case.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.69** 应爻是医生，看它带的是什么，就知道医生是哪一类人：持父母，一定是僧道人家，或者兼会别的技艺；持兄弟，是庸医，或者先要药钱；持财爻，是富贵人家的医生，或者是女医生；临子孙，是专门行医的人，或者是僧道；持太岁，一定是世代行医的；持日辰而被太岁刑克的，是刚学医的；持月建，是官医，再临子孙，用药有神效。医生年老年少，用生旺墓绝来推。
  The Other line is the doctor, and what it carries shows what sort of man he is: holding Resource, a monk or Daoist household, or one who also has other arts; holding Peer, a quack, or one who asks the price of the medicine first; holding Wealth, a doctor of wealth and rank, or a woman doctor; sitting on Output, a specialist physician, or a monk or Daoist; holding Tai Sui, a doctor from a family of doctors; holding the Day but punished or controlled by Tai Sui, a beginner in medicine; holding the Month, an official physician, and if it also sits on Output, his medicine works wonders. Whether the doctor is old or young is reasoned from birth, strength, tomb and extinction.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.70** 动爻、日辰能生飞爻、克飞爻，却不能生克伏神，因为伏神是隐藏潜伏的意思。占病遇到鬼伏在世爻下面，这病一定不能断根：就算子孙发动，也只能眼前见效，过后到伏鬼旺相的月份日子，这病一定再犯。
  The moving lines and the Day can generate and control a flying line but cannot generate or control a hidden line, for a hidden line lies out of view. In divining illness, if Pressure lies hidden under the Self line, the illness surely cannot be cut off at the root: even if Output moves, it can take effect only for the present, and after this, in the month or day when the hidden Pressure is strong, the illness will surely break out again.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.71** 卦身上坐着鬼，病就不能脱身。鬼衰弱没有气，是缠缠绵绵难好、拖延不愈的象。世爻旺相出现在卦里，却长久卧床，就算有好医生也没有效果。世爻持着鬼，以及主象、身爻临在官墓上的，也都是这样。
  With Pressure on the hexagram Body, the illness does not leave the body. If Pressure is weak and without force, it is an image of lingering, hard to heal, dragging on. If the Self line appears strong in the hexagram and yet he lies long in bed, even a good doctor has no effect. The same holds when the Self line holds Pressure, and when the main image or the body sits on the tomb of Pressure.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.72** 卦里父母爻动，子孙就不能专权，本来不吉；但卦里又不能没有父母爻，要静不要动。因为人的气脉都属父母。所以占医生，如果没有这一爻，这个医生一定是乡野草泽的医人：虽然会用药，脉理却不明白，只能凭猜测。
  When the Resource line moves in the hexagram, Output cannot hold sole power, which is in itself not lucky; yet the hexagram cannot be without the Resource line, and it should be still, not moving. For a person's breath and pulse all belong to Resource. So in divining a doctor, if this line is missing, he is surely a rustic country doctor: though he can use medicine, he does not understand the pulse and goes by guesswork.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.73** 官鬼虽然是凶煞，也不能没有。它出现在卦里，就有子孙克制它、日辰刑制它，用药有效。它不上卦，病就隐伏着，病根不知道，症候没法判定，随意用药，也难以见效。
  The Pressure line is a malign killer, yet it cannot be missing. If it appears in the hexagram, Output controls it and the Day punishes it, and medicine is effective. If it is not in the hexagram, the illness lies hidden, its root cause is unknown, its symptoms cannot be decided, and medicine used at random also hardly takes effect.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.75** 父母爻发动，子孙受制，药一定没有效。如果子孙有气，又有日辰、动爻来救（冲、合父母爻，或者克父母爻），救得有用，才可以活，但也必须多服才好。父母变成子孙，也是医生太慢。
  When the Resource line moves and Output is restrained, the medicine surely has no effect. If Output has force, and the Day or a moving line comes to rescue it (clashing or combining with the Resource line, or controlling it) so that the rescue works, he may live, but even so it is best to take much of it. Resource changing into Output is also the doctor being too slow.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.76** 看官鬼爻的五行来用药：火、土官鬼，病一定发热，宜用寒凉的药去攻；金、水官鬼，病多半发寒，一定用温热的药来治。火鬼在生旺的地方又遇到生扶，一定用大寒的药攻它，休囚死绝就用凉药；水鬼在生旺的地方又遇到合助，要用大热的药，休囚死绝就用温药。火鬼在阴宫阴爻，是阴虚火动的症候，可以用滋阴降火的药；水鬼在阳宫内卦，是血气虚损的症候，可以用补中益气的药。要会变通，其余照这个类推。
  Choose the medicine by the phase of the Pressure line. A Fire or Earth Pressure line: the illness is surely hot, and should be attacked with cold and cool medicine; a Metal or Water Pressure line: the illness is mostly cold, and is surely treated with warm and hot medicine. A Fire Pressure in a place of birth or strength that also meets generation and support must be attacked with very cold medicine, and with cool medicine if resting, trapped, dead or extinct; a Water Pressure in a place of birth or strength that also meets combination and help needs very hot medicine, and warm medicine if resting, trapped, dead or extinct. A Fire Pressure in a yin palace on a yin line is the syndrome of yin deficiency with stirring fire, and medicine that nourishes yin and lowers fire may be used; a Water Pressure in the inner trigram of a yang palace is the syndrome of depleted blood and qi, and medicine that supplements the centre and boosts qi may be used. One must be flexible; the rest follow this.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.77** 凡是占服药，看子孙在哪一爻，就知道该请哪个方向的医生：子孙在子爻，宜请北方的医生；在丑爻，宜请东北方的医生，依此类推。又如寅爻是子孙，五行属木，那位医生姓名带木字旁或草字头，或者是属虎的；就算不在东北，也都能治。其余照这个类推。
  In divining the taking of medicine, look at which line Output is on, and one knows from which direction a doctor can treat him: Output on the Zi line, a doctor from the north; on the Chou line, a doctor from the north-east, and so on. Again, if the Output line is Yin, which belongs to Wood, the doctor has a name with the wood radical or the grass radical, or was born in a tiger year; though not from the north-east, all of these can treat him. The rest follow this.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.78** 财爻管饮食，靠它养生，但它一动就会去生助鬼爻，反而被它所害。财若属水，一定忌鱼鲜和生冷的东西，药才见效；属木，忌动风的东西；属火，忌烤炒的热东西；属金，忌坚硬和咸的东西；属土，忌油腻和滑的东西。财爻不动，不可乱说。又要忌鬼爻所属生肖的东西，比如丑忌牛，酉忌鸡。
  The Wealth line governs food and drink, on which life is sustained, but when it moves it generates and helps the Pressure line and one is harmed by it instead. If Wealth is Water, fish, fresh seafood and raw or cold things must be avoided, and only then does the medicine work; if Wood, avoid things that stir wind; if Fire, roasted, fried and hot things; if Metal, hard and salty things; if Earth, greasy and slippery things. If Wealth does not move, one may not speak at random. Also avoid what belongs to the zodiac animal of the Pressure line: for example Chou avoids the ox and You avoids the fowl.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.79** 青龙是喜悦的神，再临在木爻上，来生合世爻和主象，病人一定要把家里的事丢开、放宽心怀，然后吃药才有效。
  The Azure Dragon is the spirit of joy; if it also sits on a Wood line and generates or combines with the Self line and the main image, the patient must throw off household affairs and relax his mind, and only then is the medicine effective.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.80** 财爻在外卦发动，主呕吐。如果它生合世爻、用爻，用药催吐就会好；如果在内卦，疏通利下最好。如果财爻发动来刑克世爻和主象，在外卦就是吃药吃不下，在内卦就是吃了药之后拉肚子。
  A Wealth line moving in the outer trigram means vomiting. If it generates or combines with the Self and Subject lines, treating with a medicine that makes him vomit heals it; if it is in the inner trigram, a purge that opens the bowels is best. If the Wealth line moves to punish or control the Self line and the main image, then in the outer trigram the medicine is not kept down, and in the inner trigram there is diarrhoea after the medicine.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.81** 子孙属火，又在离宫，宜用热药来治；如果在别的宫，又居外卦，一定用艾灸才会好；如果在内卦，或者虽然在外卦却属本宫的，都照这个断。
  If Output is Fire and also in the Li palace, hot medicine should be used to treat it; if it is in another palace and in the outer trigram, it is healed only by moxibustion; if it is in the inner trigram, or, though in the outer, belongs to the home palace, judge in each case by this.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.82** 子孙在坎卦发动，都宜发汗（旧注：不管是什么五行；王洪绪：子孙属水，或者在坎宫）；官鬼属木，要先散风邪，用药才有效。这只是举一个例子，五行八卦都照这个推。
  Output moving in the Kan trigram is always suited to inducing sweat (the old commentary: whatever its phase; Wang Hongxu: Output that is Water, or in the Kan palace); a Wood Pressure line calls for dispersing the wind pathogens first, and then the medicine is effective. This is only one instance, and the five phases and eight trigrams are all reasoned in this way.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.83** 用爻休囚墓绝，一定要用补药才有效。如果用爻得时旺相，又有生扶合助，就要用克伐的药来治，再补反而有害。
  If the Subject line is resting, trapped, in a tomb or extinct, only a tonic is effective. If the Subject line is in season and strong and also has generation, support and combination to help it, it must be treated with medicine that attacks and subdues, and to tonify further does harm instead.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.84** 子孙属金，适合用刀针。鬼爻属水，如果用刀针，金能生水，反而助长病势，病一定加重。土鬼不宜用热药，水鬼不宜用寒药，火鬼不宜用风药，金鬼不宜用丸药。
  If Output is Metal, knife and needle are of use. If the Pressure line is Water and knife and needle are used, Metal can generate Water and helps the force of the illness instead, and the illness surely grows heavier. An Earth Pressure avoids hot medicine, a Water Pressure cold medicine, a Fire Pressure wind medicine, and a Metal Pressure pills.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.86** 卦里有两爻子孙发动，用药不必连着服，因为它们分了权，要多服才见效；或者用两种汤药，轮流间隔着服就有效。如果占的是疮疽肿毒，可以说内服托里的药，外面敷贴。
  When two Output lines move in the hexagram, medicine need not be taken continuously, because they divide the power, and it takes much to be effective; or use two kinds of decoction and take them alternately, and it works. If the divination is about sores, carbuncles and swollen toxin, one can say to use medicine to support the inside and a poultice on the outside.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.87** 官鬼发动来冲克间爻，或者鬼在两个间爻上发动，胸膈一定不舒服，要用宽胸的汤药。间爻遇到兄弟发动，是气不顺（气逆），药宜调气。
  When Pressure moves to clash or control a between line, or moves on the two between lines, the chest and diaphragm are surely uneasy, and a decoction that eases the chest must be used. A between line meeting a moving Peer means the qi is not flowing smoothly (counterflow of qi), and the medicine should regulate qi.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.88** 卦里子孙爻空亡、官鬼衰弱安静，如果有父母爻发动来生合世爻、身爻和主象，就不用吃药。再变出子孙，不如丢开家里的事，到深山里静养，或者进寺庙道观闭门修养，不吃药也会自己安好。
  If the Output line in the hexagram is Void and the Pressure line weak and still, and a Resource line moves to generate or combine with the Self line, the body and the main image, no medicine need be taken. If it also changes into Output, better to abandon household affairs and recuperate quietly in the deep mountains, or to enter a temple or Daoist abbey and recuperate behind a closed door, and without medicine he will recover of himself.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.89** 子孙出现在卦里却落空，而第五爻动、变出不落空的子孙，不用选求名医服药，路上遇到的乡野医生反而能治好。如果子孙不出现、六爻也没有变出子孙，而日辰带着子孙来生合，想不到的时候自然有医生来治。
  If Output appears in the hexagram but is Void, and the fifth line moves and changes out an Output that is not Void, there is no need to seek a famous doctor and take medicine: a rustic doctor met on the road can cure him instead. If Output does not appear and none of the six lines changes it out, but the Day carries Output and generates or combines with it, a doctor will come to treat him unexpectedly.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.90** 世爻和应爻比和，卦里又没有子孙，这药没有损害也没有益处，吃不吃都一样，必须换一个医生，另外求治，才能痊愈。
  When the Self and Other lines are at peace with each other and there is no Output in the hexagram, this medicine neither harms nor helps, and to take it or not is the same; he must change the doctor and seek treatment elsewhere before he can be cured.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.91** 财爻、官鬼都发动，势头已经凶险，子孙又落空，吃药没有益处，就算有好医生也一定没有功效，不如不吃，病反而渐渐好起来。
  When Wealth and Pressure both move, the situation is already bad, and if Output is also Void, taking medicine does no good; even with a good doctor there will surely be no effect, and it is better not to take it, and he will gradually recover.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.92** 凡是占医药，要诚心默默祷告，问用哪个人的药有效还是无效，不必说出医生的姓氏。卜家依照这一章来断，自然没有替医生推荐的毛病，心诚没有不感通的，卦没有不灵验的，这不是彼此都安心吗？
  Whoever divines about medicine must pray silently and sincerely, asking whether the medicine of a certain person will or will not be effective, and need not state the doctor's surname. A diviner who judges by this chapter will have no abuse of touting doctors; sincerity never fails to reach, and the hexagram never fails to prove true; is that not peace of mind for both sides?
- **LY.ILL.HZL.06** 以应为医生，以子孙为药；鬼爻旺相，最忌独发。卦里的疾病，以用爻为主，以鬼为病。金鬼：肺腑病、喘嗽、气急、虚怯、瘦瘠，或疮疥、血光，或筋骨病；木鬼：四肢不遂，肝胆主病，右痪左瘫，口眼歪斜；水鬼：沉塞、痼冷、腰痛、肾气淋沥、遗精、白浊、吐泻；火鬼：头痛发热、心胸焦渴，加朱雀则狂言谵语，阳症、伤寒、嗝逆；土鬼：脾胃发胀，黄肿虚浮，瘟疫时气。
  Take the Other as the doctor and Output as the medicine; the Pressure line strong is most feared moving alone. The illness of the hexagram takes the Subject line as the main and the ghost as the illness. A Metal ghost: illness of the lungs and viscera, panting and coughing, shortness of breath, deficiency and timidity, emaciation, or sores and scabies, bleeding, or an illness of the sinews and bones; a Wood ghost: the four limbs do not obey, the liver and gallbladder govern the illness, paralysis right and left, mouth and eyes awry; a Water ghost: stoppage, obstinate cold, pain in the waist, dribbling of the kidney qi, seminal emission, turbid urine, vomiting and diarrhoea; a Fire ghost: headache and fever, parched heart and chest, with the Vermilion Bird raving and delirium, a yang pattern, cold damage, hiccups; an Earth ghost: distension of the spleen and stomach, yellow swelling and puffiness, epidemics and seasonal qi.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.09** 金鬼不宜针，木鬼不宜草木，水鬼不宜汤饮汤洗之类，火鬼不宜灸熨，土鬼不宜服丸药；金鬼可以炙，木鬼用药方，火鬼带服鬼剂，水鬼宜服热剂，土鬼宜服木药。金鬼利南方，木鬼利西方，水鬼利土值，火鬼利北方，土鬼利东方，去求请医生。又：丑鬼不可食牛肉，未是子孙就该吃羊肉。
  A Metal ghost must not be needled, a Wood ghost must not have grasses and woods, a Water ghost must not have hot drinks or hot washing and the like, a Fire ghost must not have moxibustion or hot compresses, an Earth ghost must not take pills; a Metal ghost can be burned with moxa, a Wood ghost takes a prescription, a Fire ghost takes ghost medicine along, a Water ghost should take a hot remedy, an Earth ghost should take Wood medicine. A Metal ghost is favoured by the south, a Wood ghost by the west, a Water ghost by the earth quarter, a Fire ghost by the north and an Earth ghost by the east, in seeking and inviting a doctor. Again: a Chou ghost must not eat beef, and where Wei is Output one should eat mutton.
- **LY.ILL.TXF.12** 伏爻克飞爻，药奏神效；子孙临在应位，医生遇上济世之才。注说：世是病人，应和外卦是医药；药如果克病，病就好；病人克药，药就没有灵验；外卦和应生世，药虽然对症，见效迟。世动克药：如果是水鬼，服药不纳；金鬼无气、久病犯真（此处含混），服药无效；木鬼，病一定犯了条贯，即使有药也没有益处；火鬼旺相，症一定刚烈，药也止不住它的热；土鬼，病沉重，药不对症，不能治病；应爻加鬼克世，那就是误了药、损了人。占病以子孙为医药：临应发动克世，必遇良医，病马上痊愈；卦里没有子孙，服药无效；世应比和、卦里又没有子孙，宜换医生；子孙临应爻被世克，虽遇良医病也没有治；外卦和应虽克世，卦里没有福德也难安，可以说有药应当治，只恐怕良医没有缘；子孙虽克世，外爻和应却不克，虽有良医，药饵不中，终究没有大害；虎杀如果临着子孙发动，要防医生不精细。
  The hidden line controlling the flying line: the medicine works with wondrous effect; Output sitting on the Other's place: the doctor meets one of world-saving talent. The commentary says: the Self is the sick person, the Other line and the outer trigram are the doctor and the medicine; if the medicine controls the illness, the illness is cured at once; if the sick person controls the medicine, the medicine has no effect; if the outer trigram and the Other generate the Self, though the medicine suits the symptoms it takes effect slowly. The Self moving and controlling the medicine: if it is a Water ghost, the medicine taken is not kept down; a Metal ghost without qi, in a long illness that has offended 'the true' (the words are unclear), taking medicine is ineffective; a Wood ghost: the illness surely offends the regulations, and even medicine is of no benefit; a strong Fire ghost: the symptoms are surely fierce and even medicine cannot stop the heat; an Earth ghost: the illness is heavy, the medicine does not suit the symptoms and cannot treat the illness; the Other line with a ghost added controlling the Self is the wrong medicine harming the person. In divining illness Output is the medicine and doctor: sitting on the Other and moving to control the Self, one meets a good doctor and the illness is cured at once; with no Output in the hexagram, taking medicine is ineffective; with the Self and Other harmonious and no Output in the hexagram, one should change the doctor; Output on the Other line controlled by the Self, though one meets a good doctor the illness is not treated; the outer trigram and the Other though controlling the Self, if the hexagram has no Output (the fortune spirit) it is still hard to be at peace, and one may say there is medicine that should treat it but fear the good doctor has no affinity; Output though controlling the Self, if the outer line and the Other do not control it, though there is a good doctor and the medicine does not hit, in the end there is no great harm; if a Tiger killing sits on Output and moves, beware the doctor not being careful.
- **LY.ILL.TXF.13** 必须钻研五行，才可以攻医各种病。注说：火鬼宜凉剂；水鬼宜温剂，或者丸药；木鬼宜针；金鬼宜灸；土鬼宜剉散。火鬼在月建旺的时候，宜大寒之剂；无气，不过用清凉的药。其余照这样推。金鬼利于请南方的医生，木鬼利西方的医生，水鬼利四散的医生，火鬼利北方的医生，土鬼利东方的医生。如果前一卦是水鬼、后一卦是火鬼，先要四散地医治，最后由北方的医生收功。
  One must study the five phases before one can attack and treat the myriad illnesses. The commentary says: a Fire ghost suits cooling remedies; a Water ghost suits warming remedies, or pills; a Wood ghost suits the needle; a Metal ghost suits moxa; an Earth ghost suits cut-and-ground powders. When a Fire ghost is strong on the Month, it suits very cold remedies; without qi, only cooling and clearing medicines; the rest follow this. A Metal ghost favours a doctor from the south, a Wood ghost a doctor from the west, a Water ghost doctors from all quarters, a Fire ghost a doctor from the north, an Earth ghost a doctor from the east. If the earlier hexagram is a Water ghost and the later a Fire ghost, one must first treat with doctors from all quarters, and the work is completed at the end by a doctor from the north.
- **LY.ILL.WWJQ.01** 文王金钱课的用神表说：占儿女、女婿、外甥、门徒、忠臣、医生、药物、僧道（原文写作「曾道」）、兵将、六畜、禽鸟，凡是占小辈的，都用子孙作用神；子孙就是福德之神，诸事见到它都吉利，只有占功名、词讼忌讳它。
  The table of the Subject in the Wenwang Jinqian Ke says: in divining children, a son-in-law, a sister's son, disciples, loyal ministers, doctors, medicines, monks and Daoists (written 'zeng dao' in the original), soldiers and officers, livestock and birds, and in general in divining those of the younger generation, use Output as the Subject; Output is the spirit of fortune and virtue and is auspicious to meet in everything, and is only feared in divining fame and rank and lawsuits.
- **LY.ILL.YM.34** 占医药的总纲：拿子孙爻当用。子孙临日、月是上等，旺相是次等，休囚是下等，空、破、散、绝就是庸医。这一章开头先说了几句话：得志的时候愿意做良相，不得志的时候愿意做良医；君子借医来施展志向，做儿子的要懂得医理，吉凶取舍能不慎重吗？章里分六问细讲：占药、占医、治病、用药、往医、行道；头两问是病家问，后四问（治病、用药、往医、行道）都是医生自己问。
  The outline for divining medicine and doctors: take Output as the one to use. Output sitting on the Day or Month is best, thriving and strong is next, resting and trapped is lowest, and Void, broken, scattered or in Extinction is a mediocre doctor. The chapter opens with a few remarks: when successful one wishes to be a good minister, when not, a good doctor; the gentleman carries out his will through medicine, and a son must know medicine, so how can one not be careful in choosing between fortune and misfortune? The chapter then treats six questions in detail: divining the medicine, divining the doctor, treating the illness, using the medicine, going to treat, and the doctor's practice; the first two are asked by the patient's family and the last four (treating, using, going, practising) are all asked by the doctor himself.
- **LY.ILL.YM.35** 卦里没有子孙，药就不好；官鬼强，病就治不好。子孙强、官鬼弱，像烈日融冰；子孙弱、官鬼强，像用铅刀去攻玉。财爻动、鬼爻静，子孙动，都没有功效。鬼伏着，病根拔不掉；变出鬼，病会传变；鬼落空，药就是「老」药。鬼临世爻而兴旺，我的病就曲折反复；鬼临应爻而旺相，那副药就古怪蹊跷。注说：要审察子孙和官鬼的强弱，鬼当值日、月时，即使子孙得到生扶也不算完全好，要比较子孙能不能制住鬼，才是吉占；鬼落空是躲开了子孙，药打不中病，所以叫「老」；鬼在世，病难很快好，鬼在应，医生诡诈难信。
  Without Output the medicine is not good, and with a strong Pressure line the illness cannot be cured. Output strong and Pressure weak is a fierce sun melting ice; Output weak and Pressure strong is a lead knife attacking jade. With Wealth moving and the ghost still, or Output moving, there is no effect. A hidden ghost means the root is not removed; a change into a ghost means the illness passes on and changes; a Void ghost makes the medicine 'aged'. A ghost thriving on the Self makes my illness twist and turn, and a ghost strong on the Other makes that medicine dubious. The note says: one must examine the strength of Output and Pressure; when the ghost is on duty in the Day or Month, even if Output meets generation and support it is not wholly good, and one must compare whether Output can subdue the ghost before it is an auspicious divination; a Void ghost evades Output and so the medicine does not hit the illness, hence 'aged'; a ghost on the Self means the illness is hard to cure quickly, and a ghost on the Other means the doctor is deceitful and hard to trust.
- **LY.ILL.YM.36** 应爻指的是医生，不是拿来占能不能克住病的：所以应落空，遇不上这个医生；应被冲破，医生不专心；应散，医生的见识反倒糊涂；应克世，医生爱财、多诈；世应相冲，治疗断断续续，做不到底。
  The Other stands for the doctor, and is not for divining whether the illness will be overcome: so with the Other Void, one cannot meet this doctor; broken, the doctor is not devoted; scattered, his insight is confused instead; the Other controlling the Self, the doctor loves profit and is full of deceit; Self and Other clashing, the treatment breaks off and is not carried to the end.
- **LY.ILL.YM.37** 占药：水属的子孙旺，宜用汤剂；土属的子孙旺，宜用丸药；火像艾灸，金像针，木像药末，以这些作象。火鬼喜欢凉药，木鬼喜欢祛风的药；土对应脾，金对应肺，水对应肾，也以这些作象。占医生：要考察子孙属于哪一类，木属东、金属西，乾是僧人、坤是妇人，也以这些作象。注说：子孙是药，鬼是病，占该用什么药、是什么病，就用这个办法；如果占的是这味药吉不吉，就看子孙的衰旺，不要死板地认为子孙属土就该用丸药。占该请哪个方位、哪种人当医生，也是这个办法；单独占医生好不好，还是用子孙的强弱，比如问请僧人当医生，得乾卦而子孙落空，怎么能说吉？
  Divining medicine: a strong Water Output suits a decoction, a strong Earth Output suits pills; Fire is moxibustion, Metal needles, Wood powders, taken as the images. A Fire ghost likes cooling medicine and a Wood ghost likes wind medicine; Earth is the spleen, Metal the lung and Water the kidney, also taken as images. Divining the doctor: examine what kind the Output belongs to; Wood is east and Metal west, Qian a monk and Kun a woman, again taken as images. The note says: Output is the medicine and the ghost is the illness, and to divine which medicine to use or what the illness is, use this method; but if what is divined is whether a medicine is auspicious, use the strength of Output, and do not rigidly hold that an Earth Output must mean pills. To divine which direction and which kind of person to use as doctor the method is the same; but to divine on its own whether the doctor is good, still use the strength of Output: for instance, if one asks about a monk-doctor and obtains Qian with Output Void, how could one call it auspicious?
- **LY.ILL.YM.38** 医生问这个病能不能治，先看应爻，因为对医生来说应爻就是病人；应爻坏了、或随墓，这个病就治不好。如果占的是病人的某个亲属，仍然以用神来断。
  When a doctor asks whether an illness can be treated, the Other line comes first, for to him the Other is the patient; if the Other is spoiled or follows into the tomb, the illness cannot be cured. If what is divined is one of the patient's relations, judgment still goes by the Subject.
- **LY.ILL.YM.39** 医生问这味药能不能奏效，也是拿子孙爻当用：子孙强、鬼弱，药就见效；子孙虚、鬼实，药就没有功效。注说：和占药一样。
  When a doctor asks whether a medicine will take effect, Output is again the line to use: with Output strong and the ghost weak it takes effect, and with Output empty and the ghost full it has no effect. The note says: it is the same as divining medicine.
- **LY.ILL.YM.40** 医生问上门去治病有没有利，就从世、应来推：应克世的，病家没有情义；卦里没有财爻的，酬谢微薄；落空的，有欺骗的心思；冲破的，没有专心；散了、冲了的，不会有个结果。
  When a doctor asks whether going to treat will be profitable, judge by the Self and the Other: the Other controlling the Self means little feeling; a hexagram with no Wealth line means a meagre gift; Void means an intent to deceive; broken, no wholehearted devotion; scattered or clashed, no end.
- **LY.ILL.YM.41** 医生问自己行医的前程：财爻、官爻强，名和利就能遂愿。所以医术不精，不足以造福万物；求医不谨慎，不足以救人性命；自己不耽误别人，也不耽误自己。
  When a doctor asks about the course of his practice, if the Wealth and Pressure lines are strong, profit and fame are achieved. So medicine that is not refined cannot benefit things, and seeking a doctor without care cannot heal life; if one does not mislead others, one does not mislead oneself.
- **LY.ILL.YY.21** 内卦的世爻、用爻是病人，外卦的应爻、子孙是医生和药。外卦克内卦，应爻和子孙克世、克用的，药有效；内卦克外卦，世爻、用爻克应、克子孙的，药没有效；外卦生合内卦，应爻和子孙生合世的，药对症但见效慢；外卦应爻克内卦世爻而卦里没有子孙的，遇不到好医生。应爻带子孙发动来克世的，药有效；世克应和子孙的，是名医但药没有效；子孙克世而外卦应爻不克内卦世爻的，药没效也没害。应爻带鬼发动、刑害克冲世爻的，是用错药伤了人；子孙落在辰午酉亥自刑之地的，用药有差错；日辰、动爻冲伤子孙的，药没有效；子孙变出父母或鬼的，药收不到功；子孙变出子孙的，药杂不精；子孙带白虎煞发动的，是庸医误人。世下伏官、遇子孙发动的，药虽好而病根除不掉；世持衰鬼的，病轻而药难治。世应比和而卦无子孙、或子孙空绝的，宜换药换医；子孙有两个都动的，换医就好；鬼和子孙都落空的，不治自愈；鬼和子孙都发动的，医药和祈祷一起用；子孙和财爻都动，鬼得到帮助，病难治。
  The Self line and Subject line in the inner trigram are the patient, and the Other line and Output in the outer trigram are the doctor and medicine. If the outer controls the inner, and the Other and Output control the Self and the Subject, the medicine is effective; if the inner controls the outer, and the Self and Subject control the Other and Output, it is not effective; if the outer generates and combines with the inner, and the Other and Output generate and combine with the Self, the medicine fits the illness but works slowly; if the Other in the outer trigram controls the Self in the inner and there is no Output in the hexagram, one does not meet a good doctor. If the Other line carries Output and moves to control the Self, the medicine is effective; if the Self controls the Other and Output, the doctor is famous but the medicine does not work; if Output controls the Self but the Other in the outer trigram does not control the Self, the medicine has no effect and does no harm. If the Other line carries the ghost and moves to punish, harm, control or clash the Self, wrong medicine harms the person; if Output stands on Chen, Wu, You or Hai, which punish themselves, the medicine is mistaken; if the Day or a moving line clashes with and wounds Output, it does not work; if Output changes into Resource or a ghost, it does not bring completion; if Output changes into Output, the medicines are mixed and not refined; if Output carries the Tiger and a killing star and moves, a mediocre doctor harms people. If Pressure is hidden beneath the Self and Output moves, the medicine is good but the root of the illness is not removed; if the Self holds a weak ghost, the illness is light but the medicine has difficulty curing it. If the Self and Other are in harmony and there is no Output, or Output is Void or in Extinction, change the medicine and doctor; if two Output lines both move, changing the doctor leads to recovery; if the ghost and Output are both Void, he recovers without treatment; if the ghost and Output both move, use doctors and prayers together; if Output and Wealth both move, the ghost is helped and the illness is hard to treat.
- **LY.ILL.YY.22** 《法法赋》说：天医生世爻的，是好医生；天医克世爻的，是庸医；日辰克医生的，医术不精；医生克日辰的，用药不恰当。世爻、用爻属金的，天医在巳有效；属木的，天医在亥有效；属火的，天医在寅有效；属水、土的，天医在申有效。
  The Fafa Fu says: if the Heavenly Doctor generates the Self, he is a good doctor; if it controls the Self, a mediocre one; if the Day controls the doctor, his medical learning is not refined; if the doctor controls the Day, the medicine used is not fitting. If the Self line and Subject line are Metal, the Heavenly Doctor at Si is effective; if Wood, at Hai; if Fire, at Yin; if Water or Earth, at Shen.
- **LY.ILL.YY.23** 《董和筮秘》说：脉要仔细察，以身爻为主。月卦临在金上，脉一定是弦、紧、代、革；临在木上，脉一定是洪、长、虚、动；临在水上，脉一定是芤、滑、濡、动；值火，脉一定是数、大、促、短；值土，脉一定是沉、实、滞、涩。
  The Dong He Shimi says: the pulse should be examined carefully, with the Body line as the master. If the month-hexagram falls on Metal, the pulse is surely wiry, tight, intermittent and leathery; on Wood, surging, long, vacuous and stirring; on Water, hollow, slippery, soft and stirring; on Fire, rapid, large, hurried and short; on Earth, deep, full, sluggish and rough.
- **LY.ILL.YY.24** 推断请来的医生是什么样的人，拿子孙做用：子孙属金，适合西方的、姓氏带金字旁的人；属木，适合东方的、姓氏带草头或木字旁的人；属水，适合北方的、姓氏带三点水的人；属火，适合南方的、姓氏带日字旁或火字旁的人；属土，适合姓氏带土字旁的，以及用丹药散剂的医生。子孙带贵人，适合有官职的人；子孙带华盖，适合僧人道士。
  To work out what kind of person the doctor is, Output is the Subject: if Output is Metal, it suits the west, a person whose surname has the metal radical; if Wood, the east, a surname with the grass or wood radical; if Water, the north, a surname with the water radical; if Fire, the south, a surname with the sun or fire radical; if Earth, a surname with the earth radical, and a doctor who works with cinnabar and powders. If Output carries the Noble, a person holding an official post; if Output carries the Canopy, a monk or Daoist.
- **LY.ILL.YY.25** 用药不要轻易乱投。官鬼属金的，适合灸，不适合丸药（火能克金，土生金）；鬼属木的，适合针，不适合汤药（金能克木，水生木）；鬼属水的，适合温药、丸药，不适合针（土克水，金生水）；鬼属火的，适合凉药、汤药，不适合草头药和饮片（水克火，木生火）；鬼属土的，适合咬咀，不适合灸（木克土，火生土）。此外，鬼伤到两个间爻的，适合宽胸膈；世爻旺又有扶助的，不妨再进补；财爻在外卦发动合用爻的，适合吐法，在内卦发动合用爻的，适合泻法；用爻、鬼爻出现或在外卦的，适合治外表，伏藏在内卦的，适合治内。
  Medicine is not to be given lightly. If the Pressure line is Metal, moxibustion suits and pills do not (Fire can control Metal, while Earth generates Metal); if the ghost is Wood, acupuncture suits and decoctions do not (Metal can control Wood, while Water generates Wood); if Water, warm medicines and pills suit and acupuncture does not (Earth controls Water, while Metal generates Water); if Fire, cool medicines and decoctions suit and herbal medicines and sliced drugs do not (Water controls Fire, while Wood generates Fire); if Earth, crushed herbs suit and moxibustion does not (Wood controls Earth, while Fire generates Earth). Besides: if the ghost harms the two between lines, ease the chest and diaphragm; if the Self is strong and supported, there is no harm in tonics again; if Wealth moves in the outer trigram and combines with the Subject line, an emetic suits, and in the inner trigram, a purgative; if the Subject line and the Pressure line appear openly or are in the outer trigram, treat the exterior, and if hidden in the inner trigram, treat the interior.
- **LY.ILL.YY.26** 周杰说：有的病是在这里受的，该治的却在那里。比如金的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肝经受了病（金来克木），但应当治肺，不能治肝（这就是所说的去掉害我的那一个，其余照这个办法推）；木的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是脾经受病（木来伤土），该治肝，不能治脾；水的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是心经受病（水来伤火），该治肾，不能治心；火的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肺经受病（火来伤金），该治心，不能治肺；土的虎鬼冲伤用爻，是肾经受病（土来伤水），该治脾，不能治肾（出自《松径玄谈》）。颗师说：子孙发动的，是吃错了药。
  Zhou Jie says: in some cases the illness is received in one place while the treatment lies in another. For example, if a Metal Tiger ghost clashes with and wounds the Subject line, the liver channel has received the illness (Metal comes to control Wood), yet one may treat the lungs and must not treat the liver (this is what is meant by removing what harms me; the rest follow this); a Wood Tiger ghost wounding the Subject line: the spleen channel has received the illness (Wood comes to wound Earth), treat the liver, not the spleen; a Water Tiger ghost: the heart channel (Water wounds Fire), treat the kidneys, not the heart; a Fire Tiger ghost: the lung channel (Fire wounds Metal), treat the heart, not the lungs; an Earth Tiger ghost: the kidney channel (Earth wounds Water), treat the spleen, not the kidneys (from the Songjing Xuantan). Master Ke says: when Output moves, the wrong medicine has been taken.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.10** 身上临着福德，病势虽凶险也可以医治，有两种情形。自己占病：子孙持世，不吃药也会痊愈；临空亡、月破的，到填实空、填实破的那天就好。替别人占六亲的病：不必看用神旺衰，就知道他平安，因为子孙是喜悦的神，只要旺相，如果在卦中发动，也是吉庆。觉子补充：占父母的病，子孙持世又发动，会克掉父母的元神；妻子占丈夫的病，子孙动会伤丈夫，这些情形它都不是喜悦的神，要通变来看。
  With the body sitting on Output-virtue, though the danger looks great the illness can be treated, and there are two cases. Divining one's own illness: with Output holding the Self line, one recovers without medicine; if it sits on Void or Month-break, he recovers on the day the Void or the break is filled. Divining a relation's illness for another: one need not look at whether the Subject is strong or weak to know he will be safe, because Output is the spirit of joy, and so long as it is strong, if it moves in the hexagram it is also a sign of good fortune. Juezi adds: in divining a parent's illness, an Output line that holds the Self line and moves controls away the Support of the Resource line; when a wife divines her husband's illness, a moving Output harms the husband; in these cases it is not the spirit of joy, and one must look at it flexibly.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.26** 问卜求神、请医吃药是有道理的：不灵验有两个原因，一是术不精，精了没有不灵验的；二是病要分有根无根，有根的可以救，无根的救不了，就像移栽花木，有根的上面遮盖、下面浇灌，自然慢慢活过来，无根的树、无蒂的花，浇了盖了也没有用。人不懂这个，一次不灵验就不信神和药，是冤枉了它。
  There is reason in consulting the oracle and praying to the spirits, calling a doctor and taking medicine: when it fails there are two causes, one that the art is not refined, for if refined nothing fails, and the other that an illness must be told as rooted or rootless, the rooted being savable and the rootless not, as with moving a flowering plant, where one that has roots is covered above and watered below and revives gradually, while a tree without root or a flower without stem gains nothing from watering and covering. People who do not understand this lose faith in the spirits and medicine at the first failure, and wrong them.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.27** 官鬼这一爻，不是鬼也不是病，是忧神：替儿子占病的，父母爱子的心，没病时都怕他生病担忧，何况已经病了；自己占病的，性命在一呼一吸之间，一个人起不来，全家惊慌，谁不担忧？所以官鬼就是一家人的忧神，能解这份忧的，只有子孙爻。鬼持世或在卦中发动，得到子孙发动来制服它，这个医生可以请，一下手就成功；子孙临在应爻上，是高明的医生，因为子孙克掉的是忧神，我就没有忧愁了。
  The Pressure line is neither ghost nor illness but the spirit of worry: in divining a son's illness, the parents' love for their son is such that even before he is ill they fear illness and worry, and how much more when he is already ill; in divining one's own illness, life hangs on a breath, and when one person cannot rise the whole household is alarmed, and who would not worry? So the Pressure line is the spirit of worry of the whole household, and what can relieve that worry is only the Output line. If Pressure holds the Self line or moves in the hexagram and Output moves to restrain it, this doctor can be called and his hand will succeed; if Output sits on the Other line he is a brilliant doctor, because what Output controls away is the spirit of worry, and then I have no worry.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.29** 应爻当作医生，不宜落在破、空、墓、绝上。自己求医、替六亲求医，都拿应爻当医生。应爻如果空、破、入墓、入绝、休囚衰弱，或者旺相却被日月和动爻冲克，或者动了变成鬼、变成绝、变出回头克，药就不见效。
  The Other line is taken as the doctor and should not be broken, Void, in a tomb or extinct. Whether one seeks a doctor for oneself or for a relation, the Other line is the doctor. If the Other line is Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct, resting, trapped or weak, or is strong but clashed or controlled by the Day, Month or a moving line, or moves and changes into Pressure, into extinction or into a returning control, the medicine has no effect.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.30** 子孙制服鬼，最喜欢旺相、有生扶。子孙临在世爻、应爻上，或者在卦中发动，也要旺相，不受刑、冲、克、害，不遇破、墓、绝、空，药就一定见效；子孙动了变成鬼、变出克，那药就不精；动了变空，到填实空的那天有效；动了变出生、变旺、变进神，药更灵。古人认为子孙动变子孙，药一定杂乱，不对！子孙动变成旺相的子孙，是另外改换了药品，好比仙丹。
  Output restrains Pressure and most wants to be strong and to be generated and supported. If Output sits on the Self or Other line, or moves in the hexagram, it too must be strong, suffer no punishment, clash, control or harm, and meet no break, tomb, extinction or Void, and then the medicine surely takes effect; if Output moves and changes into Pressure or into a control, the medicine is not refined; if it moves and changes into Void, it is effective on the day the Void is filled; if it moves and changes into generation, strength or an Advancing spirit, the medicine is even more effective. The ancients held that Output moving and changing into Output means a mixed medicine, but this is wrong! Output moving and changing into an Output that is strong means that the medicines have been changed, and it is as good as an immortal's elixir.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.31** 医生克用爻，近病就好。自己占病，应爻克世爻，是克制我的病；应爻克用神，也这样断。但是不适宜久病和体弱的人，因为它不光克掉了病，身体也受到伤害。应爻临着兄弟、官鬼而去克用神，以及克世爻的，不管久病近病，一定受它的害。
  The doctor controlling the Subject line: a recent illness heals at once. In divining one's own illness, if the Other line controls the Self line, it restrains my illness; if the Other line controls the Subject, judge the same. It is unsuitable only for one long ill or of weak body, for it controls not only the illness but harms the body too. If the Other line sits on Peer or Pressure and controls the Subject, or controls the Self line, whether the illness is long or recent, harm from it is certain.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.32** 父母爻持世，再好的药也难调治。自己占病，父母持世，药不见功效，宜于静养，远离色欲，平息气恼就可以了；兄弟持世，有子孙在卦中发动的，可以请医生，只嫌鬼旺相、鬼兴起，主吃错药；财爻持世，千万不要误吃油腻甘美的东西。旺相又遇到生扶，能找到良医；休囚又遇到冲克，没有药可以医。
  With the Resource line holding the Self line, even wonderful medicine is hard to prescribe. In divining one's own illness, with Resource holding the Self line the medicine shows no effect, and it is fitting to rest quietly, keep away from lust and calm one's anger, and that will do; with Peer holding the Self line, if Output moves in the hexagram a doctor may be called, and the only fear is that Pressure is strong and rising, which means taking the wrong medicine; with the Wealth line holding the Self line, take care not to eat rich food by mistake. If strong and generated and supported, a good doctor can be found; if resting or trapped and clashed or controlled, there is no medicine that can treat him.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.33** 儿子不替父亲占药，妻子不替丈夫卜医。儿子占父亲的病，宜父爻旺相；可是占药、占医，又要拿子孙当用神，父爻旺就会伤子孙，一个爻不能同时当两样用。妻子占丈夫的病，宜官鬼旺相；占医占药，也拿子孙当用神，子孙旺又会伤丈夫，所以不可以替他占医药。
  A son does not divine medicine for his father, nor a wife a doctor for her husband. A son divining his father's illness wants the Resource line strong; but in divining medicine or a doctor Output is taken as the Subject, and a strong Resource line harms Output, and one line cannot serve two uses. A wife divining her husband's illness wants the Pressure line strong; in divining a doctor or medicine Output is also taken as the Subject, and a strong Output harms the husband, so one may not divine medicine or a doctor on his behalf.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.34** 请医生要往子孙所在的方位去请，治病则以应爻来定。卦里子孙爻旺相的，从子孙所在的方位请医生，比如午火子孙，旺相或发动，就请南方的医生。子孙休囚、空、破的，再看应爻旺不旺，如果应爻旺相、又生合用神，就往应爻所在的方位请，比如应爻临申金，名医一定在西南。卦里有一个爻单独发动、而且生用神的，就从这个爻的方位请，并用五行来定医生的姓名：水爻动，姓名带三点水；火爻动，姓名像火；木爻动，带草字头、木字旁；土爻动，姓名里有土；金爻动，姓名里有金。比如占妻子的病，妻爻属水，得申金发动来生它，就请姓钱的医生。
  Call the doctor from the direction of Output; for treatment, the Other line decides. If the Output line in the hexagram is strong, call the doctor from the direction of Output; for example, with Wu Fire Output, strong or moving, call a doctor from the south. If Output is resting, trapped, Void or broken, look at whether the Other line is strong; if the Other line is strong and generates or combines with the Subject, call from the direction of the Other line; for example, with the Other line on Shen Metal, a famous doctor is surely in the south-west. If one line in the hexagram moves alone and generates the Subject, call from the direction of that line, and fix the doctor's name by the five phases: a Water line moving, a name with the water radical; Fire, a name resembling fire; Wood, a name with the grass or wood radical; Earth, a name with earth in it; Metal, a name with metal in it. For example, in divining a wife's illness, if the wife's line is Water and Shen Metal moves and generates it, call a doctor with the surname Qian.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.36** 医生自己占要不要去治，和替别人占卜差不多：世爻是自己，应爻是病人。世爻旺相，子孙或财爻持世，世爻生应爻，世动变进神、变回头生，日月和动爻临着财星或子孙来生世爻，都主手到病除。近病：应爻值旬空，或动了变空、变回头生、变旺、变退，或日月、动爻生应爻，以及卦逢六冲、卦变六冲的，都该赶快去救，别让别人先到而成了功。久病：应爻旺相，或遇到日月、动爻来生，或动了变生、变旺，应爻临子孙、临财爻，动而生世爻的，虽然是久病，我一定能除根。除了这些，都不要去治，白白损了名声。
  A doctor divining for himself whether to go and treat is much like divining for another: the Self line is oneself and the Other line is the patient. If the Self line is strong, Output or Wealth holds the Self line, the Self line generates the Other line, the Self line moves and changes into an Advancing spirit or into a returning generation, or the Day, Month or a moving line sits on the Wealth star or Output and generates the Self line, all mean that the hand arrives and the illness is gone. For a recent illness: if the Other line falls into Void, or moves and changes into Void, a returning generation, strength or retreat, or the Day, Month or a moving line generates the Other line, or the hexagram is Six-Clash or changes into Six-Clash, one should hurry to the rescue and not let others arrive first and succeed. For a long illness: if the Other line is strong, or is generated by the Day, Month or a moving line, or moves and changes into generation or strength, or sits on Output or Wealth, or moves and generates the Self line, then though the illness is long I shall surely remove the root. Apart from these, do not go to treat, for it only harms one's reputation.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.37** 占药能不能服，拿子孙当用神。医生治垂危的病、想用某味药，可以起死回生，又怕一有闪失；泛海行船、长途旅行没有地方找医生、有人传来奇方；半夜来不及请医、手边的古方或家里的药散确实对这个病却不敢擅用，都要占。只要子孙持世或发动，服了立刻可以解忧，必定见效。子孙不管是金木水火土哪一行，旺相或发动就是喜悦的神；不可以又因为金临子孙就用针、火属子孙就用灸。
  To divine whether a medicine may be taken, take Output as the Subject. It should be divined when a doctor treating a dying patient wants a certain medicine that may bring him back from death, and fears one failure; when one is crossing the sea or on a long journey with nowhere to find a doctor and someone passes on a rare prescription; when in the depth of night there is no time to call a doctor and the old prescriptions or powders kept at home truly fit the illness but one does not dare use them on one's own authority. If Output holds the Self line or moves, taking it at once relieves the worry, and it will surely work. Whatever phase Output is, metal, wood, water, fire or earth, so long as it is strong or moving it is the spirit of joy; one must not take Metal on Output to mean the needle, or Fire as Output to mean moxibustion.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.38** 前面几篇都说子孙持世，病就马上好，但一定要子孙不受克才行，后来的人不可以不知道。比如有人生了杨梅疮，占这位医生好不好：虽然是子孙持世、应爻是医生，但初爻巳火发动克申金，又有卯木生火，世爻虽旺，巳日一定添灾；他不听，还是请了这位医生，卯日、辰日服药还平安，巳日忽然病症变了，全身疼痛难以忍受。
  The earlier passages all say that with Output holding the Self line the illness heals at once, but it is so only when Output is not controlled, which later people must know. For example, a man with syphilitic sores divined whether a certain doctor was a good one: though Output held the Self line and the Other line was the doctor, the first line, Si Fire, moved and controlled Shen Metal, with Mao Wood also generating Fire, so that though the Self line was strong there would be added trouble on the Si day; he did not listen and called the doctor, and on the Mao and Chen days the medicine kept him safe, but on the Si day the symptoms suddenly changed and pain all over the body was unbearable.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.40** 子孙旺相又发动的，不吃药就好，并不是教人拿这个子孙当治病的药；前辈贤人常常看到子孙旺动，占病病好、占医医良，就把子孙爻当成治病的药，金生子孙就断用针，火持子孙就断用灸，不知道子孙旺相病自己就好了，哪里用得着针灸，子孙落空被破，针灸又有什么益处。各种书又说火鬼忌热、水鬼忌寒，水鬼在生旺之地要用大热的药，火鬼旺相宜用大寒的药去攻；李我平不知道他们从哪里看出来的：假使用神属土、儿子占父亲的病，父爻临土，火鬼发动是生用爻的元神，怎么能用大寒的药去治元神？妻子占丈夫的病，鬼是用神，丈夫临水鬼，又怎么能用大热的药去攻用神？死板地照大寒大热去断，坑害了不知多少性命。
  Where Output is strong and moving, one recovers without medicine, and this does not teach us to take that Output as the medicine that treats the illness; the worthies of old often saw Output strong and moving, found that in divining illness the illness healed and in divining a doctor the doctor was good, and so took the Output line as the medicine, judging the needle where Metal generates Output and moxibustion where Fire holds Output, without realising that if Output is strong the illness heals of itself, so what need is there of needle and moxa, and if Output is Void or broken what good would they do. The books also say that a Fire Pressure fears heat and a Water Pressure fears cold, that a Water Pressure in a place of birth or strength must be treated with very hot medicine and a strong Fire Pressure attacked with very cold; Li Woping does not know where they saw this: suppose the Subject is Earth, as when a son divines his father's illness and the Resource line sits on Earth, a moving Fire Pressure is the Support that generates the Subject line, and how could one treat the Support with very cold medicine? When a wife divines her husband's illness, Pressure is the Subject, and if the husband sits on a Water Pressure, how could one attack the Subject with very hot medicine? To judge rigidly by very cold and very hot has sunk who knows how many lives in the pit.

## 神祟(Spirits and curses: prayer, vows and what to sacrifice)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/神祟.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSQS.02** 卜筮全书的「六爻诸占定位」表里，鬼神一行按初爻到六爻的次序是：井神、司命、家先、土地、社司、天神。
  In the table of the position of the six lines for the various divinations in the Bushi Quanshu, the row for spirits runs, from the first line to the sixth: the well spirit, the Life-Governing Spirit, the family ancestors, the Earth God, the village altar spirit and the heavenly spirit.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.37** 疾病论里按八卦推是哪一路神鬼作祟：乾宫发动，是欠了天庭的愿；坤宫鬼爻兴旺，是冒犯了地府；艮宫鬼爻伤身，是路神作祸；震宫克世，是狱司降灾；鬼临在第五爻，是路头神；官鬼在第三爻，是家里作祟；坎宫鬼动，要求溺水之魂；巽宫鬼兴，要祭上吊之祟；坎宫交重是船车之鬼，艮象土爻发动是路上、狱中之魂。
  The chapter on illness works out from the trigrams which kind of spirit or ghost is at work: Qian moving, a vow owed to the Heavenly Court; Kun ghost thriving, it disturbs the Earth Prefecture; Gen ghost harming the person, a road spirit causing harm; Zhen controlling the Self line, a spirit of the prison sends down calamity; the ghost on the fifth place, the Road-Head spirit; Pressure on the third line, a haunting within the house; a Kan ghost moving, seek the soul of one drowned; a Xun ghost rising, sacrifice to the haunting of one who hanged himself from a beam; Kan moving is a ghost of boats and carts, a Gen Earth line moving is a soul of the road or the prison.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.38** 疾病论里接着按日、岁、五行和宫来断神鬼：日辰值着贵官，可以向当地的城隍祷告；太岁临官贵，宜求五道和家堂；兑象官鬼兴旺，赶紧还口愿；火爻鬼动，赶快祭灶君；离鬼休囚持世，要祈禳北斗；乾鬼旺相克身，宜向天神还素愿；衰官在外卦、从别宫来，是外来作祟；休鬼在内卦、本宫动，可以辨出是家亲。
  The chapter on illness goes on judging spirits by the Day, the Year, the elements and the palace: with the Day on the noble officer, one may petition the City God of the district; with the Year on the noble officer, it is fitting to beseech the Five Roads and the household shrine; with Pressure in the Dui image rising, hurry to repay a spoken vow; with a Fire ghost moving, quickly sacrifice to the Hearth Lord; a Li ghost resting or confined and holding the Self line, pray to the Dipper; a Qian ghost strong and controlling the Body, petition the heavenly spirits and repay a vegetarian vow; a weak Pressure line moving outside and coming from another palace is an outside haunting; a resting ghost rising inside and moving from the home palace can be identified as the family's own kin.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.39** 疾病论里最后几联讲神鬼：应爻的鬼克身，遇到的是外来的鬼；官爻生世爻，是连着亲戚的；被日辰冲动而发出来的，是无主的鬼；土位被堵塞又兴起，一定有坟；土爻动，是死在牢狱里的囚犯；火爻兴旺，是产亡的鬼；只要知道旺的日子，就看四季；细察休囚的日子，就找八节。
  The last couplets of the chapter on illness on spirits and ghosts: a ghost on the Other line controlling the Body is an outside ghost; a Pressure line generating the Self line is of kin connections; one stirred out by the Day's clash is a ghost without an owner; with the Earth place stopped up and rising, there is surely a grave; an Earth line moving is one who died as a prisoner in jail; a Fire line rising is one who died in childbirth; know the strong days and look to the four seasons, examine the resting and confined closely and seek the eight festivals.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.40** 疾病论里按八卦说鬼的模样和它带来的病：乾金之鬼是白发老头；坎鬼休囚，是耳聋的鬼作祟；艮宫的鬼，手瘫；震宫的官鬼，脚有病；巽宫的官鬼发动，折腿、腰驼；离象的鬼兴旺，眼睛昏暗；坤土之鬼是花白头发的老母；兑金的死鬼是缺嘴唇的魂。
  The chapter on illness gives, by trigram, the look of the ghost and the illness it brings: the ghost of Qian Metal is a white-haired old man; a Kan ghost resting or confined is a deaf ghost haunting; a Gen ghost, a paralysed hand; the Pressure line of Zhen, disease of the foot; the Pressure line of Xun moving, a broken leg and a bent waist; a ghost rising in the Li image, dim and dark eyes; the ghost of Kun Earth is an old mother with mottled grey hair; the dead ghost of Dui Metal is the soul of one with a split lip.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.41** 疾病论里讲是哪一位亲人、哪一种愿：要看祖宗、叔伯，用父母爻推；要看姨母、妻子、奴仆，用财爻断；阳爻是男子，阴爻是女人；白虎、丧门，是亲人在座；青龙、天乙贵人，是尊贵的家亲；青龙、朱雀叫素愿，白虎、勾陈是晕盟；朱雀、白虎进乾、离动，只说拜愿烧香；勾陈临震、巽交重，就说愿心枷锁；鬼静、生身还是吉，官鬼兴动、克世，告神也凶。
  The chapter on illness on which kin and which vow: to see the ancestors and uncles, work it out from the Resource line; to see the aunt, wife or servants, judge by the Wealth line; a yang line is a man, a yin line a woman; the White Tiger and Mourning Gate are kin in their seats; the Azure Dragon and Heavenly Noble are honoured family kin; the Azure Dragon and Vermilion Bird are called vegetarian vows, the White Tiger and Hook Earth are sure to be yun-meng oaths; the Vermilion Bird and White Tiger in Qian or Li and moving speak only of making vows and burning incense; Hook Earth on Zhen or Xun and moving speaks of the cangue and lock of a vow; a ghost still and generating the Body is still auspicious, Pressure rising and controlling the Self line is ominous even when one petitions the spirits.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.44** 六神论神鬼心决是六首歌，每首一位神：青龙是善愿、观音、三官；朱雀是家堂、灶君，鬼为吐血和劳怯，目疾、伤亡也有缘故；勾陈是土地、城隍，鬼为痢疾和蛊胀，遇土爻是咽塞而死；螣蛇逢官缢死，孩童汤火受伤；白虎临太岁是大将军，横死伤亡；玄武是真武帝，鬼爻作祟断为落水伤亡。
  The verses on the six spirits and the heart-secret of spirits and ghosts are six songs, one for each spirit: the Azure Dragon is good vows, Guanyin and the Three Officials; the Vermilion Bird is the household shrine and the Hearth Lord, its ghost means spitting blood and consumptive weakness, and eye disease, injury and death have their cause in it; Hook Earth is the Earth God and the City God, its ghost means dysentery and swelling of the belly, and meeting an Earth line one dies of a blocked throat; the Coiling Snake meeting Pressure means death by hanging, and children are hurt by scalding and fire; the White Tiger on Tai Sui is the great general, with violent death and injury; the Dark Tortoise is the Emperor Zhenwu, and its ghost doing harm is judged a death by drowning.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.45** 六神论神崇决也是六首歌，讲六神的爻发动时，病是哪一路神鬼、该向谁祭祀还愿：青龙爻动，是往年的佛愿在催讨，赶紧告东厨和祖先；朱雀爻兴，是游方的五鬼、梦中的口愿；勾陈爻动，是土地、家堂、灶王，时疫、五瘟要求祭祀；螣蛇爻发，病缠身，是东厨、土地、家亲和天曹的口愿；白虎爻兴，是神前的愿、佛前的香；玄武爻兴，病患凶，家里人彼此不和，要靠北阴求土地。
  The verses on the six spirits and the secret of haunting spirits are also six songs, saying, when the line of each spirit moves, which kind of spirit or ghost the illness comes from and to whom to sacrifice or repay a vow: the Azure Dragon line moving, a Buddhist vow of past years is pressing for repayment, hurry to tell the Eastern Kitchen and the ancestors; the Vermilion Bird line rising, wandering Five Ghosts and a vow spoken in a dream; Hook Earth moving, the Earth God, household shrine and Hearth King, with epidemics and the Five Plagues calling for sacrifice; the Coiling Snake line moving, illness clings to the body, from the Eastern Kitchen, the Earth God, family kin and a vow spoken to the Celestial Bureau; the White Tiger line rising, a vow before the gods and incense before the Buddha; the Dark Tortoise line rising, illness is ominous, the people of the household are at odds, and one must rely on the North Yin to beseech the Earth God.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.46** 妖孽赋讲震宫螣蛇带鬼、遇酉冲刑时的怪异：空中的椅子桌子动，听得到声音，踢踏的响声像人在走；大蛇常出现，窑里的器具响得惊人；桶、箱作孽，家里的人生病。
  The Fu on monsters on the oddities when a Coiling Snake carrying a ghost in the Zhen palace is clashed and punished by You: chairs and tables move in the air and sounds are heard, a stamping sound like a person walking; a great snake often appears, and kiln wares clatter alarmingly; casks and chests do harm, and the people of the household fall ill.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.47** 搜鬼论按十二地支列出鬼爻发动时所应的怪异：寅爻为鬼，蛇、虎来作怪，六畜见血、钱财亡失，家里人口有病患，要赶紧保平安；卯爻为鬼，是隔墙带着血的鬼，作灾，母亲卧病在床。
  The essay on searching out the ghost lists, by the twelve branches, what a moving ghost line answers: with Yin as the ghost, snakes and tigers come making trouble, livestock bleed and wealth is lost, the household has illness and one must quickly secure safety; with Mao as the ghost, it is a ghost with blood from across the wall, it makes calamity, and the mother lies ill in bed.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.37** 怎么知道这家的人多做噩梦：螣蛇带着鬼来持世；出鬼怪：螣蛇、白虎临在门上；有人投水：玄武入水，煞神临在鬼上；有人上吊：螣蛇、木鬼落在世爻上；要有孝服（丧事）来：卜筮正宗说是白虎交重（发动）、排在鬼上，卜筮大全说是丧门、吊客两颗星排在鬼上。
  How to know that a household is troubled by dreams: the Coiling Snake carrying the ghost holds the Self position; that ghosts and strange things appear: the Coiling Snake and White Tiger sit at the door; that someone will throw himself into water: the Dark Tortoise enters Water and a killing spirit sits on the ghost; that someone will hang himself: the Coiling Snake and a Wood ghost sit on the Self line; that mourning dress will come: in the Bushi Zhengzong the White Tiger moves and is set upon the ghost, in the Bushi Daquan the stars Mourning Gate and Guest of Condolence are set upon the ghost.
- **LY.ILL.EL.29** 以官鬼和动爻为主，以五行和六兽（六神）为凭。凡是论神灵的职司，必须凭官鬼，只要不逢旬空，或旺或衰都是作祟，只要出现在卦上，不论动静，一概按神仙来推。先看鬼落在哪一行，再察官鬼临的是哪个六兽，再查病的来源是缓是急，就知道是什么祟作怪。注说：如果占神灵的职司，要推官鬼；鬼如果空亡或不上卦，一定没有鬼祟，不可妄说；鬼如果不空，不论衰旺动静，都作为神灵的职司；如果发动或持世，神力狂暴，冒犯了应当赶快祷告；还要看鬼落在哪一爻、哪个六兽上，才说是什么鬼、什么神，六兽和五行列在后面。
  The Pressure line and the moving lines are the main lines, and the five phases and the six beasts (the six spirits) are the ground. In judging the offices of the spirits one must rely on the Pressure line; so long as it does not fall on Void, strong or weak it makes haunting, and so long as it appears in the hexagram, moving or still, it is in all cases reckoned as an immortal spirit. See first which phase the ghost falls on, then examine which of the six beasts the Pressure line sits on, then check whether the source of the illness is slow or urgent, and one knows what haunting is doing the harm. The commentary says: if divining the office of a spirit, work out the Pressure line; if the ghost is Void or absent from the hexagram, there is surely no haunting by a ghost, and one must not speak at random; if the ghost is not Void, whether weak or strong, moving or still, it stands for a spirit's office; if it moves or holds the Self, the spirit's power is fierce and wild, and if offended one should pray at once; look also at which line and which beast the ghost sits on, and only then say which ghost and which spirit; the six beasts and five phases are set out below.
- **LY.ILL.EL.30** 金鬼，是死在刀下的魂，喘咳、横死的鬼：症状缓的，是关公、总管；症状急的，是丧部、伤司。带青龙，是汉寿亭侯（关公）；朱雀，是金都元帅；勾陈、螣蛇，是七煞；白虎，是丧殃；玄武，是曹堂、西府。把它送走，病体就安康。注说：金鬼，主刀枪伤死的鬼、喘咳的鬼、横死的鬼；症状缓的，宜向关爷和总管祷告；病急的，必须酬谢丧煞和伤神；带青龙，是云长公（关公）有所妨碍；临朱雀，是金元七作祟为灾；带勾陈、螣蛇，是七煞土；带白虎，是丧煞和伤司；带玄武，是水伤、水道一类。
  Metal is the soul beneath the blade and the ghost of asthma-cough and violent death: if mild, Guan Gong and the Chief Official; if severe, the Mourning Department and the Injuring Office. With the Azure Dragon, the Marquis of Hanshou; the Vermilion Bird is the Golden Capital Marshal; Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake are the Seven Killings; the White Tiger is Mourning Calamity; the Dark Tortoise is the Cao Hall and the Western Bureau. Send it away, and the sick body is well. The commentary says: a Metal ghost governs ghosts of death by blade and spear, ghosts of asthma-cough and ghosts of violent death; if the symptoms are mild, pray to Lord Guan and the Chief Official; if the illness is urgent, one must make offering to the Mourning Killing and the Injuring spirit; with the Azure Dragon, it is an obstruction from Lord Yunchang; on the Vermilion Bird, Lord Jin Yuanqi is making the calamity; with Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake, it is the Seven Killings Earth; with the White Tiger, it is the Mourning Killing and the Injuring Office; with the Dark Tortoise, it is the water injury and water-way sort.
- **LY.ILL.EL.31** 木鬼，是被杖责的魂，疯病、上吊的鬼：症状缓的，是山神、五圣；症状急的，是东兵（东岳）、家堂。白虎同在，就在门外酬谢伤神，用锣鼓追送；青龙同位，就在堂前酬还原愿，用笙萧；勾陈，是九良星煞；螣蛇，是树圣、山神；朱雀、玄武，是草野三郎，祭祀时要提防，灾非都落在你身上。注说：木鬼，主受刑责、戴枷锁的鬼、疯病鬼、上吊死的鬼；症状缓的，必是冒犯了山神、土地和五圣尊神；症状急的，必是干犯了东岳和家堂的众神；带白虎，赶快酬谢大小伤司；带青龙，宜酬还枷锁的愿，以及祷告喜庆的神；带勾陈，是冒犯了九良星；土带螣蛇，如果不是山神，一定是树上的神，又是作犯土；带朱雀或玄武，都是草野三郎。
  Wood is the soul of one beaten with the rod, the ghost of madness and of hanging from a beam: if mild, the Mountain God and the Five Sages; if severe, the Eastern Soldiers (the Eastern Peak) and the household shrine. With the White Tiger in the same place, thank the Injuring spirit outside the gate and send it off with gongs and drums; with the Azure Dragon in the same place, redeem the vow in front of the hall with pipes and flutes; Hook Earth is the Nine Good Stars and the killing stars; the Coiling Snake is the Tree Sage or Mountain God; the Vermilion Bird and the Dark Tortoise are the Third Lord of the wilds, and in sacrificing be careful, for calamity and blame will all fall on you. The commentary says: a Wood ghost governs ghosts of punishment, the cangue and fetters, ghosts of madness and ghosts of hanging; if the symptoms are mild, it is surely from offending the Mountain God, the Earth God and the honoured Five Sages; if urgent, surely from offending the Eastern Peak and the gods of the household shrine; with the White Tiger, quickly make offering to the greater and lesser Injuring Offices; with the Azure Dragon, redeem the vow of the cangue and pray to the spirits of festivity; with Hook Earth, there is an offence against the Nine Good Stars; Earth with the Coiling Snake, if not the Mountain God, must be the spirit in a tree, and is also the offence-making earth; with the Vermilion Bird or the Dark Tortoise, it is the Third Lord of the wilds.
- **LY.ILL.EL.32** 水鬼，是投河、奔井的魂，服卤、腰疼的鬼：症状缓的，是水仙、施相；症状急的，是河太、金龙。阳龙（阳的青龙），断作云台法主，也是冒犯了萧公；阴龙，推作南海慈尊，又冲犯了杜氏；玄武，是佑圣真君；朱雀，恐怕是对江河许过愿；遇到勾陈、螣蛇，断作坑厕；逢白虎，论作水伤。注说：水鬼，主淹死的鬼、腰疼的鬼、服卤死的鬼；症状缓的，是水仙五圣和镇海施相公；症状急的，是冒犯了水中的河太、或曹三和金龙四大王；阳鬼值青龙，是三官大帝和五圣的神；阴鬼值青龙，是观世音和杜氏夫人；鬼带玄武，是北极真君；带朱雀，是在水池上许的愿心；带勾陈或螣蛇，就说是水口上冒犯的；如果水鬼变出兄弟、或水的兄弟变出鬼、又带勾陈螣蛇的，就是坑厕的土神；鬼临白虎，必须向水部的伤司求。
  Water is the soul of one who threw himself into a river or ran to a well, the ghost of one who drank brine ('you' in the text, for 'lu') or of back pain: if mild, the Water Immortal and Minister Shi; if severe, He Tai and the Golden Dragon. A yang Dragon is judged the Master of the Yuntai Rite, and also offends Lord Xiao; a yin Dragon is worked out as the Compassionate Honoured One of the Southern Sea, and also clashes with Lady Du; the Dark Tortoise is the True Lord Yousheng; the Vermilion Bird: beware a vow made to river or lake; meeting Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake, judge it the pit and privy; meeting the White Tiger, judge it the water injury. The commentary says: a Water ghost governs ghosts of drowning, ghosts of back pain and ghosts of death by drinking brine; if the symptoms are mild, it is the Water (the text says Fire) Immortal and the Five Sages, and the Sea-Pacifying Lord Shi; if urgent, it is an offence against He Tai in the water, or Cao San and the Golden Dragon Fourth Great King; a yang ghost on the Azure Dragon is the Three Officials the Great Emperors and the spirits of the Five Sages; a yin ghost on the Azure Dragon is Guanyin and Lady Du; a ghost with the Dark Tortoise is the True Lord of the North Pole; with the Vermilion Bird, a vow made by a pool; with Hook Earth or the Coiling Snake, say an offence at a water mouth; if the Water ghost changes into Peer, or a Water Peer changes into a ghost and carries Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake, it is the earth spirit of the pit and privy; a ghost on the White Tiger must seek the Injuring Office of the Water Department.
- **LY.ILL.EL.33** 火鬼，是毒疮、痨病的魂，带血、焚烧的鬼：症状缓的，是东厨、香愿；症状急的，是陆相、华山。青龙，冒犯了五福星；勾陈、螣蛇，动了三煞的土；白虎，是玄坛再加上横死的鬼；玄武，是南堂和水神一起；朱雀，是华光、司命，并且酬还口愿才安宁。注说：火鬼，主疮毒鬼、痨病鬼、带血鬼、烧死的鬼、心疼的鬼；症状缓的，宜酬谢灶神和香愿；症状急的，也有轻重之分：轻病宜酬陆引，陆相就是南堂；重病宜拜华山，华山就是五福大神；带青龙也是五福；带勾陈或螣蛇都是三煞土；带白虎是赵玄坛和痴癫鬼；带玄武是南堂和水神；带朱雀是华光，华光就是五显灵官，又是司命，就是灶神。
  Fire is the soul of poisonous sores and consumption, the ghost of bleeding and of burning: if mild, the Eastern Kitchen and the incense vow; if severe, Minister Lu and Huashan. The Azure Dragon offends the Five Blessings star; Hook Earth and the Coiling Snake move the earth of the Three Killings; the White Tiger, the Dark Altar, and a violent-death ghost added; the Dark Tortoise, the Southern Hall and the water spirit together; the Vermilion Bird, Huaguang and the Director of Fate, and the spoken vow redeemed, then there is peace. The commentary says: a Fire ghost governs ghosts of sores and poison, ghosts of consumption, ghosts of bleeding, ghosts of burning to death and ghosts of heart pain; if the symptoms are mild, thank the Kitchen God and the incense vow; if urgent, there are still degrees: a light illness suits an offering to Lu Yin, Minister Lu being the Southern Hall; a heavy illness suits worship at Huashan, Huashan being the Great God of the Five Blessings; with the Azure Dragon it is also the Five Blessings; with Hook Earth or the Coiling Snake it is the Three Killings Earth; with the White Tiger it is Zhao Xuantan and a ghost of madness; with the Dark Tortoise it is the Southern Hall and a water spirit; with the Vermilion Bird it is Huaguang, and Huaguang is the Five Manifest Spirit Officials and also the Director of Fate, who is the Kitchen God.
- **LY.ILL.EL.34** 土鬼，是瘟疫的魂，膨胀、虚黄的鬼：症状缓的，是庙神、土府；症状急的，是贤圣、城隍。青龙，是素土；勾陈，叫土皇；白虎，是金神，忌它；玄武，是坑厕的妨害；螣蛇，应当作犯，并且求本境的神；朱雀，合于天曹，并且谢飞游的土。注说：土鬼，主瘟疫鬼、膨胀鬼、黄病鬼；症状缓的，是庙里的神、大的是土神；症状急的，是五方贤圣、又是城隍；带青龙，是正土，宜念素诰；带勾陈，是土皇，宜中奏；带白虎，是金神，就是七煞；带玄武，是水口上的作犯，如果鬼变出兄弟、或兄弟变出鬼，就是坑厕；土带螣蛇是作犯，土又是螣蛇，也是当地的杀神祠；带朱雀是飞土；如果在乾、兑两卦，就是天曹；即使不在乾、兑卦中，如果带天咒、地咒或负结煞，也是天曹土。天咒煞说：正月二月鼠来，三月四月酉，五月六月马头，七月猴走，八月鸡，九月犬，十月逢猪，子、兔、丑、鼠，是天咒；地咒煞从正月起在卯上顺行十二位；负结煞说：正月二月猪亏，三月四月牛，五月六月其星向兔游，七月八月蛇宫，九月十月未，十一月十二月酉中求。
  Earth is the soul of epidemic, the ghost of swelling and pale-yellow emptiness: if mild, the temple gods and the earth mansion; if severe, the sages and the City God. The Azure Dragon is the plain Earth; Hook Earth is called the Earth Emperor; the White Tiger is the Golden Spirit, which is to be avoided; the Dark Tortoise is the hindrance of the pit and privy; the Coiling Snake should be the offence-maker, and one seeks the god of the local region; the Vermilion Bird accords with the Heavenly Bureau, and one thanks the Flying-Wandering Earth. The commentary says: an Earth ghost governs ghosts of epidemic, ghosts of swelling and ghosts of jaundice; if the symptoms are mild, it is a god within a temple, or an earth god of greater rank; if urgent, it is the sages of the five quarters and also the City God; with the Azure Dragon, it is the right Earth, and a plain prayer is suitable; with Hook Earth, it is the Earth Emperor, and a central memorial is suitable; with the White Tiger, it is the Golden Spirit, that is, the Seven Killings; with the Dark Tortoise, it is an offence at a water mouth, and if the ghost changes into Peer or Peer changes into a ghost, it is the pit and privy; Earth with the Coiling Snake is the offence-maker, and Earth is also the Coiling Snake, which is also the local killing-god shrine; with the Vermilion Bird it is the flying earth; if it is in the Qian or Dui hexagram it is the Heavenly Bureau, and even if it is not in Qian or Dui, if it carries the Heavenly Curse, the Earth Curse or the Burden-Knot killing it is also the Heavenly Bureau earth. The Heavenly Curse killing says: 'in the first and second months the Rat comes, third and fourth the You, fifth and sixth the Horse's head, seventh the Monkey runs, eighth the Rooster, ninth the Dog, tenth meets the Pig, Zi, Rabbit, Chou, Rat': this is the Heavenly Curse; the Earth Curse killing runs forward twelve places from Mao in the first month; the Burden-Knot killing says: 'first and second the Pig is deficient, third and fourth the Ox, fifth and sixth its star wanders toward the Rabbit, seventh and eighth the Snake's palace, ninth and tenth Wei, eleventh and twelfth seek in You'.
- **LY.ILL.EL.35** 看鬼落在哪一卦、哪一爻发动，就知道在那里冒犯了什么神、什么煞，因而牵连上身：官鬼在坎位，是北方来侵，或是兴动水口；鬼到离宫，是南向的妨碍，或是在灶前动工；艮主山林，或是遭遇东北；坤成坟墓，或是值西南；乾是西北，兑是西方，或是冒犯天曹、修寺观；巽是东南；震是东方，或是砍伐树林并起造。六爻的鬼，必是造墙作墓；五爻的官，必是砌路修街；四爻，断门栏，或是在檐下动工；三爻，推房内，或是动犯桥梁；二爻，是修厨房、作灶；初爻，是穿井、开沟；鬼在世爻上，是本宅动了工；在应爻上，是对门在挖掘；临门的爻，是开窗不便；变出兄弟，是挖坑、建造不通。注说：以上说的，都是把上面的鬼逐一说明白，不必再注。
  Look at which trigram and which line the ghost moves in, to know where a god or a killing was offended and thereby brought on the body: the Pressure line in the Kan place means encroachment from the north, or works at a water mouth; a ghost at the Li palace means obstruction facing south, or works in front of the stove; Gen governs mountain and forest, or meets the northeast; Kun makes graves, or falls southwest; Qian is northwest and Dui is west, or an offence against the Heavenly Bureau in repairing a temple or abbey; Xun is southeast; Zhen is east, or felling woods and building. A ghost on the sixth line surely means building a wall or making a grave; the Pressure line on the fifth surely means paving a road or repairing a street; the fourth, judge gates and railings, or works under the eaves; the third, work out the inside of the house, or works offending bridges and beams; the second is repairing the kitchen or making a stove; the first is digging a well or opening a ditch; on the Self line, works on one's own house; on the Other line, digging opposite the door; on the door line, opening a window is inconvenient; changing into Peer, a pit or structure is blocked. The commentary says: all of the above sets the ghosts out one by one, and there is no need for further notes.
- **LY.ILL.EL.36** 亥是天门，和张壬作扰害；带青龙的象，宜叩拜三元；子是北斗，加上河伯作为灾殃；加了玄武的爻，应当酬谢圣帝；丑，是被牛顶撞的魂；寅，是被虎伤的鬼；卯，要禳解东岳；辰，要酬谢龙王；巳，推为火德尊星、被蛇所伤的鬼；午，断为金枪教主、被马踏的魂；未，叫伽蓝；申，说是元帅；酉，叫雌雄二煞；戌，遇上恶犬伤人。注说：亥鬼是张壬，就是祠山大帝；外鬼带青龙，是三官大帝；内鬼带青龙，是水仙五圣；子鬼是北斗、又是河伯水官，在外带玄武，是驿帝，在内带玄武，是水伤；丑鬼是土神，又是土伤之鬼；寅鬼是东岳，又是虎伤之鬼；卯鬼也是东岳；辰鬼是土神，又是龙王；巳鬼是火德星君和蚕室，又是蛇伤之鬼；午鬼是金枪教主，就是五显灵官，以及马伤之鬼；未鬼是土神，又是伽蓝土地；申鬼是元帅将军的职司和伤司，又有寺观中所冒犯的神；酉鬼是佛象和丧煞，又是少女；戌鬼是土神，又是犬伤之鬼。
  Hai is the Heavenly Gate and the harm done by Zhang Ren; with the image of the Azure Dragon, it is proper to appeal to the Three Primes; Zi is the Northern Dipper together with the River Count, as calamity; a line with the Dark Tortoise added should make offering to the Holy Emperor; Chou is the soul gored by an ox; Yin is the ghost wounded by a tiger; Mao: exorcise the Eastern Peak; Chen: thank the Dragon King; Si: work out the honoured Fire Virtue star and the ghost wounded by a snake; Wu: judge the Golden Spear Master and the soul trampled by a horse; Wei is called the Sangharama; Shen is said to be the Marshal; You is named the two killings, male and female; Xu meets a fierce dog wounding a person. The commentary says: the Hai ghost is Zhang Ren, that is, the Great Emperor of Ci Mountain; an outer ghost with the Azure Dragon is the Three Officials the Great Emperors; an inner ghost with the Azure Dragon is the Water Immortal and the Five Sages; a Zi ghost is the Northern Dipper and also the River Count and Water Official, and with the Dark Tortoise outside it is the Post Emperor, and inside it is the water injury; a Chou ghost is an earth spirit and also a ghost wounded by earth; a Yin ghost is the Eastern Peak and also a ghost wounded by a tiger; a Mao ghost is also the Eastern Peak; a Chen ghost is an earth spirit and also the Dragon King; a Si ghost is the Fire Virtue Star Lord and the silkworm house, and also a ghost wounded by a snake; a Wu ghost is the Golden Spear Master, that is, the Five Manifest Spirit Officials, and a ghost wounded by a horse; a Wei ghost is an earth spirit and also the Sangharama Earth God; a Shen ghost is the office of marshals and generals and the Injuring Office, and also a god offended in a temple or abbey; a You ghost is a Buddha image and the Mourning Killing, and also a young woman; a Xu ghost is an earth spirit and also a ghost wounded by a dog.
- **LY.ILL.EL.37** 六爻，是上仓的至圣、坟墓的神；五爻，是中界的至尊、路途的鬼；四爻，推为檐外伤、朝门前的魍魉；三爻断，鬼在六爻，是上苍的素原、以及坟墓土神、或远方的鬼；鬼在五爻，是中界至尊，就是东岳，又是栏路五圣、五路大神、或倒在路上的鬼；鬼在四爻，是大小伤神、以及右仪门的将军、又是门前的鬼；鬼在三爻，是家堂和郡王，就是府城隍，又是床婆、弟兄的鬼，以及房内的鬼、桥上的鬼；鬼在二爻，是县城隍和灶神，又是夫妻等的鬼、厨下的鬼；鬼在初爻，是土地和井泉童子、以及井前的鬼，如果没有井，就是土鬼。
  The sixth line is the supreme sage of the upper granary and the god of graves; the fifth is the supreme honoured one of the middle realm and the ghost of the road; the fourth is worked out as an injury beyond the eaves and the goblins before the court gate; the third judge: a ghost on the sixth line is the plain vow of the upper heaven, the earth god of graves, or a ghost from a far place; a ghost on the fifth line is the supreme honoured one of the middle realm, that is, the Eastern Peak, and also the Five Sages of the barrier road, the Great God of the Five Roads, or a ghost fallen on the road; a ghost on the fourth line is the greater and lesser Injuring gods, and the general at the right ceremonial gate, and also a ghost before the door; a ghost on the third line is the household shrine and the Prince of the Commandery, that is, the City God of the prefecture, and also the Bed Mother and a ghost of brothers, and a ghost within the room and a ghost on the bridge; a ghost on the second line is the City God of the county and the Kitchen God, and also a ghost of husband and wife and a ghost beneath the kitchen; a ghost on the first line is the Earth God and the Well-and-Spring Boy, and a ghost before the well, and if there is no well it is an earth ghost.
- **LY.ILL.EL.38** 官鬼临在世爻上，要注明六象的神；鬼落在空亡中，不要断作哪一份殃祟。注说：鬼在初爻持世，是家堂作祸；鬼在二爻持世，上气为实，并且是社坛作祟；鬼在三爻持世，是冒犯了桥道中的鬼，如果不是桥道，就是园中花木的精怪；鬼在四爻持世，是冒犯了五道，也有师王佛实的灾；鬼在五爻持世，原文说没有（可说的）不言；鬼在六爻持世，是山神为害，以及星宿降灾；六爻里鬼虽然持世，落空了就不说。
  The Pressure line on the Self line: note the spirits of the six images; a ghost in Void: do not judge a haunting of any part of calamity. The commentary says: a ghost on the first line holding the Self is the household shrine making trouble; a ghost on the second holding the Self, the upper qi is real, and the altar of the soil haunts; a ghost on the third holding the Self offends the ghost of a bridge or road, and if not a bridge or road, it is the spirit of garden flowers and trees; a ghost on the fourth holding the Self offends the Five Roads, and there is also calamity from teachers, kings and Buddhas (the text says 'shi'); with the fifth, if there is no ghost, nothing is said; a ghost on the sixth holding the Self is the Mountain God doing harm and stars descending calamity; though the ghost in the six lines holds the Self, if it is Void nothing is said.
- **LY.ILL.EL.39** 青龙、财爻、子孙变出官爻，是福神来照应；兑宫、朱雀、文书变成鬼象，是先前许的愿在催。注说：青龙如果临财、玄武临福（子孙），变出官爻，必是福神见咎；福神，大的是五福和茶延，小的是五圣和五路；凡是判断鬼神，必须审察病源的轻重，重的就是大神，轻的就是小祟；再看朱雀临父母变出鬼爻，必是先年许下的愿；如果落在兑卦，又是冒犯了天曹。
  The Azure Dragon, Wealth and Output changing into a Pressure line: the fortune spirit shines on it; Dui, the Vermilion Bird and the writing image becoming a ghost image: the earlier vow presses. The commentary says: if the Azure Dragon sits on Wealth and the Dark Tortoise on Output (the fortune spirit) and it changes into a Pressure line, it is surely Output (the fortune spirit) showing fault; the fortune spirit, at the great end, is the Five Blessings and the Tea Feast, and at the small end the Five Sages and the Five Roads; in judging ghosts and spirits one must examine the lightness or weight of the source of the illness, the heavy being a great god and the light a small haunting; look further at the Vermilion Bird on the Resource line changing into a Pressure line, which is surely a vow made in a former year; if it sits in the Dui hexagram, it is again an offence against the Heavenly Bureau.
- **LY.ILL.EL.40** 父母爻在内发动，才断祠堂里的宗相；父母爻在外发动，可以说是外族的高亲；财爻在内宫动，必是妻子的魂、妾的魄；妻财在外卦摇动，不是奴仆就是情人；兄弟发动，是手足之亲、相知的人；子孙发动，是儿孙的鬼，僧道的灵。注说：父爻在内卦以及在亲宫动的，就说是本宗的尊长；父爻居外卦以及在他宫动的，就说是外姓的尊亲；财爻临内卦或在亲宫动的，必是先亡的妻妾；财爻在他宫以及外卦动的，如果不是奴仆，必是恩爱的情人；兄弟动的，或是兄弟、或是姨妹、或是朋友的魂；子孙动的，是儿女、是侄、是女婿，又是僧道的魂。
  The Resource line moving inside is judged the ancestral image of the ancestral hall; the Resource line moving outside may be called a high relative of another clan; Wealth moving in the inner palace is surely a wife's spirit and a concubine's soul; Wealth stirring in the outer trigram is either a servant or a lover; Peer thriving is a kin of hand and foot, an intimate; Output moving is the ghost of sons and grandsons, the spirit of a monk or Daoist.
