  If the Pressure line moves, examine its palace; if the hexagram and lines are all still, seek it in the outer trigram; if there is no ghost, look for the hidden one and examine the flying line above it; if the hidden ghost is also Void, devote oneself to cultivating virtue. The note says: if the Pressure line moves alone, examine the ghost by the eight palaces; if the lines and the ghost are all still, examine it by the outer trigram; if the Pressure line is hidden, examine it by the flying line above the ghost; if the hidden ghost is again Void, there is no haunting by a ghost, and one need only cultivate virtue. This section is solely to examine the ghost, not to judge the spirit.
- **LY.ILL.YM.56** 卦里只有一个官鬼，专门用五行来断；有两个官鬼，专门用六神来断；官鬼发动的，专门用八卦；官鬼乱动的，专门用诸星；官鬼在世爻上的，专门用六爻。注说：卦里有一个官，用五行取象；有两个官，用六神取象，比如下面的官临白虎，就说是武神，上面的官临青龙，就说是树神；有动的鬼，用八宫取象，比如鬼在乾宫动，就说是天神，鬼在坤宫动，就说是地祇；卦爻乱动、或者其中有官在动，就看哪颗星临在官上来取象。这一节是专门断神的。
  With one Pressure line, go by the five phases alone; with two Pressure lines, go by the six spirits alone; with a moving Pressure line, go by the eight trigrams alone; with Pressure lines moving in disorder, go by the various stars alone; with the Pressure line on the Self, go by the six lines alone. The note says: where the hexagram has one Pressure line, take the image by the five phases; where it has two, take the image by the six spirits, so that if the lower one carries the White Tiger it is called a martial spirit, and if the upper one carries the Azure Dragon it is called a tree spirit; where it has a moving ghost, take the image by the eight palaces, so that a ghost moving in Qian is called a heavenly spirit and in Kun an earthly deity; where the lines move in disorder, or a Pressure line among them moves, take the image by whichever star sits on the Pressure line. This section is solely for judging the spirit.
- **LY.ILL.YM.57** 神和鬼虽有分别，考察的办法却没有两样。所以官在乾宫发动，是触犯了天神，是冲犯了寺庙，是年老父辈、僧人的鬼，是王侯宫殿的灵；在坤宫动，是亵渎了地祇，是妨碍了坟墓，是母亲、老妇人的鬼，是皇后妃子的神；震是东方，是九天的神，是道的宗主，是掌管蚕桑舟车的，是受辱折了脚的鬼，是雷火部的威灵；巽是东南，是掌管风浪、镇守大海的神，是花果园林里作祟的，是长女、妇人的魂；坎是北方，在天是北斗，在地是北极，是百川、四海、恒河的灵，它的广大是这样，天下的水神火神，确实不能改易；离是火德，在星是荧惑，对读书人和百姓是香火，对一家是灶神，对骨肉亲人是中女的鬼；艮是山神，是五岳，是荒坟，以及童子的鬼；兑是西方，是佛家的宗主，是咒骂诅咒的孽，是巫祝的欺诳，是和尚尼姑的鬼，是酒色的邪。注说：这是官鬼独自发动、其他卦爻都静时，用八宫的象来断。
  Spirits and ghosts differ, but the way of examining them is not two. So when the Pressure line moves in Qian, a heavenly spirit has been offended, a temple has been struck against, and it is the ghost of an elder or a monk, the spirit of a prince or marquis and his great halls; moving in Kun, the earthly deity has been profaned and the graves obstructed, and it is the ghost of a mother or an old woman, the spirit of an empress or a consort; Zhen is the east: a spirit of the Nine Heavens, the head of the Way, in charge of silkworms and mulberries, boats and carts, the ghost of one who was disgraced or broke a leg, the awesome spirit of the Thunder and Fire department; Xun is the southeast: a spirit in charge of wind and waves and of pacifying the sea, a haunting of flowers, fruit, gardens and groves, the soul of an eldest daughter or a woman; Kan is the north: in heaven the Northern Dipper, on earth the North Pole, the spirit of the hundred rivers, the four seas and the Ganges, so vast is it, and the spirits of water and fire under heaven are firmly beyond change; Li is the virtue of Fire: among the stars Yinghuo, to gentry and commoners the incense fire, in the household the stove, and among kin the ghost of the middle daughter; Gen is the mountain spirit, the Five Peaks, a wild grave, and the ghost of a boy or child; Dui is the west, the head of the Buddhists: the baleful work of curses, the deceit of impersonator and invoker, the ghost of a monk or nun, the evil of wine and lust. The note says: this is judgment by the images of the eight palaces when the Pressure line moves alone and the other lines are all still.
- **LY.ILL.YM.58** 初爻，是井祟，是井里的鬼，是掌管居住的神；二爻，是灶祟，是灶下的亡魂，是掌管境内城邑的神；三爻，是家堂的祟，是闺房里的鬼，是掌管都郡桥埠的神；四爻，是门户的祟，是关隘的神；五爻，是道路的祟，是王侯的神；六爻，是坟墓的祟，是慈悲的尊神，是奴仆的亡魂。世在初爻的，是家神；世在二爻的，是土神；世在三爻，是园神；世在四爻，是五神；世在上爻，是山神。注说：这是卦里得一个鬼，现在哪一爻，以及世爻带鬼落在什么象上，用六爻的象来断。
  The first line: a haunting by the well, a ghost in the well, a spirit in charge of dwellings; the second: a haunting by the stove, one who died beside the stove, a spirit in charge of the district and its town; the third: a haunting of the household shrine, a ghost of the women's quarters, a spirit in charge of cities, prefectures, bridges and wharves; the fourth: a haunting of the doors, a spirit of passes and defiles; the fifth: a haunting of the road, a spirit of princes and marquises; the sixth: a haunting of the graves, a compassionate honoured one, one who died a servant. With the Self on the first line, a household spirit; on the second, an earth spirit; on the third, a garden spirit; on the fourth, the Five Spirits; on the top, a mountain spirit. The note says: where the hexagram has one ghost, on whichever line it appears, and in whatever image the ghost on the Self lies, judgment by the images of the six lines is as above.
- **LY.ILL.YM.59** 青龙的象，是掌管喜庆财利的神，东方的圣贤，文官班列里的师相；是桃李的精怪，是胎产而死的鬼。朱雀也是离的神，掌管词讼、纠察善恶，掌管交易，主管生育，守在端门，权衡中鼎；是言语触犯了天曹，或者是牢里被冤枉的鬼。勾陈是一位土神，掌管禾黍，主管社稷；是工匠巧人的亡魂，是摔断跌伤的鬼。螣蛇属火，是黄帝的左右；是妖邪怪异的骚扰，是飞游砍伐的神，是绳结、惊狂的鬼。白虎是金甲的神，是丧服的祟，是兵灾凶险、老虎横行，被称作元帅的就像它。北方的玄武，是强横的神，是盗贼的鬼，是河里井里死去的，是妓女的魂。注说：卦里有两个鬼，上面的鬼临哪个六神、下面的鬼临哪个六神，用六神的象来断，是这样的。
  The image of the Azure Dragon: a spirit in charge of festivity and gain, a sage of the east, a teacher-minister among the civil ranks; a demon of peach and plum, a ghost of childbirth. The Vermilion Bird is also a spirit of Li: it presides over suits, oversees good and evil, has charge of trade and governs birth, hunts at the Main Gate and weighs the central cauldron; a case of words offending the Heavenly Bureau, or a ghost wrongly imprisoned. Hook Earth is an earth spirit, in charge of millet and grain and governing the altars of soil and grain; the departed spirit of a craftsman, a ghost of breaks and falls. The Coiling Snake belongs to Fire and attends the Yellow Emperor; it is the disturbance of demons and oddities, a spirit of flying wanderers and felling, a ghost of knotted cords, fright and madness. The White Tiger is a golden-armoured spirit, a haunting of mourning dress, the disaster of weapons and the rampage of tigers; those called marshals are its image. The Dark Tortoise of the north is a violent spirit, a ghost of thieves and bandits, one who died in a river or well, the soul of a prostitute. The note says: where the hexagram has two ghosts, whichever spirit the upper one carries and whichever the lower one carries, judgment by the images of the six spirits is as above.
- **LY.ILL.YM.60** 父母爻是祖先，妻财爻是臣妾，兄弟爻是手足纠缠，子孙爻是儿女带来的孽；世是本宗，应是异姓。注说：六亲有动的，用六亲的象来断；只有变出鬼的和独自发动的，尤其厉害；世是本家的鬼，应是别姓的亡魂。
  The Resource line is the ancestors, the Wealth line is servants and concubines, the Peer line is the entanglement of brothers and kin, and the Output line is the affliction of sons and daughters; the Self is the same lineage, and the Other is a different surname. The note says: where a relation line moves, judgment by the images of the six relations is as above; one that changes into a ghost or moves alone is especially severe; the Self is a ghost of one's own house, and the Other is the departed of another surname.
- **LY.ILL.YM.61** 犯了亥、子，就是天将斗神，可以祷告；犯了巳、午，就是火德财神，可以禳解；树神、东岳，是寅、卯的官；佛家的丧车，是申、酉的象；辰、戌是处处都有的土神，丑、未是家家都有的地鬼。注说：十二支的神值在鬼上，就用十二支的象来断，是这样的。
  If it offends Hai and Zi, the Heavenly Generals and the spirit of the Dipper may be prayed to; if it offends Si and Wu, the Fire Virtue and the god of wealth may be exorcised; the tree spirit and the Eastern Peak are officials of Yin and Mao; the Buddhist funeral carriage is the image of Shen and You; Chen and Xu are the earth spirits found everywhere, and Chou and Wei the earth ghosts found in every house. The note says: where a spirit of the twelve branches sits on the ghost, judgment by the images of the twelve branches is as above.
- **LY.ILL.YM.62** 还有官鬼出现在贵人上的，是戴冠冕的神；天喜，是带来生意兴隆、吉庆的神；丧门，是丧服的神；天火、火德、天触、灶司、天贼，是强横劫夺而死的亡魂；天狱，是受笞刑、杖刑、徒刑、流放的鬼；官符主管诉讼，羊刃掌管兵刃，驿马是行商旅客的冤魂，天河是溺水者的冤屈；华盖是僧人道士，咸池是娼妓，暗金是产妇，血衂是血鬼；太岁是土德；刀针是被斩杀、绞死、受伤，坵墓是坟墓的咎，丧车是棺椁的殃，折煞是摔断跌伤的鬼；飞廉是游神，将星是武神，天医是医神，天马是被马踏的；浴盆是婴孩溺死，摧尸是伏尸的祸，孤神是断了后嗣的鬼；太阴、月德从王母那里受福，太阳、天德靠上帝保佑。注说：鬼临哪颗星宿，就用诸星的象来断；天河是水库，是天上浩大的府库，所以从辰起逐月顺行，刀针是金宿，正月应当从乾起，所以从亥起顺行；其余详见诸星章。
  Further, a Pressure line appearing on the Noble is a spirit in cap and robes of office; Heavenly Joy is a spirit of good trade and celebration; Mourning Gate is a spirit of mourning dress; Heavenly Fire, Fire Virtue, Heavenly Touch, Stove Official and Heavenly Thief are the departed of violence and robbery; Heavenly Prison is a ghost of flogging, beating, penal servitude and exile; Official Talisman presides over lawsuits, Goat Blade governs weapons, the Post Horse is the grievance of merchants and travellers, and Heavenly River is the wrong of the drowned; Huagai is monks and Daoists, Xianchi is courtesans, Hidden Metal is women in childbirth, and Bleeding is a blood ghost; the Tai Sui is the Earth Virtue; Blade and Needle is beheading, strangling and wounds, Mounds and Tombs is the fault of the graves, Funeral Carriage is the misfortune of coffins, and Breaking Killing is a ghost of breaks and falls; Feilian is the wandering spirit, the General Star is the martial spirit, Heavenly Doctor is the medicine spirit, and Heavenly Horse is trampling by a horse; Bath Basin is the drowning of infants, Corpse Breaker is the calamity of the buried corpse, and Lone Spirit is a ghost with no heir; Great Yin and Month Virtue receive blessing from the Queen Mother, the Sun and Heavenly Virtue depend on the protection of the Supreme Lord. The note says: whichever star the ghost falls on, judgment by the images of the various stars is as above; Heavenly River is a water store, the vast treasury of heaven, and so it runs forward month by month starting from Chen; Blade and Needle is a Metal lodge, and the first month should start from Qian, so it begins at Hai and runs forward; the rest is detailed in the chapter on the stars.
- **LY.ILL.YM.63** 天地四方之内，没有哪里没有鬼神，但各地方各有风俗所尊奉的，地水火风所掌管的，有的只显灵在一个乡，有的应验在一个国，很难一一细说。俗话说，千古以前还没有的神，怎么知道；千古以后再成的神，又拿什么去比拟。考察鬼神，只要用五行、八宫，变化着去取象，作为后学的规范，古今都能通用。如果一定要落实指定是哪位鬼神、还死抠着不放，那就是诬妄了。
  Within the four vaults of heaven there is no place without ghosts and spirits, yet each quarter has those its customs revere and those that earth, water, fire and wind govern, some manifest in a single village, some proving true over a whole country, hard to set out one by one. It is said: of spirits that did not exist a thousand ages ago, how can they be known; of spirits that will come to be a thousand ages hence, what can they be compared to. In examining ghosts and spirits, simply take the image by the five phases and the eight palaces with their changes, as a pattern for later learners, and it applies to past and present alike. To point insistently at a particular ghost or spirit and cling to it is to make a false claim.
- **LY.ILL.YM.64** 大体上神多半是保佑的，鬼多半是作祟的；灾病的到来，多半是自己造的孽。如果不洗心悔过、除掉孽的根本，只是聚集巫师、摆上牲畜去讨好鬼神，鬼神未必享用。何况宰杀生灵来为自己的性命祈祷，那是加重自己的罪孽。所以说：祭祀没有比解禳更好的，解禳没有比修身更好的，说的就是这个。我在鬼神这一章里，再三告诫。
  In general spirits mostly bless and ghosts mostly haunt; for calamity and illness mostly come from one's own doing, and if one does not cleanse the heart and repent to remove the root of the fault, but merely gathers shamans and lays out animal victims to please ghosts and spirits, the ghosts and spirits will not necessarily partake. How much more so to roast and slaughter living creatures to pray for my own life: that only makes the fault heavier. Hence it is said: of sacrifice none is better than exorcism, and of exorcism none is better than cultivating oneself; this is what it means. In this chapter on ghosts and spirits I have given warning again and again.
- **LY.ILL.YY.27** 以官鬼为用爻：卦里没有鬼，或者鬼遇到空、绝，就没有鬼崇。官鬼来生、合世爻和用爻的，适合祈祷；官爻持着世爻、用爻的，先祈祷后医治；鬼发动刑害克冲世爻、用爻，而有福神发动来救助的，适合禳解保佑；世爻、用爻持着财、又遇火鬼发动的，适合大家一起保祷。问祈禳的是哪一路神鬼：阳鬼是神，阴官是鬼；鬼带文书、贵人的，是神。
  The Pressure line is the Subject: if there is no ghost in the hexagram, or the ghost meets Void or Extinction, there are no ghostly influences. If the Pressure line generates or combines with the Self and the Subject, prayer is fitting; if Pressure holds the Self and Subject, pray first and treat afterwards; if the ghost moves to punish, harm, control or clash the Self and Subject and Fortune moves to rescue and help, exorcism and protection suit; if the Self and Subject hold Wealth and a Fire ghost moves, a communal rite of protection suits. To ask which spirit or ghost the prayer is for: a yang ghost is a spirit and a yin Pressure line is a ghost; a ghost carrying the Document and the Noble is a spirit.
- **LY.ILL.YY.28** 按五行和阴阳分神和鬼：阳金鬼是庵堂、关圣，带白虎的是七煞；阴金鬼是死于刀伤、自刎、痨病、喘嗽、虎伤的魂。阳木鬼是东岳、五圣、家先香火；阴木鬼是自缢、疯病、跌扑、被杖刑打死的魂。阳水鬼是观音、真武、三官、水神、张神、河泊、水官；阴水鬼是投河、喝卤水、呕血而死的魂。阳火鬼是玄坛、五福、火神、灶司的香火；阴火鬼是死于疮毒、痨病、瘟疫、带血、心痛、烧死的魂。阳土鬼是城隍、土谷、太岁、皮场大王和土神；阴土鬼是死于瘟疫、咽喉病、臌胀、黄疸、被压死的魂。
  Spirits and ghosts by element and by yin and yang: a yang Metal ghost is the temples and halls and Guan Sheng, and with the Tiger the Seven Killings; a yin Metal ghost is the souls of those who died of blade wounds, cutting their own throats, consumption, wheezing cough or a tiger's bite. A yang Wood ghost is the Eastern Peak, the Five Sages and the incense of family forebears; a yin Wood ghost the souls of those who hanged themselves or died of madness, falls and blows, or a beating with the rod. A yang Water ghost is Guanyin, Zhenwu, the Three Officials, the water spirits, the Zhang spirit, the Lord of the River and the Water Official; a yin Water ghost the souls of those who threw themselves into a river, drank brine or vomited blood. A yang Fire ghost is the incense of the Dark Altar, the Five Blessings, the Fire God and the Kitchen Official; a yin Fire ghost the souls of those who died of sores and poison, consumption, epidemic, bleeding, heart pain or burning. A yang Earth ghost is the City God, the Earth and Grain gods, the Tai Sui, the Great King of the Hide Market and earth spirits; a yin Earth ghost the souls of those who died of epidemic, throat disease, swelling, jaundice or being crushed.
- **LY.ILL.YY.29** 看鬼带的煞：鬼带金、白虎、刑煞的，是死于非命；鬼落在游魂卦的外卦的，是客死他乡；带天火、天烛、独火煞的，是烧死；带天贼、天盗、劫煞的，是因盗致死；带沐浴、咸池煞的，是奸淫、娼妓；带木狼、天缢煞的，是缢死；带风波、浴盆、浮沉煞的，是溺死；带羊刃、刀砧煞的，是屠夫刽子手的鬼；带暗金、血刃煞的，是产亡的鬼；带天刑、天狱、地狱、牢狱、入狱煞的，是牢狱的鬼；带刀砧、羊刃、劫煞的，是自刎的鬼；带华盖、孤神的，是僧道、绝了后的鬼；带螣蛇、天怪煞的，是精怪妖邪。鬼落在太岁上的，是当年的岁君；落在月、日、时上的，是游荡在野外的神。
  By the stars the ghost carries: a ghost carrying Metal, the Tiger and punishing stars is one who died an unnatural death; a ghost in the outer trigram of a Wandering Soul hexagram, one who died a stranger in a far land; with Heavenly Fire, Heavenly Candle and Lone Fire, burned to death; with Heavenly Thief, Heavenly Robber and Robbery-Killing, dead through robbers; with Bath and Xianchi, adultery and prostitution; with Wood Wolf and Heavenly Hanging, hanged; with Wind-and-Waves, Bath Basin and Floating-Sinking, drowned; with Goat Blade and Chopping Block, a butcher or executioner ghost; with Hidden Metal and Blood Blade, a ghost who died in childbirth; with Heavenly Punishment, Heavenly Prison, Earthly Prison, Jail and Entering-Prison, a jail ghost; with Chopping Block, Goat Blade and Robbery-Killing, a ghost who cut his own throat; with Canopy and Lone Spirit, a monk or Daoist, one who left no heir; with the Coiling Snake and Heavenly Monster, a sprite, monster or evil. A ghost on the Tai Sui is the Year Lord of the year, and one on the Month, Day or hour a wandering field spirit.
- **LY.ILL.YY.30** 看是谁的鬼：父母爻下伏着鬼、或变出鬼的，阳爻是父亲，阴爻是母亲；兄弟爻下伏鬼、变鬼的，阳爻是兄弟，阴爻是姐妹；妻财爻下伏鬼、变鬼的，阳爻是妻子和奴仆，阴爻是妾和婢女；子孙爻下伏鬼、变鬼的，阳爻是男的，阴爻是女的。鬼在本宫内卦的，是同姓的鬼；在别的宫、外卦的，是异姓的鬼。发动的是大鬼，不动的是小鬼；衰弱、入墓的是年老的旧鬼，生旺的是壮年的新鬼，胎、养是孩童的鬼（阳男阴女）。按八卦：乾宫的鬼是祖宗和父亲，坤宫的鬼是祖母和母亲，震、坎、艮宫的鬼是男的，巽、离、兑宫的鬼是女的。问有几个鬼，按一水、二火、三木、四金、五土的数来推，旺相的加倍，休的照原数，囚、死的减半。
  Whose ghost it is: Resource with a ghost hidden beneath it or changing into a ghost — yang is the father, yin the mother; Peer with a ghost hidden beneath or changing into one — yang brothers, yin sisters; Wealth with a ghost — yang a wife or manservant, yin a concubine or maid; Output with a ghost — yang male, yin female. A ghost in the inner trigram of the home palace is a ghost of the same surname, and one in the outer trigram of another palace a ghost of another surname. A moving ghost is a great ghost and a still one a small ghost; weak or in a tomb, an old ghost of long standing; born or strong, a ghost of the prime of life, newly dead; at Womb or Nurture, a child's ghost (yang a boy, yin a girl). By the eight trigrams: a ghost in the Qian palace is an ancestor or the father, in Kun a grandmother or the mother, in Zhen, Kan or Gen male, in Xun, Li or Dui female. To ask how many ghosts there are, work by the numbers Water one, Fire two, Wood three, Metal four, Earth five: if strong, double it; if resting, as given; if trapped or dead, halve it.
- **LY.ILL.YY.31** 曹子虚说：祈禳用什么祭品礼数，要看鬼的食神。鬼属甲，食神在丙，禄在巳，用炒鸡、煎豆腐、酒、礼、纸钱纸马送走；鬼属乙，食神在丁，禄在午，用干肉脯、炒豆、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属丙，食神在戊，禄在巳，同属甲的祭品；属丁，食神在己，禄在午，同属乙的祭品；属戊，食神在庚，禄在申，用三牲、馒头、果饼、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属己，食神在辛，禄在酉，用鸡肉、鱼、馄饨、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属庚，食神在壬，禄在亥，用猪头、三牲、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属辛，食神在癸，禄在子，用池里的鱼、血羹、麦面、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属壬，食神在甲，禄在寅，用三牲、时鲜的果子、新蔬菜、酒、礼、纸钱纸马；属癸，食神在乙，禄在卯，用鸡、鹅、鸭、蔬果、鸭蛋、酒、礼、纸钱纸马。送到哪个方向，以鬼爻的地支来定。如果是神，祈祷的礼数把子孙当作福神：子孙带青龙、朱雀的，适合素祭；带勾陈、螣蛇、白虎、玄武的，适合荤盘；子孙伏在水鬼或水父母下、或变出水鬼水父母的，适合念佛经；伏在火鬼或火父母下、或变出火鬼火父母的，适合道教的醮；子孙变出财、或伏在财下的，适合福礼；变出兄、或伏在兄下的，适合演戏、大家一起保祷。这是祈禳的占法。
  Cao Zixu says: what sacrifice and rite to use for prayer and exorcism depends on the ghost's food-star. If the ghost belongs to Jia, the food-star is Bing and the emolument is at Si, and one sends it off with stir-fried chicken, fried bean curd, wine, ritual gifts and paper money and horses; Yi: food-star Ding, emolument at Wu, dried meat, fried beans, wine, gifts and paper money and horses; Bing: food-star Wu, emolument at Si, as for Jia; Ding: food-star Ji, emolument at Wu, as for Yi; Wu: food-star Geng, emolument at Shen, the three sacrificial animals, steamed buns, fruit cakes, wine and gifts; Ji: food-star Xin, emolument at You, chicken, fish, wonton, wine and gifts; Geng: food-star Ren, emolument at Hai, a pig's head, the three animals, wine and gifts; Xin: food-star Gui, emolument at Zi, pond fish, blood soup, wheat noodles, wine and gifts; Ren: food-star Jia, emolument at Yin, the three animals, seasonal fruit, fresh vegetables, wine and gifts; Gui: food-star Yi, emolument at Mao, chicken, goose, duck, vegetables and fruit, duck eggs, wine and gifts. The direction in which to send it is fixed by the branch of the Pressure line. If it is a spirit, in the rites of prayer Output is taken as the spirit of blessing: Output carrying the Dragon and the Bird, a vegetarian sacrifice; carrying the Hook, Snake, Tiger and Tortoise, a meat platter; hidden beneath a Water ghost or Water Resource, or changing into one, Buddhist scripture; beneath a Fire ghost or Fire Resource, or changing into one, a Daoist ritual; changing into or beneath Wealth, a blessing rite; changing into or beneath Peer, a staged play and communal protection. This is the divination of prayer and exorcism.
- **LY.ILL.YY.32** 又有旧愿没还的：子孙伏在鬼下面、发动克世爻、用爻，或者子孙伏在岁煞下面、发动伤世爻、用爻的，一定是有旧愿没有还；旺相的愿大，休囚的愿小。问是因为什么许的愿，以官鬼为用：鬼伏在世爻下、用爻下的，是为自己许的愿；鬼伏在父母、财、子孙下的，是为父母、妻子、子女许的愿；鬼伏在鬼下的，是因为病、官司许的愿；鬼伏在兄下的，是为兄弟朋友、争斗赌博许的愿。六亲变出鬼的也是这样（出自《源髓诀》）。
  There is also an old vow not yet paid: Output hidden beneath the ghost and moving to control the Self and Subject, or Output hidden beneath the Year-killing star and moving to harm the Self and Subject, means surely an old vow is not yet paid; if strong the vow is large, if resting or trapped small. To ask why the vow was made, the Pressure line is the Subject: a ghost hidden beneath the Self or the Subject line, a vow made for oneself; hidden beneath Resource, Wealth or Output, made for parents, wife or children; hidden beneath a ghost, made over illness or lawsuit; hidden beneath Peer, made over brothers, friends, brawls or gambling. The same holds when a relation changes out into a ghost (from the Yuan Sui Jue).
- **LY.ILL.ZS.23** 占鬼神，卦里的鬼要是休囚，或者空、破、墓、绝，都不是鬼神在作祟。鬼爻旺相是神，休囚衰弱是鬼：临金，旺相是武神和西方的神，衰弱是死于刀剑的鬼；临木，旺相是文神和东方的神，衰弱是依草附木的妖、或被杖责悬梁而死的鬼；临水，旺相是河海和北方的神，衰弱是死在池井江湖里的鬼；临火，旺相是火神和南方的神、雷公电母、窖灶的神，衰弱是被汤火烧死的鬼；临土，旺相是土神和中央的神、掌管山川社稷的神，衰弱是墙倒屋塌压死的鬼。
  In divining ghosts and spirits, if the Pressure line in the hexagram is resting or trapped, or Void, broken, in a tomb or extinct, it is not the harm of ghosts or spirits. A strong Pressure line is a god and a weak one a ghost: on Metal, if strong a martial god and a god of the west, if weak a ghost that died by sword or blade; on Wood, if strong a civil god and a god of the east, if weak a goblin clinging to grass and trees or a ghost that died by the rod or by hanging from a beam; on Water, if strong a god of river and sea and of the north, if weak a ghost that drowned in a pond, well, river or lake; on Fire, if strong the god of fire and a god of the south, the Duke of Thunder and Lady Lightning, the gods of the cellar and the stove, if weak a ghost that died by scalding or burning; on Earth, if strong the god of the soil and a god of the centre and a god in charge of mountains, rivers and the altars of the land and grain, if weak a ghost that died buried under a fallen wall or collapsed house.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.24** 古时候按五行分是哪路神，比如金官就说是天将、关公、金刚、伍公、岳公这些武神，觉子认为不对：武神有朝廷敕封的、有没有封的，各地风俗又不同，书里没记的更多，不可能一座庙一座庙地祭。该问病人是不是在哪座武神庙里许了愿没有还、或者在哪座庙里弄脏糟蹋了；凡是心里怀疑哪位神，就指着那位神占一卦，鬼爻旺或发动就是这位神，不上卦再占别处的神。鬼临木火水土的，都这样办。得罪了正神的，用香花纸马祭；招惹了邪祟的鬼，用浆水和纸钱，夜深人静时送走，金鬼向西送，水鬼向北送。
  In ancient times the gods were told apart by the five phases, for example a Metal Pressure was said to be a heavenly general, Guan Gong, a Vajra, Wu Gong, Yue Gong and the other martial gods; Juezi thinks this wrong: martial gods include those enfeoffed by imperial decree and those not, local customs differ, and those not recorded in books are still more, so one cannot sacrifice at temple after temple. One should ask the patient whether he vowed something in the temple of some martial god and has not paid it, or defiled and abused some temple; whatever god he suspects in his heart, point to that god and divine, and if the Pressure line is strong or moving it is that god, and if it is not in the hexagram divine the god of some other place. Pressure on Wood, Fire, Water or Earth is treated in the same way. If he has offended an orthodox god, sacrifice with incense, flowers and paper horses; if he has provoked an evil haunting ghost, use rice-water and paper money and send it off in the stillness of the night, a Metal ghost to the west and a Water ghost to the north.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.25** 祭送鬼神，对时行灾病、瘟疫、癖积一类的病有用（野鹤多次验过）；疯、痨、气、蛊是自己没有调养好、酒色伤了身，和鬼神没有关系，祭送没有益处，该做的是节制饮食、远离色欲、平息怒气、养护精神。有病不吃药、专靠杀牲祭神的，反而害了性命。李我平批评有的书指定哪位神（关帝、岳王、关圣），又说祭了降福、不祭降灾，那是作威作福的邪神。
  Sacrificing and sending off the ghosts and spirits is of use for seasonal calamities, epidemics and hard lumps (the Old Man of Yehe verified it many times); madness, consumption, qi disorder and drum-belly come from failing to look after oneself and from wine and lust damaging the body, have nothing to do with ghosts and spirits, and gain nothing from sacrifice, what is to be done being to moderate food, keep away from lust, calm anger and nourish the spirit. Those who take no medicine when ill and rely wholly on slaughtering animals to the gods only harm life. Li Woping criticises books that name a particular god (Emperor Guan, King Yue, the Sage Guan) and say that those who sacrifice are blessed and those who do not are sent calamity, which he calls evil gods who lord it and dispense favour.
- **LY.ILL.ZSZ.07** 周易尚占八卦断例里有三个卦的断辞把病和鬼神联在一起：巽卦「惟占病人，金神为崇」，占病人的时候是金神（白虎）在作祟；旅卦「病者祷禳」，病人要祷告、禳解；临卦「病犯崇侵」，病是撞上了鬼神作祟的侵扰。
  In the judgments by example of the Zhouyi Shangzhan three hexagrams link illness with spirits: Xun, 'only in divining a sick person: a Metal spirit (the White Tiger) is the curse'; Lü (the Traveller), 'the sick should pray and perform exorcism'; Lin, 'illness meets the encroachment of a curse'.

## 他人病(Whose illness: parents, children, husband or wife, brothers, servants)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/他人病.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSQS.14** 占家宅的歌里说：财爻发动，妻子有病；再遇丧门，暴病而死。旧注：丧门的取法是正月未、二月辰、三月丑、四月戌，四个位置轮着用到十二个月；丧门在内卦三爻发动，主这家一定有人暴病而死。
  The verse on dwellings says: when Wealth moves, the wife is ill; if the Mourning Gate is met as well, sudden illness and death. The old note: the Mourning Gate is placed at Wei in the first month, Chen in the second, Chou in the third and Xu in the fourth, the four places turning through the twelve months; if the Mourning Gate is in the inner three lines and moves, someone in the household is sure to die of a sudden illness.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.15** 占家宅的歌里说：动爻从阴变阳，女子有忧（旧注：女子有病）；从阳变阴，损伤男子（旧注：男子有灾）。
  The verse on dwellings says: when a moving line turns from yin into yang, there is worry for the woman (the old note: the woman is ill); when it turns from yang into yin, the man is harmed (the old note: the man has calamity).
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.16** 占家宅的歌里说：子孙发动，有喜事，没有官非，也没有灾病和盗贼；兄弟发动，耗财，妻妾有病，是非和谋望又多阻隔。
  The verse on dwellings says: when Output moves, there is joy, no official trouble, and no calamity, illness or thieves either; when Peer moves, money is spent, the wife and concubines fall ill, and quarrels and plans meet many obstacles.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.18** 六亲章里讲病的两处：父母爻带着杀神，又和朱雀同时并在一起，重病怎么还能起得了床；妻财犯了勾陈，妻子有病灾。
  Two places on illness in the chapter on the six relations: when the Resource line carries a killing spirit and is joined by the Vermilion Bird, how can a grave illness ever rise from bed; when Wealth offends Hook Earth, the wife has illness and misfortune.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.19** 占行人的歌里说：官鬼爻和兄弟爻一起发动，多半应在行人生病；官鬼化出官鬼，更不吉祥。
  The verse on travellers says: when the Pressure line and the Peer line move together, it mostly points to the traveller falling ill; Pressure turning into Pressure is still more inauspicious.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.23** 断易通玄赋里说：阳爻动，男子受阻滞；阴爻动，女子有灾殃。火珠林的旧注讲得明白：问病的事，遇到阳爻发动，主男人有病；阴爻发动，主女人有灾。
  The Duanyi Tongxuan Fu says: when a yang line moves the man is held up; when a yin line moves the woman meets misfortune. The old note in the Huozhu Lin is explicit: in asking about illness, if a yang line moves, the man is ill; if a yin line moves, the woman has calamity.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.25** 占家宅的一篇说：父母、子孙都旺，老人平安，年轻人康健；螣蛇、白虎安静，灾不生，祸不作。
  The essay on divining a house says: when Resource and Output are both strong, the old are at peace and the young are healthy; when the Coiling Snake and White Tiger are still, calamity does not arise and misfortune does not happen.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.27** 柳神经里讲家里会有什么样的病人：父爻落在墓上，家里多有生病的人；火爻下面伏着鬼，一定生出眼病的人；水位下面藏着官，一定有又瞎又聋的人；披头煞发动，要出疯癫的人；五鬼煞兴起，一定生出眼睛看不见的人。
  The Liushen Jing on what kind of sick person the household will have: with the Resource line on a tomb, the household has many sick people; with a ghost hidden under a Fire line, someone with an eye disease is sure to appear; with an officer hidden in the Water place, there will surely be people blind or deaf; when the Loosened Hair star moves, a mad person will appear; when the Five Ghosts rise, someone with darkened eyes is sure to be born.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.30** 搜精六爻说：（初爻）克第五爻，是一家之长有病；克第二爻，是宅母有危险。第六爻，白虎带着鬼爻又旺，灾病牵连到祖父母。
  The essay on searching out the essence of the six lines says: [the first line] controlling the fifth line, the head of the household is ill; controlling the second line, the mistress of the house is in danger. On the sixth line, with the White Tiger carrying a strong ghost, calamity and illness reach the grandparents.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.54** 搜精六爻讲第三爻（门户）时说：龙德、贵人临在上面，添上新的喜事、生出新的门户；劫杀和鬼爻互相干犯，兄弟们疾病受苦。
  On the third line (the gate and doors) the essay on searching out the essence of the six lines says: with Dragon Virtue and the Noble on it, new joys are added and a new household arises; with the Robbery killing and the ghost interfering with each other, the brothers suffer from illness.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.02** 易卦发蒙歌的自注给了三条六亲发动时要担心的事：父母爻动，担心子孙；财爻动、又合上杀，担心父母生病；兄弟爻动，忌妻妾的疾病。
  The notes to the Yigua Fameng Ge give three things to worry about when a relation line moves: when the Resource line moves, worry for Output (the children); when the Wealth line moves and joins a killing, worry that the Resource (the parents) will fall ill; when the Peer line moves, the illness of wife and concubine is to be feared.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.09** 推占来情的歌说：卦身在财的位置上、想求财，也要担心父母和妻子的灾，变进空亡奴婢会逃，要知道生死，占的是新来的事。自注说：财爻持世，或在别的爻上动，主妻妾有灾、要担心父母；落了空亡又带杀，主有丧亡的事；一串担忧是：财爻动担心父母，父母爻动担心子孙，子孙爻动担心官事，官鬼爻动担心兄弟，兄弟爻动担心失财和妻妾，这是取五行相克的道理。
  The verses on reading the purpose of the asker say: with the Body at the place of Wealth, wishing to seek wealth, one must also worry for the parents and for the wife's calamity; if it changes into Void, the servants run off; to know life and death, it is a divination of what has newly come. Its own note says: when the Wealth line holds the Self, or moves on another line, it means calamity for wife and concubine and worry for the parents; if it falls Void and carries a killing, it means a death; and the chain of worries is: when the Wealth line moves, worry for the Resource; when the Resource line moves, worry for Output; when the Output line moves, worry for official affairs; when the Pressure line moves, worry for Peer; when the Peer line moves, worry for loss of wealth and for wife and concubine; this takes the sense of control among the five phases.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.34** 「何知」口诀里关于亲人病灾的六问：父母有病，是白虎临着（父母）这一爻，再加刑克；父母有灾殃，是财爻发动、煞神来伤；子孙有病，是父母爻发动来克制子孙；子孙有灾，卜筮大全说是白虎落到福德（子孙）上，卜筮正宗说是父母落到福德上；小孩要死，是子孙落空亡再加白虎；兄弟去世，是用神落空亡又被白虎所伤。
  Six questions of the 'how to know' rhyme about illness and calamity among kin. A parent is ill when the White Tiger sits on the (Resource) line together with punishment and control; a parent meets calamity when the Wealth line moves and a killing spirit wounds; a child is ill when the Resource line moves and controls Output; a child meets misfortune when, in the Bushi Daquan, the White Tiger arrives upon Output, or, in the Bushi Zhengzong, Resource arrives upon Output; a small child will die when Output is Void and the White Tiger is added; a brother dies when the Subject falls into Void and the White Tiger wounds it.
- **LY.ILL.CA.05** 占病先要问是哪一层亲人，吉凶必须问清楚。父母爻空亡，要防父母；财爻无气，妻子受损；子孙化成鬼，必定遭死；兄弟爻上有杀，一定不吉。
  In divining illness first ask which of the relations it is; good and ill must be made clear. Resource Void: guard the parents; the Wealth line without qi: the wife is harmed; Output changing into a ghost will meet death; Peer with a killing sitting on it is surely not steadfast.
- **LY.ILL.CA.07** 子占父母的病：什么时候好？子孙要兴隆，父母要安。兄动，缠绵难脱体；子孙兴、财发，入黄泉。父占子病：兄兴、子动，死里逃生；鬼摇、父动，终究难保；财动，缠绵，病反增。夫问妻的病：财明、子动，便安然；鬼爻发动，还沉重；父发、兄摇，定断弦。妻占夫的病：鬼静、财兴，可以放心；兄、子交重，恩义断绝；父摇，难愈，病沉沉。兄弟来占病：父兴、兄静，自然安全；鬼摇、财动，黄泉客；子动，留连几日卧床。奴婢的病：鬼摇，病更连绵；财安、子发，没有别的事；父动、兄兴，不得痊愈。
  A son divining a parent's illness, when will it be cured? Output must thrive and Resource must be at peace; Peer moving, it drags on and is hard to shake off; Output rising and Wealth moving, it enters the Yellow Springs. A parent divining a child's illness: Peer rising and Output moving, one dies and yet lives; the ghost shaking and Resource moving, in the end he cannot be kept; Wealth moving, it drags on and the illness increases. A husband asking of his wife's illness: Wealth clear and Output moving, at once she is at ease; the Pressure line moving, it is still heavy; Resource issuing and Peer shaking, surely the string is cut. A wife divining her husband's illness: the ghost still and Wealth rising, one may set the heart at rest; Peer and Output crossing heavily, love and duty are cut; Resource shaking, hard to cure, the illness deep. Brothers coming to divine an illness: Resource rising and Peer still, of itself safe; the ghost shaking and Wealth moving, a guest of the Yellow Springs; Output moving, it lingers several days in bed. A servant's illness: the ghost shaking, the illness drags on the more; Wealth at peace and Output issuing, no other matter; Resource moving and Peer rising, no recovery.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.05** 问病一定要分清六亲，吉凶要弄分明。父母爻空亡，要防父母；财爻无气，损伤妻子的身体；子孙化为鬼，必定遭死；兄弟带煞临上，一定不贞。
  In asking after illness be sure to tell the six relations apart and sort out good and ill clearly. Resource Void: guard the parents; the Wealth line without qi harms the wife's person; Output changing into a ghost will meet death; a Peer with a killing sitting on it is surely not firm.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.08** 按病人和占卜人的关系分六首：子占父病，鬼要兴隆、父要安，兄动缠绵难脱体，子孙兴、财发就入黄泉；父占子病，兄兴子旺死里逃生，鬼摇父动终难保，财动病反增；夫占妻病，财明子动便安然，鬼爻发动更沉重，父发兄摇妻亡；妻占夫病，鬼静财兴可放心，兄弟交重恩义绝，父摇难愈；占兄弟病，父兴兄静病回生，鬼摇财动是黄泉客，子动躺几天；占奴婢病，鬼摇更缠绵，财安子发无事，父动兄兴不再生。
  Six poems divided by the relation between the sick person and the diviner. A son divining a father's illness: the ghost must thrive and Resource must be at peace; Peer moving, it drags on and is hard to shake off; Output rising and Wealth moving, it enters the Yellow Springs. A father divining a son's illness: Peer rising and Output strong, he dies and yet lives; the ghost shaking and Resource moving, in the end he cannot be kept; Wealth moving, the illness grows the more. A husband divining his wife's illness: Wealth clear and Output moving, at once she is at ease; the Pressure line moving, it grows heavier still; Resource issuing and Peer shaking, surely the string is cut. A wife divining her husband's illness: the ghost still and Wealth rising, one may set the heart at rest; Peer crossing heavily, love and duty are cut; Resource shaking, hard to cure. Divining a brother's illness: Resource rising and Peer still, the illness turns and he lives; the ghost shaking and Wealth moving, a guest of the Yellow Springs; Output moving, he lingers several days in bed. Divining a servant's illness: the ghost shaking, it drags on the more; Wealth at peace and Output issuing, no other matter; Resource moving and Peer rising, he does not live again.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.39** 父母爻的用处：占双亲的疾病，父母爻是用爻，宜旺不宜空，空就必危；占子孙，父母爻是恶杀，宜静不宜动，动就必死。子孙爻的用处：占病，子孙是医药，当权发动，病就安；卦里没有它，服药无效；占小孩和奴仆，子孙是用爻，不落空亡，终究没有大害。官鬼爻的用处：占兄弟的病，官鬼是克杀，旺相发动，病必危险；占身命，遇凶必定丧亡、疾病。
  The uses of the Resource line: divining the illness of the parents, the Resource line is the Subject line, and it should be strong and not Void, and Void means peril; divining children, the Resource line is an evil killing, and it should be still and not move, and moving means death. The uses of the Output line: divining illness, Output is the medicine, and if it holds power and moves the illness is at ease; if it is not in the hexagram, medicine has no effect; divining small children and servants, Output is the Subject line, and if it does not fall Void there is in the end no great harm. The uses of the Pressure line: divining a brother's illness, the Pressure line is the controlling killing, and if strong and moving the illness is surely dangerous; divining the person's fortune, meeting ill surely means death and illness.
- **LY.ILL.EL.02** 哪个爻变出鬼，是哪个人犯的；哪个人的本命落在官鬼上，是哪个人遭殃；再看六亲里谁受克，轻重就能判断得分毫不差。再说全家有没有人生病，要去找用象：父爻变出鬼，灾落到父母身上；兄弟变出官，祸延及兄弟姐妹；子孙变出官爻，灾殃从儿女来；财爻的用象，病在妻子和仆人；世爻变出官爻，病落在自己身上；应位是鬼，妻妾不安宁。再查每个人的本命，落在官鬼上的，一定成灾，比如占得某卦，午火临官，就说属马的人受患；鬼如果空亡，不这样断，要再看六亲的象，不能受伤。财爻动，祸事忧及长辈；鬼兴旺，灾殃波及兄弟；兄弟摇动，妻子生病；父爻动，儿女有灾；如果妻子来占，子孙动，丈夫受患；其余占问，子孙兴旺都是平安。问有没有病，卦里忌神不动、鬼不发动、用神不遇旬空月破，就说平安。
  Whichever line changes into a ghost, that person has offended; whichever natal branch sits on the Pressure line, that person suffers; moreover see which of the six relations is controlled, and severity can be judged to the finest degree. Again, to judge whether anyone in the whole household falls ill, one must find the Subject image: if the Resource line changes into a ghost, calamity reaches the father and mother; if Peer changes into Pressure, misfortune spreads to brothers and sisters; if Output changes into a Pressure line, misfortune comes through the children; for the Wealth image, the illness lies with the wife and servants; if the Self changes into a Pressure line, the illness falls on oneself; if the Other's place is the ghost, wife and concubines are unsettled. Then check each person's natal branch: whoever's falls on the Pressure line will surely suffer calamity, so that if a certain hexagram is obtained and Wu Fire sits on the Pressure line, one says the person born in the year of the Horse suffers; if the ghost is Void, do not judge this way, but look further at the image of the six relations, which must not be harmed. Wealth moving, misfortune worries the elders; the ghost thriving, calamity reaches the brothers; the Peer stirring, the wife falls ill; the Resource moving, the children have calamity; if the wife divines and Output moves, the husband suffers; in all other questions Output thriving means peace. To ask whether there is illness: if the Adversary is still, the ghost does not move, and the Subject does not meet Void or Month-break, say all is well.
- **LY.ILL.EL.18** 替别人问，看应爻，如果临月破最难逃过；遇冲遇克，人难救；逢旺逢生，病必消；生应的元神宜发动，克它的忌象怕交重；卦身有气，还是吉；应位遇上官鬼，祸必招来。注说：替别人占病，应作用爻，如果临月破、旬空，他的命难保；应如果衰弱，又遭动象或日月来伤、或应变成墓绝，就主凶危；应即使休囚，如果变为生旺、或有动爻和日月相扶，命还有救。凡是生应的爻是元神，宜动；克应的象是忌客，宜空。鬼临在应上，病一定难痊。再察卦身，也不可临月破，又不可成墓绝之乡；卦身或墓、或绝于月建之中，都是凶兆。
  In asking for another, look at the Other line; if it sits in Month-break, it is hardest to escape; meeting clash or control, the person is hard to save; meeting strength or generation, the illness surely dissolves; the Support that generates the Other should move, and the adversary image that controls it fears moving; if the Body has qi it is still auspicious; if Pressure meets the place of the Other, misfortune is sure to be invited. The commentary says: in divining another's illness, the Other is the Subject line; if it sits in Month-break or Void, his life is hard to keep; if the Other is weak and is wounded by a moving image or by the Day and Month, or the Other changes into a tomb or Extinction, it means danger; though the Other is resting or trapped, if it changes into generation or strength, or a moving line and the Day and Month support it, life can still be saved. Whatever generates the Other is the Support and should move; whatever controls the Other is the adversary guest and should be Void. With the ghost on the Other, the illness is surely hard to cure. Then examine the Body: it must not sit in Month-break, nor become a place of tomb or Extinction; if the Body is in a tomb or in Extinction under the Month, both are ominous signs.
- **LY.ILL.EL.21** 占父母及家主尊长之类，取父母爻为用神，官鬼为元神，妻财为忌神，子孙为仇神，兄弟为泄气；占兄弟朋友之类，兄弟为用神，父母为元神，官鬼为忌神，妻财为仇神，子孙为泄气；占子孙卑幼之类，子孙为用神，兄弟为元神，父母为忌神，官鬼为仇神，妻财为泄气；占妻妾、弟媳子媳奴婢之类，妻财为用神，子孙为元神，兄弟为忌神，父母为仇神，官鬼为泄气；占丈夫、官员之类，官鬼为用神，妻财为元神，子孙为忌神，兄弟为仇神，父母为泄气；占自己取世作用爻，生世的为元神，克世的为忌神；占他人取应作用爻，生应的为元神，克应的为忌神；这个办法不光占病，凡是看卦，没有不用它的。
  In divining a parent, or the master of the house or an elder, take the Resource line as the Subject, the Pressure line as the Support, the Wealth line as the Adversary, Output as the enemy spirit and Peer as the drain; in divining a brother or friend, Peer is the Subject, Resource the Support, Pressure the Adversary, Wealth the enemy spirit and Output the drain; in divining a son or a junior, Output is the Subject, Peer the Support, Resource the Adversary, Pressure the enemy spirit and Wealth the drain; in divining a wife, a concubine, a sister-in-law, a daughter-in-law or a maid, Wealth is the Subject, Output the Support, Peer the Adversary, Resource the enemy spirit and Pressure the drain; in divining a husband or an official, Pressure is the Subject, Wealth the Support, Output the Adversary, Peer the enemy spirit and Resource the drain; in divining oneself take the Self as the Subject line, what generates the Self as the Support and what controls the Self as the Adversary; in divining another take the Other as the Subject line, what generates the Other as the Support and what controls the Other as the Adversary; this method is not only for divining illness, and in looking at any hexagram there is none that does not use it.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.122** 《黄金策》身命章说：「正卦的内卦不利，李密儿童时期困顿坎坷。」旧注解释：正卦的内卦三爻是初限（早年）；如果遇到官鬼、凶神来克战，主童年多病，像李密九岁才能走路，是因为幼年多病。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on the person and fate says: 'With the inner trigram of the main hexagram unfavourable, Li Mi's childhood was one of hardship.' The old commentary explains: the inner three lines of the main hexagram are the first period of life; if Pressure or an ominous spirit attacks it, it means many illnesses in childhood, as Li Mi could walk only at nine because he was often ill in his early years.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.126** 《黄金策》家宅章说：「要看卦爻的内外，才能知道人和宅的兴衰。」旧注：内三爻是宅，外三爻是人；宅去克人，主病患连年，多有吵闹骚扰；人去克宅，主修缮住房、创建房屋、整旧更新。王洪绪：内卦第二爻克外卦第五爻，叫宅去克人，凶；外卦五爻克二爻，叫人来克宅，吉；内卦二爻生五爻，叫宅去生人，吉。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on the household says: 'Look at the inner and outer of the lines to know the rise and fall of the people and the house.' The old commentary: the inner three lines are the house and the outer three the people; when the house controls the people, it means years of illness and disturbances of many kinds; when the people control the house, it means repairing a dwelling or building a house, mending the old and renewing. Wang Hongxu: the second line of the inner trigram controlling the fifth line of the outer trigram is called the house controlling the people, ominous; the fifth line of the outer trigram controlling the second is called the people controlling the house, auspicious; the second line of the inner trigram generating the fifth is called the house generating the people, auspicious.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.133** 《黄金策》国朝章（占国家的事）说：「年克伤衰弱的世爻，国君驾崩就不会多久了。」旧注：太岁刑、冲、克、害世爻，主国君生病，或者内乱将要发作；如果克世爻、而世爻又衰弱，一定有国君驾崩的忧虑。王洪绪：以下是国君自己占卜的情形，太岁刑冲克害世爻，主疾病或内乱将作。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on the state (divining matters of the state) says: 'The Year harming a weak Self line: the dragon leaves Dinghu before long.' The old commentary: Tai Sui punishing, clashing, controlling or harming the Self line means the ruler falls ill, or domestic trouble is about to break out; if it controls the Self line and the Self line is weak, there is sure to be fear for the ruler's death. Wang Hongxu: what follows is the case of the ruler divining for himself; Tai Sui punishing, clashing, controlling or harming the Self line means illness or domestic trouble about to break out.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.134** 《黄金策》产育章说：「胎爻临着官鬼，怀孕时就有生病的忧虑；财化成子孙，分娩时就是不用吃药的喜事。」旧注：鬼临在胎爻上，这个妇人怀孕期间常常有病；胎爻被官鬼冲克也一样；如果胎爻虽然临着官鬼，但化出子孙，或者财化成福爻，怀孕时虽然多病，分娩就安泰。王洪绪：鬼临胎爻，主孕妇有病，或者财合福爻，分娩就安泰。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on childbirth says: 'With the Womb line on Pressure, there is the worry of illness from the moment of pregnancy; Wealth turning into Output, at delivery there is the joy of needing no medicine.' The old commentary: a ghost on the Womb line means the woman is often ill during pregnancy, and so too if the Womb line is clashed or controlled by Pressure; if the Womb line, though on Pressure, turns into Output, or Wealth turns into a fortune line, she is often ill during pregnancy but delivery is safe and sound. Wang Hongxu: a ghost on the Womb line means the pregnant woman is ill, or if Wealth combines with the fortune line, delivery is safe and sound.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.135** 《黄金策》产育章说：「官鬼落空、伏藏，一定是遗腹子。」旧注：卦里没有官鬼或者官鬼落空亡，主产妇的丈夫已经死了，这必是遗腹子；但是没有财、福、白虎就不要这样断；如果有鬼伏在白虎爻下，或者青龙伏在鬼墓爻下，就主丈夫病卧在床，不是死；旺相而落空，是有病；鬼爻入墓，或者身临鬼墓，如果不是有病，就只是在狱里。王洪绪只讲了遗腹子一层：卦没有官鬼，或者官鬼在真空墓绝的地方，主产妇的丈夫已死；官爻伏着而旺相、有提拔的，是父亲远出，背生儿。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on childbirth says: 'With the officer Void or hidden, surely a posthumous child.' The old commentary: if the hexagram has no Pressure line or it is Void, the husband of the woman in childbirth is already dead and it is surely a posthumous child; but without Wealth, a fortune line or the White Tiger one does not judge so; if a ghost is hidden under the White Tiger line, or the Azure Dragon is hidden under the ghost's tomb line, it means the husband is bedridden with illness and not dead; strong but Void, he is ill; a ghost line in the tomb, or the Body on the ghost's tomb, if not illness it is only imprisonment. Wang Hongxu gives only the posthumous child: a hexagram without Pressure, or Pressure in a place of true Void, tomb or extinction, means the woman's husband is already dead; a Pressure line hidden but strong and with help means the father has gone far away, a child born behind his back.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.136** 《黄金策》种作章说：「世爻遇到三刑，种田的人要带着病。」旧注：世爻代表种田的人，被日辰、动爻刑冲克害，最不利；官鬼是疾病、官司、火灾盗贼一类；如果带着白虎、三刑，农夫一定带病；世爻持着官爻，也有病，不然就有差役在身；再加朱雀，恐怕是官司。王洪绪：带白虎三刑，农夫一定带病，世持官爻也是这样。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on farming says: 'With the Self line meeting the three punishments, the farmer carries an illness.' The old commentary: the Self line stands for the man who works the field, and if the Day or a moving line punishes, clashes, controls or harms it, it is the most unfavourable; Pressure is illness, lawsuits, fire and theft; if it carries the White Tiger and the three punishments, the farmer is sure to carry an illness; if the Self line holds the Pressure line there is illness too, or else some duty or service is laid on him; with the Vermilion Bird added, it is probably a lawsuit. Wang Hongxu: carrying the White Tiger and the three punishments, the farmer is sure to carry an illness, and so too if the Self line holds the Pressure line.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.137** 《黄金策》蚕桑章的旧注说：世爻代表养蚕的妇女；世爻临在官鬼上，养蚕的妇人一定有病；世爻无缘无故自己落空，或者化成死、墓、绝、空，养蚕的妇人要有大难，不然就是懒于抬蚕喂养；如果又带自刑，她自己也有病。
  An old commentary in the Huangjin Ce's chapter on silkworms says: the Self line stands for the woman who raises the silkworms; if it sits on Pressure, the silkworm woman is sure to be ill; if the Self line falls Void for no reason, or turns into death, tomb, extinction or Void, the silkworm woman is to meet great trouble, or else she is lazy about lifting and feeding the silkworms; if it also carries a self-punishment, she herself is ill too.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.142** 《黄金策》求师章的旧注说：卦里没有父母爻，看鬼爻化出什么来断老师是什么人：鬼带贵人化出，多半是生员；不带贵人，或者临在衰、绝上，一定是吏人；再加白虎，或者带着刑害，就是有病的人。
  An old commentary in the Huangjin Ce's chapter on seeking a teacher says: if the hexagram has no Resource line, what the teacher is is judged from what the ghost line turns out as: a ghost carrying the Noble is mostly a licentiate; one not carrying the Noble, or sitting on decline or extinction, is surely a clerk; with the White Tiger added, or carrying punishment and harm, he is a man who is ill.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.144** 《黄金策》行人章说：「用爻如果藏在官鬼爻下面，要参看飞神上的六神。」旧注：用爻伏在官鬼爻下，一定被凶事缠住：临着青龙，是因为酒色生了病回不来；临朱雀，是官非口舌回不来；临勾陈，是跌倒受伤；临螣蛇，是牵连惊恐；临白虎，是卧病不起；临玄武，是被盗失财回不来；凡是遇到土鬼就是病，火鬼就是官司，可以不论六神；卦里没有官鬼，也照上面断。王洪绪：临白虎或者官鬼属土，卧病不回；其余临勾陈、螣蛇、玄武各有所断。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on travellers says: 'If the Subject line is hidden under the officer, look at the six spirit on the flying line.' The old commentary: with the Subject line hidden under the Pressure line, one is surely held back by ill events: on the Azure Dragon, wine and lust have brought illness and he does not return; on the Vermilion Bird, official trouble and quarrels; on Hook Earth, a fall and injury; on the Coiling Snake, entanglement and fright; on the White Tiger, bedridden and unable to rise; on the Dark Tortoise, robbed and having lost money; whenever it meets an Earth ghost it is illness and a Fire ghost a lawsuit, and one need not discuss the six spirits; if the hexagram has no Pressure line, judge in the same way. Wang Hongxu: on the White Tiger, or with Pressure of the Earth element, bedridden and not returning; on Hook Earth, the Coiling Snake and the Dark Tortoise, other outcomes.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.145** 《黄金策》行人章说：「墓持着墓、墓发动，一定卧病呻吟。」旧注：用爻入墓又化成墓，或者持着鬼墓，或者卦里有鬼墓爻发动，或者用爻伏在鬼墓爻下面，都主病卧在别人家，所以不回来；如果伏在官爻下面，也是这样；带朱雀，或者化出文书，一定是在狱中，不是病。王洪绪：用爻像墓化成墓，或持鬼墓，或伏在鬼墓爻下，都主卧病不回；如果伏在官爻下，或者临白虎，一定是在狱中，不是病。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on travellers says: 'A tomb holding a tomb and moving: surely lying ill and groaning.' The old commentary: if the Subject line enters a tomb and turns into a tomb, or holds the ghost's tomb, or the hexagram has the ghost's tomb line moving, or the Subject line is hidden under the ghost's tomb line, all mean he lies ill in another's house and so does not return; if it is hidden under the Pressure line, the same; if it carries the Vermilion Bird or turns into documents, he is surely in prison and not ill. Wang Hongxu: if the Subject line is a tomb turning into a tomb, or holds the ghost's tomb, or is hidden under the ghost's tomb line, all mean lying ill and not returning; if it is hidden under the Pressure line, or sits on the White Tiger, he is surely in prison and not ill.
- **LY.ILL.HZL.17** 鬼伏在子孙下，只宜散忧；若要取用官，须官鬼旺相、透出到值日的日辰才行。若子孙旺相，占看丈夫的病，就死。
  The ghost hidden under Output is only fit for dispelling worries; if the official is to be used, the Pressure line must be strong and come out on the ruling day. If Output is strong, divining a husband's illness, he dies.
- **LY.ILL.YM.21** 取用神的办法，看的是关系，不看年纪和亲疏：主人即使年纪小，用父母爻；仆人即使年纪老，用妻财爻；同宗的人即使远到五服，称叔的用父爻，称侄的用子孙爻；异姓的表亲即使近到三党之内，也用应爻，只有岳父母、舅父母才用父爻；占现任的官，即使关系亲密，也用官爻而不用应爻；占师傅，即使关系疏远，也用父爻而不用应爻。
  The method of taking the Subject goes by the relation, not by age or closeness: a master, though young, takes the Resource line; a servant, though old, takes the Wealth line; a clansman, even as distant as the five mourning grades, takes the Resource line if addressed as uncle and the Output line if addressed as nephew; kin of another surname, even as near as the three clans, take the Other line, and only a father- or mother-in-law or a maternal uncle or aunt takes the Resource line; a serving official, however close, takes the Pressure line and not the Other; a teacher, however distant, takes the Resource line and not the Other.
- **LY.ILL.YM.22** 占女人的病，除了姑、姨、姐妹以外，其余都推作同类，用妻财爻：姑姨这样的亲人也看父母爻，姐妹这样的同辈同样按兄弟爻推，除此以外都用财爻。占同辈的人，除了同宗的兄弟以外，都属于不同本源，用应爻；称作兄弟、但不在宗服之内的，一律用应爻。
  In divining a woman's illness, apart from paternal aunts, maternal aunts and sisters, all are reckoned as the same kind and take the Wealth line: aunts are looked at through the Resource line, sisters are reckoned as brothers, and all others take the Wealth line. In divining someone of one's own generation, apart from brothers of the same clan, all are of a different root and take the Other line; those addressed as brothers who are outside the clan's mourning grades all take the Other line.
- **LY.ILL.YM.23** 臣子占君主，用九五爻，不论是皇亲贵戚、异姓的臣子还是平民；宗室的官员占他自己的本主，就按本主在朝廷里的长幼辈分来取：太子的臣子占太子，用朝廷的储君当子，取子孙爻；皇帝弟兄的臣子占皇弟皇兄，用朝廷的枝叶当兄弟，取兄弟爻；太后的臣子占太后，取父母爻。君主占臣子，用应爻，因为那是辅佐我的人。对守门的宦官用财爻；将领占士卒用子孙爻，为的是制住鬼；师徒的情谊用子爻。取用神这件事，占卜的人屡屡弄错，不能不审慎；占筮的人取用神常有两种困惑，以致吉凶不灵验，所以在这里说得详细，其他的取用也照这样推。
  A minister divining his sovereign takes the ninth-five line, whether he is of the imperial kin, of another surname or a commoner. An official of the imperial clan divining his own lord takes by the lord's seniority at court: the Crown Prince's minister divining the Crown Prince takes the court's heir apparent as the Output line; the minister of the emperor's brothers divining an imperial brother takes the branches of the court as brothers, the Peer line; the Empress Dowager's minister divining the Empress Dowager takes the Resource line. A sovereign divining his minister takes the Other line, for he is my aide. For palace gate-keepers and eunuchs the Wealth line is taken; a general divining his soldiers takes Output, in order to control the ghost; and in the bond of teacher and pupil the Output line is taken. In taking the Subject diviners have repeatedly erred, and one cannot but be careful; diviners often fall into two kinds of confusion in taking the Subject, so that fortune and misfortune do not prove true, and therefore it is set out in detail here, and other cases may be reckoned by it.
- **LY.ILL.YM.33** 还有占父亲的病而鬼在坤宫发动，他的母亲跟着也生病；占儿子的病而鬼变到巽宫，他的女儿接着遭灾：这就是说，爻变出来的鬼也是同样。所以疾病这一章离不开用爻和鬼的象。注：鬼在哪一宫发动，以及鬼变到哪一宫，就说那一宫所代表的人也要提防灾病。举了三个例子：辛酉日占女儿的病，巽变涣，子孙遇长生，这个女儿不会死，但鬼动变入坎宫，次子接着生病，后来还死了；丙辰日占二女儿出痘，革变咸，子孙值月建，二女儿无事，但内卦变到艮，官鬼落在少男宫里，后来小儿子也出了痘。总之占疾病，生死靠用爻，趋避、变通、救治的门路，都在于详细考察鬼爻。
  Again, where a father's illness is divined and the ghost moves in Kun, his mother falls ill after him; where a son's illness is divined and the ghost changes into Xun, his daughter suffers calamity in turn: so the ghost that appears by change is also the same. So the chapter on illness never leaves the Subject line and the image of the ghost. The note says: whichever palace the ghost moves in, and whichever palace it changes into, say that the person that palace stands for should also guard against calamity and illness. It gives cases: on a Xinyou day a woman's illness was divined, Xun changing into Huan, Output met Birth and the woman did not die, but the ghost moved into the Kan palace and the middle son fell ill in succession and later died; on a Bingchen day the pox of a second daughter was divined, Ge changing into Xian, Output stood on the Month and the second daughter came to no harm, but the inner trigram changed into Gen, the Pressure line sat in the palace of the youngest son, and afterwards the youngest son also had the pox. In short, in divining illness life and death rest on the Subject line, and the gates of seeking and avoiding, altering and remedying all depend on a careful examination of the Pressure line.

## 痘疹(Smallpox and measles)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/痘疹.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.01** 痘、痧、疮、疹是随着六气五行的流转一阵一阵流行的，想问平安还是危险，要凭易卦。先看用神是旺是衰，再考究各爻是动是静：用神被生、扶、拱、合，痘就长得有根；被克、害、刑、冲，花就遭到毒雨。凡占痘痧，一定要先分清用神、原神的衰旺，再考究忌神、仇神的动静。
  Pox, measles, sores and eruptions come and go with the turning of the six qi and the five phases; to ask whether it will be safe or dangerous one must rely on the hexagram. First look at whether the Subject is strong or weak, then at whether the lines move or are still: if the Subject is generated, supported, embraced or combined, the pox grows a living root; if it is controlled, harmed, punished or clashed, the flowers are struck by poison rain. In all divining of pox and measles one must first tell how strong or weak the Subject and the Support are, then examine how the Adversary and the enemy spirit move or stand still.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.02** 看是谁出花来取用神：兄弟出花，拿兄弟爻做用神、父母爻做原神，父母爻发动来生它就吉；子侄出花，拿子孙爻做用神，父母爻发动就克制子孙，是凶，而兄弟爻发动对子侄反而是好的；妻子、奴婢、妾出花，拿财爻做用神，兄弟爻发动克制财爻，也不合适。
  The Subject is taken by who is having the pox. For a brother, the Peer line is the Subject and the Resource line the Support, and if the Support moves and generates it, it is lucky. For a child or nephew, the Output line is the Subject, and if Resource moves it controls Output, which is unlucky, while a moving Peer is welcome; for a wife, servant, maid or concubine, the Wealth line is the Subject, and a moving Peer controls Wealth, which is likewise unsuitable.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.03** 最吉的是官爻安静、用神旺相，最凶的是鬼爻旺相、忌神兴旺发动。官鬼就是痘花，不适合受到伤损，也不适合发动，动了恐怕会变坏；如果遇到刑、冲、克、害、伏藏这些象，是凶险不顺的症候，即使勉强出得起来，也难以善终；用神旺相、官鬼安静又得到生、扶、拱、合的，是顺症，不用忧虑。
  Most auspicious is Pressure at rest and the Subject strong; most ominous is Pressure strong and the Adversary rising. The Pressure line is the pox flowers themselves: it should not be harmed and should not move, for if it moves it may turn bad; if it meets punishment, clash, control, harm or hiding, it is a dangerous and adverse condition, and even if it barely erupts it is hard to bring to a good end; if the Subject is strong and the Pressure line is at rest and is generated, supported, embraced or combined, it is a favourable condition and there is nothing to fear.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.04** 用神在日辰上得长生，或者变出长生，即使一百年之内也没有忧虑，眼下的痘疹又何必担心。
  If the Subject is born at the Day, or changes into Birth, then even within a hundred years there is nothing to worry about, so why worry now about the pox.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.05** 原神落在死、绝的地方，一个月之外终究有害处：用神休囚又受伤克，再遇上原神落在死、绝之处，一朝一夕都难拖延；用神出现又旺相，而原神安静地遇到死、绝，或者发动却变出伤克的，眼下当令虽然看得到收功，出了这个月、退了气，仍然有想不到的祸害。
  A Support that falls on Death or Extinction brings harm in the end after a month: if the Subject is resting or trapped and is also harmed and controlled, and the Support meets a place of Death or Extinction, it can hardly last from morning to evening; if the Subject is present and strong but the Support is still and meets Death or Extinction, or moves and changes into harm or control, then for now, in season, one may see it come to completion, but once the month is out and its force retreats there is still an unforeseen harm.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.06** 官爻强，痘就难以开朗；福神旺，花一定稀疏。官鬼爻是痘症本身，适合安静、不适合发动，动了就有变化；安静而衰的痘稀，旺而发动的痘密。父母爻是痘花的主，也适合安静有气，最忌讳发动后变出伤克。如果福神旺、官鬼衰，痘花一定稀疏明朗，一定能好好收功。
  If Pressure is strong the pox will hardly open up clear; if Output is strong the flowers will surely be sparse. The Pressure line is the pox itself; it should be at rest and not move, for if it moves there is change: at rest and weak the pox is sparse, strong and moving the pox is dense. The Resource line is the master of the pox flowers and should likewise be at rest and have force, and most dreaded is moving and changing into harm or control. If Output is strong and Pressure weak, the pox flowers are sure to be sparse and clear, and will surely come to a good completion.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.07** 用神不适合落在墓库上：痘喜欢起发，发出来以后又喜欢人神志清楚；用神入了墓库，一开始难以起发，后来病人一定神志昏沉疲倦，主难以收功。
  The Subject should not sit in a tomb or storehouse: the pox likes to erupt, and once erupted likes the patient to be clear in spirit; if the Subject enters a tomb, at first it is hard for it to erupt, and later the patient is surely dull and drowsy in spirit, which means it is hard to bring to completion.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.08** 出花的人要身体强旺才容易收功；用神休囚，一定是身体虚弱，再没有原神、日、月来生扶的，后来也会有变化。
  A person having the pox should be of strong body, and then it is easy to bring to completion; if the Subject is resting or trapped, the body is surely weak, and if in addition no Support, Day or Month generates or supports it, there will be change later on.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.09** 卦里官爻出现得多，要防备贼痘为害：官鬼不适合多，鬼多的话痘就分粗和细；如果没有子孙出现，恐怕在密密的痘当中夹着有毒的痘，名叫贼痘，如果不把它除去，就会害了满身的痘。
  If many Pressure lines appear in the hexagram, guard against the harm of thief pox: there should not be many Pressure lines, and with many the pox divide into coarse and fine; if no Output appears, there may be poisonous pox among the dense ones, called thief pox, and if it is not removed it will spoil the pox over the whole body.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.10** 卦里福神多，要担心进补反而招来祸殃：子孙爻不适合出现得多，只要旺相有气就行；如果出现得多，就不适合用补药、吃补的东西，用了补，反而恐怕有害。
  If many Output lines appear, beware that taking tonics may invite calamity: Output lines should not appear in numbers, and it is enough that one is strong and has force; if many appear, it is not suitable to use tonic medicines or eat tonic foods, and if tonics are used they may do harm instead.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.11** 卦里乱动都不吉利，伏吟也是凶：各种卦都怕乱动，何况是痘花；凡是占卜都怕伏吟，难道只有痘症才怕吗。
  Lines moving in disorder are all inauspicious, and Hidden Chant is also ominous: every hexagram fears lines moving in disorder, let alone the pox flowers; all divining fears Hidden Chant, is it only so for pox?
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.13** 财爻在卦中发动，适合调理脾胃：财爻代表饮食，适合旺、不适合空，空了就不想吃东西；适合安静、不适合发动，发动了就生助官鬼，恐怕因为吃得太多而伤了脾胃，所以要调养。
  If Wealth moves in the hexagram, it is right to regulate the spleen and stomach: Wealth stands for food and drink; it should be strong and not Void, for if Void the patient has no wish to eat; it should be at rest and not move, for if it moves it generates and helps Pressure, and for fear that eating too much will harm the spleen and stomach, it must be regulated and nourished.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.14** 兄弟爻在卦里兴起，要调理胸怀：财是用来调理的东西，兄是防气的神；如果遇到兄弟爻发动，就伤克财爻，主吃得少，或者缺少调理；如果落在间爻，适合宽胸理气；如果带朱雀，一定是生了气而不想吃东西。
  If Peer rises in the hexagram, the chest and mind must be regulated: Wealth is the thing for regulation and Peer is the spirit that guards against breath and temper; if the Peer line moves, it harms and controls the Wealth line, which means little food is taken or regulation is lacking; if it is on a between line, ease the chest and regulate the qi; if it carries the Vermilion Bird, the patient is surely angry and does not want to eat.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.15** 贪吃口腹反而增加忧患，多半是帮了官爻、伤了世爻：财爻发动就生官鬼；如果卦中又见官鬼发动而克世爻、卦身、主象的，一定是因为贪吃而使病加重，或者没出痘之前就已经停食了。
  Indulging the mouth and belly adds to the trouble, mostly because it helps Pressure and harms the Self: when the Wealth line moves it generates Pressure; if in the hexagram Pressure also moves and controls the Self, the Body or the main image, it is surely because of indulging the mouth and belly that the illness has grown worse, or the patient had already stopped eating before the pox came out.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.16** 爱某种味道才肯吃东西，一定是因为帮了福神、生了世身：兄弟爻本来克财，一发动就不想吃东西；如果子孙也发动，动来生世爻、卦身、用神的象，叫做助福生世，主出痘的人起初不想吃，因为爱吃某一种味道，把胃口引开了，才吃得下东西。
  Eating because one loves a certain flavour is surely because it helps Output and generates the Self and the Body: Peer by nature controls Wealth, and when it moves the patient has no wish to eat; if Output also moves and moves to generate the Self, the Body or the image of the Subject, this is called helping Output to generate the Self, and it means the person with the pox at first has no wish to eat, but because he loves one flavour his appetite is drawn open and only then can he take food.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.17** 卦遇到六冲，痘难起发；爻逢到六合，痘好收功。占近病，喜欢遇到六冲，叫做冲散灾殃；只有占痘症不是这样，叫做花遇到冲就败，好比风雨侵袭花朵，开始难起发，后来也不能收功。如果遇到六合卦，或者用神逢到生、合，就容易起发，一定能好好收功。
  A hexagram meeting Six-Clash makes the pox hard to erupt; lines meeting Six-Combine bring a good completion. In divining a recent illness one is glad to meet Six-Clash, which is called clashing the calamity apart; only in divining pox is it not so, for it is said that a flower meeting a clash is ruined, like wind and rain attacking flowers: at first it hardly erupts, and later it does not come to completion. If it meets a Six-Combine hexagram, or the Subject meets generation or combination, it erupts easily and will surely come to a good completion.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.18** 痘闷着发不出来，都是因为用神伏藏又受了伤；发出来却不灌浆，只是因为官爻落空又加受制。用神出现、没有遇到日月动爻的刑冲克害，是大吉的兆头；用神伏藏、又受日月动爻的刑冲克害，要提防这个痘，很难过四五天，这是屡次应验的；官爻旬空、月破，又遇日辰动爻的克害，痘虽然起发了，七八天之内恐怕不能灌浆，难以收功。
  The pox stifled and unable to erupt is all because the Subject is hidden and further harmed; erupted but not filling with fluid is only because Pressure is Void and further controlled. If the Subject appears and meets no punishment, clash, control or harm from the Day, Month or moving lines, it is a very auspicious sign; if the Subject is hidden and is struck by punishment, clash, control or harm from the Day, Month or moving lines, one must guard against this pox, and it is hard to get past four or five days, as has been proved again and again; if the Pressure line is Void or in Month-break and further meets control or harm from the Day or a moving line, then although the pox may erupt, within seven or eight days it may not fill with fluid, and it is hard to bring to completion.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.19** 原神如果坏了，即使用神出现也不吉祥；主象受了伤，得到救护就没有妨碍。原神就是生用神的爻：原神旬空，或者伏藏又受伤克，主症状虚、身体弱，不补就不能起发，起发了也没有力气灌浆；原神遇到真空、真破，或者伏藏又受伤太过，或者变出回头克伤它，叫做原神受伤，用神没有根，怎么能得到生，不是吉祥的兆头。如果主象遇到伤克，而原神带着月建、日辰、动爻来救护，痘症虽然凶险，可以断定没有妨碍。
  If the Support is spoiled, then even if the Subject appears it is inauspicious; if the main image is wounded but gains rescue and protection, there is no obstruction. The Support is the line that generates the Subject: if it is Void, or hidden and struck by harm or control, the condition is one of emptiness and the body weak, so that without tonics it cannot erupt and once erupted has no strength to fill with fluid; if the Support meets true Void or true breaking, or is hidden and too badly wounded, or changes into a returning control that wounds it, this is called the Support wounded, the Subject has no root, and how can it be born? It is not an auspicious sign. If the main image meets harm or control but the Support, backed by the Month, the Day or a moving line, comes to rescue and protect it, then although the pox is dangerous, it may be judged that there is no obstruction.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.20** 福神或鬼爻如果带青龙，才适合种痘：子孙爻带青龙，或者官鬼爻带青龙，没有遇到日、月、动爻的刑冲克害，占种痘是大吉的兆头；如果官爻旬空或者伏藏，福神又不带青龙，即使种了痘也出不来。
  Output or Pressure carrying the Azure Dragon means it is fit to inoculate: if the Output line carries the Azure Dragon, or the Pressure line carries the Azure Dragon, and meets no punishment, clash, control or harm from the Day, Month or moving lines, then in divining about inoculation it is a very auspicious sign; if the Pressure line is Void or hidden and Output does not carry the Azure Dragon, then even if the pox is sown it will not come out.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.21** 用神或者煞神如果带白虎，先别种痘。具体说，或者用神带白虎，或者忌神带白虎，用神又受到日、月、动爻的刑冲克害，不适合种痘，恐怕反而被害。
  If the Subject or the killing spirit carries the White Tiger, hold off sowing the pox for now. Specifically, either the Subject carries the White Tiger or the Adversary carries the White Tiger, and the Subject is struck by punishment, clash, control or harm from the Day, Month or moving lines: it is not suitable to inoculate, for fear of being harmed instead.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.22** 玄武冲世爻、冲卦身，是不洁的妇人冲撞魇魅，使痘起了变化。具体说，玄武带着财爻发动，来冲世爻、卦身、用神的象，是因为不洁的妇人冲撞魇魅，使痘花起了变化；玄武带着应爻发动，来冲世爻、卦身、主象，是被外人闯进来冲撞魇魅而起了变化。
  The Dark Tortoise clashing the Self and the Body means an unclean woman has clashed and cursed and made the pox change. Specifically, if the Dark Tortoise carries the Wealth line and it moves to clash the Self, the Body or the image of the Subject, it is because an unclean woman has clashed and cursed and caused the pox flowers to change; if the Dark Tortoise carries the Other line and it moves to clash the Self, the Body or the main image, an outsider has burst in and clashed and cursed and caused the change.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.23** 白虎带官爻或带用神，火毒很重而没有清干净。白虎是血神，如果带官鬼或带用神，又遇到日、月、动爻的刑冲克害，不是生痰，一定是毒结在体内；毒结的部位按宫来推：乾宫在头脸，坤宫在腹，震宫在脚，巽宫在大腿，艮宫在手，离宫在眼，坎宫在耳，兑宫在口。
  The White Tiger on Pressure or on the Subject means the fire poison is heavy and not yet cleared. The White Tiger is the blood spirit; if it sits on Pressure or on the Subject and meets punishment, clash, control or harm from the Day, Month or moving lines, then if it is not phlegm being produced, it is surely poison lodged within; the place where the poison lodges is worked out by the palace: the Qian palace, the head and face; Kun, the belly; Zhen, the foot; Xun, the thigh; Gen, the hand; Li, the eye; Kan, the ear; Dui, the mouth.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.24** 卦身这一爻主出痘的人从头到尾的事：落在福德这个吉神上，没有痘毒；落在官鬼上，一定有毒结成病的地方，按官鬼的五行说会留下什么毛病：金鬼是肺经炎毒未消，鼻孔里生疮或右耳有毛病；木鬼是肝经火毒未消，两眼里出痘或右耳有毛病；水鬼是肾经火毒未消，两耳干枯，嘴唇有毛病；火鬼是心经火毒未消，舌头干焦，眼睛有毛病；土鬼是脾经火毒未清，嘴像鱼嘴，鼻梁有毛病。己土官鬼带着卦身的，如果休囚、又见福神发动，用药就能好；如果没有福神克制，反而加上白虎附着，就有损失，是终身的毛病。
  The Body line governs the whole course of the person with the pox from start to finish: if it sits on Output, the fortunate spirit, there is no pox poison; if it sits on Pressure, there is surely a place where poison lodges and becomes a lingering ailment, and by the element of Pressure it is worked out as follows: a Metal ghost, inflammatory poison of the lung channel not dispersed, with sores in the nostrils or an ailment of the right ear; Wood, fire poison of the liver channel not dispersed, pox within both eyes or an ailment of the right ear; Water, fire poison of the kidney channel not dispersed, both ears dry and withered and an ailment of the lips; Fire, fire poison of the heart channel not dispersed, a dry scorched tongue and an eye ailment; Earth, fire poison of the spleen channel not cleared, a mouth like a fish's mouth and an ailment at the bridge of the nose. If a Ji-Earth Pressure line sits on the Body and is resting or trapped, and Output is seen moving, medicine can cure it; if no Output controls it and the White Tiger clings to it instead, there is loss, and it is a lifelong ailment.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.25** 爻里的煞，要凭八卦来论一生留下什么毛病：白虎、鬼在乾宫，毛病在头；在兑卦，在脸；在震卦，在脚；在巽卦，在大腿；在坎卦，在耳朵；在离卦，在眼睛；在艮卦，在手；在坤卦，在肚子。以上八个宫里所遇到的鬼爻，如果遇到福神克制，就容易医好；如果再加上白虎带着，就是终身的毛病。
  The killing spirit within the lines should be judged by the eight trigrams for the lifelong ailment: if the Tiger ghost sits in the Qian palace, the ailment lingers in the head; in the Dui image, in the face; in Zhen, in the foot; in Xun, in the thigh; in Kan, in the ear; in Li, in the eye; in Gen, in the hand; in Kun, in the belly. For the Pressure line in any of the eight palaces above, if Output meets and controls it, treatment easily cures it; if the White Tiger is added and sits on it, it is a lifelong ailment.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.26** 金对应肺腑，加重疼痛、加重咳嗽都不相宜；火属心经，发疮、发斑是大忌。具体说，官鬼属金，毒发在肺部，主身体作痛或咳嗽，要防鼻子歪偏；官鬼属火，毒发在心经，是火毒的症候，起初要防发斑，接着要防发疮和舌头长久僵硬。衰而静的轻，旺而动的更重。
  Metal is the lungs and viscera, and increased pain or increased cough is unfavourable; Fire belongs to the heart channel, and breaking out in sores or spots is a great taboo. Specifically, if the Pressure line is Metal, the poison breaks out in the lungs, bringing bodily pain or cough, and one must guard against a crooked nose; if the Pressure line is Fire, the poison breaks out in the heart channel and is a fire-poison condition; at first guard against spots, then against sores and a stiffened tongue that stays so. If weak and still it is light; if strong and moving it is heavier still.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.27** 木鬼是风邪还没有发散到表面，水宫则是寒凉的食物还停积着。官爻属木，一定是风寒没有发散出去，肝经受了毒，要防呛咳、气喘、发痒和两眼直瞪；官爻属水，毒发在肾经，要防腰疼和两耳焦干，还有寒凉的食物停积在里面，发热、浆水留着不化。
  A Wood ghost is a wind evil not yet expelled to the surface; in the Water palace cold food is still stagnant. If the Pressure line is Wood, it is surely because wind-cold has not been expelled and the liver channel has taken the poison, and one must guard against choking cough, wheezing, itching and a fixed stare of the eyes; if the Pressure line is Water, the poison breaks out in the kidney channel, and one must guard against lower-back pain and both ears becoming scorched and dry, and there is also cold food stagnant within, with fever and lingering fluid.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.28** 麻面是官爻乘着辰、戌、丑、未这四个土，破碎是遇到三处冲。官鬼就是痘花，如果落在辰、戌、丑、未四个土上，土属脾经，主痘又粗又扁又多又密，所以叫麻面，要防嘴像鱼嘴；如果官爻遇到年、月、日（注里写「二建」）来冲，灌浆以后要防破碎、泄气。
  A pockmarked face is Pressure riding the four Earths, and breaking and shattering is meeting three clashes. The Pressure line is the pox flowers; if it sits on the four Earths, Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei, Earth belongs to the spleen channel, and the pox are coarse, flat, many and dense, so it is called a pockmarked face, and one must guard against a mouth like a fish's mouth; if the Pressure line meets a clash from the Year, Month or Day (the commentary writes 'two rulers'), then after the fluid has filled one must guard against breaking apart and the leaking of qi.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.29** 玄武主阴虚、痘色发黑收缩，勾陈应验为胀闷、面色发黄浮肿。玄武带官爻，或带忌神，以及阴虚的症候，要防浆水收缩、痘色发黑塌陷；勾陈如果附在鬼爻上，主胀闷、发黄、浮肿。
  The Dark Tortoise means yin deficiency, blackened and shrunken pox; the Hook Earth responds with swelling, oppression and a yellow, puffy look. If the Dark Tortoise sits on the Pressure line, or on the Adversary, or in a condition of yin deficiency, guard against the fluid shrinking and the pox turning black and sunken; if the Hook Earth clings to the Pressure line, it means swelling, oppression, yellowness and puffiness.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.30** 螣蛇带木摆动像惊风，朱雀带火炽盛是真的血热。螣蛇带着木爻的官鬼，主刚开始还没见到痘点的时候，像是惊风；如果卦中官鬼属火，又带朱雀，是血热火毒的症候，必须用大黄、黄连这类药，一定要清火泻毒才能得救；如果服得迟，病势更猛，痘隐没下去、焦黑，没法挽回。
  The Coiling Snake with Wood swaying is like infantile convulsions; the Vermilion Bird with blazing Fire is truly blood heat. If the Coiling Snake sits on a Wood Pressure line, before the spots are yet seen at the beginning it seems like infantile convulsions; if in the hexagram the Pressure line is Fire and sits on the Vermilion Bird, it is a condition of blood heat and fire poison, and one must use rhubarb, coptis and the like: only by clearing fire and draining poison can there be rescue; if it is taken late the enemy is fiercer, the pox sink out of sight and turn scorched and black, and it cannot be retrieved.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.31** 白虎和忌神、煞神一起交重（发动），哭声将要到来：白虎带着忌神发动，克伤用神，又没有原神来扶持保护的，马上就出现危险。
  The White Tiger together with the Adversary and killing spirits all moving means the sound of wailing is coming: if the White Tiger sits on the Adversary and it moves to control and wound the Subject, and there is no Support to hold and protect it, danger appears at once.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.32** 青龙会合恩星发动，庆贺的人一起到来：青龙带着原神发动，生合用神，痘一定能收功。
  The Azure Dragon meeting the kindly star and moving means congratulations arrive together: if the Azure Dragon sits on the Support and it moves to generate and combine with the Subject, the pox will surely come to completion.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.33** 生死取决于五行生克之中，轻重取决于六神落在哪里、带在谁身上。生死全靠生克的轻重，再兼看六神，这一节是全章的大旨。
  Life and death are settled within the generating and controlling of the five phases, and light or heavy is decided by where the six spirits sit and what they hold. Life and death rest wholly on the weight of generating and controlling, together with a look at the six spirits; this passage is the main purpose of the whole chapter.
- **LY.ILL.BSZZ.35** 怎么知道这家有痘疹的病：螣蛇爻遇到火，被火烧着，就断定了。
  How to know that a household has the pox: the Coiling Snake line is burned by Fire, and that settles it.
- **LY.ILL.EL.06** 以官鬼为主，五行六象为凭。还没种花的时候来问种花，卦里没有官鬼的，痘就没有芽；鬼空、鬼绝，没有斑疹；官动、官兴，有痘瘀。占出痘，要看官爻：鬼如果空亡、或不上卦，一定不出；卦里纵有鬼动，也遇上休囚、或遇冲散、或落绝乡、或化出克绝，都不是出痘；官鬼发动而不临死绝刑伤，鬼即使休囚，如果变为有气，就种痘花；鬼虽不动，遇上旺相的时期，也应当起发；卦里虽无鬼，若本月临官，反倒生出痘痧。年上临官，这年出痘；月中值鬼，这个月栽花；鬼休囚、子孙旺，痘应稀朗；鬼旺、福（子孙）衰，痘更稠密；若见螣蛇临火发动，一定是麻疹和痘，断无差错。问哪一年种痘，就看鬼临生旺的年，卦里没有官，再看哪一年值鬼；占哪一个月栽花，就推鬼逢生旺的月；要知道哪一天，也看鬼旺的日子，大同小异，一个道理推；卦里官鬼休囚、子孙旺相，或子孙在世爻上，纵然出痘，也稀疏；官鬼旺相、子孙休囚，痘出来时稠密非常；还要论螣蛇临火发动，鬼虽安静，也必发痘疹。
  The Pressure line is the main line, and the five phases and six images are the ground. When asked, before the flowers are sown, about sowing the flowers, if the hexagram has no Pressure line the pox has no sprout; with the ghost Void or in Extinction there are no spots or rash; with Pressure moving and thriving there is pox with dark stasis. In divining the pox coming out, look at the Pressure line: if the ghost is Void or absent from the hexagram, it will surely not come out; even if the hexagram has a moving ghost, if it meets resting or trapped, or clash and scattering, or falls in Extinction, or changes into control or Extinction, none of it is the pox coming out; if the Pressure line moves and is not on death, Extinction, punishment or wounding, then even if the ghost is resting or trapped, if it changes into strength the flowers are sown; though the ghost does not move, meeting the period when it is strong, the pox should still rise; and though the hexagram has no ghost, if the month itself sits on Pressure, pox and rash are born instead. Pressure on the year: the pox comes out that year; the ghost on duty in the month: the flowers are planted that month; ghost resting and Output strong, the pox is sparse and clear; ghost strong and Output weak, denser still; if the Coiling Snake on Fire moves, it is sure to be measles and pox, and the judgment will not be wrong. To ask in what year to sow the pox, look at the year in which the ghost is on strength or generation, and where the hexagram has no Pressure line, look for the year on which a ghost sits; to divine in what month to plant the flowers, work out the month in which the ghost meets strength; to know the day, look also at the days when the ghost is strong, the same reasoning with small differences; if in the hexagram the Pressure line is resting or trapped and Output strong, or Output is on the Self, then even if the pox comes out it is sparse; if the Pressure line is strong and Output resting or trapped, the pox comes out extremely dense; also, if the Coiling Snake on Fire moves, though the ghost is still, pox and rash will surely come out.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.148** 《黄金策》年时章说：「其次观察百姓，喜欢（二爻）在旺相的地方。」旧注：二爻是人民的位置；遇到子孙，四季安乐；逢到官鬼，太岁多灾，发动克世，百姓多去做盗贼；二爻自己落空又化成空，百姓多有暴死的；空而动、又生合应爻，百姓多迁徙到外郡；火鬼化成父母，小孩多出痘疹。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on the year says: 'Next observe the common people; it is good for them to be in a place of strength and birth.' The old commentary: the second line is the place of the people; meeting Output, the four seasons are peaceful and happy; meeting Pressure, with Tai Sui, the year has many calamities, and if it moves and controls the Self, many of the people turn to banditry; if the line is Void and turns into Void, many of the people die suddenly; if Void and moving and it generates or combines with the Other line, many of the people move away to other counties; a Fire ghost turning into Resource, many small children break out in pox and rash.
- **LY.ILL.YM.01** 痘疹也是天降的灾，叫做天花，也叫圣疮，一出就关系到孩子的性命。还没出花就来问的，拿鬼爻作凭据：鬼空，痘的根芽还没有萌发；鬼静，气机还没有动；鬼散，痘虽在流行也轮不到他；鬼破而安静，痘的壳不裂开；鬼破而发动，得的可能是别的病；鬼绝，还没有生花。
  Pox is also a calamity from heaven, called heavenly flowers and also the sacred sore, and once it stirs it bears on a child's life. If the question is asked before the flowers have come, take the ghost as the token: if the ghost is Void, the roots and shoots of the pox have not yet sprouted; if still, the working of qi has not yet stirred; if scattered, although the pox is going about it does not reach him; if broken and still, the husk does not split; if broken and moving, the illness may be a different one; if in Extinction, the flowers have not yet been born.
- **LY.ILL.YM.02** 鬼动了，痘将要出现；鬼被冲了，痘将要开；鬼旺了，痘将要起；鬼当月、当日，就在这个月、这一天出痘。官鬼当值的日、月，痘就该出现：比如丑月乙卯日，占有没有花，得到履卦变同人卦，当天就见了标；另有一人也占，得履卦变睽卦，到第二年二月才出痘，因为卯鬼发动所以应得快，卯鬼安静所以应得迟。
  When the ghost moves the pox is about to show; when the ghost is clashed it is about to open; when the ghost is strong it is about to rise; when the ghost is on duty in the month or day, it comes out in that month or on that day. The day or month on which the Pressure line is on duty, the pox should appear: for example, in a Chou month on a Yimao day, someone divined whether the flowers would come and obtained Lu changing into Tongren, and the marks showed that same day; another person also divined and obtained Lu changing into Kui, and the pox began only in the second month of the following year, because the Mao ghost moved and so it answered quickly, and the Mao ghost was still and so it answered slowly.
- **LY.ILL.YM.03** 问哪一年出痘，要探究鬼象的得失；卦里有两个鬼时，要考察其中一爻的虚实。官鬼如果值太岁，或者遇到空、破、休、囚，就说还没出，后来即使日、月会合，也不能说就有：比如己亥年辰月辛未日，占有没有痘，得同人卦，亥鬼旬空，整年平安，可见不能因为太岁就是鬼就断当年应痘。如果卦里有两个鬼，一个空一个动，就取动的；一个强一个弱，就取强的，都应当出痘；这就是所说的考察一爻的虚实。
  For the year, seek the gain or loss of the ghost's image; where there are two ghosts, examine the emptiness or fullness of one line. If the Pressure line falls on the Tai Sui, or meets Void, breaking, resting or trapped, say it has not yet come out; even if later the Day and Month meet, one cannot say there is pox: for example, in the Jihai year, the Chen month, on the Xinwei day, someone divined whether there would be pox and obtained Tongren; the Hai ghost was in Void and all year long he was well and at peace, so one cannot take the Tai Sui being the ghost as meaning the pox answers that year. If the hexagram has two ghosts, one Void and one moving, take the moving one; if one strong and one weak, take the strong one; in each case the pox should come out; this is what 'examine the emptiness or fullness of one line' means.
- **LY.ILL.YM.04** 鬼落在世爻上，是自己招来的；落在应爻上，是别人招来的；在内卦，是本乡流行的灾，在外卦，是别处流传来的祸。出去躲避反而得了痘，是游魂卦、鬼发动；回来却没得痘，是归魂卦、鬼安静；伏着的鬼有气的，痘也会出现。官鬼一动，天花就开了：在世爻是单独发出来的，在应爻是被人传染缠上的，在六爻是童仆起的根，在三爻是弟兄接着种下的；内卦是本地，外卦是外乡；游魂是出去，归魂是回来。鬼在内卦空，本地没事；鬼在外卦空，到外乡可以躲过；鬼动而卦是归魂，回家有痘；鬼空而卦是游魂，出去就能免。大体上鬼爻旺相，即使伏藏也会萌发，鬼爻休囚，即使现出来也会平息。
  On the Self the pox is called down by oneself; on the Other, called by others; in the inner trigram it is a passing calamity of the home district, in the outer trigram a spreading misfortune from elsewhere. Going away to avoid it and yet catching the pox is a Wandering Soul hexagram with the ghost moving; coming home and not catching it is a Returning Soul hexagram with the ghost still; a hidden ghost that has force also shows. When the Pressure line moves the heavenly flowers open: on the Self it is a lone outbreak, on the Other it is caught by contagion, on the sixth line the root is from a servant boy, on the third line brothers sow it in succession; the inner is the home district and the outer another place; Wandering Soul is going away and Returning Soul is coming back.
