If the ghost is Void in the inner trigram the home district is safe; if Void in the outer trigram one can avoid it by going elsewhere; if the ghost moves in a Returning Soul hexagram, there is pox on returning home; if the ghost is Void in a Wandering Soul hexagram, going out escapes it. In general, if the Pressure line is strong, even hidden it will sprout; if it is resting or trapped, even openly present it will subside.
- **LY.ILL.YM.05** 占花的稀密，问鬼的旺衰，用八卦分手足，用六爻定身形，用五行定颜色。鬼临日、月，旺相又有生扶，花一定稠密；遇到空、破、休、囚、死、绝，痘一定疏朗。鬼在哪个宫发动，花就集中在哪里；不动的，就看哪一爻旺：发在艮宫的手臂上密，动在兑宫的口和脸颊上多，坎、离在上身，震、巽在下身；旺在初爻，先出在脚上，旺在六爻，开始发在脸上，五爻在胸背，二爻在腿膝，三、四爻在腰、大腿、心口、肚子。火鬼发的，颜色红紫；金鬼动的，虚白；水鬼发的，黑而塌陷；木是杂而细，土是肿而大。但是一定要论用神的生死才能定吉凶，所以水鬼动而主象旺的，痘出现黑钉，后来仍能收功。痘症多变，假如遇到用象破空，或者某个鬼发动，就变成那种病而死，比如未月甲辰日，占孩子的痘，得到屯卦变革卦，到戊申日变成脾病而治不好。
  To divine whether the flowers are sparse or dense, ask whether the ghost is strong or weak; the eight trigrams divide hands and feet, the six lines fix the parts of the body, the five elements bring out the colours. If the ghost sits on the Day or Month, is strong and supported, the flowers are surely dense; if it meets Void, breaking, resting, trapped, death or extinction, the pox is surely sparse and clear. Wherever the ghost moves, in which palace, that fixes where the flowers cluster; if it does not move, see which line is strong: from the Gen palace, dense on the arms; moving in the Dui palace, many on the mouth and cheeks; Kan and Li the upper body, Zhen and Xun the lower; strong on the first line, first on the feet; on the sixth line, starting on the face; the fifth the chest and back, the second the legs and knees, the third and fourth the waist, thighs, heart and belly. A Fire ghost gives red-purple, a Metal ghost hollow white, a Water ghost black and sunken; Wood is mixed and fine, Earth swollen and large. But one must judge whether the Subject lives or dies before fixing fortune, so when a Water ghost moves and the main image is strong, the pox shows black nails and yet comes to completion. Pox often changes: if the image of the Subject is broken or Void, or some ghost moves, it changes into that illness and he dies — for example, in the Wei month on the Jiachen day a child's pox was divined and Zhun changing into Ge obtained, and on the Wushen day it turned into a spleen illness that could not be cured.
- **LY.ILL.YM.06** 如果问是谁出痘，鬼爻正当他的本命的，或者鬼变成（某人所属的）卦宫的，就应在这个人身上。考察哪个六亲变出鬼、鬼变成哪个六亲，就知道出痘的是这个人；但也必须鬼旺又动才会发。一家之内，官鬼正当哪个人的本命，就说这个人有痘；又，鬼动在艮宫，是少男；鬼变成离象，是中女；财爻变鬼，是奴仆；鬼变成子孙，是幼儿；总之要在鬼爻旺又动的前提下才这样说。
  If asked who will have the pox, it answers on the one whose natal branch the ghost stands on, or on the person whose palace the ghost changes into. Examine which relation changes into the ghost and which relation the ghost changes into, and that is the person who has the pox; but the ghost must also be strong and moving before it breaks out. Within one household, whoever's natal branch the Pressure line stands on, say that person has the pox; again, a ghost moving in the Gen palace is the youngest son; a ghost changing into the Li image, the middle daughter; a Wealth line changing into a ghost, a servant; a ghost changing into Output, an infant; in general one says so only where the Pressure line is strong and moving.
- **LY.ILL.YM.07** 如果问孩子什么时候出痘，是官鬼当值的年、月、日，以及生旺官鬼的年、月、日；上下的鬼填补它的空，变出的鬼、伏着的鬼落实它的实体。远近要分开占：问哪一岁就以年计，问什么时候就以月计，先看填实的时间，再看生旺的年月来断；如果卦里有两个鬼，一个空一个实，反而以填补空鬼的时间为期；卦里一个官也没有，而伏爻、变爻上有的，就以变出、伏着的鬼所值的时间为期。
  If asked when a child will have the pox, it is the year, month or day on which the Pressure line is on duty, and the year, month or day that strengthens the Pressure line; the ghosts above and below fill its emptiness, and the changed or hidden ghost makes its substance real. Far and near are divined separately: asking which year, count by years; asking when, count by months; first the time of filling the emptiness, then the years and months of strength; if the hexagram has two ghosts, one Void and one full, the time of filling the Void ghost is taken as the term instead; if the hexagram has no Pressure line at all but the hidden or changed lines have one, take the time held by the changed or hidden ghost as the term.
- **LY.ILL.YM.08** 已经出痘，再占整体的吉凶：七天之内，要戒备三冲，就是花还没开就先谢了。卦是反吟、伏吟、入墓、入绝的，和本命随鬼入墓的，即使用爻旺也难治；这四种忌讳之外，再考察用神。鬼旺而用爻旺，花丛茂盛、根牢固，是吉；鬼坏而用爻坏，花憔悴、根枯，是凶。生旺用神的日子是有成的时候，冲鬼象的时辰是有变的日期。大体上，感染不感染取决于鬼，有害没害取决于用爻；鬼变鬼的，痘后有疮痍；交错发动的，是瘄和痘掺杂。注说：这是专就用爻来说吉凶，不再论鬼的衰旺；如果用爻有生扶救助，鬼旺不过是花密；鬼爻被填实、被冲动，不过是要防变；鬼变鬼和用爻变鬼，不过是痘后余毒；鬼交错发动，或者有别的病夹杂，也没到大害的地步。
  Once the pox has come and one divines the great image's fortune, within seven days the three clashes are the thing to guard against, meaning the flower withers before it has opened. A hexagram that is Reversed, Hidden, in a tomb or in Extinction, and one where the natal branch follows the ghost into a tomb, are hard to treat even if the Subject line is strong; apart from these four taboos, then examine the Subject. If the ghost is strong and the Subject line strong, the flowers are luxuriant and the roots firm, which is auspicious; if the ghost is spoiled and the Subject line spoiled, the flowers droop and the roots wither, which is ominous. The day that strengthens the Subject is the time of completion, the hour that clashes the ghost image the time of change. In general whether the child catches it depends on the ghost, whether it does harm on the Subject line; a ghost changing into a ghost means sores after the pox; lines moving in tangles mean measles and pox mixed together. The note says: this speaks of fortune purely by the Subject line and no longer discusses whether the ghost is strong or weak; if the Subject line has generation, support and rescue, a strong ghost only means dense flowers; if the Pressure line is filled or stirred by a clash, it only means guarding against change; a ghost changing into a ghost, or the Subject line changing into a ghost, only means residual poison after the pox; ghosts moving in tangles, or another illness riding along, does not amount to great harm.
- **LY.ILL.YY.35** 以官鬼为用爻。鬼不上卦，或者鬼变出死、墓、绝、胎、空，以及被刑、害、克、破的，都不出痘。如果鬼虽然衰、死、受伤而变出生旺，或者没有鬼而月建临官，或者鬼安静而日正当生旺，以及火鬼带螣蛇旺盛发动的，都会出痘。想知道出痘疹的日期，以官鬼生旺的年、月、日来断。想知道痘疹的稀密：鬼衰、子孙旺，以及子孙持身、世的，一定稀疏明朗；鬼旺、子孙衰，以及鬼持身、世的，一定稠密。父母爻动的重，兄爻动的轻。福神临在空、死、墓、绝、胎上，以及受到刑、害、克、冲的，性命一定难保。
  The Pressure line is the Subject. If the ghost does not appear in the hexagram, or changes into Death, tomb, Extinction, Womb or Void, or is punished, harmed, controlled or broken, the pox will not come out. If the ghost is weak, dead or wounded but changes into Birth or strength, or there is no ghost but the Month stands at Office, or the ghost is still and the day is exactly its Birth or strength, or a Fire ghost with the Snake is strong and moving, the pox will come out. To know when the pox will come out, judge by the year, month or day in which the Pressure line is born or strong. To know whether it will be sparse or dense: if the ghost is weak and Output strong, or Output holds the Body or the Self, it will surely be sparse and clear; if the ghost is strong and Output weak, or the ghost holds the Body or the Self, it will surely be dense. If Resource moves it is heavy, if Peer moves it is light. If Fortune falls on Void, Death, tomb, Extinction or Womb, or is punished, harmed, controlled or clashed, his life can hardly be preserved.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.18** 问发热是不是出痘：旧法说官鬼旺的就是出花痘，野鹤试过不灵验，说那是揣摩出来的说法，只看子孙的衰旺就行，子孙旺的，出花也没有妨碍。子孙被日月、世爻克，卦又是六冲，是花还没开就先谢了，是不祥的兆头。
  To ask whether a fever is smallpox: the old method says a strong Pressure line means smallpox is flowering; the Old Man tried it and it did not prove true, saying it was a guess, and one need only look at whether Output is strong or weak: if Output is strong, no harm comes even if the flowers come out. Output controlled by the Day, the Month and the Self line, with the hexagram Six-Clash, means the flowers fade before they open, an ill omen.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.19** 平常问什么时候出花，拿鬼爻当用神：鬼爻静的，逢到值日、逢到冲的时候；鬼爻动的，逢到值日、逢到合；鬼爻衰的，在生旺的年份；鬼旺、鬼多的，在墓库的年岁；鬼空、鬼破的，在填实的时候；鬼伏藏的，在它出现的年岁。已经见花来问吉凶的，卦忌六冲，还忌子孙变鬼、鬼变子、父变子、子变父、子孙伏藏又空又破，都不是吉兆。
  In ordinary times, when asking when the flowers will come out, the Pressure line is the Subject: if it is still, at a day it rules or a clash; if moving, when it rules the day or meets a combination; if weak, in a year of birth or strength; if strong or many, in the year of its tomb; if Void or broken, at the time it is filled; if hidden, in the year it appears. For one who asks about fortune when the flowers have already come out, the hexagram must avoid Six-Clash, and also Output changing into Pressure, Pressure into Output, Resource into Output, Output into Resource, and Output hidden and also Void and broken: none of these is a lucky sign.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.20** 问花出得稀还是密：鬼爻旺相、临着日月、又有动爻生扶的，花一定稠密；如果遇到空、破、休囚、入墓入绝，痘就稀疏明朗。鬼在乾宫发动，多长在头上；鬼在坤卦摇动，肚子上成丛；艮宫多在手上，兑宫多在口上，坎、离在上半身，震、巽在下半身。颜色：火鬼紫红，金鬼虚白，水鬼要防发黑塌陷，木鬼又杂又细，土鬼肿大。
  To ask whether the flowers will be sparse or dense: if the Pressure line is strong, sits on the Day or Month and is generated and supported by a moving line, the flowers are surely dense; if it meets Void or a break, or is resting, trapped, in a tomb or extinct, the pox are sparse and clear. Pressure moving in the Qian palace: mostly on the head; stirring in Kun: clusters on the belly; Gen, more on the hands; Dui, more on the mouth; Kan and Li, the upper body; Zhen and Xun, the lower body. As to colour: a Fire Pressure, purple-red; a Metal Pressure, pale white; a Water Pressure, beware of black and sunken pox; a Wood Pressure, mixed and fine; an Earth Pressure, swollen and large.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.21** 上面问的几件事应当分开占，不能用一个卦兼着断，大体上全看子孙爻。子孙临着日月、又被日月和动爻生扶，动了变出吉的，不受刑伤冲克，爻又不乱动的，花虽然密，也可以保全性命；痘就算塌陷了，仍然能收到效果。
  The matters asked above should be divined separately and cannot be judged from one hexagram together; in general everything depends on the Output line. If Output sits on the Day or Month, is generated and supported by the Day, Month or a moving line, moves and changes into something lucky, suffers no punishment, harm, clash or control, and the lines do not move wildly, then though the flowers are dense the child can survive whole, and even if the pox sink, the treatment still takes effect.

## 取象(Reading the images: colour, direction and kind of person or spirit)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/取象.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSQS.12** 占庄稼的歌里，第六爻代表种田的人，主灾祸；第六爻被官鬼克，担忧病疾。
  In the verse on crops, the sixth line stands for the farmer and means calamity; if Pressure controls the sixth line, there is worry over illness.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.04** 月建直符这一段说：月破的那个位置是白虎，这个月里有人生病，阴是女的、阳是男的；子孙、兄弟、父母、妻财这几类亲人，都可以预先判断是谁；如果又带着杀、和鬼在一起，主孝服；在外象，是出远路的人的事。
  The passage on the on-duty spirit of the Month says: the break of the Month is the White Tiger, and within that month someone is ill, yin being a woman and yang a man; the relatives of the four kinds Output, Peer, Resource and Wealth can all be judged in advance; if it also carries a killing and sits with the ghost, it means mourning dress; in the outer image it concerns a traveller on a far road.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.38** 白虎属金，兑旺乾相，旺在秋，属阴，是西方之神；它若持世和交重，是患病、丧死、破财，附在六爻上没有不凶的。日六神内动：白虎内动，惊而忧疾病。白虎临在财上，损害、病和忧愁。玄武入土，家中有疾病。白虎空亡，病没有事。
  The White Tiger belongs to Metal, strong in Dui and helped by Qian, strong in autumn, yin, and the spirit of the west; if it holds the Self or is crossed and heavy it means illness, mourning and death, and loss of wealth, and attached to the six lines it is never not ominous. The day spirit moving inside: the White Tiger moving inside frightens and worries about illness. The White Tiger on Wealth: harm, illness and worry. The Dark Tortoise entering Earth: illness in the house. The White Tiger Void: the illness comes to nothing.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.41** 福德（子孙）所主的类象里，有缉捕、医药、闲人、鱼贩。官鬼是鬼爻，独发主病灾，是损失的兆；官鬼是神、邪、病、祟、盗。来情看冲克哪一爻：冲克子孙爻，是冲福瑞的物件和医药。
  Among the images governed by Output (Fortune-and-Virtue) are arrests, medicine, idlers and fishmongers. The Pressure line is the ghost line, and moving alone it means illness and calamity, a sign of loss; the Pressure line is gods, evil, illness, curses and thieves. The reason for the divination is looked at by which line is clashed or controlled: a clash on the Output line is a clash with lucky things and medicine.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.43** 螣蛇入火，主瘟气、麻疹、痘疹的病；入金，怕遇上病得半死；若爻入水，病来伤人，小孩要退一双。白虎临在子孙位，子孙今年忌；临在财、妻妾位，恐怕有人灾殃；临在父母位，父母切忌；临在兄弟宫，不久入黄泉。玄武在牛、猪空亡处当着，瘴气死人成为瘟疫。有长期生病的人，是八纯卦；有妻子病，是身克财；重丧，是鬼化财；有长辈不安，是青龙入水、患疮；有聋人，是兑卦入乾。
  The Coiling Snake entering Fire: plague, measles and smallpox; entering Metal: one fears meeting an illness half deadly; if the line enters Water the illness comes to injure, and the children retreat by a pair. The White Tiger sitting on the Output place: Output must beware this year; on Wealth and the wife's or concubine's place: fear a calamity to a person; on Resource: the parents must beware; on Peer: before long he enters the Yellow Springs. The Dark Tortoise standing at Chou and Hai Void: miasma deaths become an epidemic. A long-standing sick person: a pure hexagram; a wife's illness: the Body controlling Wealth; a double mourning: the ghost changing into Wealth; an elder unwell: the Azure Dragon entering Water, with sores; a deaf person: Dui changing into Qian.
- **LY.ILL.GLJ.14** 进门的时候看到的人和物、听到的话，先断一个大概，讲到病的几句是：慌慌张张来问事，必凶，病的人躺在床上；进门先见一个老年男子，病人平安，没有损伤，像枯木碰上了春天；进门看见小孩哭哭啼啼，家里不吉利，破财、病患都有；进门见遇三个人说话，官司里官鬼发动，病人最要紧的是（见到）鸟、鸡、子之类，阎王一定要请他回去；进门见一盏灯，病人见凶星，要斋神求福，保祷度过就不相牵连；进门一炷香，家宅大吉大昌，官司马上消散，病人得以离床。
  What is seen and heard on entering the door gives a first rough judgment, and the lines that speak of illness are these: if he comes in a flurry to ask, it is surely ominous, and the sick are in bed; if an old man is seen first on entering, the sick one is safe and unharmed, like a withered tree meeting spring; if a child is seen crying on entering, the household is unlucky, and loss of money and illness are all present; if on entering he meets three people talking, then in an official matter Pressure moves, and the sick one's chief matter is (the sight of) a bird, a hen or a child, and Yama will surely call him back; if on entering he sees a lamp, the sick one meets an ominous star, and he should keep the fast to the spirits and ask for blessing, and with prayer he passes through without being dragged in; if on entering there is a stick of incense, the household is greatly fortunate and prosperous, the official matter dissolves at once, and the sick one gets up from his bed.
- **LY.ILL.YY.02** 鬼谷把六爻这样分：六爻是头脑，配黄泉；五爻是心肺，配棺椁；四爻是脾脏，配福德；三爻是肝肾，配哭声；二爻是腿和大腿，配吊客；初爻是足，配丧门。
  Guigu divides the six lines as follows: the sixth line is the head and brain, matched with the Yellow Springs; the fifth is the heart and lungs, with the Coffin; the fourth is the spleen, with Fortune; the third is the liver and kidneys, with Wailing; the second is the legs and thighs, with the Guest of Condolence; the first is the feet, with the Mourning Gate.
- **LY.ILL.YY.09** 僧一行占病的办法，从八卦里取用：父亲用乾，母亲用坤，长子用震，次子用坎，三子用艮，长女用巽，次女用离，三女用兑。如果所用的卦不出现，或者是替别人占，就按年龄：一岁到二十岁，男的用艮，女的用兑；二十一岁到四十岁，男的用坎，女的用离；四十一岁到六十岁，男的用震，女的用巽；六十岁以上，男的用乾，女的用坤。正卦里没有用的象，就取伏卦。主象变出死、墓、绝，或者变出克它、破它的，都主死。
  The monk Yixing's method of divining illness takes the Subject from the eight trigrams: father takes Qian, mother Kun, eldest son Zhen, second son Kan, third son Gen, eldest daughter Xun, second daughter Li, third daughter Dui. If the trigram to be used does not appear, or one is divining for another, go by age: from one to twenty, a male uses Gen and a female Dui; from twenty-one to forty, a male Kan and a female Li; from forty-one to sixty, a male Zhen and a female Xun; over sixty, a male Qian and a female Kun. If the main hexagram has no image to use, take the hidden hexagram. If the main image changes into Death, tomb or Extinction, or into what controls or breaks it, in every case it means death.

## 风险(Warnings: what to guard against)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/疾病/风险.md`

- **LY.ILL.BSQS.11** 神杀断例说：三丘、五墓、飞帘、浴盆这四样煞，不要拿来问病；占怀孕见了它们，也主忧虑惊恐。
  The verse of examples on judging by spirit-stars says: do not ask about illness when the Three Mounds, the Five Tombs, the Flying Curtain and the Bathing Basin are present; in divining a pregnancy too they bring worry and fright.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.17** 占迁居的歌里说：朱雀又临在官鬼爻上，官司口舌就有来由；再克世、又持世，生病会遇到危险困厄，打官司会被囚禁。
  The verse on moving house says: if the Vermilion Bird sits on a Pressure line, there is cause for lawsuits and quarrels; if it also controls and holds the Self line, illness meets danger and distress, and a lawsuit ends in imprisonment.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.51** 岁前神煞：十二个神煞都从太岁起，顺着一位一位轮下去：太岁、剑锋、伏尸同位，第二位太阳、天空，第三位丧门主孝服，第四位勾绞、贯索，第五位官符、五鬼，第六位死符、小耗，第七位栏干、大耗，第八位暴败、天厄，第九位飞廉、白虎，第十位福德、卷舌，第十一位天狗、吊客，第十二位病符，千万不要遇上。
  The stars before the Year: all twelve start from Tai Sui and go round forward place by place: Tai Sui, Sword Edge and Hidden Corpse in one place; second Great Yang and Heavenly Emptiness; third the Mourning Gate, meaning mourning dress; fourth Hook-Strangle and Cord-Through; fifth Official Tally and the Five Ghosts; sixth Death Tally and Small Loss; seventh Railing and Great Loss; eighth Sudden Ruin and Heavenly Distress; ninth Feilian and the White Tiger; tenth Fortune Virtue and the Curled Tongue; eleventh the Heavenly Dog and the Guest of Condolence; twelfth the Sickness Tally, which one must not meet.
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.52** 神煞歌例点明占病大忌的五个煞：天哭杀（正五九月羊、二六十月猴、三七十一月鸡、四八十二月犬）；四季小杀（春羊夏龙秋牛冬犬，临身命，轻灾也凶）；三丘五墓（春丑夏辰秋未冬戌为三丘，五墓取对宫，病人作福也难留）；勾陈杀（从辰逆行，占病及讼大忌）；白虎杀（从申顺行，占病大忌）。
  The verses on spirit-stars name five killings that are great taboos in divining illness: the Weeping killing (the sheep in the first, fifth and ninth months, the monkey in the second, sixth and tenth, the rooster in the third, seventh and eleventh, the dog in the fourth, eighth and twelfth); the small killing of the four seasons (the sheep in spring, the dragon in summer, the ox in autumn, the dog in winter; on the Body or the natal line even a light misfortune is ominous); the Three Mounds and Five Tombs (Chou in spring, Chen in summer, Wei in autumn, Xu in winter are the Three Mounds, the Five Tombs are taken from the opposite palace, and a sick person can hardly be kept even by making offerings); the Hook Earth killing (backward from Chen, a great taboo for illness and lawsuits); the White Tiger killing (forward from Shen, a great taboo for illness).
- **LY.ILL.BSQS.55** 占船家（以船为家的人家）的一章里说：螣蛇爻发动，主有暴病的忧虑。
  The chapter on divining a boat household says: with the Coiling Snake line moving, there is worry of a sudden illness.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.06** 日下大杀按日干排：甲乙日在亥（猪），丙丁日在未（羊），戊己日在戌（狗），庚辛日在寅（虎），壬癸日在巳（蛇）；父母、子孙遇到这个杀，必有悲伤号哭进到内堂。自注说：占病要忌这个杀，爻动了才断。
  The Great Killing under the Day is laid out by the Day stem: Hai (the pig) on Jia and Yi days, Wei (the sheep) on Bing and Ding days, Xu (the dog) on Wu and Ji days, Yin (the tiger) on Geng and Xin days, Si (the snake) on Ren and Gui days; if Resource or Output meets this killing, wailing and grief are sure to enter the inner hall. Its own note says: this killing is to be avoided in an illness divination, and is judged only when the line moves.
- **LY.ILL.BSYG.07** 推占来情的歌里有一联：卦身在世爻上、想出门远行的时候，鬼又正好当门，官司、疾病都要忌。自注说，「当门」指鬼在第三、四爻。
  In the verses on reading the purpose of the asker there is a couplet: when the Body is on the Self line and one wants to go travelling, and the ghost stands at the door as well, official trouble and illness are to be feared. Its own note says that 'at the door' means the ghost is on the third or fourth line.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.29** 生助官鬼的，不过是妻财，不宜发动，动了衰鬼就变成旺鬼；旺鬼遇到它，势头更凶。比如申日占得离卦，鬼临应爻克世，本来不是好兆，再加妻财发动、生助鬼爻，凶更厉害，这就是助鬼伤身；凡是卦里鬼爻克身、世的，没有财，凶有限；若有两个财爻都动，祸就大凶。占病遇到它：若占的是父母，必定顷刻危险；其余人问病，也是沉重缠绵，一时难以脱身；若是替本人占，或是因财致病，或是房事过频，是大凶的兆。以上各种事，若有子孙发动、解神来救，或许可以反凶成吉、变祸为祥。
  What generates and helps the Pressure line is only Wealth, which should not move, for moving it turns a weak ghost into a strong ghost; a strong ghost meeting it, its force is fiercer still. For example, divining the hexagram Li on a Shen day, the ghost sits on the Other line and controls the Self, which is not a good sign to begin with, and Wealth moving in addition to generate and help the ghost line makes the ill fiercer still: this is helping the ghost to injure the Body; whenever the ghost line in a hexagram controls the Body or the Self, with no Wealth the ill is limited, and if two Wealth lines both move the calamity is greatly ominous. Divining illness and meeting it: if a parent (the Resource line) is divined for, it will surely bring peril in a moment; for other askers about illness it is also heavy and lingering, and hard to shake off in the end; if it is divined in one's own name, it may be illness brought on by wealth, or excess of sexual activity, and it is a sign of great ill. In all the above matters, if Output moves and a resolving spirit comes to rescue, it may be possible to turn ill into luck and calamity into fortune.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.33** 勾陈杀：正月从辰起，逆行向卯，轮十二个位置；占病时它发动，凶。天哭杀：正五九月是羊（未）、二六十月是猴（申）、三七十一月是鸡（酉）、四八十二月是犬（戌）；若临在身、世上，就忧愁哭泣；占病时征兆发动，主大凶。四季小杀：春忌羊头（未）、夏是龙（辰）、秋牛（丑）、冬犬（戌）；占病忌它发动。旌旗杀：春兔（卯）、夏鼠（子）、秋鸡（酉）、冬马（午）；占病忌它发动。
  The Hook Earth killing: it starts from Chen in the first month and goes backward towards Mao, rotating through the twelve places; in divining illness, if it moves it is ominous. The Weeping killing: in the first, fifth and ninth months the sheep (Wei), in the second, sixth and tenth the monkey (Shen), in the third, seventh and eleventh the rooster (You), in the fourth, eighth and twelfth the dog (Xu); if it sits on the Body or the Self there is worry and weeping; in divining illness, if the sign moves it means great ill. The Small killing of the four seasons: in spring avoid the sheep's head (Wei), in summer the dragon (Chen), in autumn the ox (Chou), in winter the dog (Xu); in divining illness it must not move. The Banner killing: in spring the rabbit (Mao), in summer the rat (Zi), in autumn the rooster (You), in winter the horse (Wu); in divining illness it must not move.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.35** 神煞月令表里凶神的八行，断语都说到病：岁杀，病而有官灾，凶，月杀也这样断；飞杀，突然发病又有飞鸟，凶；阴杀，阴谋和病患，凶；小杀，小孩的疾病，凶；天杀，疾病困厄，凶，和丧杀一样断；地杀，疾病凶、恶而凶；天阴，女人的疾病，凶；大月，流行的疾病，凶。
  Eight fierce rows of the table of spirits and killings by month whose judgments speak of illness: the Year Killing, illness with official calamity, ominous, and the Month Killing is judged the same way; the Flying Killing, sudden illness and flying birds, ominous; the Yin Killing, secret schemes and illness, ominous; the Small Killing, illness of small children, ominous; the Heaven Killing, illness and distress, ominous, judged like the Mourning Killing; the Earth Killing, illness is ominous, vicious and ominous; Heaven Yin, illness of a woman, ominous; the Great Month, epidemic illness, ominous.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.50** 断易天机在神煞月令表前列了一份十二位的流年神煞：从当年的年支（太岁）起，顺着数下去，依次是太岁、太阳、丧门、太阴、官符、死符、岁破、龙德、白虎、福德、吊客、病符。
  Before the monthly table of spirit-stars the Duanyi Tianji lists twelve year-stars: starting from the branch of the current year (Tai Sui) and counting forward, they are Tai Sui, Great Yang, Mourning Gate, Great Yin, Official Tally, Death Tally, Year-break, Dragon Virtue, White Tiger, Fortune Virtue, Guest of Condolence and Sickness Tally.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.52** 占家宅时：吉神遇上绝处逢生，家宅又有兴隆的样子；凶神遇上绝处逢生，灾祸正要退下去，祸又转回来——病治好了，官事又到了。后面还有一句「占国同此义推」，占国家大事也照这个意思推。
  Divining the house: when a lucky spirit meets life in a place of extinction, the house again has the look of prosperity; when an ominous spirit meets life in a place of extinction, the calamity is about to withdraw and then misfortune comes again: the illness is treated and mends, and an official matter arrives. The book adds, 'divining the state is inferred in the same sense'.
- **LY.ILL.DYTJ.53** 随官入墓有三种（身、世、命随鬼入墓），不问占什么事都不是吉兆。占官司遇到它，恐怕有牢狱禁系的忧患，或者官司散了而人有危险，或者官司进行中出现疾病；出行遇到它，多半去不成，硬要去更不好，一定是去了以后生病。
  Following the ghost into a tomb has three kinds (the Body, the Self or the natal line following the ghost into a tomb), and whatever is divined it is no lucky sign. Divining a lawsuit and meeting it, there may be the worry of prison and confinement, or the lawsuit breaks up and the person is in danger, or illness appears in the course of the lawsuit; divining a journey and meeting it, one most likely cannot go, and if one goes it is the worse, and surely there will be illness after leaving.
- **LY.ILL.EL.01** 这一章讲防灾避患，吉的事用子孙爻为主，凶的事用官鬼爻为凭。防灾避患最忌官爻：官爻安静、休囚，祸招不来；遇到旺、遇到生，灾很快就起；官爻没有或落陷，病就悄悄消了；官爻不伤世和卦身，就没有魔障；如果发动，就难逃；日、月制住它，殃咎就散；子孙一动，邪妖就灭。注说：占自己有没有病，要仔细看官鬼，鬼逢生旺，灾一定来得快；落空亡或不上卦，永远不会成灾；鬼发动怎么会不生灾？不克世和卦身，终究没有疾厄；官爻虽然动，只要子孙同时发动、或者子孙临日月、或者子孙在世爻上，万祸都悄悄消了；没有鬼就没有灾。
  This chapter is about guarding against calamity and avoiding trouble: for good outcomes take Output as the main line, for bad ones the Pressure line as the ground. To guard against calamity one fears the Pressure line: still, resting or trapped, it brings no misfortune; meeting strength or generation, calamity rises quickly; absent or fallen, the illness quietly fades; if it does not harm the Self and the Body there is no demonic obstruction; if it moves, it can hardly be escaped; if the Day and Month control it, the calamity disperses; when Output moves, evil and demons are wiped out. The commentary says: in divining whether one has illness, examine the Pressure line closely; if the ghost meets generation or strength, calamity is sure to come quickly; if it falls into Void or is absent from the hexagram, it will never cause trouble; if the ghost moves, how could it not breed calamity? But if it does not control the Self and the Body, in the end there is no illness; even if the Pressure line moves, if Output moves with it, or Output sits on the Day or Month, or Output is on the Self, ten thousand misfortunes quietly fade; no ghost, no calamity.
- **LY.ILL.EL.23** 以用爻为主，福德为凭。天行酷暑，难道没有避暑的办法？人染了患灾，也有躲灾的地方。悟道要选清闲的地方，修真要访幽僻的住处。这四样都宜子孙发动，各样都忌官鬼兴旺；占自己，怕世爻休囚墓绝，问别人，用爻和体怕空亡。注说：凡是占避暑、养病、悟道、修真这些事，都宜福德发动，各自忌讳官爻发动；占自己，以世爻为主，卜别人，以应象为凭；如果问亲人，要推用象，若临旺相、或遇生扶，却灾能脱身、悟道能成功；用神如果遇月破、旬空、或临墓绝，不光没有福，反而招来祸殃。子孙旺相或发动的，参禅打坐没有不成功的，避暑却灾必定如愿；官鬼交重或旺相的，心里想求安，反而遭遇不测。
  The Subject line is the main line, and the fortune spirit is the ground. When the heat of the season rages, is there not a way to escape it? When a person is infected with calamity, there is a place to ward it off. To realise the Way, choose a quiet and leisured place; to cultivate the truth, visit a secluded dwelling. For all four Output moving is favourable, and in each a thriving Pressure line is feared; in divining oneself, the Self line resting, in a tomb or in Extinction is feared, and in asking about another, the Subject and the body fearing Void. The commentary says: in all matters of escaping the heat, convalescing, realising the Way and cultivating the truth, the fortune spirit moving is favourable, and in each the Pressure line moving is feared; in divining oneself the Self line is the main line, and in divining another the Other image is the ground; if asking about a relative, work out the Subject image, and if it sits on strength or meets generation and support, one escapes calamity and the Way is achieved; if the Subject meets Month-break or Void, or sits in a tomb or Extinction, there is not only no blessing but a misfortune is brought on. With Output strong or moving, sitting in meditation is never without success, and escaping the heat and warding off calamity are sure to be as wished; with the Pressure line moving or strong, the heart seeks peace and on the contrary meets the unforeseen.
- **LY.ILL.EL.24** 父母爻扶身，宜去书馆；福神生世，利于去僧堂。注说：父母爻生世，宜去文墨的地方和长辈的家；子孙生世，利于住在僧道的门下和晚辈小孩的地方；兄弟生世，宜去兄弟朋友的家；妻财生世，应去妻族亲友、仆隶下人的住处；官鬼生世，偏宜在官宦人家安身。一卦都安，才是修行的路；六爻都破，怎么是养静的窝？用爻旺的遇上青龙，宜去这个地方；忌象兴旺又加白虎，就不要去那一家。注说：各爻都占得随住处而安；一卦六冲，往来不定；用爻有气，又值青龙，利于有所往；忌象交重，又临白虎，这个地方不要去。
  The Resource line supporting the Self suits going to a schoolroom, and the fortune spirit generating the Self favours the monks' hall. The commentary says: the Resource line generating the Self suits places of letters and the homes of elders; Output generating the Self favours living with monks and Daoists and among the young; Peer generating the Self suits going to the homes of brothers and friends; Wealth generating the Self, go to the dwellings of the wife's kin, friends, servants and underlings; the Pressure line generating the Self suits above all lodging in an official's house. When the whole hexagram is at peace, that is the road of cultivation; when all six lines are broken, how could it be a nest for quiet nurture? When the Subject line is strong and meets the Azure Dragon, it suits going to this place; when the adversary image thrives and adds the White Tiger, do not go to that house. The commentary says: if all lines are divined, one is at peace wherever one lodges; a hexagram of six clashes means going and returning are unsettled; if the Subject line has qi and also holds the Azure Dragon, it is favourable to go; if the adversary image moves and sits on the White Tiger, do not go to this place.
- **LY.ILL.EL.25** 动的爻见到螣蛇，还怕惊恐；交遇驿马，更担心奔波劳顿；玄武值鬼爻兴旺，失财欠利；朱雀临兄爻发动，饶舌不宁。艮卦有官，不要登山岭；坎宫见鬼，不要去江湖；在震，正东惹祸；临乾，西北招殃；兑里财变官爻，尤忌色迷；巽里兄弟的鬼象，风患难防。注说：螣蛇动，如果遇上惊惶；驿马临兄爻、鬼爻动，不利于出游；驿马动，临财、福，千里都安；鬼临玄武发动，必遭失脱；朱雀值兄爻发动，是非要谨慎。艮宫鬼动，忌去东北，不要去山林；坎卦鬼动，北方不利，水池不合宜；震宫鬼动，不要待在船里，并且忌向东行；乾宫西北为凶，不要游庙宇；兑宫鬼动，必有吵闹是非，若是财变官爻，不要贪美色；巽卦鬼兴以及兄爻化鬼的，都怕冒风；离宫鬼动，虑见火惊；坤卦鬼兴，不要住在墓旁。主合咸池，不要贪美色；用爻冲华盖、或华盖克用爻、或鬼临华盖动，都怕僧道的门下惹祸，如果去避暑却灾就成凶，只有悟道修行，反而成正果。又看世爻在内卦、兴亲宫，宜居在近处；世临他宫、兴外卦，利在远方；内卦旺，本地如意；外卦旺，他乡遂意。
  A moving line seeing the Coiling Snake still fears fright; meeting the Post Horse, it further worries about rushing about; the Dark Tortoise on a thriving Pressure line loses wealth and lacks profit; the Vermilion Bird on a moving Peer line brings gossip and unrest. Gen with a Pressure line: do not climb the mountain ridges; Kan with a ghost: do not go to river and lake; in Zhen, misfortune in the due east; on Qian, calamity from the northwest; in Dui, Wealth changing into a Pressure line, beware above all of being lost in lust; in Xun, the ghost image of Peer, wind sickness is hard to guard against. The commentary says: the Coiling Snake moving, one may meet a fright; the Post Horse on a Peer or a moving Pressure line is unfavourable for travel; the Post Horse moving on Wealth or the fortune spirit, all is safe for a thousand li; the ghost on the Dark Tortoise moving, loss is sure; the Vermilion Bird on a moving Peer line, take care of quarrels. A ghost moving in the Gen palace: avoid going northeast, do not go to the mountains and forest; a ghost stirring in Kan: the north is unfavourable and pools are unsuitable; a ghost moving in Zhen: do not stay in a boat, and avoid going east; the northwest of Qian is ominous, do not visit temples; a ghost moving in Dui, there will surely be an uproar and quarrel, and if Wealth changes into a Pressure line, do not covet beauty; a ghost thriving in Xun, or a Peer line turning into a ghost, both fear exposure to wind; a ghost moving in Li, fear a fright from fire; a ghost thriving in Kun, do not live beside graves. If the Subject combines with Xianchi, do not covet beauty; if the Subject clashes Huagai, or Huagai controls the Subject line, or a ghost on Huagai moves, trouble is feared at the doors of monks and Daoists, and if one goes to escape the heat and ward off calamity it turns ominous, whereas realising the Way and cultivating oneself on the contrary bears true fruit. Also look at the Self: in the inner trigram, thriving in a kin palace, it suits living near; the Self in another palace, thriving in the outer trigram, favours a far place; a strong inner trigram means the home region is as one wishes, and a strong outer trigram means a foreign land is as one wishes.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.55** 应爻发动来生、合世爻，是有人来问安：带财爻、子孙的，一定有送东西的；带兄弟的，只是来看望。如果应爻虽然生合，却变出妻财反过来刑克世爻（或者应爻生合世爻而刑克主象），要是有人送东西，千万不要吃，不然反而受伤害。占长辈的病，尤其要忌。
  When the Other line moves to generate or combine with the Self line, someone will come to ask after him: if it carries Wealth or Output, there will surely be gifts; if Peer, only a plain visit. But if the Other line, though generating or combining, changes out Wealth that turns to punish or control the Self line (or the Other line generates and combines with the Self line while punishing or controlling the main image), then any gift sent should on no account be eaten, or it does harm instead; in divining an elder it is even more to be avoided.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.127** 《黄金策》家宅章说：「丧门星加上白虎星，要担忧疾病和丧亡。」旧注：丧门、白虎叫作「四利三元」，是凶神，见后面凶煞一节；如果见到这一爻发动，就有病患连绵、丧亡接连而来，种种不吉祥，要仔细审察。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on the household says: 'The Mourning Gate joined by the White Tiger: beware of illness and death.' The old commentary: the Mourning Gate and the White Tiger are called the 'Four Gains and Three Origins' and are ominous spirits, to be seen below among the ominous killings; if this line is seen moving, there is illness dragging on, deaths following one on another and all kinds of ill omens, to be examined with care.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.128** 《黄金策》出行章说：「鬼临在飞爻的位置，还没离开门户就迟疑不前。」旧注解释：官鬼加上白虎、丧门、吊客、死符、病符这些星，恐怕有丧亡、疾病的事。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on travel says: 'With the ghost on the flying place, one has not yet left the door and hesitates.' The old commentary explains: Pressure joined by the White Tiger, Mourning Gate, Guest of Condolence, Death Tally or Sickness Tally may bring a death or an illness.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.138** 《黄金策》仕宦章说：「鬼杀伤了身，灾殃就免不了。」旧注：官鬼发动，生合世爻的是用神，伤克世爻的是鬼杀；用神扶世，一定有进取；鬼杀伤身，一定有凶祸，按化出来的六亲来断；如果不是上面这些，就是自己一定有灾病，世爻落空避开就没有妨碍。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on official careers says: 'A ghost killing harming the Body: calamity cannot be avoided.' The old commentary: when the Pressure line moves, one that generates or combines with the Self line is the Subject, and one that harms or controls the Self line is a ghost killing; the Subject supporting the Self line brings advancement, a ghost killing harming the Body brings ill fortune, judged by the relation it turns into; if it is not the above, then he himself is sure to have calamity or illness, and no harm if the Self line is Void and escapes.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.143** 《黄金策》求馆章说：「身的位置受了伤，即使成了也不利。」旧注：世爻和身被月建、日辰、动爻刑克，即使有能成的样子，日后也不称心；如果是鬼爻来刑克，就有官非、疾病一类的事。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on seeking a tutoring post says: 'With the Body's place harmed, even if it succeeds it is unfavourable.' The old commentary: if the Self and the Body are punished or controlled by the Month, the Day or a moving line, though it looks as if it can succeed, afterwards it is still not as wished; if it is the ghost line that punishes or controls it, there are official troubles or illness and the like.
- **LY.ILL.HJC.146** 《黄金策》娼家章说：「官鬼落在刑的地方，门上就招来恶病的人。」旧注：鬼爻带着刑爻、生合世和身，多半招来带病的人往来；（鬼）和世爻生合、又和财刑冲，要防妓女也被传染；鬼化成鬼，或者化成忌爻刑克世、身，恐怕因为留宿的客人招来祸事；有制伏就没有妨碍，有救就可以解开。王洪绪：鬼带刑爻生合世、身，多招恶病的人往来；与世生合、与财刑冲，要防妓女也被传染。
  The Huangjin Ce's chapter on courtesan houses says: 'With the officer in a place of punishment, the door draws people with vile illness.' The old commentary: a ghost carrying a punishing line and generating or combining with the Self and Body draws many people carrying illness; if it generates or combines with the Self line and punishes or clashes Wealth, beware that the courtesan too is infected; a ghost turning into a ghost, or turning into a taboo line that punishes or controls the Self and Body, may bring misfortune from a lodging guest; if it is restrained there is no harm, and if there is rescue it can be resolved. Wang Hongxu: a ghost carrying a punishing line and generating or combining with the Self and Body draws many people with vile illness; if it generates or combines with the Self line and punishes or clashes Wealth, beware that the courtesan too is infected.
- **LY.ILL.YM.28** 一开始占筮来预防疾病的：用神坏了，病就起来；鬼发动，病就发作。遇到时行的灾病，专门看鬼，鬼发动，就是瘟疫来扰他的家，只能修身反省、等着它过去。哪个人的本命冲犯了鬼，是天降的祸；哪个六亲变出了鬼，是人自己招的；鬼变成哪个人，是流行的灾。随墓、助伤，大凶，可怕。
  In a first divination to guard against illness: when the Subject line is spoiled the illness arises, and when the ghost moves the illness breaks out. When a seasonal calamity is going about, look at the ghost alone; if it moves, an epidemic is troubling his household, and there is nothing to do but cultivate oneself, reflect and wait. Whichever natal branch offends the ghost, it is a heavenly misfortune; whichever relation turns into the ghost, it is man-made; into which person the ghost turns, that is a spreading calamity. Following into the tomb and helping to harm are greatly ominous, and to be feared.
- **LY.ILL.YM.32** 所以鬼动是招来祸的缘由，福动是远离灾的要领；福神可以趋近，祸神可以躲开。于是鬼在坎宫发动，就不要贪酒食；在兑宫发动，就不要迷恋欲色；震宫忌坐船，巽宫忌进园子，乾宫不可以喜欢去僧房，艮宫不可以喜欢住山里，坤宫不吃牛，离宫是因为爱吃烤炙的东西。注说：如果是子孙在这一宫发动，这个地方反而有利有福，和前面说的正好相反；乾宫利于僧堂，坤宫利于田地，坎宫适合江海，艮宫适合山，离宫乐于文章，兑宫爱好酒，震宫喜欢舟车，巽宫喜欢园囿。
  So a moving ghost is the cause that brings misfortune, and a moving Output is the key to keeping calamity away: the fortune spirit can be sought and the misfortune spirit avoided. When the ghost moves in Kan, do not be greedy for wine and food; in Dui, do not be lost in desire and lust; for Zhen avoid boarding a boat, for Xun avoid entering a garden; for Qian one must not favour monks' rooms, for Gen one must not delight in mountain dwellings; for Kun do not eat beef, and for Li it is from a fondness for roasted food. The note says: if Output moves in the same palace, that place is advantageous and blessed, the reverse of what was said before; Qian favours the monks' hall and Kun the fields, Kan suits river and sea and Gen the mountain, Li delights in writing and Dui in wine, Zhen likes boats and carriages and Xun gardens and parks.
- **LY.ILL.ZS.17** 占瘟病，卦里子水克世爻、月建也克世爻，是大凶的兆头；要避开，就搬到南方，夜里睡南面的床，让火旺、水枯，可以保平安。
  In divining an epidemic illness, when Zi Water controls the Self line and the Month controls it too, it is a sign of great misfortune; to avoid it, move to the south and sleep at night on a south-facing bed, so that Fire is strong and Water dries up, and he may be kept safe.
- **LY.ILL.ZSZ.01** 周易尚占旁通部的「四时吉凶神杀例」表列了三个占病要忌的煞，位置按春、夏、秋、冬的次序排：五墓是未、戌、丑、辰，发动、持（世）都凶；浴盆是辰、未、戌、丑，落在水神（玄武）或水爻上都凶；三丘是丑、辰、未、戌，发动、又有兄弟或官鬼，主死。
  The table of auspicious and ominous spirit-stars of the four seasons in the Parallel Tables of the Zhouyi Shangzhan lists three stars to avoid in asking about illness, set out in the order spring, summer, autumn, winter: the Five Tombs at Wei, Xu, Chou, Chen, ominous when they move or hold the Self; the Bathing Basin at Chen, Wei, Xu, Chou, ominous when it falls on a Water spirit (the Dark Tortoise) or a Water line; the Three Mounds at Chou, Chen, Wei, Xu, which mean death when they move together with Peer or Pressure.
